FILE: scripts\decodePoolTxDataV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET = 7219004800n;

const TARGET_HEX = TARGET
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(16, '0');

const LTS = [
    '99809465000008',
    '99809465000010',
    '99809465000012',
];

function hex8(n: number): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function inspectCell(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth: number
) {
    const pad = '  '.repeat(depth);

    console.log(
        `${pad}${path}: bits=${cell.bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    if (cell.bits.length >= 32) {
        const s = cell.beginParse();
        const op = s.preloadUint(32);

        console.log(
            `${pad}opcode=0x${hex8(op)}`
        );
    }

    /*
     * Print first 128 bytes only.
     */
    const bytes = Math.min(
        Math.floor(cell.bits.length / 8),
        128
    );

    if (bytes > 0) {
        const s = cell.beginParse();
        let line = '';

        for (let i = 0; i < bytes; i++) {
            const b = s.loadUint(8);

            line += b
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, '0');

            if (
                (i + 1) % 32 === 0
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${pad}${line}`
                );
                line = '';
            } else {
                line += ' ';
            }
        }

        if (line.length > 0) {
            console.log(
                `${pad}${line}`
            );
        }
    }

    /*
     * Exact TARGET search.
     */
    if (cell.bits.length >= 64) {
        for (
            let offset = 0;
            offset + 64 <= cell.bits.length;
            offset++
        ) {
            const s = cell.beginParse();

            if (offset > 0) {
                s.skip(offset);
            }

            const value =
                s.loadUintBig(64);

            if (value === TARGET) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `${pad}**************** TARGET FOUND ****************`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}path   : ${path}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}offset : ${offset}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}width  : 64`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}value  : ${value}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}hex    : 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `${pad}************************************************`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        inspectCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function decodeBoc(
    boc: string,
    label: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(label);

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `BOC length: ${boc.length}`
    );

    try {
        const buffer =
            Buffer.from(boc, 'base64');

        console.log(
            `Decoded bytes: ${buffer.length}`
        );

        const cells =
            Cell.fromBoc(buffer);

        console.log(
            `Root cells: ${cells.length}`
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < cells.length;
            i++
        ) {
            inspectCell(
                cells[i],
                `root[${i}]`,
                0
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `BOC decode error: ${
                e instanceof Error
                    ? e.message
                    : String(e)
            }`
        );
    }
}

async function getTransactions() {
    const url =
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions' +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(
            POOL.toString()
        )}` +
        '&limit=20';

    const response =
        await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const json =
        await response.json();

    if (
        !json.ok ||
        !Array.isArray(json.result)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'Invalid Toncenter response'
        );
    }

    return json.result;
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 RAW POOL TRANSACTION DATA DECODER'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Pool: ${POOL.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target: ${TARGET}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target hex: 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Fetching transactions once...'
    );

    const txs =
        await getTransactions();

    console.log(
        `Received ${txs.length} transactions`
    );

    for (
        const wantedLt of LTS
    ) {
        const tx =
            txs.find(
                (x: any) =>
                    x.transaction_id?.lt ===
                    wantedLt
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );

        console.log(
            `LT ${wantedLt}`
        );

        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );

        if (!tx) {
            console.log(
                'Transaction not found in this response.'
            );
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `hash: ${
                tx.transaction_id?.hash ?? ''
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `utime: ${tx.utime ?? ''}`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TOP-LEVEL TRANSACTION FIELDS'
        );

        for (
            const key of Object.keys(tx)
        ) {
            const value =
                tx[key];

            if (
                typeof value === 'string'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${key}: string(${value.length})`
                );
            } else if (
                value === null
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${key}: null`
                );
            } else if (
                Array.isArray(value)
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${key}: array(${value.length})`
                );
            } else if (
                typeof value === 'object'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${key}: object`
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    `${key}: ${typeof value}`
                );
            }
        }

        /*
         * The most important field.
         */
        if (
            typeof tx.data === 'string'
        ) {
            decodeBoc(
                tx.data,
                `LT ${wantedLt} - TRANSACTION DATA`
            );
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'No string transaction data found.'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

import { Cell, Address } from '@ton/core';

const ROOSTER_BODY =
    'te6cckEBAwEA+wABtA+KfqUAAAAAAAAAAGWvMQekAAgBJcKCNcqNEl5nZZFRPVIHIbH+mfdyL0yHcjzn7g37c6MAPGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3IHAOhgQEB4TfAlt+AEkQYMDTZ/VmiNvcexCcb43c5kFbdpMw6Xr9dxAln32QQA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bgAAAADVcWO/AAgBPUi8zardIAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvBNfZ+HI=';

const PTON_BODY =
    'te6cckEBAwEA0wABZAHzg10AAAAAAAAAAEDuVLkoAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvAQHhN8CW34ATx+W4S2lVi0em9t4WAONLV9rDl23ZaiL4mXY/VqMKbfADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhuAAAAANVxY78ACAE9SLzNqt0gB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8E6/avkA==';

function decodeProvidePayload(body: string, name: string) {
    console.log(`\n========================================`);
    console.log(`${name} PROVIDE-LIQUIDITY PAYLOAD`);
    console.log(`========================================`);

    const root = Cell.fromBase64(body);
    const payload = root.refs[0];

    const s = payload.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('\n[ROOT PAYLOAD]');
    console.log('Opcode:', '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'));

    const routerWallet = s.loadAddress();
    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const excessesAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('Router wallet:', routerWallet?.toString());
    console.log('Refund address:', refundAddress?.toString());
    console.log('Excesses address:', excessesAddress?.toString());

    const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('Deadline:', deadline.toString());
    console.log('Remaining root bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining root refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    if (payload.refs.length === 0) {
        console.log('ERROR: inner payload ref missing');
        return;
    }

    const inner = payload.refs[0];
    const i = inner.beginParse();

    console.log('\n[INNER PAYLOAD]');
    console.log('Inner bits:', inner.bits.length);
    console.log('Inner refs:', inner.refs.length);

    // Try to decode the fields according to STON.fi SDK
    // createProvideLiquidityBody:
    // minLpOut
    // receiverAddress
    // bothPositive
    // dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount
    // dexCustomPayload

    try {
        const minLpOut = i.loadCoins();

        console.log('minLpOut:', minLpOut.toString());

        const receiver = i.loadAddress();

        console.log('Receiver:', receiver?.toString());

        const bothPositive = i.loadBit();

        console.log('bothPositive:', bothPositive);

        const forwardGas = i.loadCoins();

        console.log('Forward gas amount:', forwardGas.toString());

        console.log('Remaining inner bits:', i.remainingBits);
        console.log('Remaining inner refs:', i.remainingRefs);

        if (i.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const customPayload = i.loadRef();

            console.log('Custom payload ref: PRESENT');
            console.log('Custom payload bits:', customPayload.bits.length);
            console.log('Custom payload refs:', customPayload.refs.length);

            if (customPayload.bits.length >= 32) {
                const cs = customPayload.beginParse();
                const customOpcode = cs.loadUint(32);

                console.log(
                    'Custom payload first uint32:',
                    '0x' + customOpcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('\nDecode error:', e);
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== STON.fi v2.2 PROVIDE-LIQUIDITY DECODE =====');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');

    decodeProvidePayload(
        ROOSTER_BODY,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    decodeProvidePayload(
        PTON_BODY,
        'PTON'
    );

    console.log('\n===== COMPLETE =====');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

FILE: scripts\decodeProvideLp.ts

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const filePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        'initial_rooster_pool_body.boc'
    );

    const bocBase64 = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8').trim();
    const root = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(bocBase64, 'base64')
    )[0];

    const rootSlice = root.beginParse();

    // Jetton Transfer
    rootSlice.loadUint(32);
    rootSlice.loadUintBig(64);
    rootSlice.loadCoins();
    rootSlice.loadAddress();
    rootSlice.loadAddress();
    rootSlice.loadMaybeRef();
    rootSlice.loadCoins();
    rootSlice.loadBit();

    const payload = rootSlice.loadRef();

    console.log('===== PROVIDE LP PAYLOAD =====');
    console.log('Bits:', payload.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', payload.refs.length);

    const s = payload.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'Decimal:',
        opcode
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining bits:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('\n===== PAYLOAD CELL TREE =====');

    function dumpCell(
        cell: Cell,
        level: number,
        index: string
    ) {
        const indent = ' '.repeat(level * 2);

        console.log(
            `${indent}[${index}] bits=${cell.bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            dumpCell(
                cell.refs[i],
                level + 1,
                `${index}.${i}`
            );
        }
    }

    dumpCell(payload, 0, 'P');

    console.log('\n===== PAYLOAD HEX =====');
    console.log(
        payload.toBoc().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('\nNO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Cell, Address } from '@ton/core';

const ROOSTER_BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101030100fb0001b40f8a7ea5000000000000000065af3107a400080125c28235ca8d125e676591513d520721b1fe99f7722f4c87723ce7ee0dfb73a3003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0dc81c03a1810101e137c096df801244183034d9fd59a236f71ec4271be377399056dda4cc3a5ebf5dc40967df641003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1b800000003559da46c002004f522f336ab74801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f0426e06378';

const PTON_BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101030100d300016401f3835d000000000000000043b9aca00801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f0101e137c096df8013c7e5b84b69558b47a6f6de1600e34b57dac3976dd96a22f899763f56a30a6df003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1b800000003559da46c002004f522f336ab74801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f044bf01448';

function addressToString(address: Address | null) {
    return address
        ? address.toString()
        : 'null';
}

function decodeProvideLp(
    name: string,
    body: Cell
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(name);
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    const root = body.beginParse();

    console.log(
        'Root opcode:',
        '0x' +
        root.loadUint(32)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('');

    const routerWallet =
        root.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'routerWalletAddress:',
        addressToString(routerWallet)
    );

    const refundAddress =
        root.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'refundAddress:',
        addressToString(refundAddress)
    );

    const excessesAddress =
        root.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'excessesAddress:',
        addressToString(excessesAddress)
    );

    const deadline =
        root.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        'deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining root bits:',
        root.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining root refs:',
        root.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('');

    if (root.remainingRefs < 1) {
        console.log(
            'ERROR: Expected payload reference not found.'
        );
        return;
    }

    const payload =
        root.loadRef().beginParse();

    console.log('----- PROVIDE_LP REF -----');
    console.log('');

    const minLpOut =
        payload.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    const receiverAddress =
        payload.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'receiverAddress:',
        addressToString(receiverAddress)
    );

    const bothPositive =
        payload.loadUint(1);

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive.toString()
    );

    const customPayloadGas =
        payload.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount:',
        customPayloadGas.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining payload bits:',
        payload.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining payload refs:',
        payload.remainingRefs
    );

    if (payload.remainingRefs > 0) {

        const customPayload =
            payload.loadMaybeRef();

        console.log(
            'dexCustomPayload:',
            customPayload
                ? 'PRESENT'
                : 'NONE'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            'dexCustomPayload:',
            'NONE'
        );

    }

    console.log('');
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('        STON.FI V2.2 PROVIDE_LP DECODER');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const roosterCell =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                ROOSTER_BOC,
                'hex'
            )
        )[0];

    const ptonCell =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                PTON_BOC,
                'hex'
            )
        )[0];

    /*
     * Root cell contains the Jetton/pTON
     * transfer opcode.
     *
     * PROVIDE_LP is inside REF 1.
     */

    decodeProvideLp(
        'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP BODY',
        roosterCell.refs[0]
    );

    decodeProvideLp(
        'pTON PROVIDE_LP BODY',
        ptonCell.refs[0]
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                    END');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address, Cell, Slice } from '@ton/core';

const PROVIDE_LP_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAnQAB4TfAlt+AEkQYMDTZ/VmiNvcexCcb43c5kFbdpMw6Xr9dxAln32QQA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bgAAAADVeEfvAAQBNQI8nxRgB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8E7nX8Iw==';

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const EXPECTED_USER =
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix';

function loadAddress(
    s: Slice,
    label: string
): string {

    const address =
        s.loadAddress();

    const value =
        address.toString();

    console.log(
        `${label}: ${value}`
    );

    return value;
}

function loadCoins(
    s: Slice,
    label: string
): bigint {

    const value =
        s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        `${label}: ${value.toString()}`
    );

    return value;
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        '       OFFLINE STON.FI V2.2 PROVIDE_LP DECODER'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO NETWORK REQUEST'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION'
    );

    console.log('');

    const boc =
        Buffer.from(
            PROVIDE_LP_BOC,
            'base64'
        );

    const cells =
        Cell.fromBoc(boc);

    console.log(
        `BOC CELLS: ${cells.length}`
    );

    if (cells.length === 0) {

        console.log(
            'ERROR: BOC contains no cells.'
        );

        return;
    }

    const root =
        cells[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOT CELL'
    );

    console.log(
        '------------------------------------------------------------'
    );

    console.log(
        `BITS: ${root.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REFS: ${root.refs.length}`
    );

    const s =
        root.beginParse();

    const op =
        s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `OPCODE: 0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    if (op !== 0x37c096df) {

        console.log(
            'ERROR: This is not provide_lp.'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'OPERATION: STON.Fi provide_lp'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'PROVIDE_LP ROOT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    /*
     * Correct V2 provide_lp layout:
     *
     * opcode
     * token_wallet1
     * refund_address
     * excesses_address
     * tx_deadline:uint64
     * cross_provide_lp_body:^Cell
     */

    const tokenWallet1 =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'TOKEN WALLET 1'
        );

    const refundAddress =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'REFUND ADDRESS'
        );

    const excessesAddress =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'EXCESSES ADDRESS'
        );

    const deadline =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE: ${deadline.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE HEX: 0x${deadline.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `REMAINING ROOT BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REMAINING ROOT REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`
    );

    if (s.remainingRefs !== 1) {

        console.log(
            'WARNING: expected exactly 1 body reference.'
        );
    }

    const body =
        s.loadRef();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'CROSS_PROVIDE_LP_BODY'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `BITS: ${body.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REFS: ${body.refs.length}`
    );

    const b =
        body.beginParse();

    /*
     * cross_provide_lp_body:
     *
     * min_lp_out:Coins
     * to_address:MsgAddress
     * both_positive:uint1
     * fwd_amount:Coins
     * custom_payload:(Maybe ^Cell)
     */

    const minLpOut =
        loadCoins(
            b,
            'MIN LP OUT'
        );

    const receiver =
        loadAddress(
            b,
            'RECEIVER'
        );

    const bothPositive =
        b.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `BOTH POSITIVE: ${bothPositive}`
    );

    const fwdAmount =
        loadCoins(
            b,
            'FWD AMOUNT'
        );

    const hasCustomPayload =
        b.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `CUSTOM PAYLOAD PRESENT: ${hasCustomPayload}`
    );

    let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasCustomPayload) {

        if (b.remainingRefs > 0) {

            customPayload =
                b.loadRef();

            console.log(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD: REF'
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD: INLINE / INVALID'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'FINAL RESULT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TOKEN WALLET 1 : ${tokenWallet1}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REFUND ADDRESS : ${refundAddress}`
    );

    console.log(
        `EXCESSES       : ${excessesAddress}`
    );

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE       : ${deadline.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `MIN LP OUT     : ${minLpOut.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `MIN LP DISPLAY : ${Number(minLpOut) / 1e9} LP`
    );

    console.log(
        `RECEIVER       : ${receiver}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BOTH POSITIVE  : ${bothPositive}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FWD AMOUNT     : ${fwdAmount.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FWD TON        : ${Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9}`
    );

    console.log(
        `CUSTOM PAYLOAD : ${hasCustomPayload}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'CROSS CHECKS'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN WALLET 1 == pTON wallet : ${
            tokenWallet1 === EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `RECEIVER == user wallet       : ${
            receiver === EXPECTED_USER
        }`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TOKEN WALLET 1 RAW:'
    );

    try {

        console.log(
            Address.parse(tokenWallet1).toRawString()
        );

    } catch {
        console.log(
            'RAW CONVERSION ERROR'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'OFFLINE DECODER FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const USER =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

function printBits(
  label: string,
  cell: Cell,
) {
  const slice = cell.beginParse();

  console.log(`${label}`);
  console.log("bits:", slice.remainingBits);
  console.log("refs:", slice.remainingRefs);

  const bits = slice.loadBits(slice.remainingBits);

  console.log(
    "hex:",
    bits.toString(),
  );
}

function printTree(
  name: string,
  cell: Cell,
  depth = 0,
) {
  const p = "  ".repeat(depth);

  console.log();
  console.log(`${p}${name}`);
  console.log(`${p}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
  console.log(`${p}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
  console.log(
    `${p}hash: ${cell.hash().toString("hex")}`,
  );

  for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
    printTree(
      `ref[${i}]`,
      cell.refs[i],
      depth + 1,
    );
  }
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 RAW PROVIDE_LP INSPECTION");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");

  const routerAddress =
    Address.parse(ROUTER);

  const userAddress =
    Address.parse(USER);

  const roosterMaster =
    Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

  const ptonMaster =
    Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
      routerAddress,
    );

  const params =
    await routerContract
      .getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        provider.provider(routerAddress),
        {
          userWalletAddress: userAddress,
          sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
          otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
          sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
          minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
          gasAmount: 300000000n,
          forwardGasAmount:
            FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
          transferExcessAddress:
            userAddress,
        },
      );

  if (!params.body) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK returned empty body",
    );
  }

  const body = params.body;

  console.log();
  console.log("===== JETTON TRANSFER ROOT =====");

  const root = body.beginParse();

  console.log("bits:", root.remainingBits);
  console.log("refs:", root.remainingRefs);

  const opcode =
    root.loadUint(32);

  console.log(
    "opcode:",
    "0x" +
      opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
  );

  root.loadUintBig(64);

  const amount =
    root.loadCoins();

  console.log(
    "amount:",
    amount.toString(),
  );

  const destination =
    root.loadAddress();

  console.log(
    "destination:",
    destination?.toString(),
  );

  const response =
    root.loadAddress();

  console.log(
    "response:",
    response?.toString(),
  );

  const custom =
    root.loadBit();

  console.log(
    "custom payload:",
    custom ? "present" : "none",
  );

  const forwardTon =
    root.loadCoins();

  console.log(
    "forward TON:",
    forwardTon.toString(),
  );

  const forwardIsRef =
    root.loadBit();

  console.log(
    "forward payload:",
    forwardIsRef
      ? "REFERENCE"
      : "INLINE",
  );

  if (!forwardIsRef) {
    throw new Error(
      "Expected forward payload reference",
    );
  }

  const forward =
    root.loadRef();

  console.log();
  console.log("===== FORWARD PAYLOAD TREE =====");

  printTree(
    "forward",
    forward,
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("===== FORWARD ROOT RAW =====");

  const forwardSlice =
    forward.beginParse();

  console.log(
    "remaining bits:",
    forwardSlice.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "remaining refs:",
    forwardSlice.remainingRefs,
  );

  const provideOpcode =
    forwardSlice.loadUint(32);

  console.log(
    "opcode:",
    "0x" +
      provideOpcode
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0"),
  );

  const routerWallet =
    forwardSlice.loadAddress();

  console.log(
    "routerWallet:",
    routerWallet?.toString(),
  );

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "===== AFTER OPCODE + ROUTER WALLET =====",
  );

  console.log(
    "remaining bits:",
    forwardSlice.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "remaining refs:",
    forwardSlice.remainingRefs,
  );

  printBits(
    "Remaining inline bits",
    forwardSlice.asCell(),
  );

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "===== RAW FIRST 32 / 64 / 128 BITS =====",
  );

  const rawCell =
    forwardSlice.asCell();

  const rawSlice =
    rawCell.beginParse();

  const total =
    rawSlice.remainingBits;

  if (total >= 32) {
    console.log(
      "next32:",
      "0x" +
        rawSlice
          .loadUint(32)
          .toString(16)
          .padStart(8, "0"),
    );
  }

  const rawSlice64 =
    rawCell.beginParse();

  if (total >= 64) {
    console.log(
      "next64:",
      rawSlice64
        .loadUintBig(64)
        .toString(),
    );
  }

  const rawSlice128 =
    rawCell.beginParse();

  if (total >= 128) {
    const first128 =
      rawSlice128.loadBits(128);

    console.log(
      "next128 hex:",
      first128.toString(),
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "===== NESTED REFERENCE =====",
  );

  if (forwardSlice.remainingRefs > 0) {
    const nested =
      forwardSlice.loadRef();

    printTree(
      "nested ref",
      nested,
    );

    printBits(
      "Nested raw bits",
      nested,
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "No nested reference.",
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "===== FORWARD FINAL =====",
  );

  console.log(
    "remaining bits:",
    forwardSlice.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "remaining refs:",
    forwardSlice.remainingRefs,
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" RAW INSPECTION COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");
}

import { Address, Cell, Slice, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000001';

const client = new TonClient({
    endpoint:
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

function loadAddress(
    s: Slice,
    label: string
): string {

    try {

        const address =
            s.loadAddress();

        console.log(
            `${label}: ${address.toString()}`
        );

        return address.toString();

    } catch (error) {

        console.log(
            `${label}: ADDRESS ERROR`
        );

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );

        return 'ERROR';
    }
}

function loadCoins(
    s: Slice,
    label: string
): bigint {

    try {

        const value =
            s.loadCoins();

        console.log(
            `${label}: ${value.toString()}`
        );

        return value;

    } catch (error) {

        console.log(
            `${label}: COINS ERROR`
        );

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );

        return 0n;
    }
}

function decodeProvideLp(
    cell: Cell
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'STON.FI V2.2 PROVIDE_LP DECODER - CORRECT TL-B'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    const s =
        cell.beginParse();

    const op =
        s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `OPCODE: 0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    if (op !== 0x37c096df) {

        console.log(
            'ERROR: NOT provide_lp'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'OPERATION: provide_lp'
    );

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     *
     * provide_lp payload does NOT contain query_id.
     *
     * TL-B:
     *
     * provide_lp#37c096df
     * token_wallet1:MsgAddress
     * refund_address:MsgAddress
     * excesses_address:MsgAddress
     * tx_deadline:uint64
     * cross_provide_lp_body:^Cell
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '------------------------------------------------------------'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROUTER-SIDE PROVIDE_LP DATA'
    );

    console.log(
        '------------------------------------------------------------'
    );

    const tokenWallet1 =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'TOKEN WALLET 1'
        );

    const refundAddress =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'REFUND ADDRESS'
        );

    const excessesAddress =
        loadAddress(
            s,
            'EXCESSES ADDRESS'
        );

    const deadline =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE: ${deadline.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE HEX: 0x${deadline
            .toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `ROOT REMAINING BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ROOT REMAINING REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`
    );

    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {

        console.log(
            'ERROR: provide_lp body REF not found'
        );

        return;
    }

    const body =
        s.loadRef();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'CROSS PROVIDE_LP BODY'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `BITS: ${body.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REFS: ${body.refs.length}`
    );

    /*
     * TL-B:
     *
     * cross_provide_lp_body:
     * min_lp_out:Coins
     * to_address:MsgAddress
     * both_positive:uint1
     * fwd_amount:Coins
     * custom_payload:(Maybe ^Cell)
     */

    const b =
        body.beginParse();

    const minLpOut =
        loadCoins(
            b,
            'MIN LP OUT'
        );

    const receiverAddress =
        loadAddress(
            b,
            'RECEIVER ADDRESS'
        );

    const bothPositive =
        b.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `BOTH POSITIVE: ${bothPositive}`
    );

    const fwdAmount =
        loadCoins(
            b,
            'FWD AMOUNT'
        );

    const hasCustomPayload =
        b.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `CUSTOM PAYLOAD PRESENT: ${hasCustomPayload}`
    );

    let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasCustomPayload) {

        if (b.remainingRefs > 0) {

            customPayload =
                b.loadRef();

            console.log(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD: REF'
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD: INLINE'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'FINAL DECODED PROVIDE_LP'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN WALLET 1 : ${tokenWallet1}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REFUND ADDRESS : ${refundAddress}`
    );

    console.log(
        `EXCESSES       : ${excessesAddress}`
    );

    console.log(
        `DEADLINE       : ${deadline.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `MIN LP OUT     : ${minLpOut.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `MIN LP DISPLAY : ${Number(minLpOut) / 1e9}`
    );

    console.log(
        `RECEIVER       : ${receiverAddress}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BOTH POSITIVE  : ${bothPositive}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FWD AMOUNT     : ${fwdAmount.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FWD TON        : ${Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9}`
    );

    console.log(
        `CUSTOM PAYLOAD : ${hasCustomPayload}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'ADDRESS CROSS-CHECK'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `TOKEN WALLET 1 : ${tokenWallet1}`
    );

    console.log(
        'EXPECTED pTON  : EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
    );

    console.log(
        `MATCH pTON     : ${
            tokenWallet1 ===
            'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
        }`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'EXPECTED RECEIVER:'
    );

    console.log(
        'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
    );

    console.log(
        `MATCH RECEIVER: ${
            receiverAddress ===
            'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
        }`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'RAW PROVIDE_LP BOC'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        cell.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    if (customPayload) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD BOC'
        );

        console.log(
            customPayload
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64')
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'DECODER FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        '     EXTRACT REAL PROVIDE_LP FROM TRANSACTION'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        `TARGET LT: ${TARGET_LT}`
    );

    const user =
        Address.parse(USER_WALLET);

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            user,
            {
                limit: 10,
            }
        );

    const tx: any =
        transactions.find(
            (item: any) =>
                String(item.lt) ===
                TARGET_LT
        );

    if (!tx) {

        console.log(
            'TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND'
    );

    console.log(
        `LT: ${tx.lt}`
    );

    console.log(
        `HASH: ${Buffer
            .from(tx.hash())
            .toString('base64')}`
    );

    for (
        const message
        of tx.outMessages.values()
    ) {

        if (!message.body) {
            continue;
        }

        const test =
            message.body.beginParse();

        if (test.remainingBits < 32) {
            continue;
        }

        const op =
            test.loadUint(32);

        if (op !== 0x0f8a7ea5) {
            continue;
        }

        const transfer =
            message.body.beginParse();

        transfer.loadUint(32);

        const transferQueryId =
            transfer.loadUintBig(64);

        const jettonAmount =
            transfer.loadCoins();

        const destination =
            transfer.loadAddress();

        const responseDestination =
            transfer.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER JETTON TRANSFER'
        );

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log(
            `TRANSFER QUERY ID: ${transferQueryId}`
        );

        console.log(
            `JETTON AMOUNT: ${jettonAmount.toString()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `DISPLAY: ${Number(jettonAmount) / 1e9} ROOSTER`
        );

        console.log(
            `DESTINATION: ${destination.toString()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `RESPONSE DEST: ${responseDestination.toString()}`
        );

        const hasCustomPayload =
            transfer.loadBit();

        console.log(
            `CUSTOM PAYLOAD: ${hasCustomPayload}`
        );

        if (hasCustomPayload) {

            const customIsRef =
                transfer.loadBit();

            if (customIsRef) {
                transfer.loadRef();
            }
        }

        const forwardTon =
            transfer.loadCoins();

        console.log(
            `FORWARD TON: ${forwardTon.toString()}`
        );

        const forwardIsRef =
            transfer.loadBit();

        console.log(
            `FORWARD PAYLOAD REF: ${forwardIsRef}`
        );

        let payload: Cell;

        if (forwardIsRef) {

            payload =
                transfer.loadRef();

        } else {

            payload =
                transfer.asCell();
        }

        console.log(
            `FORWARD PAYLOAD BITS: ${payload.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `FORWARD PAYLOAD REFS: ${payload.refs.length}`
        );

        decodeProvideLp(
            payload
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER transfer not found.'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\decodePtonProvideLpOfflineV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000001n;

const client = new TonClient({
    endpoint:
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

function shortAddress(a: Address | null | undefined): string {
    return a ? a.toString() : 'null';
}

function hexOpcodeFromBody(body: any): string {
    try {
        if (body.bits.length < 32) {
            return 'TOO_SHORT';
        }

        const s = body.beginParse();
        const op = s.loadUint(32);

        return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'ERROR';
    }
}

async function run() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 POOL TRACE AFTER PROVIDE_LP');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('');

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET USER TX LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 50,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL TRANSACTIONS:', txs.length);

    let found = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < txs.length; i++) {
        const tx = txs[i];

        const lt = tx.lt;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `---------------- TRANSACTION ${i + 1} ----------------`
        );

        console.log('LT:', lt.toString());
        console.log(
            'HASH:',
            tx.hash().toString('base64')
        );

        if (tx.inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
            const src =
                tx.inMessage.info.src;

            const value =
                tx.inMessage.info.value.coins;

            console.log('');
            console.log('INCOMING INTERNAL');
            console.log('SOURCE:', shortAddress(src));
            console.log(
                'VALUE:',
                value.toString()
            );
            console.log(
                'VALUE TON:',
                Number(value) / 1e9
            );

            const body =
                tx.inMessage.body;

            console.log(
                'BODY BITS:',
                body.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                'BODY REFS:',
                body.refs.length
            );

            console.log(
                'OPCODE:',
                hexOpcodeFromBody(body)
            );

            const srcRaw =
                src.toRawString();

            const routerRaw =
                ROUTER.toRawString();

            if (srcRaw === routerRaw) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '*** SOURCE IS STON.FI ROUTER ***'
                );

                console.log(
                    'ROUTER -> POOL MATCH: TRUE'
                );

                found++;

                console.log('');
                console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');

                console.log(
                    Buffer.from(
                        body.toBoc()
                    ).toString('hex')
                );

                console.log('');
                console.log('BODY BOC BASE64:');

                console.log(
                    Buffer.from(
                        body.toBoc()
                    ).toString('base64')
                );
            }
        }

        if (tx.outMessages.size > 0) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'OUTGOING MESSAGES:',
                tx.outMessages.size
            );

            for (
                let j = 0;
                j < tx.outMessages.size;
                j++
            ) {
                const msg =
                    tx.outMessages.get(j);

                if (msg.info.type === 'internal') {
                    console.log(
                        `  OUT ${j + 1} ->`,
                        msg.info.dest.toString()
                    );

                    console.log(
                        '     VALUE:',
                        msg.info.value.coins.toString()
                    );

                    console.log(
                        '     OPCODE:',
                        hexOpcodeFromBody(msg.body)
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log('RESULT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log(
        'ROUTER -> POOL TRANSACTIONS FOUND:',
        found
    );

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'No Router -> Pool transaction was found'
        );

        console.log(
            'in the latest 50 Pool transactions.'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'This does NOT mean the transaction did not exist.'
        );

        console.log(
            'It may simply be outside the returned window.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRACE FINISHED');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
}

run().catch((err) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('TRACE ERROR');
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodePtonV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const BOC_FILE = 'pton_v22_generated.boc';

const EXPECTED_PTON_AMOUNT = 500_000_000n;

const EXPECTED_ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT = 346725530921n;

const EXPECTED_BOTH_POSITIVE = true;

const EXPECTED_CUSTOM_FORWARD_GAS = 0n;

const PTON_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

export async function run() {

    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );

    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 pTON BOC DECODER'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * BOC FILE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_FILE)) {
        throw new Error(
            `BOC FILE NOT FOUND: ${BOC_FILE}`
        );
    }

    let boc = fs.readFileSync(BOC_FILE);

const rawMagic = boc.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex');

if (rawMagic !== 'b5ee9c72') {
    const base64Text = boc.toString('utf8').trim();

    try {
        boc = Buffer.from(base64Text, 'base64');
    } catch {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID BOC FILE: not raw BOC and not valid Base64`
        );
    }
}

    console.log(
        'BOC FILE:',
        path.resolve(BOC_FILE)
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC SIZE:',
        boc.length,
        'bytes'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * BOC MAGIC
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const magic =
        boc.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex');

    console.log(
        'BOC MAGIC:',
        magic
    );

    if (magic !== 'b5ee9c72') {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID BOC MAGIC: ${magic}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | BOC magic | actual=b5ee9c72 | expected=b5ee9c72'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PARSE BOC
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const cells =
        Cell.fromBoc(boc);

    console.log(
        'ROOT CELLS:',
        cells.length
    );

    if (cells.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `EXPECTED 1 ROOT CELL, GOT ${cells.length}`
        );
    }

    const root =
        cells[0];

    const slice =
        root.beginParse();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * pTON TON_TRANSFER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        '\n----- pTON TON_TRANSFER -----'
    );

    const opcode =
        slice.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
    );

    if (opcode !== PTON_TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID pTON TON_TRANSFER OPCODE: 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * QUERY ID
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const queryId =
        slice.loadUint(64);

    console.log(
        'queryId:',
        queryId
    );

    if (queryId !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID queryId: ${queryId}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | queryId = 0'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * TON / pTON AMOUNT
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const tonAmount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'tonAmount:',
        tonAmount.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(tonAmount) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    if (tonAmount !== EXPECTED_PTON_AMOUNT) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID TON AMOUNT: ${tonAmount}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | pTON amount'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * REFUND ADDRESS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const refundAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    if (!refundAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'REFUND ADDRESS IS NULL'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'refundAddress:',
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    if (!refundAddress.equals(EXPECTED_USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'REFUND ADDRESS DOES NOT MATCH USER'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | refund address'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FORWARD PAYLOAD
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const hasForwardPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'hasForwardPayload:',
        hasForwardPayload
    );

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'FORWARD PAYLOAD IS MISSING'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | forward payload exists'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FORWARD PAYLOAD CELL
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const forwardPayload =
        slice.loadRef();

    console.log(
        '\n----- PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD -----'
    );

    const lpSlice =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PROVIDE_LP OPCODE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const lpOpcode =
        lpSlice.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'opcode:',
        '0x' + lpOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    if (lpOpcode !== PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID PROVIDE_LP OPCODE: 0x${lpOpcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | PROVIDE_LP opcode'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * ROUTER'S ROOSTER JETTON WALLET
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const routerWalletAddress =
        lpSlice.loadAddress();

    if (!routerWalletAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROUTER WALLET ADDRESS IS NULL'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'routerWalletAddress:',
        routerWalletAddress.toString()
    );

    if (!routerWalletAddress.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET)) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROUTER ROOSTER WALLET DOES NOT MATCH EXPECTED ROOSTER WALLET'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | router ROOSTER wallet'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PROVIDE_LP REFUND ADDRESS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const lpRefundAddress =
        lpSlice.loadAddress();

    if (!lpRefundAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'PROVIDE_LP REFUND ADDRESS IS NULL'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'lpRefundAddress:',
        lpRefundAddress.toString()
    );

    if (!lpRefundAddress.equals(EXPECTED_USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'PROVIDE_LP REFUND ADDRESS DOES NOT MATCH USER'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | PROVIDE_LP refund address'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * EXCESSES ADDRESS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const excessesAddress =
        lpSlice.loadAddress();

    if (!excessesAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'EXCESSES ADDRESS IS NULL'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'excessesAddress:',
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    if (!excessesAddress.equals(EXPECTED_USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'EXCESSES ADDRESS DOES NOT MATCH USER'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | excesses address'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * DEADLINE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const deadline =
        lpSlice.loadUint(64);

    console.log(
        'deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    if (deadline === 0) {
        throw new Error(
            'INVALID DEADLINE: 0'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | deadline exists'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PROVIDE_LP PARAMETERS REF
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (lpSlice.remainingBits !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `UNEXPECTED INLINE BITS BEFORE PARAMETERS REF: ${lpSlice.remainingBits}`
        );
    }

    if (lpSlice.remainingRefs !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `EXPECTED 1 PARAMETERS REF, GOT ${lpSlice.remainingRefs}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | PROVIDE_LP parameters stored in reference'
    );

    const parametersCell =
        lpSlice.loadRef();

    const parametersSlice =
        parametersCell.beginParse();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * MIN LP OUT
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const minLpOut =
        parametersSlice.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    if (minLpOut !== EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID minLpOut: ${minLpOut}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | minLpOut'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * RECEIVER ADDRESS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const receiverAddress =
        parametersSlice.loadAddress();

    if (!receiverAddress) {
        throw new Error(
            'RECEIVER ADDRESS IS NULL'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'receiverAddress:',
        receiverAddress.toString()
    );

    if (!receiverAddress.equals(EXPECTED_USER)) {
        throw new Error(
            'RECEIVER ADDRESS DOES NOT MATCH USER'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | receiver address'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * BOTH POSITIVE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const bothPositive =
        parametersSlice.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    if (bothPositive !== EXPECTED_BOTH_POSITIVE) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID bothPositive: ${bothPositive}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | bothPositive = 1'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * CUSTOM PAYLOAD FORWARD GAS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const customPayloadForwardGas =
        parametersSlice.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'customPayloadForwardGas:',
        customPayloadForwardGas.toString()
    );

    if (
        customPayloadForwardGas !==
        EXPECTED_CUSTOM_FORWARD_GAS
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `INVALID custom payload forward gas: ${customPayloadForwardGas}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | custom payload forward gas = 0'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * CUSTOM PAYLOAD
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const hasCustomPayload =
        parametersSlice.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'hasCustomPayload:',
        hasCustomPayload
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'UNEXPECTED CUSTOM PAYLOAD'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | no custom payload'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PARAMETERS REF EXHAUSTION
     * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

    console.log(
        'REMAINING PARAMETER BITS:',
        parametersSlice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING PARAMETER REFS:',
        parametersSlice.remainingRefs
    );

    if (
        parametersSlice.remainingBits !== 0 ||
        parametersSlice.remainingRefs !== 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'UNEXPECTED DATA REMAINS IN PROVIDE_LP PARAMETERS'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | PROVIDE_LP parameters fully consumed'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * PROVIDE_LP CELL EXHAUSTION
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        '\n----- PROVIDE_LP CELL REMAINDER -----'
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING PROVIDE_LP BITS:',
        lpSlice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING PROVIDE_LP REFS:',
        lpSlice.remainingRefs
    );

 const remainingBits = Number(lpSlice.remainingBits);
const remainingRefs = Number(lpSlice.remainingRefs);

if (
    remainingBits !== 0 ||
    remainingRefs !== 0
) {
        throw new Error(
            'UNEXPECTED DATA REMAINS IN PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | PROVIDE_LP has no extra data'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * OUTER TON_TRANSFER REMAINDER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        '\n----- OUTER TON_TRANSFER REMAINDER -----'
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING OUTER BITS:',
        slice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING OUTER REFS:',
        slice.remainingRefs
    );

    if (
        slice.remainingBits !== 0 ||
        slice.remainingRefs !== 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'UNEXPECTED DATA REMAINS IN TON_TRANSFER'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'PASS | TON_TRANSFER has no extra data'
    );

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FINAL CHECK
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        '\n=============================================================='
    );

    console.log(
        'FINAL pTON BOC CHECK'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        tonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'refundAddress:',
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Router ROOSTER wallet:',
        routerWalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'PROVIDE_LP refundAddress:',
        lpRefundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'excessesAddress:',
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'receiverAddress:',
        receiverAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'EXPECTED ROUTER:',
        EXPECTED_ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'EXPECTED pTON wallet:',
        EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\nALL STRUCTURAL pTON BOC CHECKS PASSED'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const CALLBACK_OPCODE = 0x06ecd527;

const EXPECTED_USER =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

/*
 * ============================================================
 * REAL CALLBACK #1
 * LT: 99809465000012

 */

const CALLBACK_BOC_1 =
    'te6cckEBAgEApwABeQbs1ScAAAAAAAAAAGtg6IfxbEMD0DSAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4boEeT4oggBAMmAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwEnZKz8=';

 * LT: 98384505000011

const CALLBACK_BOC_2 =
    'te6cckEBAgEAqQABfQbs1ScAAAAAAAAAAGWvMQekAAQ7msoAgB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G6pNBSYYuCAEAyYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvA3RKrDA==';

function formatInteger(value: bigint): string {
    return value
        .toString()
        .replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}


function formatToken9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    if (fraction.length === 0) {
        return whole.toLocaleString();
    }

    return `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`;
}


function addressString(address: Address | null): string {
    if (!address) {
        return 'NULL';
    }

    return address.toString();
}


function decodeCallback(
    name: string,
    lt: string,
    bocBase64: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${name}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:');
    console.log(lt);

    const boc = Buffer.from(bocBase64, 'base64');

    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC SIZE:');
    console.log(`${boc.length} bytes`);

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(boc);

    if (cells.length === 0) {
        throw new Error('BOC contains no cells');
    }

    const root = cells[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT CELL:');
    console.log(
        `${root.bits.length} bits / ${root.refs.length} refs`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT CELL BOC:');
    console.log(
        root.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    const s = root.beginParse();

    /*
     * ============================================================
     * OPCODE
     * ============================================================
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' HEADER');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(
        '0x' +
        opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED:');
    console.log(
        '0x' +
        CALLBACK_OPCODE.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('OPCODE MATCH:');
    console.log(
        opcode === CALLBACK_OPCODE
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    if (opcode !== CALLBACK_OPCODE) {
        throw new Error(
            `Wrong opcode: 0x${opcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')}`
        );
    }


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * QUERY ID
     *
     * cb_add_liquidity#6ecd527
     * query_id:uint64
     * ============================================================
     */

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('');
    console.log('QUERY ID:');
    console.log(queryId.toString());


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * TOTAL AMOUNT 0
     *
     * tot_am0:Coins
     * ============================================================
     */

    console.log('');

    console.log(formatToken9(totalAmount0));

     * ============================================================
     * TOTAL AMOUNT 1
     *
     * tot_am1:Coins
     * ============================================================
     */

    const totalAmount1 = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('tot_am1 RAW:');
    console.log(formatInteger(totalAmount1));

    console.log('');
    console.log('tot_am1 TOKEN UNITS:');
    console.log(formatToken9(totalAmount1));


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * USER ADDRESS
     * ============================================================
     */

    const userAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' USER ADDRESS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER ADDRESS:');
    console.log(addressString(userAddress));

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED USER:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_USER);

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER MATCH:');
    console.log(
        addressString(userAddress) === EXPECTED_USER
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * MIN LP OUT
     * ============================================================
     */

    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' LP PARAMETERS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT RAW:');
    console.log(formatInteger(minLpOut));


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * FORWARD AMOUNT
     * ============================================================
     */

    const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log('FWD AMOUNT RAW:');
    console.log(formatInteger(fwdAmount));


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * CUSTOM PAYLOAD
     *
     * Maybe ^Cell
     *
     * 0 = absent
     * 1 = reference follows
     * ============================================================
     */

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log('HAS CUSTOM PAYLOAD:');
    console.log(
        hasCustomPayload
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {

        if (s.remainingRefs <= 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'custom_payload flag is 1 but no reference exists'
            );
        }

        const customPayload = s.loadRef();

        console.log('');
        console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD:');

        );

            customPayload.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );
    }

     * ADDITIONAL DATA
     *

     *     to_user_address:MsgAddress

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT REMAINING REFS BEFORE ADDITIONAL REF:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    if (s.remainingRefs <= 0) {
        throw new Error(
            'No additional_data reference found'
        );
    }

    const additionalCell = s.loadRef();

    console.log('');

    );

    console.log('');

        additionalCell.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    const a = additionalCell.beginParse();


    /*
     * to_user_address

    const toUserAddress = a.loadAddress();

    console.log('');

     * ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('ADDITIONAL REMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(a.remainingBits);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADDITIONAL REMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(a.remainingRefs);


    /*
     * ============================================================
     * IMPORTANT SUMMARY
     * ============================================================
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` SUMMARY - ${name}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:');
    console.log(lt);

    console.log('');
    console.log('QUERY ID:');
    console.log(queryId.toString());

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        `${formatInteger(totalAmount0)} raw`
    );
