    console.log('tot_am0 TOKEN:');
    console.log(
        formatToken9(totalAmount0)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('tot_am1:');
    console.log(
        `${formatInteger(totalAmount1)} raw`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('tot_am1 TOKEN:');
    console.log(
        formatToken9(totalAmount1)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
    console.log(
        formatInteger(minLpOut)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FWD AMOUNT:');
    console.log(
        formatInteger(fwdAmount)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER MATCH:');
    console.log(
        addressString(userAddress) === EXPECTED_USER
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT PARSE COMPLETE:');
    console.log(
        s.remainingBits === 0 &&
        s.remainingRefs === 0
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ADDITIONAL PARSE COMPLETE:');
    console.log(
        a.remainingBits === 0 &&
        a.remainingRefs === 0
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0xeec5;
const END   = 0xef44;

const op = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x0b, "end"],
  [0x0c, "br"],
  [0x0d, "br_if"],
  [0x0f, "return"],
  [0x10, "call"],
  [0x20, "local.get"],
  [0x21, "local.set"],
  [0x22, "local.tee"],
  [0x28, "i32.load"],
  [0x36, "i32.store"],
  [0x37, "i64.store"],
  [0x41, "i32.const"],
  [0x45, "i32.eqz"],
  [0x46, "i32.eq"],
  [0x47, "i32.ne"],
  [0x4d, "i32.le_u"],
  [0x6a, "i32.add"],
  [0x6b, "i32.sub"],
  [0x71, "i32.and"],
  [0x72, "i32.or"],
  [0x74, "i32.shl"],
  [0x76, "i32.shr_u"],
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    s += 7;
  }
}

console.log("REAL FUNC 122 — INSTRUCTION DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const addr = p;
  const byte = wasm[p++];
  const name = op.get(byte) ?? `UNKNOWN 0x${byte.toString(16)}`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    byte === 0x10 ||
    byte === 0x20 ||
    byte === 0x21 ||
    byte === 0x22 ||
    byte === 0x0c ||
    byte === 0x0d ||
    byte === 0x41
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (byte === 0x28 || byte === 0x36 || byte === 0x37) {
    const align = uleb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = uleb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    extra = ` align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ${name}${extra}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const op = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x00, "unreachable"],
  [0x01, "nop"],
  [0x02, "block"],
  [0x03, "loop"],
  [0x04, "if"],
  [0x05, "else"],
  [0x0b, "end"],
  [0x0c, "br"],
  [0x0d, "br_if"],
  [0x0e, "br_table"],
  [0x0f, "return"],
  [0x10, "call"],
  [0x11, "call_indirect"],

  [0x20, "local.get"],
  [0x21, "local.set"],
  [0x22, "local.tee"],

  [0x28, "i32.load"],
  [0x29, "i64.load"],
  [0x2d, "i32.load8_u"],
  [0x36, "i32.store"],
  [0x37, "i64.store"],
  [0x3a, "i32.store8"],

  [0x41, "i32.const"],
  [0x42, "i64.const"],

  [0x45, "i32.eqz"],
  [0x46, "i32.eq"],
  [0x47, "i32.ne"],

  [0x4a, "i32.lt_s"],
  [0x4b, "i32.lt_u"],
  [0x4c, "i32.gt_s"],
  [0x4d, "i32.gt_u"],
  [0x4e, "i32.le_s"],
  [0x4f, "i32.le_u"],

  [0x6a, "i32.add"],
  [0x6b, "i32.sub"],
  [0x6c, "i32.mul"],
  [0x71, "i32.and"],
  [0x72, "i32.or"],
  [0x73, "i32.xor"],
  [0x74, "i32.shl"],
  [0x75, "i32.shr_s"],
  [0x76, "i32.shr_u"],

  [0x7c, "i64.add"],
  [0x7d, "i64.sub"],
  [0x7e, "i64.mul"],
  [0x83, "i64.and"],
  [0x84, "i64.or"],
  [0x86, "i64.shl"],
  [0x88, "i64.shr_u"],
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findFuncBody(funcIndex: number) {
  let p = 8;
  let importedFunctions = 0;
  let bodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const size = uleb(p);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
      let q = start;

      const count = uleb(q);
      q = count.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q);
        q = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = uleb(q);
        q = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = wasm[q++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {
          importedFunctions++;
          const type = uleb(q);
          q = type.next;
        }
        else if (kind === 0x01) {
          const elem = uleb(q);
          q = elem.next;

          const limits = wasm[q++];

          const min = uleb(q);
          q = min.next;

          if (limits & 1) {
            const max = uleb(q);
            q = max.next;
          }
        }
        else if (kind === 0x02) {
          const limits = wasm[q++];

          const min = uleb(q);
          q = min.next;

          if (limits & 1) {
            const max = uleb(q);
            q = max.next;
          }
        }
        else if (kind === 0x03) {
          const valType = wasm[q++];
          const mut = wasm[q++];
        }
      }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
      let q = start;

      const count = uleb(q);
      q = count.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const bodySize = uleb(q);
        const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

        bodies.push({
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  const bodyIndex = funcIndex - importedFunctions;

  return {
    importedFunctions,
    bodyIndex,
    ...bodies[bodyIndex],
  };
}

const FUNC = 2413;

const body = findFuncBody(FUNC);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REAL FUNC 2413 — EXACT INSTRUCTION DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNC INDEX    : ${FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED      : ${body.importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`BODY INDEX    : ${body.bodyIndex}`);
console.log(`BODY START    : 0x${body.start.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`BODY END      : 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE     : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = body.start;

while (p < body.end) {
  const addr = p;
  const byte = wasm[p++];

  const name =
    op.get(byte) ??
    `UNKNOWN 0x${byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    byte === 0x10 ||
    byte === 0x20 ||
    byte === 0x21 ||
    byte === 0x22 ||
    byte === 0x0c ||
    byte === 0x0d ||
    byte === 0x41 ||
    byte === 0x42
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (
    byte === 0x28 ||
    byte === 0x29 ||
    byte === 0x2d ||
    byte === 0x36 ||
    byte === 0x37 ||
    byte === 0x3a
  ) {
    const align = uleb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = uleb(p);
    p = offset.next;

    extra =
      ` align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
  }
  else if (byte === 0x11) {
    const type = uleb(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = uleb(p);
    p = table.next;

    extra =
      ` type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
  }

  console.log(
    `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${name}${extra}`
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0; // بعداً با BODY واقعی FUNC 259 جایگزین می‌شود
const END = 0;

const op = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x0b, "end"],
  [0x0c, "br"],
  [0x0d, "br_if"],
  [0x0e, "br_table"],
  [0x0f, "return"],
  [0x10, "call"],
  [0x11, "call_indirect"],

  [0x20, "local.get"],
  [0x21, "local.set"],
  [0x22, "local.tee"],

  [0x28, "i32.load"],
  [0x29, "i64.load"],
  [0x2c, "i32.load8_s"],
  [0x2d, "i32.load8_u"],
  [0x30, "i32.load8_s"],
  [0x31, "i32.load8_u"],

  [0x36, "i32.store"],
  [0x37, "i64.store"],
  [0x3a, "i32.store8"],

  [0x41, "i32.const"],
  [0x42, "i64.const"],

  [0x45, "i32.eqz"],
  [0x46, "i32.eq"],
  [0x47, "i32.ne"],

  [0x4a, "i32.lt_s"],
  [0x4b, "i32.lt_u"],
  [0x4c, "i32.gt_s"],
  [0x4d, "i32.gt_u"],
  [0x4e, "i32.le_s"],
  [0x4f, "i32.le_u"],

  [0x50, "i64.eqz"],
  [0x51, "i64.eq"],
  [0x52, "i64.ne"],

  [0x6a, "i32.add"],
  [0x6b, "i32.sub"],
  [0x6c, "i32.mul"],
  [0x71, "i32.and"],
  [0x72, "i32.or"],
  [0x73, "i32.xor"],
  [0x74, "i32.shl"],
  [0x75, "i32.shr_s"],
  [0x76, "i32.shr_u"],
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return {
        value,
        next: pos,
      };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findFuncBody(funcIndex: number) {
  let p = 0;

  // WASM header
  if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid WASM");
  }

  p = 8;

  let importedFunctions = 0;
  let functionBodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const secSize = uleb(p);
    const secStart = secSize.next;
    const secEnd = secStart + secSize.value;

    if (sectionId === 2) {
      let q = secStart;
      const count = uleb(q);
      q = count.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const modLen = uleb(q);
        q = modLen.next + modLen.value;

        const nameLen = uleb(q);
        q = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

        q++; // kind

        const typeIndex = uleb(q);
        q = typeIndex.next;

        if (wasm[q - 1] === 0x00)
          importedFunctions++;
      }
    }

    if (sectionId === 10) {
      let q = secStart;

      const count = uleb(q);
      q = count.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const bodySize = uleb(q);
        const bodyStart = bodySize.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

        functionBodies.push({
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = secEnd;
  }

  const bodyIndex = funcIndex - importedFunctions;

  if (bodyIndex < 0 || bodyIndex >= functionBodies.length)
    throw new Error(
      `FUNC ${funcIndex} is not a defined function`
    );

  return {
    importedFunctions,
    bodyIndex,
    ...functionBodies[bodyIndex],
  };
}

const FUNC = 259;

const body = findFuncBody(FUNC);

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`IMPORTED      : ${body.importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`BODY INDEX    : ${body.bodyIndex}`);
console.log(`BODY START    : 0x${body.start.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`BODY END      : 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE     : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = body.start;

while (p < body.end) {
  const addr = p;
  const byte = wasm[p++];

  const name =
    op.get(byte) ??
    `UNKNOWN 0x${byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    byte === 0x10 ||
    byte === 0x20 ||
    byte === 0x21 ||
    byte === 0x22 ||
    byte === 0x0c ||
    byte === 0x0d ||
    byte === 0x41 ||
    byte === 0x42
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);

    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    p = r.next;
  }
  else if (
    byte === 0x28 ||
    byte === 0x29 ||
    byte === 0x2c ||
    byte === 0x2d ||
    byte === 0x30 ||
    byte === 0x31 ||
    byte === 0x36 ||
    byte === 0x37 ||
    byte === 0x3a
  ) {
    const align = uleb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = uleb(p);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const start = 0x7672;
const end   = 0x76e7;

const names: Record<number, string> = {
  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",
  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",
  0x7e: "i64.add",
  0x7c: "i64.or",
  0x83: "i64.extend_i32_u",
  0x84: "i64.extend_i32_s",
  0x37: "i64.store"
};

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

console.log("FUNC 87 KEY INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = start;

while (p < end) {
  const op = wasm[p];
  const addr = p++;

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ${names[op]} ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
    const r = leb(p);
    p = r.next;
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ${names[op]} ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x37) {
    const align = leb(p);
    p = align.next;

    const off = leb(p);
    p = off.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (names[op]) {
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ${names[op]}`
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)}  opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAfwABbVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAAMD0DRAjyfFEMAPGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3QBAIWAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeJFURJA==';

const ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgQABcVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAFF0h26AAECPJ8UQwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDdAEAhYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6B+o0s';

const CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x06ecd527;
const ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;

function displayRooster(value: bigint): string {
    return `${Number(value) / 1_000_000_000}`;
}

function displayPton(value: bigint): string {
    return `${Number(value) / 1_000_000_000}`;
}

function decodeCbAddLiquidity() {
    console.log('\n==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 REAL LIQUIDITY RESULT DECODER');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC, 'base64')
    )[0];

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    // IMPORTANT:
    // cb_add_liquidity_v2 has NO query_id after opcode.
    //
    // Layout:
    // opcode:uint32
    // tot_am0:Coins
    // tot_am1:Coins
    // user_address:MsgAddress
    // min_lp_out:Coins
    // fwd_amount:Coins
    // custom_payload:Maybe ^Cell
    // additional_data:^Cell

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('\n===== CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY =====');

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected:',
        '0x' + CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    if (opcode !== CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected opcode: 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // REAL TOKEN AMOUNTS
    // ------------------------------------------------

    const totalAmount0 = s.loadCoins();
    const totalAmount1 = s.loadCoins();

    const userAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();
    const forwardAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('\n===== ACTUAL TOKEN AMOUNTS =====');

    console.log('\nAmount0 nano:', totalAmount0.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount0 ROOSTER:',
        displayRooster(totalAmount0)
    );

    console.log('\nAmount1 nano:', totalAmount1.toString());
    console.log(
        'Amount1 pTON:',
        displayPton(totalAmount1)
    );

    console.log('\n===== USER / LP PARAMETERS =====');

    console.log(
        'User address:',
        userAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward amount:',
        forwardAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\nRemaining bits before additional fields:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // ADDITIONAL DATA
    // ------------------------------------------------

    if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        const additional = s.loadRef().beginParse();

        console.log('\n===== ADDITIONAL FIELDS =====');

        const toUserAddress = additional.loadAddress();
        const refundAddress = additional.loadAddress();
        const excessAddress = additional.loadAddress();

        console.log(
            'to_user_address:',
            toUserAddress.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'refund_address:',
            refundAddress.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'excess_address:',
            excessAddress.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            '\nAdditional remaining bits:',
            additional.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'Additional remaining refs:',
            additional.remainingRefs
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== VALIDATION =====');

    console.log(
        'Amount0 expected 100 ROOSTER:',
        totalAmount0 === 100_000_000_000n
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'CHECK'
    );

    console.log(
        'Amount1 expected 249908 pTON:',
        totalAmount1 === 249_908n
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'CHECK'
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut expected 150109265:',
        minLpOut === 150_109_265n
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'CHECK'
    );

    console.log('\n==============================================');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('==============================================');
}

function decodeAddLiquidity() {
    console.log('\n===== 0x50c6a654 ADD_LIQUIDITY =====');

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC, 'base64')
    )[0];

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected:',
        '0x' + ADD_LIQUIDITY.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    console.log(
        'Outer remaining bits:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Outer remaining refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );
}

try {
    decodeCbAddLiquidity();
    decodeAddLiquidity();
} catch (error) {
    console.error('\nDECODE ERROR:');
    console.error(error);
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC_HEX =
    'b5ee9c724101020100a700017906ecd52700000000000000006b60e887f16c4303d034801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86e811e4f8a2080100c9801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18f003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1bc049d92b3f';

function printBits(label: string, bits: any) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label);
    console.log(`length = ${bits.length}`);
    console.log(bits.toString());
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 REAL CALLBACK STRUCTURE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const boc = Buffer.from(BOC_HEX, 'hex');
    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(boc);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT COUNT:');
    console.log(roots.length);

    const root = roots[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT BITS:');
    console.log(root.bits.length);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT REFS:');
    console.log(root.refs.length);

    printBits('ROOT BITS RAW:', root.bits);

    const slice = root.beginParse();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // OPCODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('FIELD 1 - OPCODE');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'opcode = 0x' +
        opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // QUERY ID
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('FIELD 2 - QUERY ID');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        'query_id = ' +
        queryId.toString()
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // REMAINING ROOT
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('AFTER OPCODE + QUERY ID');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    console.log(
        'Remaining bits = ' +
        slice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs = ' +
        slice.remainingRefs
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // READ REMAINING ROOT BITS WITHOUT GUESSING FIELD TYPES
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (slice.remainingBits > 0) {

        const remaining = slice.loadBits(
            slice.remainingBits
        );

        printBits(
            'REMAINING ROOT BITS:',
            remaining
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // REF CELLS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));
    console.log('REFERENCE CELLS');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    root.refs.forEach((ref, index) => {

        console.log('');
        console.log(`REF[${index}]`);

        console.log(
            'bits = ' +
            ref.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'refs = ' +
            ref.refs.length
        );

        printBits(
            `REF[${index}] RAW BITS:`,
            ref.bits
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `REF[${index}] BOC HEX:`
        );

        console.log(
            ref.toBoc().toString('hex')
        );
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('STRUCTURE EXTRACTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\decodeRoosterPayloadV22.ts

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const TARGET_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;

const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function printCell(label: string, cell: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`----- ${label} -----`);

    console.log('BOC:');
    console.log(cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));

    console.log('BITS:');
    console.log(cell.beginParse().remainingBits);

    console.log('REFS:');
    console.log(cell.refs.length);

    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(opHex(getOpcode(cell)));
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    separator('ROOSTER PAYLOAD DECODE v2.2');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        ROOSTER_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        if (!tx.inMessage) continue;

        const body = tx.inMessage.body;

        if (getOpcode(body) !== OP_JETTON_TRANSFER) {
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const s = body.beginParse();

            s.loadUint(32);       // opcode
            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
            const amount = s.loadCoins();

            if (amount !== TARGET_AMOUNT) {
                continue;
            }

            found = true;

            separator('TARGET TRANSFER');

            console.log('LT:');
            console.log(tx.lt.toString());

            console.log('HASH:');
            console.log(
                tx.hash().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            console.log('ROOSTER:');
            console.log(ton(amount));

            console.log('SOURCE:');
            if (tx.inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                console.log(
                    tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
                );
            }

            /*
             * Jetton transfer layout:
             *
             * opcode
             * query_id
             * amount
             * destination
             * response_destination
             * custom_payload
             * forward_ton_amount
             * forward_payload
             */

            const destination = s.loadAddress();
            const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

            console.log('');
            console.log('DESTINATION FIELD:');
            console.log(destination.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('RESPONSE DESTINATION:');
            console.log(responseDestination.toString());

            /*
             * custom_payload
             */
            let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

            try {
                customPayload = s.loadMaybeRef();
            } catch (e) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD DECODE ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }

            if (customPayload) {
                printCell(
                    'CUSTOM PAYLOAD ROOT',
                    customPayload
                );

                const cp = customPayload.beginParse();

                console.log('');
                console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD ROOT DETAILS:');

                console.log('BITS:');
                console.log(cp.remainingBits);

                console.log('REFS:');
                console.log(cp.remainingRefs);

                if (cp.remainingBits >= 32) {
                    console.log('ROOT OPCODE:');
                    console.log(
                        opHex(cp.loadUint(32))
                    );
                }

                console.log('');

                for (
                    let i = 0;
                    i < customPayload.refs.length;
                    i++
                ) {
                    printCell(
                        `CUSTOM PAYLOAD REF ${i}`,
                        customPayload.refs[i]
                    );
                }
            } else {
                console.log('');
                console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD: NONE');
            }

            /*
             * Forward TON amount
             */
            if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
                try {
                    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('FORWARD TON AMOUNT:');
                    console.log(
                        ton(forwardTonAmount)
                    );
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('FORWARD TON AMOUNT DECODE ERROR:');
                    console.log(String(e));
                }
            }

            /*
             * Forward payload
             *
             * Either Cell ^Cell
             */
            if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
                try {
                    const either = s.loadBit();

                    console.log('');
                    console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD TYPE:');

                    if (!either) {
                        console.log('INLINE');

                        const remainingCell = Cell.fromBoc(
                            Buffer.from(
                                s.asCell().toBoc()
                            )
                        )[0];

                        printCell(
                            'FORWARD PAYLOAD INLINE',
                            remainingCell
                        );
                    } else {
                        console.log('REFERENCE');

                        const forwardPayload = s.loadRef();

                        printCell(
                            'FORWARD PAYLOAD REF',
                            forwardPayload
                        );

                        for (
                            let i = 0;
                            i < forwardPayload.refs.length;
                            i++
                        ) {
                            printCell(
                                `FORWARD PAYLOAD REF CHILD ${i}`,
                                forwardPayload.refs[i]
                            );
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD DECODE ERROR:');
                    console.log(String(e));
                }
            }

            separator('END TARGET TRANSFER');

        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('TRANSFER PARSE ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    if (!found) {
        separator('RESULT');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSFER NOT FOUND.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

FILE: scripts\decodeRoosterPoolBody.ts

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const bocPath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        'rooster_pool_body.boc'
    );

    const bocBase64 = fs.readFileSync(bocPath, 'utf8').trim();

    console.log('===== ROOSTER POOL BODY DECODER =====');
    console.log('File:', bocPath);
    console.log('BOC length:', bocBase64.length);

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(bocBase64);

    console.log('\n===== ROOT CELL =====');
    console.log('Root bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Root refs:', cell.refs.length);

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('\n===== ROOT SLICE =====');
    console.log('Remaining bits:', slice.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', slice.remainingRefs);

    console.log('\n===== FIRST 32 BITS =====');

    try {
        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log('Opcode decimal:', opcode.toString());
        console.log(
            'Opcode hex:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('Cannot read opcode:', String(e));
    }

    console.log('\n===== CELL TREE =====');
    console.log(cell.toString());

    console.log('\n===== END =====');
}

function hex(value: number | bigint): string {
    return '0x' + BigInt(value).toString(16);
}


function showAddress(
    label: string,
    actual: Address | null,
    expected?: Address
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label + ':');

    if (!actual) {
        console.log('NULL / DECODE FAILED');
        return;
    }

    console.log(actual.toString());

    if (expected) {
        console.log('Expected:');
        console.log(expected.toString());

        console.log(
            'MATCH:',
            actual.equals(expected)
        );
    }
}


function loadCoinsSafe(
    slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>
): bigint | null {
    try {
        return slice.loadCoins();
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}


function loadUintSafe(
    slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>,
    bits: number
): bigint | null {
    try {
        return slice.loadUintBig(bits);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}


function loadAddressSafe(
    slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>
): Address | null {
    try {
        return slice.loadAddress();
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}


function printAmount(
    label: string,
    value: bigint | null,
    decimals = 9
) {
    console.log('');

    if (value === null) {
        console.log(`${label}: DECODE FAILED`);
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `${label}:`,
        value.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        `${label} display:`,
        Number(value) / 10 ** decimals
    );
}


// ============================================================
// DECODE STON.fi PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function decodeProvideLpPayload(
    payload: Cell
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const s =
        payload.beginParse();


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // opcode
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const op =
        loadUintSafe(s, 32);

    console.log('');
    console.log('op:');

    if (op === null) {

        console.log(
            'DECODE FAILED'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            op.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'hex:',
            hex(op)
        );

        console.log(
            'Expected PROVIDE_LP:',
            hex(PROVIDE_LP_OP)
        );

        console.log(
            'MATCH:',
            op === BigInt(PROVIDE_LP_OP)
        );
    }


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // router wallet address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const routerWallet =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'routerWalletAddress',
        routerWallet
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // refund address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const refundAddress =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'refundAddress',
        refundAddress,
        USER_ADDRESS
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // excesses address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const excessesAddress =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'excessesAddress',
        excessesAddress,
        USER_ADDRESS
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // deadline
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const deadline =
        loadUintSafe(s, 64);

    console.log('');
    console.log('deadline:');

    if (deadline === null) {

        console.log(
            'DECODE FAILED'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            deadline.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'deadline hex:',
            hex(deadline)
        );

        try {

            console.log(
                'deadline date:',
                new Date(
                    Number(deadline) * 1000
                ).toISOString()
            );

        } catch {

            console.log(
                'deadline date: INVALID'
            );
        }
    }


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining bits before additional_data:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // additional_data reference
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'ERROR: additional_data reference missing'
        );

        return;
    }


    const additionalData =
        s.loadRef();


    console.log('');
    console.log('additional_data:');

    console.log(
        'bits:',
        additionalData.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'refs:',
        additionalData.refs.length
    );


    const a =
        additionalData.beginParse();


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // min_lp_out
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const minLpOut =
        loadCoinsSafe(a);

    printAmount(
        'minLpOut',
        minLpOut,
        0
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // receiver address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const receiverAddress =
        loadAddressSafe(a);

    showAddress(
        'receiverAddress',
        receiverAddress,
        USER_ADDRESS
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // both_positive
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const bothPositive =
        loadUintSafe(a, 1);

    console.log('');
    console.log('bothPositive:');

    if (bothPositive === null) {

        console.log(
            'DECODE FAILED'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            bothPositive.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'Interpretation:',
            bothPositive === 1n
                ? 'BOTH TOKENS POSITIVE'
                : 'SINGLE SIDE'
        );
    }


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // DEX custom payload forward gas
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const customPayloadForwardGas =
        loadCoinsSafe(a);

    printAmount(
        'dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount',
        customPayloadForwardGas,
        9
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // optional custom payload
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'additional_data remaining bits:',
        a.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'additional_data remaining refs:',
        a.remainingRefs
    );


    if (a.remainingRefs > 0) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'dexCustomPayload: PRESENT'
        );

    } else {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'dexCustomPayload: NONE'
        );
    }
}


// ============================================================
// DECODE ROOSTER JETTON TRANSFER
// ============================================================

function decodeRooster() {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER JETTON TRANSFER');
    console.log('============================================================');


    const cell =
        Cell.fromBase64(ROOSTER_BOC);


    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC hash:');

    console.log(
        cell.hash().toString('hex')
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Root bits:',
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Root refs:',
        cell.refs.length
    );


    const s =
        cell.beginParse();


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // opcode
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const op =
        loadUintSafe(s, 32);


    console.log('');
    console.log('opcode:');


    if (op === null) {

        console.log(
            'DECODE FAILED'
        );

        return;
    }


    console.log(
        op.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'hex:',
        hex(op)
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected Jetton Transfer:',
        hex(JETTON_TRANSFER_OP)
    );

    console.log(
        'MATCH:',
        op === BigInt(JETTON_TRANSFER_OP)
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // query id
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const queryId =
        loadUintSafe(s, 64);

    console.log('');
    console.log('queryId:');

    console.log(
        queryId?.toString() ??
        'DECODE FAILED'
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // amount
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const amount =
        loadCoinsSafe(s);

    printAmount(
        'ROOSTER amount',
        amount,
        9
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // destination
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const destination =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'destination',
        destination,
        ROUTER_ADDRESS
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // response destination
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const responseDestination =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'responseDestination',
        responseDestination,
        USER_ADDRESS
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // custom payload
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('custom_payload:');


    try {

        const customPayloadBit =
            s.loadBit();


        console.log(
            'presence bit:',
            customPayloadBit
        );


        // loadBit() returns boolean.
        // false = no payload
        // true  = payload exists

        if (customPayloadBit === false) {

            console.log(
                'custom_payload: NONE'
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'custom_payload: PRESENT'
            );


            const cp =
                s.loadRef();


            console.log(
                'custom payload bits:',
                cp.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                'custom payload refs:',
                cp.refs.length
            );
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.log(
            'Unable to decode custom_payload'
        );

        console.log(
            String(error)
        );
    }


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // forward TON amount
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const forwardTonAmount =
        loadCoinsSafe(s);

    printAmount(
        'forwardTonAmount',
        forwardTonAmount,
        9
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // forward payload
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('forward_payload:');


    try {

        const either =
            s.loadBit();


        console.log(
            'Either bit:',
            either
        );


        let forwardPayload: Cell;


        // loadBit() returns boolean.
        // false = inline
        // true  = reference

        if (either === false) {

            console.log(
                'Payload stored inline'
            );

            forwardPayload =
                s.asCell();

        } else {

            console.log(
                'Payload stored as reference'
            );

            forwardPayload =
                s.loadRef();
        }


        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'forward payload bits:',
            forwardPayload.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'forward payload refs:',
            forwardPayload.refs.length
        );


        decodeProvideLpPayload(
            forwardPayload
        );


    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Unable to decode forward_payload'
        );

        console.log(
            String(error)
        );
    }


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining root bits:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining root refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );
}


// ============================================================
// DECODE pTON TON_TRANSFER
// ============================================================

function decodePton() {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' pTON TON_TRANSFER');
    console.log('============================================================');


    const cell =
        Cell.fromBase64(PTON_BOC);


    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC hash:');

    console.log(
        cell.hash().toString('hex')
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Root bits:',
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Root refs:',
        cell.refs.length
    );


    const s =
        cell.beginParse();


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // opcode
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const op =
        loadUintSafe(s, 32);


    console.log('');
    console.log('opcode:');


    console.log(
        op?.toString() ??
        'DECODE FAILED'
    );


    if (op !== null) {

        console.log(
            'hex:',
            hex(op)
        );

        console.log(
            'Expected TON_TRANSFER:',
            hex(PTON_TON_TRANSFER_OP)
        );

        console.log(
            'MATCH:',
            op === BigInt(PTON_TON_TRANSFER_OP)
        );
    }


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // query id
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const queryId =
        loadUintSafe(s, 64);


    console.log('');
    console.log('queryId:');

    console.log(
        queryId?.toString() ??
        'DECODE FAILED'
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // TON amount
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const amount =
        loadCoinsSafe(s);

    printAmount(
        'TON amount',
        amount,
        9
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // destination
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const destination =
        loadAddressSafe(s);

    showAddress(
        'destination',
        destination
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // remaining
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining bits before payload:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining refs before payload:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // forward payload
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const either =
            s.loadBit();


        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'forwardPayload Either bit:',
            either
        );


        let payload: Cell;


        if (either === false) {

            console.log(
                'forwardPayload is inline'
            );

            payload =
                s.asCell();

        } else {

            console.log(
                'forwardPayload is reference'
            );

            payload =
                s.loadRef();
        }


        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'pTON forward payload bits:',
            payload.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'pTON forward payload refs:',
            payload.refs.length
        );


        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Inspect first opcode
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (payload.bits.length >= 32) {

            const ps =
                payload.beginParse();


            const payloadOp =
                loadUintSafe(ps, 32);


            console.log('');

            console.log(
                'pTON forward payload opcode:'
            );

            console.log(
                payloadOp?.toString() ??
                'DECODE FAILED'
            );


            if (payloadOp !== null) {

                console.log(
                    'hex:',
                    hex(payloadOp)
                );


                console.log(
                    'Expected PROVIDE_LP:',
                    hex(PROVIDE_LP_OP)
                );


                console.log(
                    'MATCH PROVIDE_LP:',
                    payloadOp ===
                    BigInt(PROVIDE_LP_OP)
                );
            }
        }


        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // If the payload itself is PROVIDE_LP,
        // decode it completely.
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (payload.bits.length >= 32) {

            const ps =
                payload.beginParse();


            const payloadOp =
                loadUintSafe(ps, 32);


            if (
                payloadOp ===
                BigInt(PROVIDE_LP_OP)
            ) {

                decodeProvideLpPayload(
                    payload
                );
            }
        }


    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Unable to decode pTON forward payload'
        );

        console.log(
            String(error)
        );
    }


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Remaining pTON root bits:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining pTON root refs:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 EXACT LIQUIDITY BOC DECODER'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        ' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');

    console.log(
        USER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');

    console.log(
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');

    console.log(
        ROOSTER_MASTER.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');

    console.log(
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER USER JETTON WALLET:');

    console.log(
        ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL pTON JETTON WALLET:');

    console.log(
        POOL_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // ROOSTER
    // ========================================================

    decodeRooster();


    // ========================================================
    // pTON
    // ========================================================

    decodePton();


    // ========================================================
    // COMPLETE
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' DECODING COMPLETE'
    );

    console.log(
        ' TRANSACTION SENT: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const EXPECTED = {
    jettonTransferOpcode: 0x0f8a7ea5,
    provideLpOpcode: 0x37c096df,

    roosterAmount: 266742370687260n,

    routerAddress:
        'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3',

    userAddress:
        'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix',

    routerPtonWallet:
        'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3',

    minLpOut: 346725530921n,

    forwardTonAmount: 235000000n,

    bothPositive: true,

    dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount: 0n,

    hasDexCustomPayload: false,

    queryId: 0,
};

function check(
    name: string,
    actual: unknown,
    expected: unknown
) {
    const pass = actual === expected;

    console.log(
        `${pass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | ${name} | actual=${String(actual)} | expected=${String(expected)}`
    );

    if (!pass) {
        throw new Error(
            `${name} mismatch`
        );
    }
}

function addressString(address: Address | null): string {
    if (!address) {
        return 'null';
    }

    return address.toString();
}

function checkAddress(
    name: string,
    actual: Address | null,
    expected: string
) {
    const actualString = addressString(actual);

    check(
        name,
        actualString,
        expected
    );
}

function decodeProvideLpInnerCell(
    cell: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('----- PROVIDE_LP INNER CELL -----');

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = slice.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress = slice.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive = slice.loadBit() === true;

    const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    const hasDexCustomPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    let dexCustomPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasDexCustomPayload) {
        dexCustomPayload = slice.loadRef();
    }

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'receiverAddress:',
        addressString(receiverAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount:',
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'hasDexCustomPayload:',
        hasDexCustomPayload
    );

    check(
        'minLpOut',
        minLpOut,
        EXPECTED.minLpOut
    );

    checkAddress(
        'receiverAddress',
        receiverAddress,
        EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

    check(
        'bothPositive',
        bothPositive,
        EXPECTED.bothPositive
    );

    check(
        'dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount',
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount,
        EXPECTED.dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount
    );

    check(
        'hasDexCustomPayload',
        hasDexCustomPayload,
        EXPECTED.hasDexCustomPayload
    );

    if (slice.remainingBits !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Inner cell has ${slice.remainingBits} remaining bits`
        );
    }

    if (slice.remainingRefs !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Inner cell has ${slice.remainingRefs} remaining refs`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'INNER CELL STRUCTURE: PASS'
    );

    return {
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount,
        dexCustomPayload,
    };
}

function decodeProvideLpPayload(
    cell: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('----- PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD -----');

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

    const routerWalletAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const deadline =
        slice.loadUint(64);

    const innerCell =
        slice.loadRef();

    console.log(
        'opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'routerWalletAddress:',
        addressString(routerWalletAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'refundAddress:',
        addressString(refundAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'excessesAddress:',
        addressString(excessesAddress)
    );

    console.log(
        'deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    check(
        'provideLpOpcode',
        opcode,
        EXPECTED.provideLpOpcode
    );

    checkAddress(
        'routerWalletAddress',
        routerWalletAddress,
        EXPECTED.routerPtonWallet
    );

    checkAddress(
        'refundAddress',
        refundAddress,
        EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

    checkAddress(
        'excessesAddress',
        excessesAddress,
        EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

    if (deadline <= 0) {
        throw new Error(
            'Invalid deadline'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'deadline validity: PASS'
    );

    if (slice.remainingBits !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Provide LP payload has ${slice.remainingBits} remaining bits`
        );
    }

    if (slice.remainingRefs !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Provide LP payload has ${slice.remainingRefs} remaining refs`
        );
    }

    const inner =
        decodeProvideLpInnerCell(innerCell);

    return {
        opcode,
        routerWalletAddress,
        refundAddress,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        inner,
    };
}

function decodeJettonTransfer(
    cell: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('----- JETTON TRANSFER -----');

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode =
        slice.loadUint(32);

    const queryId =
        slice.loadUint(64);

    const amount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    const destination =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination =
        slice.loadAddress();

    const hasCustomPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        customPayload = slice.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount =
        slice.loadCoins();

    const hasForwardPayload =
        slice.loadBit();

    let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {
        forwardPayload = slice.loadRef();
    }

    console.log(
        'opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'queryId:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'amount:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'destination:',
        addressString(destination)
    );

    console.log(
        'responseDestination:',
        addressString(responseDestination)
    );

    console.log(
        'hasCustomPayload:',
        hasCustomPayload
    );

    console.log(
        'forwardTonAmount:',
        forwardTonAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'hasForwardPayload:',
        hasForwardPayload
    );

    check(
        'jettonTransferOpcode',
        opcode,
        EXPECTED.jettonTransferOpcode
    );

    check(
        'queryId',
        queryId,
        EXPECTED.queryId
    );

    check(
        'roosterAmount',
        amount,
        EXPECTED.roosterAmount
    );

    checkAddress(
        'destination',
        destination,
        EXPECTED.routerAddress
    );

    check(
        'forwardTonAmount',
        forwardTonAmount,
        EXPECTED.forwardTonAmount
    );

    check(
        'hasCustomPayload',
        hasCustomPayload,
        false
    );

    check(
        'hasForwardPayload',
        hasForwardPayload,
        true
    );

    if (!forwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'Forward payload is missing'
        );
    }

    const provideLp =
        decodeProvideLpPayload(
            forwardPayload
        );

    if (slice.remainingBits !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Jetton transfer has ${slice.remainingBits} remaining bits`
        );
    }

    if (slice.remainingRefs !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Jetton transfer has ${slice.remainingRefs} remaining refs`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'JETTON TRANSFER STRUCTURE: PASS'
    );

    return {
        opcode,
        queryId,
        amount,
        destination,
        responseDestination,
        customPayload,
        forwardTonAmount,
        forwardPayload,
        provideLp,
    };
}

function loadBocFile(): Cell {
    const bocPath =
        path.resolve(
            process.cwd(),
            'rooster_v22_generated.boc'
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'BOC FILE:',
        bocPath
    );

    if (!fs.existsSync(bocPath)) {
        throw new Error(
            `BOC file not found: ${bocPath}`
        );
    }
let boc =
    fs.readFileSync(bocPath);

    console.log(
        'BOC SIZE:',
        boc.length,
        'bytes'
    );

    if (boc.length === 0) {
        throw new Error(
            'BOC file is empty'
        );
    }

   let rawMagic =
    boc.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex');

if (rawMagic !== 'b5ee9c72') {
    const base64Text =
        boc.toString('utf8').trim();

    try {
        boc = Buffer.from(
            base64Text,
            'base64'
        );
    } catch {
        throw new Error(
            'INVALID BOC FILE: not raw BOC and not valid Base64'
        );
    }
}

const magic =
    boc.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex');

console.log(
    'BOC MAGIC:',
    magic
);

check(
    'BOC magic',
    magic,
    'b5ee9c72'
);

    const cells =
        Cell.fromBoc(boc);

    if (cells.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected 1 root cell, found ${cells.length}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOT CELLS:',
        cells.length
    );

    return cells[0];
}

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );

    );
    console.log(

import { Address, Cell, Slice } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER_ADDRESS =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

function printCellInfo(label: string, cell: Cell): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log(`cell type : ${cell.type}`);
    console.log(`bits      : ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`refs      : ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`hash      : ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`);

    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        console.log(`remaining : ${s.remainingBits} bits`);

        if (s.remainingBits >= 8) {
            const count = Math.min(
                Math.floor(s.remainingBits / 8),
                32,
            );

            const bytes: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                bytes.push(s.loadUint(8));
            }

            const ascii = bytes
                .map((b) =>
                    b >= 32 && b <= 126
                        ? String.fromCharCode(b)
                        : '.',
                )
                .join('');

            const hex = bytes
                .map((b) =>
                    b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'),
                )
                .join(' ');

            console.log(`ASCII     : ${ascii}`);
            console.log(`HEX       : ${hex}`);
        }
    } catch {
        console.log('preview   : unavailable');
    }
}

function printSlice(label: string, slice: Slice): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log(`remaining bits : ${slice.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`remaining refs : ${slice.remainingRefs}`);

    // Clone the slice so the original reader position is not changed.
    const s = slice.clone();

    try {
        if (s.remainingBits >= 267) {
            const address = s.loadAddress();

            if (address) {
                console.log(`ADDRESS       : ${address.toString()}`);
                return;
            }
        }
    } catch {
        // Not an address slice.
    }

    // Try to interpret the slice as a byte string.
    try {
        const byteCount = Math.floor(s.remainingBits / 8);

        if (byteCount > 0) {
            const count = Math.min(byteCount, 64);
            const bytes: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                bytes.push(s.loadUint(8));
            }

            const ascii = bytes
                .map((b) =>
                    b >= 32 && b <= 126
                        ? String.fromCharCode(b)
                        : '.',
                )
                .join('');

            const hex = bytes
                .map((b) =>
                    b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'),
                )
                .join(' ');

            console.log(`ASCII         : ${ascii}`);
            console.log(`HEX           : ${hex}`);
        }
    } catch {
        console.log('slice preview : unavailable');
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI ROUTER DATA STRUCTURE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`ROUTER: ${ROUTER_ADDRESS}`);
    console.log(`ENDPOINT: ${ENDPOINT}`);
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const router = Address.parse(ROUTER_ADDRESS);

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(`MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`);
    console.log('');

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        latest.last.seqno,
        router,
        'get_router_data',
    );

    console.log(`EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`);
    console.log('');

    if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
        console.log('GETTER FAILED');
        console.log('RESULT RAW:');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);
        return;
    }

    const reader = result.reader;

    console.log('================ ROUTER FIELDS =================');
    console.log('');

    // FIELD 0
    const routerId = reader.readBigNumber();

    console.log('FIELD [0]');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log('type  : int');
    console.log(`value : ${routerId}`);

    // FIELD 1
    const dexType = reader.readString();

console.log('');
console.log('FIELD [1] - DEX TYPE');
console.log('----------------------------------------');
console.log(`value : ${dexType}`);

    // FIELD 2
    const locked = reader.readBigNumber();

    console.log('');
    console.log('FIELD [2]');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log('type  : int');
    console.log(`value : ${locked}`);

    console.log(
        `status: ${locked === 0n ? 'UNLOCKED' : 'LOCKED'}`,
    );

    // FIELD 3
  const adminAddress = reader.readAddress();

console.log('');
console.log('FIELD [3] - ADMIN / ADDRESS');
console.log('----------------------------------------');
console.log(`ADDRESS : ${adminAddress.toString()}`);

    // FIELD 4
    const field4 = reader.readCell();

    printCellInfo(
        'FIELD [4] - CELL',
        field4,
    );

    // FIELD 5
    const field5 = reader.readCell();

    printCellInfo(
        'FIELD [5] - CELL',
        field5,
    );

    // FIELD 6
    const field6 = reader.readCell();

    printCellInfo(
        'FIELD [6] - CELL',
        field6,
    );

    // FIELD 7
    const field7 = reader.readCell();

    printCellInfo(
        'FIELD [7] - CELL',
        field7,
    );

    // FIELD 8
    const field8 = reader.readCell();

    printCellInfo(
        'FIELD [8] - CELL',
        field8,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================ ROUTER SUMMARY =================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`ROUTER ID : ${routerId}`);
    console.log(`LOCKED    : ${locked}`);
    console.log(
        `STATUS    : ${locked === 0n ? 'UNLOCKED' : 'LOCKED'}`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FIELD [4] HASH:', field4.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('FIELD [5] HASH:', field5.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('FIELD [6] HASH:', field6.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('FIELD [7] HASH:', field7.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('FIELD [8] HASH:', field8.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('================ REMAINING ======================');

    try {
        console.log(
            `Remaining tuple items: ${reader.remaining}`,
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('Remaining count unavailable');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER_ADDRESS =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

function dumpRef(
    index: number,
    cell: any,
): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`REF [${index}]`);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    console.log(`type : ${cell.type}`);
    console.log(`bits : ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`refs : ${cell.refs.length}`);

    console.log(
        `hash : ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`,
    );

    try {
        console.log(
            `BOC  : ${cell.toBoc({
                idx: false,
            }).toString('base64')}`,
        );
    } catch {
        console.log('BOC  : unavailable');
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER FIELD [4] DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const router = Address.parse(
        ROUTER_ADDRESS,
    );

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        `MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`,
    );

    console.log('');

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        latest.last.seqno,
        router,
        'get_router_data',
    );

    console.log(
        `EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`,
    );

    if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
        console.log('GETTER FAILED');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);
        return;
    }

    const reader = result.reader;

    // [0]
    reader.readBigNumber();

    // [1]
    reader.readString();

    // [2]
    reader.readBigNumber();

    // [3]
    reader.readAddress();

    // [4]
    const field4 = reader.readCell();

    console.log('');
    console.log('FIELD [4]');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        `type : ${field4.type}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `bits : ${field4.bits.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `refs : ${field4.refs.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `hash : ${field4.hash().toString('hex')}`,
    );

    console.log('');

    try {
        console.log(
            'BOC:',
        );

        console.log(
            field4
                .toBoc({
                    idx: false,
                })
                .toString('base64'),
        );
    } catch {
        console.log(
            'BOC: unavailable',
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('FIELD [4] BIT DATA');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    try {
        const s = field4.beginParse();

        console.log(
            `remaining bits : ${s.remainingBits}`,
        );

        console.log(
            `remaining refs : ${s.remainingRefs}`,
        );

        const fullBytes =
            Math.floor(
                s.remainingBits / 8,
            );

        const bytes: number[] = [];

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < fullBytes;
            i++
        ) {
            bytes.push(
                s.loadUint(8),
            );
        }

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'HEX:',
        );

        console.log(
            bytes
                .map((b) =>
                    b
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, '0'),
                )
                .join(' '),
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'DECIMAL:',
        );

        console.log(
            bytes.join(', '),
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'Bit decode failed:',
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e),
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'FIELD [4] REFERENCES',
    );

    console.log(
        '----------------------------------------',
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < field4.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        dumpRef(
            i,
            field4.refs[i],
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}
