import { Address } from '@ton/core';

export async function run(provider: any) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    console.log('===== DECODE ROUTER LIBRARY REFERENCE =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());

    const state = await provider.provider(router).getState();

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('Router is not active.');
        return;
    }

    const code = state.state.code;

    if (!code) {
        console.log('Router code is missing.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\nCode bytes:', code.length);
    console.log('Code HEX:');
    console.log(code.toString('hex'));

    console.log('\nCode Base64 BOC:');
    console.log(code.toString('base64'));

    console.log('\n===== RAW BYTES =====');

    for (let i = 0; i < code.length; i++) {
        console.log(
            `${i.toString().padStart(2, '0')}: 0x${code[i]
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, '0')}`
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== POSSIBLE LIBRARY HASH REGION =====');

    if (code.length >= 34) {
        console.log(
            'Bytes 2..33:',
            code.subarray(2, 34).toString('hex')
        );
    }

    if (code.length >= 35) {
        console.log(
            'Bytes 3..34:',
            code.subarray(3, 35).toString('hex')
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeRouterLibraryRefs.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

export async function run(provider: any) {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('   EXTRACT ROUTER LIBRARY HASHES');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const state =
        await provider.provider(ROUTER).getState();

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('Router is not active.');
        return;
    }

    const data =
        Cell.fromBoc(state.state.data)[0];

    const libraryContainer = data.refs[1];

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Library container refs:',
        libraryContainer.refs.length
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < libraryContainer.refs.length; i++) {

        const lib = libraryContainer.refs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`===== LIBRARY REF [${i}] =====`);

        console.log('Cell bits:', lib.bits.length);
        console.log('Cell refs:', lib.refs.length);
        console.log('Exotic:', lib.isExotic);
        console.log('Cell type:', lib.type);

        /*
         * Library reference is an exotic cell and cannot
         * be parsed with beginParse().
         *
         * Its payload is:
         *
         *   8 bits  = type 2
         *   256 bits = library hash
         *
         * We therefore read the raw bitstring.
         */

        const bits = lib.bits.toString();

        console.log('');
        console.log('RAW BITS:');
        console.log(bits);

        const tagBits = bits.substring(0, 8);
        const hashBits = bits.substring(8, 264);

        console.log('');
        console.log('TAG BITS:');
        console.log(tagBits);

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH BITS:');
        console.log(hashBits);

        const tag = parseInt(tagBits, 2);

        let hashHex = '';

        for (let j = 0; j < hashBits.length; j += 8) {
            const byte = parseInt(
                hashBits.substring(j, j + 8),
                2
            );

            hashHex += byte
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(2, '0');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('TAG:', tag);
        console.log('INTERNAL LIBRARY HASH:');
        console.log(hashHex);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('              COMPLETE');
    console.log('              NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeRouterPoolProvideLpV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const ROUTER_LT = '99809465000006';
const POOL_LT = '99809465000008';

const PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

function findBody(msg: any): string | null {
    const candidates = [
        msg?.msg_data?.body,
        msg?.message_content?.body,
        msg?.body,
    ];

    for (const x of candidates) {
        if (typeof x === 'string' && x.length > 20) {
            return x;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function decodeProvideLp(boc: string) {
    const root = Cell.fromBase64(boc);
    const s = root.beginParse();

    const op = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const fromUser = s.loadAddress();
    const leftAmount = s.loadCoins();
    const rightAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const dexPayload = s.loadRef();
    const d = dexPayload.beginParse();

    const transferredOp = d.loadUint(32);
    const tokenWallet1 = d.loadAddress();
    const refundAddress = d.loadAddress();
    const excessesAddress = d.loadAddress();
    const deadline = d.loadUintBig(64);

    const provideBody = d.loadRef();
    const p = provideBody.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = p.loadCoins();
    const toAddress = p.loadAddress();
    const bothPositive = p.loadBit();
    const fwdAmount = p.loadCoins();

    let customPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (p.loadBit()) {
        customPayload = p.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        op: `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`,
        queryId,

        fromUser,
        leftAmount,
        rightAmount,

        transferredOp:
            `0x${transferredOp.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`,

        tokenWallet1,
        refundAddress,
        excessesAddress,

        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        toAddress,
        bothPositive,
        fwdAmount,

        customPayload,
    };
}

async function getTx(address: string, lt: string) {
    const url =
        `${ENDPOINT}?address=${encodeURIComponent(address)}&limit=20`;

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `HTTP ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`
        );
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter error: ${JSON.stringify(json)}`
        );
    }

    const tx = (json.result ?? []).find(
        (x: any) =>
            String(x?.transaction_id?.lt) === lt
    );

    if (!tx) {
        throw new Error(
            `Transaction LT ${lt} not found for ${address}`
        );
    }

    return tx;
}

function getProvideMessage(tx: any, direction: 'in' | 'out') {
    if (direction === 'in') {
        return tx.in_msg;
    }

    const outs = tx.out_msgs ?? [];

    return outs.find((msg: any) => {
        const body = findBody(msg);

        if (!body) return false;

        try {
            const cell = Cell.fromBase64(body);
            return cell.beginParse().loadUint(32) === PROVIDE_LP;
        } catch {
            return false;
        }
    });
}

async function main() {
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER -> POOL PROVIDE_LP DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Router TX...');
    const routerTx = await getTx(ROUTER, ROUTER_LT);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool TX...');
    const poolTx = await getTx(POOL, POOL_LT);

    const routerOut = getProvideMessage(
        routerTx,
        'out'
    );

    const poolIn = getProvideMessage(
        poolTx,
        'in'
    );

    if (!routerOut) {
        throw new Error(
            'Router provide_lp_v2 output message not found'
        );
    }

    if (!poolIn) {
        throw new Error(
            'Pool provide_lp_v2 input message not found'
        );
    }

    const routerBody = findBody(routerOut);
    const poolBody = findBody(poolIn);

    if (!routerBody || !poolBody) {
        throw new Error(
            'Could not locate message BOC'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER -> POOL');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router TX LT      :', ROUTER_LT);
    console.log('Message created LT:', routerOut.created_lt);
    console.log('Source            :', routerOut.source);
    console.log('Destination       :', routerOut.destination);
    console.log('TON value         :', routerOut.value);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER BOC:');
    console.log(routerBody);

    const routerDecoded =
        decodeProvideLp(routerBody);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER DECODED:');
    console.log(
        JSON.stringify(
    routerDecoded,
    (_, value) => typeof value === 'bigint' ? value.toString() : value,
    2
)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' POOL RECEIVED MESSAGE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool TX LT        :', POOL_LT);
    console.log('Message created LT:', poolIn.created_lt);
    console.log('Source            :', poolIn.source);
    console.log('Destination       :', poolIn.destination);
    console.log('TON value         :', poolIn.value);

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL BOC:');
    console.log(poolBody);

    const poolDecoded =
        decodeProvideLp(poolBody);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
    JSON.stringify(
        poolDecoded,
        (_, value) => typeof value === 'bigint' ? value.toString() : value,
        2
    )
);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FIELD-BY-FIELD COMPARISON');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const fields = [
        'routerWallet',
        'refundAddress',
        'excessesAddress',
        'deadline',
        'minLpOut',
        'receiverAddress',
        'bothPositive',
        'dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount',
        'customPayload',
    ] as const;

    for (const field of fields) {
        const a = String(
            (routerDecoded as any)[field]
        );

        const b = String(
            (poolDecoded as any)[field]
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(field);
        console.log('  Router:', a);
        console.log('  Pool  :', b);
        console.log('  MATCH :', a === b ? 'YES' : 'NO');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' EXPECTED HISTORICAL VALUES');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('minLpOut       : 150109265');
    console.log('deadline       : 1790714871');
    console.log('bothPositive   : true');
    console.log('pTON amount    : 249908');

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteRoundRobinEngine, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Functions } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const LIBRARY_CODE_HASH =
    'ca4c3052a1396aef50ca358ac355e2a99be4e1683eb87a2c11fbd452bb4acec5';

function printCell(label: string, cell: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${label} =====`);
    console.log('Type:', cell.type);
    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('Hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('BOC:', cell.toBoc().toString('base64'));
}

function printBits(label: string, cell: Cell) {
    if (cell.type !== -1) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(`----- ${label} RAW BITS -----`);
        console.log('SKIPPED: exotic cell');
        console.log('Type:', cell.type);
        console.log('Bit count:', cell.bits.length);
        return;
    }

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    let bits = '';

    while (slice.remainingBits > 0) {
        bits += slice.loadBit() ? '1' : '0';
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(`----- ${label} RAW BITS -----`);
    console.log(bits);
    console.log('Bit count:', bits.length);
}

        }),

        new LiteSingleEngine({

    const request = {
        kind: 'liteServer.getAccountState' as const,

        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,

            shard: master.last.shard,
            workchain: master.last.workchain,
            fileHash: master.last.fileHash,
            rootHash: master.last.rootHash,
        },

        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
            workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
            id: Buffer.from(ROUTER.hash),
        },
    };

    const result = await client.engine.query(
        Functions.liteServer_getAccountState,
        request
    );

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(result.state);
    const root = roots[0];

    console.log('STATE BYTES:', result.state.length);
    console.log('ROOT TYPE:', root.type);
    console.log('ROOT BITS:', root.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', root.refs.length);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // ROOT REF 0
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const libraryRef = root.refs[0];

    printCell('ROOT REF 0 - LIBRARY', libraryRef);

    if (libraryRef.type === 2) {
        const actualHash = libraryRef.hash()
            .toString('hex')
            .toLowerCase();

        console.log(
            'Library code hash matches:',
            actualHash === LIBRARY_CODE_HASH.toLowerCase()
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // ROOT REF 1
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const storage = root.refs[1];

    printCell('ROOT REF 1 - STORAGE', storage);
    printBits('ROOT REF 1 - STORAGE', storage);

    console.log('');

    console.log('STORAGE CHILD TREE');
    console.log('========================================');

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // STORAGE CHILD 0
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const child0 = storage.refs[0];

    printCell('STORAGE CHILD 0', child0);
    printBits('STORAGE CHILD 0', child0);

    console.log('');
    console.log('STORAGE CHILD 0 REFS:', child0.refs.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < child0.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = child0.refs[i];

        printCell(
            `STORAGE CHILD 0 -> REF ${i}`,
            ref
        );

        printBits(
            `STORAGE CHILD 0 -> REF ${i}`,
            ref
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // STORAGE CHILD 1
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const child1 = storage.refs[1];

    printCell('STORAGE CHILD 1', child1);
    printBits('STORAGE CHILD 1', child1);

    console.log('');
    console.log('STORAGE CHILD 1 REFS:', child1.refs.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < child1.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = child1.refs[i];

        printCell(
            `STORAGE CHILD 1 -> REF ${i}`,
            ref
        );

        printBits(
            `STORAGE CHILD 1 -> REF ${i}`,
            ref
        );

            );

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER =
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000006';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

function firstOpcode(body: string): string {
    try {
        const cell = Cell.fromBase64(body);
        const s = cell.beginParse();
        return '0x' + s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'INVALID_BOC';
    }
}

function findBody(msg: any): string | null {
    const candidates = [
        msg?.msg_data?.body,
        msg?.message_content?.body,
        msg?.body,
        msg?.message_content?.data,
        msg?.msg_data?.data,
    ];

    for (const x of candidates) {
        if (typeof x === 'string' && x.length > 20) {
            return x;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function printMessage(
    title: string,
    msg: any,
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(title);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log('source      :', msg?.source);
    console.log('destination :', msg?.destination);
    console.log('value       :', msg?.value);
    console.log('created_lt  :', msg?.created_lt);

    console.log('');
    console.log('MESSAGE KEYS:');

    if (msg && typeof msg === 'object') {
        console.log(Object.keys(msg).join(', '));
    }

    const body = findBody(msg);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('BODY: NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('BODY FOUND');
    console.log('BOC length:', body.length);
    console.log('BOC:', body);

    const op = firstOpcode(body);

    console.log('');
    console.log('OPCODE:', op);

    if (op === '0x37c096df') {
        console.log('');
        console.log('>>> STON.FI v2.2 PROVIDE_LP MESSAGE');
    } else if (op === '0x7362d09c') {
        console.log('');
        console.log('>>> JETTON_NOTIFY');
    } else if (op === '0x50c6a654') {
        console.log('');
        console.log('>>> ADD_LIQUIDITY_V2');
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log('>>> UNKNOWN OPCODE');
    }
}

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000007n;

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';

    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    return (
        Number(value) / 1e9
    ).toFixed(9);
}

async function main() {
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER TX 99491281000007 DECODER');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY ONLY');
    console.log('==========================================');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        ROUTER,
        {
            limit: 100,
        }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        (t) => t.lt === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND'
        );
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('LT:');
    console.log(tx.lt.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('HASH:');
    console.log(
        tx.hash().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TIME:');
    console.log(
        new Date(
            tx.now * 1000
        ).toISOString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');

    if (tx.inMessage) {
        const body =
            tx.inMessage.body;

        console.log(
            'OPCODE:',
            opcodeHex(
                getOpcode(body)
            )
        );

        if (
            tx.inMessage.info.type ===
            'internal'
        ) {
            console.log(
                'SOURCE:',
                tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'DEST:',
                tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'VALUE:',
                ton(
                    tx.inMessage.info.value.coins
                ),
                'TON'
            );
        }

        console.log(
            'BODY:',
            body.bits.length,
            'bits /',
            body.refs.length,
            'refs'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC:');

        console.log(
            body
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64')
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(' ALL OUTGOING MESSAGES');
    console.log('==========================================');

    let index = 0;

    for (
        const msg
        of tx.outMessages.values()
    ) {
        const body = msg.body;

        const op =
            getOpcode(body);

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `OUT #${index}`
        );

        if (
            msg.info.type ===
            'internal'
        ) {
            console.log(
                'SOURCE:',
                msg.info.src.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'DESTINATION:',
                msg.info.dest.toString()
            );

            console.log(
                'VALUE:',
                ton(
                    msg.info.value.coins
                ),
                'TON'
            );
        }

        console.log(
            'OPCODE:',
            opcodeHex(op)
        );

        console.log(
            'BODY:',
            body.bits.length,
            'bits /',
            body.refs.length,
            'refs'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC:');

        console.log(
            body
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64')
        );

        index++;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(
        'OUTGOING COUNT:',
        index
    );
    console.log('READ ONLY COMPLETE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('==========================================');
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('ERROR:');
    console.error(error);
});

FILE: scripts\decodeRouterV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

function cellToAddress(cell: Cell): string {
    try {
        const slice = cell.beginParse();
        return slice.loadAddress()?.toString() ?? 'null';
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_FAILED';
    }
}

function cellToString(cell: Cell): string {
    try {
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        if (slice.remainingBits < 8) {
            return 'EMPTY';
        }

        return slice.loadStringTail();
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_FAILED';
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER DATA DECODER');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = (provider as any)._tc;

    if (!client) {
        throw new Error('TonClient not found');
    }

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('Network:', provider.network());
    console.log('');

    const result = await client.runMethodWithError(
        ROUTER,
        'get_router_data',
        []
    );

    const reader = result.stack;

    console.log('===== RAW STACK ITEMS =====');
    console.log('Item count:', reader.items.length);
    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < reader.items.length; i++) {
        const item = reader.items[i];

        console.log(`--- ITEM ${i} ---`);
        console.log('Type:', item.type);

        if (item.type === 'int') {
            console.log('Value:', item.value.toString());
        }

        if (item.type === 'cell') {
            console.log('Cell:', item.cell.toString());
            console.log('Address:', cellToAddress(item.cell));
            console.log('String:', cellToString(item.cell));
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    console.log('===== EXPECTED INTERPRETATION =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router ID:');
    console.log(reader.items[0].value.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('DEX Type:');
    console.log(cellToString(reader.items[1].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool Creation Enabled:');
    console.log(reader.items[2].value.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 3 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[3].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 4 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[4].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 5 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[5].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 6 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[6].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 7 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[7].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell 8 Address:');
    console.log(cellToAddress(reader.items[8].cell));

    console.log('');
    console.log('==================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('==================================================');
}

import { Address, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const USER =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

function showAddress(
  label: string,
  address: Address | null,
) {
  console.log(
    `${label}:`,
    address ? address.toString() : "null",
  );
}

function inspectCellTree(
  name: string,
  cell: Cell,
  depth = 0,
) {
  const p = "  ".repeat(depth);

  console.log(`${p}${name}`);
  console.log(`${p}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
  console.log(`${p}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
  console.log(
    `${p}hash: ${cell.hash().toString("hex")}`,
  );

  for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
    inspectCellTree(
      `ref[${i}]`,
      cell.refs[i],
      depth + 1,
    );
  }
}

function decodeProvideLpCell(
  cell: Cell,
) {
  console.log();
  console.log("===== PROVIDE_LP CELL =====");

  const s = cell.beginParse();

  console.log(
    "Initial bits:",
    s.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "Initial refs:",
    s.remainingRefs,
  );

  if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
    console.log("Not enough bits for opcode.");
    return;
  }

  const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

  console.log(
    "Opcode:",
    "0x" +
      opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
  );

  if (opcode !== 0x37c096df) {
    console.log(
      "WARNING: opcode is not PROVIDE_LP",
    );
  } else {
    console.log("Opcode check: PASS");
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 267) {
    const routerWallet =
      s.loadAddress();

    showAddress(
      "routerWalletAddress",
      routerWallet,
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "Not enough bits for routerWalletAddress",
    );
    return;
  }

  if (s.remainingBits >= 1) {
    const minLpOut =
      s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
      "minLpOut:",
      minLpOut.toString(),
    );

    console.log(
      "Expected minLpOut:",
      MIN_LP_OUT.toString(),
    );

    console.log(
      "minLpOut check:",
      minLpOut === MIN_LP_OUT
        ? "PASS"
        : "FAIL",
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "Trailing bits:",
    s.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "Trailing refs:",
    s.remainingRefs,
  );
}

function decodeJettonTransfer(
  cell: Cell,
) {
  console.log();
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" JETTON TRANSFER EXACT DECODE");
  console.log("==============================================");

  const s = cell.beginParse();

  console.log();
  console.log("===== INITIAL =====");
  console.log("bits:", s.remainingBits);
  console.log("refs:", s.remainingRefs);

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // opcode
  // ------------------------------------------------

  if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
    throw new Error("Missing Jetton Transfer opcode");
  }

  const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 1. OPCODE =====");
  console.log(
    "Opcode:",
    "0x" +
      opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
  );

  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    "0x0f8a7ea5",
  );

  console.log(
    "Check:",
    opcode === 0x0f8a7ea5
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL",
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // query_id
  // ------------------------------------------------

  const queryId =
    s.loadUintBig(64);

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 2. QUERY ID =====");
  console.log(
    "query_id:",
    queryId.toString(),
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // amount
  // ------------------------------------------------

  const amount =
    s.loadCoins();

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 3. AMOUNT =====");
  console.log(
    "amount:",
    amount.toString(),
  );

  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(),
  );

  console.log(
    "Check:",
    amount === ROOSTER_AMOUNT
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL",
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // destination
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 4. DESTINATION =====");

  const destination =
    s.loadAddress();

  showAddress(
    "destination",
    destination,
  );

  console.log(
    "Expected Router:",
    ROUTER,
  );

  console.log(
    "Check:",
    destination?.toString() === ROUTER
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL",
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // response destination
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 5. RESPONSE DESTINATION =====");

  const responseDestination =
    s.loadAddress();

  showAddress(
    "response_destination",
    responseDestination,
  );

  console.log(
    "Expected User:",
    USER,
  );

  console.log(
    "Check:",
    responseDestination?.toString() === USER
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL",
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // custom payload
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 6. CUSTOM PAYLOAD =====");

  const customPayloadBit =
    s.loadBit();

  console.log(
    "custom_payload:",
    customPayloadBit
      ? "present"
      : "none",
  );

  if (customPayloadBit) {
    if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
      const customPayload =
        s.loadRef();

      console.log(
        "Custom payload reference: present",
      );

      inspectCellTree(
        "customPayload",
        customPayload,
      );
    } else {
      console.log(
        "WARNING: custom payload bit set but no ref",
      );
    }
  } else {
    console.log(
      "Custom payload check: PASS (none)",
    );
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // forward TON amount
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 7. FORWARD TON AMOUNT =====");

  const forwardTonAmount =
    s.loadCoins();

  console.log(
    "forward_ton_amount:",
    forwardTonAmount.toString(),
  );

  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT.toString(),
  );

  console.log(
    "Check:",
    forwardTonAmount === FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL",
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // forward payload
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 8. FORWARD PAYLOAD =====");

  if (s.remainingBits < 1) {
    throw new Error(
      "Missing forward_payload selector",
    );
  }

  const forwardPayloadIsRef =
    s.loadBit();

  console.log(
    "forward_payload mode:",
    forwardPayloadIsRef
      ? "REFERENCE"
      : "INLINE",
  );

  let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

  if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
      throw new Error(
        "Forward payload says REFERENCE but no ref exists",
      );
    }

    forwardPayload =
      s.loadRef();

    console.log(
      "Forward payload reference: PASS",
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "Forward payload is inline.",
    );

    /*
     * If inline, the remaining slice itself is
     * the forward payload.
     *
     * We preserve it as a Cell for inspection.
     */
    forwardPayload =
      s.asCell();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // root trailing
  // ------------------------------------------------

  console.log();
  console.log("===== 9. ROOT AFTER PARSE =====");

  console.log(
    "Remaining bits:",
    s.remainingBits,
  );

  console.log(
    "Remaining refs:",
    s.remainingRefs,
  );

  // ------------------------------------------------
  // forward payload decode
  // ------------------------------------------------

  if (forwardPayload) {
    inspectCellTree(
      "forwardPayload",
      forwardPayload,
    );

    decodeProvideLpCell(
      forwardPayload,
    );
  }
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 SDK JETTON TRANSFER DECODE");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");

  console.log();
  console.log("===== BUILD SDK BODY =====");

  const routerAddress =
    Address.parse(ROUTER);

  const userAddress =
    Address.parse(USER);

  const roosterMaster =
    Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

  const ptonMaster =
    Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
      routerAddress,
    );

  const params =
    await routerContract
      .getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        provider.provider(routerAddress),
        {
          userWalletAddress: userAddress,
          sendTokenAddress: roosterMaster,
          otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
          sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
          minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
          gasAmount: GAS_AMOUNT,
          forwardGasAmount:
            FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
          transferExcessAddress:
            userAddress,
        },
      );

  if (!params.body) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK returned empty body",
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "SDK body: PASS",
  );

  console.log(
    "TX to:",
    params.to.toString(),
  );

  console.log(
    "TX value:",
    params.value.toString(),
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("===== COMPLETE SDK BODY TREE =====");

  inspectCellTree(
    "SDK BODY",
    params.body,
  );

  decodeJettonTransfer(
    params.body,
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" DECODE COMPLETE");
  console.log(" NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeSegment412Targets.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8c;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f511;

const TARGETS = [
    { name: "2053", addr: 0x00189dec },
    { name: "2101", addr: 0x00189f9c },
    { name: "2612", addr: 0x0018a8b0 },
];

function u32(fileOffset: number): number {
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(fileOffset);
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function runtimeToFile(addr: number): number | null {
    // Segment 412
    if (addr >= SEG412_START && addr <= SEG412_END) {
        return 0x004a6695 + (addr - SEG412_START);
    }

    // Segment 413
    if (addr >= SEG413_START && addr <= SEG413_END) {
        return 0x004a9de0 + (addr - SEG413_START);
    }

    return null;
}

function classify(v: number): string {
    if (v === 0) return "ZERO";

    if (v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END)
        return "SEG412_PTR";

    if (v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END)
        return "SEG413_PTR";

    // Function indexes are generally much smaller than runtime pointers.
    if (v < 10000)
        return "SMALL_INDEX";

    return "VALUE";
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEGMENT 412 TARGET DECODER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const targetFile = runtimeToFile(target.addr);

    if (targetFile === null) {
        console.log(`\nTARGET ${target.name}: FILE OFFSET ERROR`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET ${target.name}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(`runtime : ${hex(target.addr)}`);
    console.log(`file    : ${hex(targetFile)}`);
    console.log(`index   : ${hex(u32(targetFile))}`);

    // Search backwards for the probable record start:
    // a record usually begins with 3 consecutive small indices.
    let recordStart = targetFile;

    for (let p = targetFile; p >= targetFile - 256; p -= 4) {
        if (p < 0 || p + 12 > wasm.length) continue;

        const a = u32(p);
        const b = u32(p + 4);
        const c = u32(p + 8);

        if (
            a < 10000 &&
            b < 10000 &&
            c < 10000 &&
            b === a + 1 &&
            c === b + 1
        ) {
            recordStart = p;
            break;
        }
    }

    const recordRuntime =
        SEG412_START + (recordStart - 0x004a6695);

    console.log(`record runtime : ${hex(recordRuntime)}`);
    console.log(`record file    : ${hex(recordStart)}`);
    console.log("");

    console.log("RECORD WORDS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const MAX_WORDS = 48;

    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_WORDS; i++) {
        const p = recordStart + i * 4;
        if (p + 4 > wasm.length) break;

        const v = u32(p);

        const runtime =
            SEG412_START + (p - 0x004a6695);

        let marker = "";

        if (runtime === target.addr)
            marker = " <<< TARGET";

        console.log(
            `+${String(i * 4).padStart(3, " ")} ` +
            `${hex(runtime)} ` +
            `file=${hex(p)} ` +
            `${hex(v)} ` +
            `${classify(v)}${marker}`
        );
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Extract pointer targets and inspect pointed structures
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("");
    console.log("POINTER DESTINATIONS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const seen = new Set<number>();

    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_WORDS; i++) {
        const p = recordStart + i * 4;
        if (p + 4 > wasm.length) break;

        const v = u32(p);

        if (
            (v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END) ||
            (v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END)
        ) {
            if (seen.has(v)) continue;
            seen.add(v);

            const destFile = runtimeToFile(v);

            console.log(
                `field +${String(i * 4).padStart(3, " ")} ` +
                `${hex(v)} ` +
                `${v >= SEG413_START ? "SEG413" : "SEG412"} ` +
                `file=${destFile !== null ? hex(destFile) : "n/a"}`
            );

            if (destFile === null) continue;

            console.log("  first 16 words:");

            for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
                const q = destFile + j * 4;

                if (q + 4 > wasm.length) break;

                const x = u32(q);

                console.log(
                    `    +${String(j * 4).padStart(2, " ")} ` +
                    `${hex(x)} ${classify(x)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeSegment669FunctionIndicesV24.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

// ============================================================
// SEGMENT 669 FUNCTION INDEX DECODER v24
// READ ONLY - OFFLINE
//
// Goal:
//   1. Decode Import Section
//   2. Decode Function Section
//   3. Map Code Body Index -> REAL WASM Function Index
//   4. Inspect functions containing Segment 669 consumers
//   5. Decode Export / Name information when available
//
// NO TON
// NO RPC
// NO WALLET
// NO TRANSACTION
// NO SIGNING
// ============================================================

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_BODIES = [8, 36, 39, 573];

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    off: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 8 && off < buf.length; i++) {
        const b = buf[off++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: off };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: -1, next: off };
}

function readName(
    buf: Buffer,
    off: number
): { value: string; next: number } {
    const n = readULEB(buf, off);
    const end = n.next + n.value;

    if (n.value < 0 || end > buf.length) {
        return { value: "", next: buf.length };
    }

    return {
        value: buf.subarray(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function u32(buf: Buffer, off: number): number {
    return (
        (buf[off] ?? 0) |
        ((buf[off + 1] ?? 0) << 8) |
        ((buf[off + 2] ?? 0) << 16) |
        ((buf[off + 3] ?? 0) << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number): string {
    return Array.from(buf.subarray(start, end))
        .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    if (
        buf.length < 8 ||
        buf[0] !== 0x00 ||
        buf[1] !== 0x61 ||
        buf[2] !== 0x73 ||
        buf[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Invalid WASM magic");
    }

    let off = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
        size: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (off < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[off++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);

        if (sizeInfo.value < 0) {
            break;
        }

        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const payloadStart = off;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (payloadEnd > buf.length) {
            throw new Error(
                `Section exceeds WASM size: id=${id}`
            );
        }

        sections.push({
            id,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
            size: sizeInfo.value,
        });

        off = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseImportSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const imports: {
        index: number;
        module: string;
        name: string;
        kind: number;
        typeIndex?: number;
    }[] = [];

    let importedFunctions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const moduleInfo = readName(buf, off);
        const module = moduleInfo.value;
        off = moduleInfo.next;

        const nameInfo = readName(buf, off);
        const name = nameInfo.value;
        off = nameInfo.next;

        const kind = buf[off++];

        let typeIndex: number | undefined;

        switch (kind) {
            // Function
            case 0x00: {
                const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
                typeIndex = typeInfo.value;
                off = typeInfo.next;

                imports.push({
                    index: importedFunctions,
                    module,
                    name,
                    kind,
                    typeIndex,
                });

                importedFunctions++;
                break;
            }

            // Table
            case 0x01: {
                // elemtype
                off += 1;

                // limits
                const flags = buf[off++];
                const min = readULEB(buf, off);
                off = min.next;

                if (flags & 0x01) {
                    const max = readULEB(buf, off);
                    off = max.next;
                }

                break;
            }

            // Memory
            case 0x02: {
                const flags = buf[off++];
                const min = readULEB(buf, off);
                off = min.next;

                if (flags & 0x01) {
                    const max = readULEB(buf, off);
                    off = max.next;
                }

                break;
            }

            // Global
            case 0x03: {
                // valtype
                off += 1;

                // mutability
                off += 1;

                break;
            }

            default:
                throw new Error(
                    `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`
                );
        }
    }

    return {
        imports,
        importedFunctions,
        count,
    };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseFunctionSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const typeIndices: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);

        typeIndices.push(typeInfo.value);

        off = typeInfo.next;
    }

    return typeIndices;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseCodeSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        bodyIndex: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        bodySize: number;
        codeStart: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);

        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = off;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (bodyEnd > section.payloadEnd) {
            throw new Error(
                `Invalid code body ${i}`
            );
        }

        let p = bodyStart;

        const localsInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const localCount = localsInfo.value;
        p = localsInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount; j++) {
            const n = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = n.next;

            // value type
            p += 1;
        }

        bodies.push({
            bodyIndex: i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            bodySize,
            codeStart: p,
        });

        off = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// EXPORT SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseExportSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    off = countInfo.next;

    const exports: {
        name: string;
        kind: number;
        index: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const nameInfo = readName(buf, off);
        const name = nameInfo.value;
        off = nameInfo.next;

        const kind = buf[off++];

        const indexInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
        const index = indexInfo.value;
        off = indexInfo.next;

        exports.push({
            name,
            kind,
            index,
        });
    }

    return exports;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CUSTOM NAME SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function parseNameCustomSection(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        payloadStart: number;
        payloadEnd: number;
    }
) {
    let off = section.payloadStart;

    const nameInfo = readName(buf, off);
    const customName = nameInfo.value;
    off = nameInfo.next;

    const result: {
        customName: string;
        functionNames: Map<number, string>;
    } = {
        customName,
        functionNames: new Map(),
    };

    if (customName !== "name") {
        return result;
    }

    while (off < section.payloadEnd) {
        const subsectionId = buf[off++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
        const size = sizeInfo.value;
        off = sizeInfo.next;

        const subEnd = off + size;

        if (subEnd > section.payloadEnd) {
            break;
        }

        // Function names subsection
        if (subsectionId === 1) {
            const countInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            off = countInfo.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const indexInfo = readULEB(buf, off);
                const index = indexInfo.value;
                off = indexInfo.next;

                const nameInfo2 = readName(buf, off);
                off = nameInfo2.next;

                result.functionNames.set(
                    index,
                    nameInfo2.value
                );
            }
        }

        off = subEnd;
    }

    return result;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SIMPLE CALL SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function scanCalls(
    buf: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    const calls: {
        offset: number;
        functionIndex: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
        // WASM call opcode = 0x10
        if (buf[p] !== 0x10) {
            continue;
        }

        const info = readULEB(buf, p + 1);

        if (
            info.value >= 0 &&
            info.next <= end
        ) {
            calls.push({
                offset: p,
                functionIndex: info.value,
            });
        }
    }

    return calls;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TARGET BODY REPORT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function reportTarget(
    wasm: Buffer,
    body: ReturnType<typeof parseCodeSection>[number],
    importedFunctions: number,
    typeIndices: number[],
    functionNames: Map<number, string>,
    exports: ReturnType<typeof parseExportSection>
) {
    const realFunctionIndex =
        importedFunctions + body.bodyIndex;

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `BODY ${body.bodyIndex} @ ` +
        `0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log(
        `body index          : ${body.bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `REAL function index  : ${realFunctionIndex}`
    );

    const typeIndex =
        typeIndices[body.bodyIndex];

    console.log(
        `type index           : ${
            typeIndex === undefined
                ? "UNKNOWN"
                : typeIndex
        }`
    );

    const name =
        functionNames.get(realFunctionIndex);

    console.log(
        `function name        : ${
            name ?? "(no name subsection entry)"
        }`
    );

    const matchingExports =
        exports.filter(
            (e) =>
                e.kind === 0x00 &&
                e.index === realFunctionIndex
        );

    if (matchingExports.length) {
        console.log(
            `exports              : ${
                matchingExports
                    .map((e) => e.name)
                    .join(", ")
            }`
        );
    } else {
        console.log("exports              : none");
    }

    console.log(
        `body start           : 0x${body.bodyStart
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body end             : 0x${body.bodyEnd
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code start           : 0x${body.codeStart
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log(
        `body size            : ${body.bodySize}`
    );

    const calls = scanCalls(
        wasm,
        body.codeStart,
        body.bodyEnd
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLS");

    if (!calls.length) {
        console.log("  none found");
    }

    for (const c of calls) {
        const targetName =
            functionNames.get(c.functionIndex);

        console.log(
            `  @0x${c.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            ` → function ${c.functionIndex}` +
            `${targetName ? ` (${targetName})` : ""}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("BODY HEX");

    console.log(
        hex(
            wasm,
            body.bodyStart,
            body.bodyEnd
        )
    );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// MAIN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 669 FUNCTION INDEX DECODER v24");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!fs.existsSync(WASM_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(
            `WASM not found:\n${WASM_PATH}`
        );
    }

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(
        `WASM path : ${WASM_PATH}`
    );

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const sections = parseSections(wasm);

    const importSection =
        sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);

    const functionSection =
        sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);

    const exportSection =
        sections.find((s) => s.id === 7);

    const codeSection =
        sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

    if (!importSection) {
        throw new Error(
            "Import Section not found"
        );
    }

    if (!functionSection) {
        throw new Error(
            "Function Section not found"
        );
    }

    if (!codeSection) {
        throw new Error(
            "Code Section not found"
        );
    }

    const imports =
        parseImportSection(
            wasm,
            importSection
        );

    const typeIndices =
        parseFunctionSection(
            wasm,
            functionSection
        );

    const bodies =
        parseCodeSection(
            wasm,
            codeSection
        );

    const exports =
        exportSection
            ? parseExportSection(
                  wasm,
                  exportSection
              )
            : [];

    const nameSections = sections.filter(
        (s) => s.id === 0
    );

    const functionNames =
        new Map<number, string>();

    let foundNameSection = false;

    for (const s of nameSections) {
        const parsed =
            parseNameCustomSection(
                wasm,
                s
            );

        if (parsed.customName === "name") {
            foundNameSection = true;

            for (const [
                index,
                name,
            ] of parsed.functionNames) {
                functionNames.set(
                    index,
                    name
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" MODULE SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `imports total       : ${imports.count}`
    );

    console.log(
        `imported functions  : ${imports.importedFunctions}`
    );

    console.log(
        `defined functions   : ${typeIndices.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `code bodies         : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `exports             : ${exports.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `name section        : ${
            foundNameSection ? "YES" : "NO"
        }`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" IMPORTED FUNCTIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (!imports.imports.length) {
        console.log("none");
    }

    for (const imp of imports.imports) {
        console.log(
            `${imp.index}` +
            ` : ${imp.module}` +
            `.${imp.name}` +
            ` type=${imp.typeIndex}`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET BODY → REAL FUNCTION INDEX");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const bodyIndex of TARGET_BODIES) {
        const body =
            bodies[bodyIndex];

        if (!body) {
            console.log(
                `BODY ${bodyIndex}: NOT FOUND`
            );
            continue;
        }

        reportTarget(
            wasm,
            body,
            imports.importedFunctions,
            typeIndices,
            functionNames,
            exports
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET CONSUMER OFFSETS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const consumerOffsets = [
        0x00007c43,
        0x0000eed8,
        0x0000ece4,
        0x0002cc30,
        0x0002cc6b,
    ];

    for (const offset of consumerOffsets) {
        const body =
            bodies.find(
                (b) =>
                    offset >= b.bodyStart &&
                    offset < b.bodyEnd
            );

        console.log(
            `0x${offset
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")} → ` +
            `${
                body
                    ? `body ${body.bodyIndex}, real function ${
                          imports.importedFunctions +
                          body.bodyIndex
                      }`
                    : "NO BODY"
            }`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no TON");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no RPC");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log(" READ ONLY: no signing");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

// Blueprint-compatible entry point.
if (require.main === module) {
    run().catch((err) => {
        console.error(err);
        process.exitCode = 1;
    });
}

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function u32(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return buf.readUInt32LE(p);
}

function hx(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function dumpWords(buf: Buffer, start: number, end: number) {
    console.log(
        `\nRange 0x${hx(start)} .. 0x${hx(end)}`
    );

    for (let p = start; p + 4 <= end; p += 4) {
        const v = u32(buf, p);

        console.log(
            `0x${hx(p)}  u32=${v.toString().padStart(10)}  hex=0x${hx(v)}`
        );
    }
}

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_OPCODE = 0x06ecd527;

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) {
        return 'UNKNOWN';
    }

    return (
        '0x' +
        op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
}

function formatTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole =
        value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1_000_000_000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toString();
}

async function main() {
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 SELECTED POOL TX DECODER'

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:

    });

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            POOL,
            {
                limit: 20,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'Fetched:',
        transactions.length
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx = transactions[i];

        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMessage) {
            continue;
        }

        const body = inMessage.body;

        const inOpcode =
            getOpcode(body);

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            `POOL TX #${i + 1}`
        );
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
        console.log(tx.now.toString());

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            opcodeHex(inOpcode)
        );

        if (
            inMessage.info.type ===

        ) {
            console.log('');

                inMessage.info.src.toString()
            );

            console.log('');

            );

            console.log('');

                formatTon(
                    inMessage.info.value.coins
                ),
                'TON'
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('INPUT BODY SIZE:');
        console.log(
            body.bits.length,
            'bits /',
            body.refs.length,
            'refs'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('INPUT BOC:');
        console.log(
            body.toBoc().toString('base64')
        );

        if (
            inOpcode === TARGET_OPCODE
        ) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '******** FOUND 0x06ecd527 ********'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'TARGET MESSAGE BODY DETECTED'
            );

            console.log(
                'This is the transaction that must be decoded next.'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'TARGET LT:',
                tx.lt.toString()
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('OUTGOING MESSAGES:');

        let outIndex = 0;

        for (
            const outMessage
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {
            const outBody =
                outMessage.body;

            const outOpcode =
                getOpcode(outBody);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `OUT #${outIndex}`
            );

            if (
                outMessage.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'DESTINATION:',
                    outMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'VALUE:',
                    formatTon(
                        outMessage.info.value.coins
                    ),
                    'TON'
                );
            }

            console.log(
                'OPCODE:',
                opcodeHex(outOpcode)
            );

            console.log(
                'BODY:',
                outBody.bits.length,
                'bits /',
                outBody.refs.length,
                'refs'
            );

            if (
                outOpcode ===
                TARGET_OPCODE
            ) {
                console.log('');

                );

                console.log(

                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')

    );

FILE: scripts\decodeSelectorTable.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x004db180;
const END   = 0x004db210;
const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE SELECTOR TABLE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`RANGE  : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = START; p + 8 <= END; p += 8) {
  const tag =
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24);

  const value =
    wasm[p + 4] |
    (wasm[p + 5] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 6] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 7] << 24);

  const uTag = tag >>> 0;
  const uValue = value >>> 0;

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
    `TAG=0x${uTag.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
    `VALUE=0x${uValue.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
    `DEC=${uValue}`
  );

  if (uValue === TARGET || uTag === TARGET) {
    console.log("  >>> TARGET MATCH <<<");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeStateWritersExact.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = {
  3336: [0x102200, 0x102250],
  6991: [0x23c4c0, 0x23c550],
  7006: [0x23d720, 0x23d7a0]
};

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function readLocal(p) {
  const x = uleb(p + 1);
  return `L${x.value}`;
}

function memName(op) {
  return {
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16"
  }[op];
}

function dump(start, end) {

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    let s = `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  `;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(p);
      s += `i32.const ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      s += `local.get L${x.value}`;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      s += `local.set L${x.value}`;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      s += `local.tee L${x.value}`;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      s += `CALL ${x.value}`;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      const y = uleb(p);
      p = y.next;

      s += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`;
    }

    else if (memName(op)) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      s += `${memName(op)} align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
    }

    else {
      const names = {
        0x0b:"END",
        0x0f:"RETURN",
        0x1a:"drop",
        0x40:"block",
        0x41:"i32.const",
        0x45:"i32.eqz",
        0x46:"i32.eq",
        0x47:"i32.ne",
        0x48:"i32.lt_s",
        0x49:"i32.lt_u",
        0x4a:"i32.gt_s",
        0x4b:"i32.gt_u",
        0x4c:"i32.le_s",
        0x4d:"i32.le_u",
        0x4e:"i32.ge_s",
        0x4f:"i32.ge_u",
        0x6a:"i32.add",
        0x6b:"i32.sub",
        0x6c:"i32.mul",
        0x72:"i32.xor",
        0x7a:"i32.rotl",
        0x0c:"br",
        0x0d:"br_if",
        0x04:"if",
        0x05:"else"
      };

      s += names[op] ?? `opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    }

    console.log(s);
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE WRITERS EXACT DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [fn, range] of Object.entries(TARGETS)) {

  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`FUNC ${fn}`);
  console.log(
    `RANGE 0x${range[0].toString(16)} .. 0x${range[1].toString(16)}`
  );
  console.log("############################################################");

  dump(range[0], range[1]);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");
