import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

const TARGETS = [
    ['ADD_LIQUIDITY', '50c6a654'],
    ['PROVIDE_LP', '37c096df'],
    ['LP_TRANSFER', '178d4519'],
    ['PTON_TRANSFER', '01f3835d'],
    ['ROOSTER_PATH', '0f8a7ea5'],
] as const;

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function cellBits(cell: Cell): string {
    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.bits.length; i++) {
        result += cell.bits.at(i) ? '1' : '0';
    }

    return result;
}

function bitsToHex(bits: string): string {
    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i + 4 <= bits.length; i += 4) {
        result += parseInt(bits.slice(i, i + 4), 2)
            .toString(16);
    }

    return result.toUpperCase();
}

function hexToBits(hex: string): string {
    let result = '';

    for (const c of hex) {
        result += parseInt(c, 16)
            .toString(2)
            .padStart(4, '0');
    }

    return result;
}

function findBitOccurrences(
    bits: string,
    pattern: string
): number[] {
    const positions: number[] = [];

    let pos = bits.indexOf(pattern);

    while (pos !== -1) {
        positions.push(pos);
        pos = bits.indexOf(pattern, pos + 1);
    }

    return positions;
}

function printWindow(
    bits: string,
    offset: number,
    radius: number = 64
) {
    const start = Math.max(0, offset - radius);
    const end = Math.min(bits.length, offset + 32 + radius);

    const window = bits.slice(start, end);

    console.log(`  Window bits [${start}..${end - 1}]`);
    console.log(`  ${window}`);

    console.log(
        `  Target starts at relative bit ${offset - start}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  Window hex: ${bitsToHex(window)}`
    );
}

function inspectCell(
    cell: Cell,
    name: string,
    path: string
) {
    const hash =
        cell.hash()
            .toString('hex')
            .toUpperCase();

    const bits = cellBits(cell);

    console.log();
    console.log('----------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(`${name}  ${path}`);
    console.log('----------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(`Hash: ${hash}`);
    console.log(`Bits: ${bits.length}`);
    console.log(`Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`Full hex: ${bitsToHex(bits)}`);

    for (const [opcodeName, opcodeHex] of TARGETS) {
        const pattern = hexToBits(opcodeHex);
        const positions = findBitOccurrences(bits, pattern);

        if (positions.length === 0) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `*** ${opcodeName} (${opcodeHex.toUpperCase()}) ***`
        );

        for (const position of positions) {
            console.log(`Bit offset: ${position}`);
            console.log(`Byte offset: ${position / 8}`);

            if (position % 8 !== 0) {
                console.log(
                    'WARNING: opcode is not byte-aligned'
                );
            }

            printWindow(bits, position);
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        inspectCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${name}.ref${i}`,
            `${path}.ref${i}`
        );
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET STON.FI v2.2 LIBRARY CELL DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`Library: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Target:  ${TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Server:  ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const libraryHash =
            Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX, 'hex');

        console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([libraryHash]);

        const entry = result.result.find(
            item =>
                Buffer.from(item.hash)
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase() === LIBRARY_HASH_HEX
        );

        if (!entry) {
            throw new Error(
                'Library entry not found'
            );
        }

        const root =
            Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(entry.data))[0];

        console.log(
            `Root hash: ${root.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        const targetPath: Cell[] = [];

        function findTarget(
            cell: Cell,
            path: string
        ): boolean {
            const hash =
                cell.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase();

            if (hash === TARGET_CELL_HASH_HEX) {
                targetPath.push(cell);

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    'TARGET CELL FOUND'
                );
                console.log(
                    `Path: ${path}`
                );

                return true;
            }

            for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
                if (
                    findTarget(
                        cell.refs[i],
                        `${path}.ref${i}`
                    )
                ) {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        }

        findTarget(root, 'root');

        if (targetPath.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell was not found'
            );
        }

        const target =
            targetPath[targetPath.length - 1];

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET CELL');
        console.log('========================================================================');

        inspectCell(
            target,
            'TARGET',
            'root.ref0.ref0.ref0.ref0'
        );

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' DECODER COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 23;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let found = false;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 1) {

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("WASM TYPE 23 DECODE");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TYPE COUNT : ${count.value}`);
    console.log(`TARGET     : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

      const form = b[p++];

      if (form !== 0x60)
        throw new Error(`Unexpected type form at ${i}`);

      const pc = uleb(p);
      p = pc.next;

      const params = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
        params.push(b[p++]);

      const rc = uleb(p);
      p = rc.next;

      const results = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
        results.push(b[p++]);

      if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {

        const name = x => {

          if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
          if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
          if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
          if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";
          if (x === 0x70) return "funcref";
          if (x === 0x6f) return "externref";

          return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
        };

        found = true;

        console.log("");
        console.log("TARGET TYPE FOUND");
        console.log("----------------------------------------");

        console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${i}`);
        console.log(`PARAM COUNT : ${params.length}`);

        params.forEach((x, n) => {
          console.log(`  PARAM ${n} : ${name(x)}`);
        });

        console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${results.length}`);

        );
      }
    }

    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

const TYPE_NAMES: Record<number, string> = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
    0x7b: "v128",
    0x70: "funcref",
    0x6f: "externref",
};

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" WASM TYPE #2 DECODER v37");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size: ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

let pos = 8;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId !== 1) {
        pos = payloadEnd;
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `Type section: offset=0x${payloadStart.toString(16)} size=${sectionSize}`
    );
    console.log();

    let p = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    const typeCount = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`Type count: ${typeCount}`);
    console.log();

    for (let typeIndex = 0; typeIndex < typeCount; typeIndex++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        const params: string[] = [];
        const results: string[] = [];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            console.log(
                `type #${typeIndex}: form=0x${form.toString(16)}`
            );
            continue;
        }

        const paramCountInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const paramCount = paramCountInfo.value;
        p = paramCountInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            const vt = buf[p++];
            params.push(
                TYPE_NAMES[vt] ??
                `unknown(0x${vt.toString(16)})`
            );
        }

        const resultCountInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        const resultCount = resultCountInfo.value;
        p = resultCountInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            const vt = buf[p++];
            results.push(
                TYPE_NAMES[vt] ??
                `unknown(0x${vt.toString(16)})`
            );
        }

        if (
            typeIndex === 2 ||
            typeIndex === 1 ||
            typeIndex === 3
        ) {
            console.log(
                `type #${typeIndex}:`
            );

            console.log(
                `  params  = (${params.join(", ")})`
            );

            console.log(
                `  results = (${results.join(", ")})`
            );

            console.log();
        }
    }

    break;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 31;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function typeName(v: number): string {
  switch (v) {
    case 0x7f:
      return "i32";
    case 0x7e:
      return "i64";
    case 0x7d:
      return "f32";
    case 0x7c:
      return "f64";
    case 0x7b:
      return "v128";
    case 0x70:
      return "funcref";
    case 0x6f:
      return "externref";
    default:
      return `UNKNOWN(${hex(v)})`;
  }
}

if (!typeSection) {
  throw new Error("Type section not found");
}

let p = typeSection.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
const typeCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("Type count:", typeCount);
console.log();

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {
  const typeStart = p;

  const form = wasm[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected type form ${hex(form)} at ${hex(typeStart)}`
    );
  }

  const paramCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
  const paramCount = paramCountInfo.value;
  p = paramCountInfo.next;

  const params: string[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
    params.push(typeName(wasm[p++]));
  }

  const resultCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
  const resultCount = resultCountInfo.value;
  p = resultCountInfo.next;

  const results: string[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
    results.push(typeName(wasm[p++]));
  }

  if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` TYPE ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log("Type offset :", hex(typeStart));
    console.log("Parameters  :", paramCount);

    params.forEach((x, j) => {
      console.log(`  param ${j}: ${x}`);
    });

    console.log();

    console.log("Results     :", resultCount);

    results.forEach((x, j) => {
      console.log(`  result ${j}: ${x}`);
    });

    console.log();

    console.log(
      `Signature   : (${params.join(", ")}) -> (${results.join(", ")})`

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPE = 50;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findTypeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 1) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("TYPE section not found");
}

const sec = findTypeSection();

let p = sec.start;

p = count.next;

  const form = b[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected function type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at type ${i}`
    );
  }

  const params = uleb(p);
  p = params.next;

  const paramTypes = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < params.value; j++) {
    paramTypes.push(b[p++]);
  }

  const results = uleb(p);
  p = results.next;

  const resultTypes = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < results.value; j++) {
    resultTypes.push(b[p++]);
  }

  if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {

    const typeName = x => {

      if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
      if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
      if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
      if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";
      if (x === 0x70) return "funcref";
      if (x === 0x6f) return "externref";

      return `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
    };

    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET TYPE FOUND");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${i}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `PARAM COUNT : ${paramTypes.length}`
    );

    paramTypes.forEach((x, n) => {
      console.log(
        `  PARAM ${n} : ${typeName(x)}`
      );
    });

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `RESULT COUNT : ${resultTypes.length}`
    );

    resultTypes.forEach((x, n) => {
      console.log(
        `  RESULT ${n} : ${typeName(x)}`
      );
    });

    console.log("");
    console.log("SIGNATURE:");

    console.log(
      `  (${paramTypes.map(typeName).join(", ")}) -> (${resultTypes.map(typeName).join(", ")})`
    );
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_TYPES = [15, 29];

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function readVec(buf: Buffer, pos: number): {
  count: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const r = readULEB(buf, pos);

  return {
    count: r.value,
    next: r.next,
  };
}

function valtypeName(v: number): string {
  switch (v) {
    case 0x7f:
      return "i32";

    case 0x7e:
      return "i64";

    case 0x7d:
      return "f32";

    case 0x7c:
      return "f64";

    case 0x7b:
      return "v128";

    case 0x70:
      return "funcref";

    case 0x6f:
      return "externref";

    default:
      return `UNKNOWN(${hex(v)})`;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DECODE WASM TYPES 15 + 29 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("WASM size:", wasm.length.toLocaleString());
console.log();

const typeSection = findSection(wasm, 1);

if (!typeSection) {
  throw new Error("Type section not found");
}

console.log("Type section");
console.log("  start:", hex(typeSection.start));
console.log("  end  :", hex(typeSection.end));
console.log();

let p = typeSection.start;

const countInfo = readVec(wasm, p);
const typeCount = countInfo.count;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("Type count:", typeCount);
console.log();

for (let typeIndex = 0; typeIndex < typeCount; typeIndex++) {
  const typeStart = p;

  const form = wasm[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    console.log(
      `Type ${typeIndex}: unsupported form ${hex(form)} at ${hex(typeStart)}`
    );
    throw new Error("Unexpected type form");
  }

  const paramInfo = readVec(wasm, p);
  const paramCount = paramInfo.count;
  p = paramInfo.next;

  const params: string[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < paramCount; i++) {
    params.push(valtypeName(wasm[p++]));
  }

  const resultInfo = readVec(wasm, p);
  const resultCount = resultInfo.count;
  p = resultInfo.next;

  const results: string[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < resultCount; i++) {
    results.push(valtypeName(wasm[p++]));
  }

  if (TARGET_TYPES.includes(typeIndex)) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` TYPE ${typeIndex}`);
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log("Type offset :", hex(typeStart));
    console.log("Form        : 0x60 (function)");
    console.log("Parameters  :", paramCount);

    for (let i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
      console.log(`  param ${i}: ${params[i]}`);
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("Results     :", resultCount);

    for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
      console.log(`  result ${i}: ${results[i]}`);
    }

    console.log();

    console.log(
      `Signature   : (${params.join(", ")}) -> (${results.join(", ")})`
    );

    console.log();
  }

  if (p > typeSection.end) {
    throw new Error("Type section overrun");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGETS = [3317, 6993, 6995];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ULEB128
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// WASM value types
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function typeName(b) {
    switch (b) {
        case 0x7f: return "i32";
        case 0x7e: return "i64";
        case 0x7d: return "f32";
        case 0x7c: return "f64";
        case 0x7b: return "v128";
        case 0x70: return "funcref";
        case 0x6f: return "externref";
        case 0x6b: return "exnref";
        default:
            return "0x" + b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;

let typeSection = null;
let functionSection = null;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 1) {
        typeSection = {
            start: payloadStart,
            end: payloadEnd
        };
    }

    if (sectionId === 3) {
        functionSection = {
            start: payloadStart,
            end: payloadEnd
        };
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (!typeSection)
    throw new Error("Type section not found");

if (!functionSection)
    throw new Error("Function section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse type section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = typeSection.start;

const typeCountInfo = readULEB(p);
const typeCount = typeCountInfo.value;
p = typeCountInfo.next;

const types = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {

    const form = buf[p++];

    // Standard function type
    if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected type form ${hex(form, 2)} at type ${i}`
        );
    }

    const paramCountInfo = readULEB(p);
    const paramCount = paramCountInfo.value;
    p = paramCountInfo.next;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
        params.push(typeName(buf[p++]));
    }

    const resultCountInfo = readULEB(p);
    const resultCount = resultCountInfo.value;
    p = resultCountInfo.next;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
        results.push(typeName(buf[p++]));
    }

    types.push({
        index: i,
        params,
        results
    });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse function section
//
// Function section contains type index for each defined body.
// Global function index = IMPORTED + body index
// ------------------------------------------------------------
p = functionSection.start;

const functionCountInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
p = functionCountInfo.next;

const functionTypeIndex = new Map();

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const typeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const typeIndex = typeInfo.value;
    p = typeInfo.next;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED + bodyIndex;

    functionTypeIndex.set(funcIndex, typeIndex);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Print
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT FUNCTION TYPES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${buf.length}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED  : ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log(`TYPE COUNT: ${types.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTIONS : ${functionCount}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const funcIndex of TARGETS) {

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${funcIndex}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const typeIndex = functionTypeIndex.get(funcIndex);

    if (typeIndex === undefined) {
        console.log("TYPE INDEX : NOT FOUND");
        continue;
    }

    const t = types[typeIndex];

    console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${typeIndex}`);

    console.log(
        `PARAM COUNT: ${t.params.length}`
    );

    if (t.params.length) {
        t.params.forEach((x, i) => {
            console.log(`  PARAM ${i}: ${x}`);
        });
    } else {
        console.log("  (none)");
    }

    console.log(
        `RESULT COUNT: ${t.results.length}`
    );

    if (t.results.length) {
        t.results.forEach((x, i) => {
            console.log(`  RESULT ${i}: ${x}`);
        });
    } else {
        console.log("  (none)");
    }

    console.log(
        `SIGNATURE  : (${t.params.join(", ")}) -> (${t.results.join(", ")})`
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("3317 CALL COMPATIBILITY");
console.log("============================================================");

const type3317Index = functionTypeIndex.get(3317);

if (type3317Index !== undefined) {

    const t = types[type3317Index];

    console.log(`FUNC 3317 TYPE : ${type3317Index}`);
    console.log(
        `PARAMS        : (${t.params.join(", ")})`
    );
    console.log(
        `RESULTS       : (${t.results.join(", ")})`
    );

    console.log("\nIMPORTANT:");
    console.log(
        "WASM call arguments are consumed from the operand stack"
    );
    console.log(
        "in parameter order from the function signature."
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

function readAddress(slice: Slice, label: string): Address | null {
    const address = slice.loadAddress();
    console.log(`${label}:`, address ? address.toString() : "null");
    return address;
}

console.log("Ref remaining refs:", r.remainingRefs);

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER DATA DECODER');
    console.log(' TESTNET / READ ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log('\nMASTERCHAIN SEQNO:');
    console.log(latest.last.seqno);

    console.log('\nCalling get_router_data...');

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            latest.last.seqno,
            ROUTER,
            'get_router_data'
        );

        console.log('\nGET_ROUTER_DATA: SUCCESS');

        console.log('\nEXIT CODE:');
        console.log(result.exitCode);

        console.log('\n');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));
        console.log(' RESULT ARRAY');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.log(
            'Number of items:',
            result.result.length
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < result.result.length; i++) {
            console.log('\n----------------------------------------');
            console.log(`ITEM [${i}]`);
            console.log('----------------------------------------');

            console.dir(result.result[i], {
                depth: 20,
            });
        }

        console.log('\n');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));
        console.log(' RESULT RAW');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.log(result.resultRaw);

        console.log('\n');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));
        console.log(' FULL RESULT OBJECT');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.dir(result, {
            depth: 30,
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('\nGET_ROUTER_DATA: ERROR');
        console.error(error);
    }

    console.log('\n');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' FINISHED');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\deepBacktraceIndex4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

function uleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 128))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw Error("ULEB");
}

function sleb(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 128))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 64))
    v |= -1 << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function skipName(b: Buffer, p: number) {
  const n = uleb(b, p);
  return n.next + n.value;
}

function sections(b: Buffer): any {
  let p = 8;
  const r: any = {};

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    r[id] = {
      start: p,
      size: s.value
    };

    p += s.value;
  }

  return r;
}

function importedFuncs(b: Buffer, sec: any) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  let count = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    p = skipName(b, p);
    p = skipName(b, p);

    const kind = b[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      p = uleb(b, p).next;
      count++;
    }
    else if (kind === 1 || kind === 2) {
      const f = uleb(b, p);
      p = f.next;
      p = uleb(b, p).next;

      if (f.value & 1)
        p = uleb(b, p).next;
    }
    else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }

  return count;
}

function getBodies(
  b: Buffer,
  sec: any,
  imported: number
) {
  let p = sec.start;

  const n = uleb(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const out: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const s = uleb(b, p);
    p = s.next;

    out.push({
      func: imported + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + s.value,
      bytes: b.subarray(p, p + s.value)
    });

    p += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

type I = {
  off: number;
  op: number;
  imm: number[];
  text: string;
};

function decode(b: Buffer, base: number): I[] {
  const a: I[] = [];

  let p = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const off = base + p;
    const op = b[p++];

    const imm: number[] = [];
    let text = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;
      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x20
          ? `local.get ${r.value}`
          : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set ${r.value}`
          : `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x23
          ? `global.get ${r.value}`
          : `global.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(b, p);
      p = t.next;

      const tab = uleb(b, p);
      p = tab.next;

      imm.push(t.value, tab.value);

      text =
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op >= 0x28 &&
      op <= 0x3e
    ) {
      const al = uleb(b, p);
      p = al.next;

      const of = uleb(b, p);
      p = of.next;

      imm.push(al.value, of.value);

      text = `load offset=${of.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a)
      text = "i32.add";

    else if (op === 0x6b)
      text = "i32.sub";

    else if (op === 0x6c)
      text = "i32.mul";

    else if (op === 0x1a)
      text = "drop";

    else if (op === 0x0f)
      text = "return";

    else if (op === 0x0b)
      text = "end";

    else if (op === 0x05)
      text = "else";

    else if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      if (p < b.length)
        p++;

      text =
        op === 0x02
          ? "block"
          : op === 0x03
          ? "loop"
          : "if";
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      const r = uleb(b, p);
      p = r.next;

      imm.push(r.value);

      text =
        op === 0x0c
          ? `br ${r.value}`
          : `br_if ${r.value}`;
    }

    a.push({
      off,
      op,
      imm,
      text
    });
  }

  return a;
}

function window(
  a: I[],
  pos: number,
  n = 12
) {
  for (
    let j = Math.max(0, pos - n);
    j <= Math.min(a.length - 1, pos + 2);
    j++
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${j === pos ? " >>>" : "    "} ` +
      `0x${a[j].off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
      a[j].text
    );
  }
}

export async function run() {
  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
  const sec = sections(wasm);

  const imported = importedFuncs(
    wasm,
    sec[2]
  );

  const bodies = getBodies(
    wasm,
    sec[10],
    imported
  );

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log(
    " DEEP BACKTRACE INDEX 114"
  );
  console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
  );
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
  console.log(`Target    : ${TARGET}`);
  console.log();

  let found = 0;
  let printed = 0;

  for (const body of bodies) {
    let a: I[];

    try {
      a = decode(body.bytes, body.start);
    }
    catch {
      continue;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      const c = a[i];

      if (
        c.op !== 0x11 ||
        c.imm[0] !== 0 ||
        c.imm[1] !== 0
      )
        continue;

      /*
       * We only care about the 66 known local-base
       * patterns:
       *
       * local.get
       * load
       * load
       * call_indirect
       */

      if (i < 3)
        continue;

      const outer = a[i - 1];
      const inner = a[i - 2];
      const base = a[i - 3];

      if (
        outer.op < 0x28 ||
        outer.op > 0x3e ||
        inner.op < 0x28 ||
        inner.op > 0x3e ||
        base.op !== 0x20
      )
        continue;

      const localId = base.imm[0];

      /*
       * Find ALL earlier definitions of this local.
       * We inspect up to 8 definitions, because a local
       * may be reassigned in different branches.
       */

      let defs = 0;

      for (
        let j = i - 4;
        j >= 0 && defs < 8;
        j--
      ) {
        if (
          (a[j].op === 0x21 ||
           a[j].op === 0x22) &&
          a[j].imm[0] === localId
        ) {
          defs++;

          const src = j - 1;

          if (src < 0)
            continue;

          const s = a[src];

          /*
           * Direct 114
           */
          if (
            s.op === 0x41 &&
            s.imm[0] === TARGET
          ) {
            found++;

            console.log(
              `FOUND 114: func=${body.func} ` +
              `local=${localId} ` +
              `def=0x${a[j].off.toString(16)} ` +
              `call=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
            );

            window(a, j);
            continue;
          }

          /*
           * LOAD source
           */
          if (
            s.op >= 0x28 &&
            s.op <= 0x3e
          ) {
            if (printed < 120) {
              console.log(
                `DEEP LOAD: func=${body.func} ` +
                `local=${localId} ` +
                `load=0x${s.off.toString(16)} ` +
                `offset=${s.imm[1]} ` +
                `def=0x${a[j].off.toString(16)} ` +
                `indirect=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
              );

              window(a, j, 8);
              printed++;
            }

            continue;
          }

          /*
           * CALL source
           */
          if (s.op === 0x10) {
            if (printed < 120) {
              console.log(
                `DEEP CALL: func=${body.func} ` +
                `local=${localId} ` +
                `returns=${s.imm[0]} ` +
                `def=0x${a[j].off.toString(16)} ` +
                `indirect=0x${c.off.toString(16)}`
              );

              window(a, j, 8);
              printed++;
            }

            continue;
          }

          /*
           * Another local
           */
          if (s.op === 0x20) {
            if (printed < 120) {
              console.log(
                `DEEP LOCAL: func=${body.func} ` +
                `local=${localId} ` +
                `from=${s.imm[0]} ` +
                `def=0x${a[j].off.toString(16)}`
              );

              window(a, j, 8);
              printed++;
            }

            continue;
          }

          /*
           * Arithmetic / other source
           */
          if (
            s.op === 0x6a ||
            s.op === 0x6b ||
            s.op === 0x6c
          ) {
            if (printed < 120) {
              console.log(
                `DEEP ARITH: func=${body.func} ` +
                `local=${localId} ` +
                `${s.text} ` +
                `def=0x${a[j].off.toString(16)}`
              );

              window(a, j, 8);
              printed++;
            }

            continue;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log();

  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );
  console.log("SUMMARY");
  console.log(
    "============================================================"
  );

  console.log(
    `Confirmed CONST(114) : ${found}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Deep traces printed  : ${printed}`
  );

  console.log();
  console.log(
    "NEXT: resolve the deepest LOAD/CALL producer."
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\deepBacktraceIndirectTarget4154.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4154;
const TARGET_SLOT = 114;
const TARGET_TYPE = 0;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0;

type Instr = {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    imm: number[];
    size: number;
    text: string;
};

type Source =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "call"; func: number; origin: string }
    | { kind: "load"; origin: string }
    | { kind: "unknown"; origin: string };

type Body = {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    bytes: Buffer;
};

function uleb(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= -1 << shift;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function readVec(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { count: number; next: number } {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);

    return {
        count: r.value,
        next: r.next,
    };
}

function readName(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { name: string; next: number } {
    const n = uleb(buf, p);
    const end = n.next + n.value;

    return {
        name: buf.subarray(n.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function parseSections(wasm: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    let typeSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let importSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let funcSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    let codeSec:
        | { start: number; size: number }
        | undefined;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const size = s.value;

        if (id === 1) {
            typeSec = { start, size };
        }

        if (id === 2) {
            importSec = { start, size };
        }

        if (id === 3) {
            funcSec = { start, size };
        }

        if (id === 10) {
            codeSec = { start, size };
        }

        p += size;
    }

    return {
        typeSec,
        importSec,
        funcSec,
        codeSec,
    };
}

function parseTypes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
) {
    let p = sec.start;
    const end = p + sec.size;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    p = v.next;

    const types: {
        params: number[];
        results: number[];
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form ${form}`
            );
        }

        const pc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = Array.from(
            wasm.subarray(p, p + pc.value)
        );

        p += pc.value;

        const rc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = Array.from(
            wasm.subarray(p, p + rc.value)
        );

        p += rc.value;

        types.push({
            params,
            results,
        });
    }

    if (p !== end) {
        throw new Error(
            `Type section mismatch ${p} != ${end}`
        );
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
) {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    p = v.next;

    let importedFuncs = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const mod = readName(wasm, p);
        p = mod.next;

        const name = readName(wasm, p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;

            importedFuncs++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            /*
             * table type:
             * elemtype + limits
             */
            p++;

            const flags = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            /*
             * memory
             */
            const flags = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            /*
             * global
             */
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }
    }

    return importedFuncs;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number }
) {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    p = v.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const r = uleb(wasm, p);

        result.push(r.value);

        p = r.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function parseBodies(
    wasm: Buffer,
    sec: { start: number; size: number },
    importedFuncs: number
): Body[] {
    let p = sec.start;

    const v = readVec(wasm, p);
    p = v.next;

    const bodies: Body[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < v.count; i++) {
        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        bodies.push({
            func: importedFuncs + i,
            start,
            end,
            bytes: wasm.subarray(start, end),
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function decode(
    bytes: Buffer,
    base: number
): Instr[] {
    const out: Instr[] = [];

    let p = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const start = p;
        const off = base + p;
        const op = bytes[p++];

        const imm: number[] = [];
        let text = `op=0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = sleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            if (op === 0x20) {
                text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
            } else if (op === 0x21) {
                text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
            } else {
                text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
            }
        } else if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text = `call ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = b.next;

            imm.push(a.value, b.value);

            text =
                `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text =
                op === 0x23
                    ? `global.get ${r.value}`
                    : `global.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x25 || op === 0x26) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text =
                op === 0x25
                    ? `table.get ${r.value}`
                    : `table.set ${r.value}`;
        } else if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x40
        ) {
            const a = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = a.next;

            const b = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = b.next;

            imm.push(a.value, b.value);

            text =
                `memory op 0x${op
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(2, "0")}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            if (p >= bytes.length) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Missing blocktype"
                );
            }

            const first = bytes[p++];

            if (first !== 0x40 && first < 0x80) {
                let b = first;
                let value = first & 0x7f;
                let shift = 7;

                while (b & 0x80) {
                    if (p >= bytes.length) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Blocktype overflow"
                        );
                    }

                    b = bytes[p++];

                    value |=
                        (b & 0x7f) << shift;

                    shift += 7;
                }

                imm.push(value);
            } else {
                imm.push(first);
            }

            text =
                op === 0x02
                    ? "block"
                    : op === 0x03
                    ? "loop"
                    : "if";
        } else if (op === 0x05) {
            text = "else";
        } else if (op === 0x0b) {
            text = "end";
        } else if (op === 0x0f) {
            text = "return";
        } else if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = r.next;

            imm.push(r.value);

            text =
                op === 0x0c
                    ? `br ${r.value}`
                    : `br_if ${r.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x1a) {
            text = "drop";
        } else if (
            op >= 0x45 &&
            op <= 0x4f
        ) {
            text =
                `i32 compare 0x${op
                    .toString(16)}`;
        } else if (
            op >= 0x6a &&
            op <= 0x78
        ) {
            text =
                `i32 arithmetic 0x${op
                    .toString(16)}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = uleb(bytes, p);
            p = n.next;

            for (
                let i = 0;
                i < n.value + 1;
                i++
            ) {
                const r = uleb(bytes, p);
                p = r.next;

                imm.push(r.value);
            }

            text = "br_table";
        }

        if (p <= start) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder stalled @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        out.push({
            off,
            op,
            imm,
            size: p - start,
            text,
        });
    }

    return out;
}

function findPreviousLocalWrite(
    instrs: Instr[],
    from: number,
    local: number
): number {
    for (
        let i = from - 1;
        i >= 0;
        i--
    ) {
        const ins = instrs[i];

        if (
            (ins.op === 0x21 ||
                ins.op === 0x22) &&
            ins.imm[0] === local
        ) {
            return i;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

function traceLocal(
    instrs: Instr[],
    local: number,
    from: number,
    depth: number,
    visited: Set<string>
): Source {
    if (depth > 20) {
        return {
            kind: "unknown",
            origin: `depth-limit local ${local}`,
        };
    }

    const key = `${local}:${from}:${depth}`;

    if (visited.has(key)) {
        return {
            kind: "unknown",
            origin: `cycle local ${local}`,
        };
    }

    visited.add(key);

    const write = findPreviousLocalWrite(
        instrs,
        from,
        local
    );

    if (write < 0) {
        return {
            kind: "unknown",
            origin: `no-write local ${local}`,
        };
    }

    const ins = instrs[write];

    /*
     * local.set/tee consumes the value immediately
     * before itself.
     *
     * Walk backward from that stack-producing value.
     */
    for (
        let i = write - 1;
        i >= 0 && i >= write - 100;
        i--
    ) {
        const x = instrs[i];

        if (x.op === 0x41) {
            return {
                kind: "const",
                value: x.imm[0],
                origin:
                    `i32.const @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        if (x.op === 0x20) {
            return traceLocal(
                instrs,
                x.imm[0],
                i,
                depth + 1,
                visited
            );
        }

        if (x.op === 0x10) {
            return {
                kind: "call",
                func: x.imm[0],
                origin:
                    `call ${x.imm[0]} @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        if (
            x.op === 0x28 ||
            x.op === 0x29 ||
            x.op === 0x2a ||
            x.op === 0x2b ||
            x.op === 0x2c ||
            x.op === 0x2d ||
            x.op === 0x2e ||
            x.op === 0x2f
        ) {
            return {
                kind: "load",
                origin:
                    `${x.text} @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
            };
        }

        /*
         * Stop at another control-flow-producing
         * or indirect-call boundary.
         */
        if (
            x.op === 0x11 ||
            x.op === 0x0f ||
            x.op === 0x0e
        ) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        kind: "unknown",
        origin:
            `unresolved write local ${local}`,
    };
}

export async function run() {
    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " DEEP BACKWARD TRACE -> FUNCTION 4154"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size       : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target function : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(
        `Target slot     : 0x${TARGET_SLOT
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")} = ${TARGET_SLOT}`
    );
    console.log(`Target type     : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
    console.log(`Target table    : ${TARGET_TABLE}`);
    console.log();

    const sections = parseSections(wasm);

    if (
        !sections.typeSec ||
        !sections.importSec ||
        !sections.funcSec ||
        !sections.codeSec
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Required WASM sections missing"
        );
    }

    const types = parseTypes(
        wasm,
        sections.typeSec
    );

    const importedFuncs = parseImports(
        wasm,
        sections.importSec
    );

    const funcTypes = parseFunctionTypes(
        wasm,
        sections.funcSec
    );

    const bodies = parseBodies(
        wasm,
        sections.codeSec,
        importedFuncs
    );

    console.log(`Types          : ${types.length}`);
    console.log(
        `Imported funcs : ${importedFuncs}`
    );
    console.log(
        `Defined funcs  : ${funcTypes.length}`
    );
    console.log(
        `Code bodies    : ${bodies.length}`
    );
    console.log();

    const decoded: {
        body: Body;
        instrs: Instr[];
    }[] = [];

    let decodeErrors = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        try {
            const instrs = decode(
                body.bytes,
                body.start
            );

            decoded.push({
                body,
                instrs,
            });
        } catch {
            decodeErrors++;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `Decoded bodies : ${decoded.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Decode errors  : ${decodeErrors}`
    );

    console.log();

    let sites = 0;
    let localDependent = 0;
    let exact = 0;
    let loadDependent = 0;
    let callDependent = 0;
    let unresolved = 0;

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "DEEP BACKWARD RESULTS"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    for (const item of decoded) {
        const instrs = item.instrs;

        for (let i = 0; i < instrs.length; i++) {
            const ci = instrs[i];

            if (
                ci.op !== 0x11 ||
                ci.imm[0] !== TARGET_TYPE ||
                ci.imm[1] !== TARGET_TABLE
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            sites++;

            /*
             * Search the immediately preceding stack producer.
             */
            let source: Source = {
                kind: "unknown",
                origin: "no-source",
            };

            for (
                let j = i - 1;
                j >= 0 && j >= i - 100;
                j--
            ) {
                const x = instrs[j];

                if (x.op === 0x41) {
                    source = {
                        kind: "const",
                        value: x.imm[0],
                        origin:
                            `i32.const @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
                    };

                    break;
                }

                if (x.op === 0x20) {
                    source = traceLocal(
                        instrs,
                        x.imm[0],
                        j,
                        0,
                        new Set<string>()
                    );

                    break;
                }

                if (x.op === 0x10) {
                    source = {
                        kind: "call",
                        func: x.imm[0],
                        origin:
                            `call ${x.imm[0]} @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
                    };

                    break;
                }

                if (
                    x.op >= 0x28 &&
                    x.op <= 0x2f
                ) {
                    source = {
                        kind: "load",
                        origin:
                            `${x.text} @ 0x${x.off.toString(16)}`,
                    };

                    break;
                }

                /*
                 * Do not cross another indirect call.
                 */
                if (x.op === 0x11) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (source.kind === "const") {
                if (source.value === TARGET_SLOT) {
                    exact++;

                    console.log();
                    console.log(
                        "************************************************************"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "*** DEFINITIVE CONST(114) BACKWARD CANDIDATE ***"
                    );
                    console.log(
                        "************************************************************"
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `Caller function : ${item.body.func}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `call_indirect   : 0x${ci.off.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `Source          : CONST(${source.value})`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `Origin          : ${source.origin}`
                    );
                }
            } else if (
                source.kind === "local"
            ) {
                localDependent++;
            } else if (
                source.kind === "load"
            ) {
                loadDependent++;
            } else if (
                source.kind === "call"
            ) {
                callDependent++;
            } else {
                unresolved++;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "SUMMARY"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `Decoded bodies          : ${decoded.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Decode errors           : ${decodeErrors}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Type-0/table-0 sites    : ${sites}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Exact CONST(114) paths  : ${exact}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Local-dependent paths   : ${localDependent}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Memory-load paths       : ${loadDependent}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Call-return paths       : ${callDependent}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Unresolved paths        : ${unresolved}`
    );

    console.log();

    if (exact > 0) {
        console.log(
            "TARGET 4154: EXACT CONST(114) BACKWARD PATH FOUND."
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            "TARGET 4154: NO EXACT CONST(114) PATH YET."
        );

        console.log(
            "Local, memory and interprocedural paths remain."
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        "SAFETY / MODE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "No transaction sent."
    );

    console.log(
        "No WASM modification."
    );

    console.log(
        "Read-only / offline."
    );
}

import { Address, TonClient, Cell } from '@ton/ton';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000001';

const client = new TonClient({
    endpoint:
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

function shortAddress(value: string): string {
    if (value.length <= 32) return value;

    return (
        value.slice(0, 14) +
        '...' +
        value.slice(-12)
    );
}

function getOpcode(body: Cell | null | undefined): string {
    if (!body) return 'NO BODY';

    try {
        const slice = body.beginParse();

        if (slice.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'LESS THAN 32 BITS';
        }

        const op = slice.loadUint(32);

        return (
            '0x' +
            op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

    } catch {
        return 'OPCODE ERROR';
    }
}

function opcodeName(op: string): string {

    switch (op.toLowerCase()) {

        case '0x37c096df':
            return 'provide_lp';

        case '0x0f8a7ea5':
            return 'STON.FI / LP message';

        case '0x178d4519':
            return 'Jetton transfer';

        case '0x01f3835d':
            return 'pTON operation';

        case '0x7369676e':
            return 'wallet signed external message';

        default:
            return 'UNKNOWN';
    }
}

function getAddress(
    info: any,
    field: 'src' | 'dest'
): string {

    try {

        const value = info?.[field];

        if (value) {
            return value.toString();
        }

    } catch {
        // ignore
    }

    return 'unknown';
}

function getValue(info: any): bigint {

    try {

        if (
            info?.type === 'internal' &&
            info.value?.coins !== undefined
        ) {
            return BigInt(
                info.value.coins
            );
        }

    } catch {
        // ignore
    }

    return 0n;
}

function printMessage(
    title: string,
    message: any
) {

    if (!message) {
        console.log(
            `${title}: NONE`
        );

        return;
    }

    const info =
        message.info;

    const src =
        getAddress(info, 'src');

    const dst =
        getAddress(info, 'dest');

    const value =
        getValue(info);

    const opcode =
        getOpcode(message.body);

    console.log('');
    console.log(title);
    console.log('----------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        `SOURCE      : ${src}`
    );

    console.log(
        `DESTINATION : ${dst}`
    );

    console.log(
        `VALUE       : ${value.toString()} nanotons`
    );

    console.log(
        `TON         : ${Number(value) / 1e9}`
    );

    console.log(
        `OPCODE      : ${opcode}`
    );

    console.log(
        `MEANING     : ${opcodeName(opcode)}`
    );

    if (message.body) {

        try {

            const boc =
                message.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64');

            console.log(
                `BODY BOC    : ${boc}`
            );

        } catch {
            console.log(
                'BODY BOC    : ERROR'
            );
        }
    }
}

async function readAddress(
    label: string,
    addressText: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log(label);

    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `ADDRESS: ${addressText}`
    );

    let address: Address;

    try {

        address =
            Address.parse(addressText);

    } catch (error) {

        console.log(
            'INVALID ADDRESS:'
        );

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );

        return;
    }

    try {

        const transactions =
            await client.getTransactions(
                address,
                {
                    limit: 10,
                }
            );

        console.log(
            `TRANSACTIONS: ${transactions.length}`
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < transactions.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const tx: any =
                transactions[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `TX ${i + 1}`
            );

            console.log(
                `LT: ${tx.lt}`
            );

            if (
                String(tx.lt) !==
                TARGET_LT
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            console.log(
                '*** TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND ***'
            );

            console.log(
                `HASH: ${Buffer.from(
                    tx.hash()
                ).toString('base64')}`
            );

            printMessage(
                'INCOMING MESSAGE',
                tx.inMessage
            );

            if (tx.outMessages) {

                let index = 1;

                for (
                    const msg
                    of tx.outMessages.values()
                ) {

                    printMessage(
                        `OUTGOING MESSAGE ${index}`,
                        msg
                    );

                    index++;
                }
            }
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.log(
            'READ ERROR:'
        );

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        '             DEEP V2.2 LIQUIDITY TRACE'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TARGET LT: ${TARGET_LT}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STEP 1: USER WALLET'
    );

    await readAddress(
        'USER WALLET',
        USER_WALLET
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STEP 2: ROOSTER WALLET'
    );

    await readAddress(
        'ROOSTER JETTON WALLET',
        ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'STEP 3: pTON ROUTER WALLET'

}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const owner = provider.sender().address;

    if (!owner) {
        throw new Error('Tonkeeper wallet address not available');
    }

    const pton = DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(
        Address.parse(
            'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
        )
    );

    console.log('pTON Master:', pton.address.toString());
    console.log('Owner:', owner.toString());
    console.log('Deploy Wallet: 0.1 TON');

    await pton.sendDeployWallet(
        provider.provider(pton.address),
        provider.sender(),
        {
            ownerAddress: owner,
            excessAddress: owner,
        }
    );

    console.log('Deploy transaction sent.');
}

FILE: scripts\deployRoosterCoin.ts
====================================================================================================
import { beginCell, toNano } from '@ton/core';
import { RoosterCoin } from '../wrappers/RoosterCoin';
import { compile, NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    // فقط Testnet
    if (provider.network() !== 'testnet') {
        throw new Error('STOP: This script is only for TON Testnet.');
    }

    // Admin = همان کیف پولی که با Tonkeeper به Blueprint متصل شده
    const adminAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!adminAddress) {
        throw new Error('STOP: Connected wallet address is not available.');
    }

    console.log('Network:', provider.network());
    console.log('Connected wallet:', adminAddress.toString());
    console.log('Admin address:', adminAddress.toString());

    const content = beginCell()
    .storeUint(2, 32)
    .endCell();

    const jettonWalletCode = await compile('JettonWallet');
    const minterCode = await compile('RoosterCoin');

    const roosterCoin = provider.open(
        RoosterCoin.createFromConfig(
            {
                adminAddress,
                content,
                jettonWalletCode,
            },
            minterCode
        )
    );

    console.log('Rooster Coin address:', roosterCoin.address.toString());

    const alreadyDeployed =
        await provider.isContractDeployed(roosterCoin.address);

    if (alreadyDeployed) {
        console.log('Rooster Coin is already deployed.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('Sending deployment transaction...');
    console.log('IMPORTANT: Approve ONLY if your wallet shows TESTNET.');

    await roosterCoin.sendDeploy(
        provider.sender(),
        toNano('0.1')
    );

    console.log('Transaction sent. Waiting for Testnet confirmation...');

    await provider.waitForDeploy(
        roosterCoin.address,
        60,
        3000
    );

    console.log('Rooster Coin deployed successfully!');
    console.log('Address:', roosterCoin.address.toString());
}
