import { Address, getMethodId } from "@ton/core";
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from "ton-lite-client";

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval"
);

// همان LiteServer که قبلاً با موفقیت به آن وصل شدیم
const HOST = "tcp://168.119.95.207:27787";

const PUBLIC_KEY = Buffer.from(
    "Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=",
    "base64"
);

export async function run() {
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log(" ROUTER LITESERVER GETTER DIAGNOSTIC");
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
    console.log("Router:", ROUTER.toString());
    console.log("Server:", HOST);

    console.log("\n===== METHOD ID =====");

    const methodId = getMethodId("get_router_data");

    console.log("Method:", "get_router_data");
    console.log("Method ID:", methodId);
    console.log(
        "Method ID hex:",
        "0x" + methodId.toString(16)
    );

    console.log("\n===== CONNECTING =====");

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: HOST,
        publicKey: PUBLIC_KEY,
    });

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
    });

    try {
        console.log("Getting masterchain info...");

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            "Masterchain seqno:",
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            "Masterchain shard:",
            master.last.shard.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            "Masterchain root hash:",
            Buffer.from(master.last.rootHash).toString("hex")
        );

        console.log(
            "Masterchain file hash:",
            Buffer.from(master.last.fileHash).toString("hex")
        );

        console.log("\n===== RUN get_router_data =====");

        const result = await client.runSmcMethod({
            blockId: master.last,
            accountId: {
                workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
                id: ROUTER.hash,
            },
            methodId: BigInt(methodId),
            params: Buffer.alloc(0),
        });

        console.log("\n===== RESULT =====");

        console.log("Result object:");
        console.log(result);

        console.log("\nResult kind:");
        console.log(result.kind);

        console.log("\nGas used:");
        console.log(result.gasUsed);

        console.log("\nExit code:");
        console.log(result.exitCode);

        console.log("\nStack:");
        console.log(result.stack);

        console.log("\n===== RAW RESULT =====");
        console.dir(result, {
            depth: 10,
        });

    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log("\n===== ERROR =====");

        console.log(
            "Message:",
            e?.message ?? e
        );

        console.log("\nError object:");
        console.dir(e, {
            depth: 10,
        });

        if (e?.stack) {
            console.log("\nStack trace:");
            console.log(e.stack);
        }
    }

    console.log("\n========================================");
    console.log("DIAGNOSTIC FINISHED");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
    console.log("========================================");

    try {
        engine.close();
    } catch {
        // ignore
    }
}

FILE: scripts\diagnoseRouterGettersDirect.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';

const ROUTER =
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

const SERVER =
    'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088';

const PUBLIC_KEY = Buffer.from(
    'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    'base64'
);

async function waitForReady(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs = 15000
) {
    return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {

        if (engine.isReady()) {
            resolve();
            return;
        }

        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            reject(new Error(
                'Timeout waiting for LiteServer ready.'
            ));
        }, timeoutMs);

        engine.once('ready', () => {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            resolve();
        });

        engine.once('error', (err) => {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            reject(err);
        });

        engine.once('close', () => {
            clearTimeout(timer);

            if (!engine.isReady()) {
                reject(new Error(
                    'LiteServer connection closed before ready.'
                ));
            }
        });
    });
}

async function main() {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ROUTER GETTER TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET CONNECTION REQUIRED.');
    console.log('');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: SERVER,
        publicKey: PUBLIC_KEY,
        client: 'tcp',
    });

    engine.on('connect', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] Engine connected');
    });

    engine.on('ready', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] Engine ready');
    });

    engine.on('close', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] Engine closed');
    });

    engine.on('error', (err: any) => {
        console.log('[EVENT] Engine error:', err);
    });

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    console.log('===== WAIT FOR CONNECTION =====');

    await waitForReady(engine);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Engine closed:', engine.isClosed());
    console.log('Engine ready:', engine.isReady());

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== MASTERCHAIN =====');

    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('Seqno:', master.seqno);
    console.log(
        'Root hash:',
        master.rootHash.toString('hex')
    );
    console.log(
        'File hash:',
        master.fileHash.toString('hex')
    );

    const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== GET ROUTER VERSION =====');

    const version = await client.runMethod(
        router,
        'get_router_version',
        Buffer.alloc(0),
        master
    );

    console.log(JSON.stringify({
        exitCode: version.exitCode,
        result: version.result,
        block: version.block,
    }, null, 2));

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== GET ROUTER DATA =====');

    const data = await client.runMethod(
        router,
        'get_router_data',
        Buffer.alloc(0),
        master
    );

    console.log(JSON.stringify({
        exitCode: data.exitCode,
        result: data.result,
        block: data.block,
    }, null, 2));

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== FINISHED =====');

    engine.close();
}

main().catch((err: any) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error('Message:', err?.message);
    console.error('Name:', err?.name);
    console.error('Code:', err?.code);
    console.error('Stack:', err?.stack);
    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\diagnoseRouterLibraryExecution.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteRoundRobinEngine, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { Functions } from 'ton-lite-client/dist/schema';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

const EXPECTED_CODE_HASH =
    'ca4c3052a1396aef50ca358ac355e2a99be4e1683eb87a2c11fbd452bb4acec5';

const EXPECTED_LIBRARY_HASH =
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

const METHOD_ID = '128085'; // get_router_data = 0x1F455

function hex(buf: Buffer): string {
    return Buffer.from(buf).toString('hex');
}

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER LIBRARY EXECUTION DIAGNOSTIC');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================');

    const engines = [
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
                'base64'
            ),
        }),
    ];

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine: new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines),
    });

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 1. MASTERCHAIN
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

    console.log('\nMASTERCHAIN');
    console.log('seqno:', master.last.seqno);
    console.log('shard:', master.last.shard);
    console.log('workchain:', master.last.workchain);

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 2. GET ROUTER STATE DIRECTLY
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const stateRequest = {
        kind: 'liteServer.getAccountState' as const,

        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,
            seqno: master.last.seqno,
            shard: master.last.shard,
            workchain: master.last.workchain,
            fileHash: master.last.fileHash,
            rootHash: master.last.rootHash,
        },

        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
            workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
            id: Buffer.from(ROUTER.hash),
        },
    };

    const stateResult = await client.engine.query(
        Functions.liteServer_getAccountState,
        stateRequest
    );

    console.log('\nSTATE RESULT');
    console.log('kind:', stateResult.kind);
    console.log('state bytes:', stateResult.state.length);

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 3. DECODE STATE BOC
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const { Cell } = await import('@ton/core');

    const stateCells = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(stateResult.state));

    if (stateCells.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected state root count: ${stateCells.length}`);
    }

    const stateRoot = stateCells[0];

    console.log('\nSTATE ROOT');
    console.log('type:', stateRoot.type);
    console.log('bits:', stateRoot.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', stateRoot.refs.length);
    console.log('hash:', stateRoot.hash().toString('hex'));

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 4. ROUTER CODE REFERENCE
    // ------------------------------------------------
    if (stateRoot.refs.length < 1) {
        throw new Error('Router state has no code reference');
    }

    const codeRef = stateRoot.refs[0];

    console.log('\nROUTER CODE REFERENCE');
    console.log('type:', codeRef.type);
    console.log('bits:', codeRef.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', codeRef.refs.length);

    const actualCodeHash = codeRef.hash().toString('hex');

    console.log('code cell hash:', actualCodeHash);
    console.log(
        'expected code hash:',
        EXPECTED_CODE_HASH
    );

    console.log(
        'CODE HASH MATCH:',
        actualCodeHash === EXPECTED_CODE_HASH ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'is exotic:',
        codeRef.isExotic ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 5. SHOW RAW LIBRARY REFERENCE BOC
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const codeBoc = codeRef.toBoc().toString('base64');

    console.log('\nCODE REFERENCE BOC');
    console.log(codeBoc);

    // The first byte of the exotic library-reference payload
    // is 0x02, followed by the 256-bit library hash.
    //
    // We already verified this exact BOC previously.
    console.log('\nEXPECTED LIBRARY HASH');
    console.log(EXPECTED_LIBRARY_HASH);

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 6. RUN get_router_data MODE 4
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const runRequest4 = {
        kind: 'liteServer.runSmcMethod' as const,

        mode: 4,

        id: {
            kind: 'tonNode.blockIdExt' as const,
            seqno: master.last.seqno,
            shard: master.last.shard,
            workchain: master.last.workchain,
            fileHash: master.last.fileHash,
            rootHash: master.last.rootHash,
        },

        account: {
            kind: 'liteServer.accountId' as const,
            workchain: ROUTER.workChain,
            id: Buffer.from(ROUTER.hash),
        },

        methodId: METHOD_ID,

        params: Buffer.alloc(0),
    };

    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log('RUN METHOD');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('method:', 'get_router_data');
    console.log('method id:', METHOD_ID);
    console.log('mode:', 4);

    const result4 = await client.engine.query(
        Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod,
        runRequest4
    );

    console.log('\nMODE 4 RESULT');
    console.dir(result4, { depth: 5 });

    console.log(
        'exit code:',
        result4.exitCode
    );

    if (result4.result) {
        console.log(
            'result bytes:',
            result4.result.length
        );

        console.log(
            'result BOC:',
            Buffer.from(result4.result).toString('base64')
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 7. RUN get_router_data MODE 20
    // ------------------------------------------------
    const runRequest20 = {
        ...runRequest4,
        mode: 20,
    };

    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log('RUN METHOD WITH LIB EXTRAS');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log('mode:', 20);
    console.log('mode bits: 4 + 16');
    console.log('expected: result + lib_extras');

    const result20 = await client.engine.query(
        Functions.liteServer_runSmcMethod,
        runRequest20
    );

    console.log('\nMODE 20 RESULT');
    console.dir(result20, { depth: 5 });

    console.log(
        'exit code:',
        result20.exitCode
    );

    console.log(
        'result bytes:',
        result20.result
            ? result20.result.length
            : 0
    );

    console.log(
        'libExtras bytes:',
        result20.libExtras
            ? result20.libExtras.length
            : 0
    );

    if (result20.result) {
        console.log(
            'result BOC:',
            Buffer.from(result20.result).toString('base64')
        );
    }

    if (result20.libExtras) {
        console.log(
            'libExtras BOC:',
            Buffer.from(result20.libExtras).toString('base64')
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------
    // 8. FINAL DIAGNOSIS
    // ------------------------------------------------
    console.log('\n========================================');
    console.log('DIAGNOSTIC SUMMARY');
    console.log('========================================');

    console.log(
        'Router code hash correct:',
        actualCodeHash === EXPECTED_CODE_HASH
    );

    console.log(
        'MODE 4 exit code:',
        result4.exitCode
    );

    console.log(
        'MODE 20 exit code:',
        result20.exitCode
    );

    console.log(
        'MODE 20 libExtras:',
        result20.libExtras
            ? result20.libExtras.length
            : 0
    );

    console.log('\nNO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('\nFATAL ERROR');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\diagnoseRouterLiteGetter.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from "@ton/core";
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from "ton-lite-client";

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    "EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval"
);

const HOST = "tcp://168.119.95.207:27787";

const PUBLIC_KEY = Buffer.from(
    "Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=",
    "base64"
);

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function run() {
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log(" ROUTER LITESERVER GETTER DIAGNOSTIC");
    console.log("========================================");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.");
    console.log("NO WALLET CONNECTION REQUIRED.");
    console.log("Router:", ROUTER.toString());
    console.log("Server:", HOST);

    console.log("\n===== CREATE ENGINE =====");

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: HOST,
        publicKey: PUBLIC_KEY,
    });

    console.log("Engine closed:", engine.isClosed());
    console.log("Engine ready:", engine.isReady());

    try {
        console.log("\n===== CONNECT =====");

        await engine.connect();

        console.log("Connect finished.");
        console.log("Engine closed:", engine.isClosed());
        console.log("Engine ready:", engine.isReady());

        // Give ADNL a moment to finish initialization.
        await sleep(1000);

        console.log("\n===== ENGINE STATUS =====");
        console.log("Engine closed:", engine.isClosed());
        console.log("Engine ready:", engine.isReady());

        if (engine.isClosed()) {
            throw new Error(
                "Engine closed immediately after connect()."
            );
        }

        if (!engine.isReady()) {
            throw new Error(
                "Engine did not become ready after connect()."
            );
        }

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        console.log("\n===== MASTERCHAIN =====");

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            "Masterchain seqno:",
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            "Shard:",
            master.last.shard.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            "Root hash:",
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString("hex")
        );

        console.log(
            "File hash:",
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.fileHash
            ).toString("hex")
        );

        console.log("\n===== RUN get_router_data =====");

        const result = await client.runMethod(
            ROUTER,
            "get_router_data",
            Buffer.alloc(0),
            master.last
        );

        console.log("\n===== RESULT =====");

        console.dir(result, {
            depth: 20,
        });

    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log("\n===== ERROR =====");

        console.log(
            "Message:",
            e?.message ?? e
        );

        console.log("\nError object:");

        console.dir(e, {
            depth: 20,
        });

        if (e?.stack) {
            console.log("\nSTACK TRACE:");
            console.log(e.stack);
        }

    } finally {
        console.log("\n===== CLOSE ENGINE =====");

        try {
            engine.close();
            console.log("Engine closed.");
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                "Close error:",
                e
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("\n========================================");
    console.log("DIAGNOSTIC FINISHED");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
    console.log("========================================");
}

run().catch((e) => {
    console.error("\nFATAL ERROR:");
    console.error(e);
    process.exit(1);
});

FILE: scripts\diagnoseTestnetPool.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient, JettonMaster } from '@ton/ton';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run() {
    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const roosterMasterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ptonMasterAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQACS30DNoUQ7NfApPvzh7eBmSZ9L4ygJ-lkNWtba8TQT-Px'
    );

    const router = client.open(
        DEX.v2_1.Router.CPI.create(routerAddress)
    );

    const roosterMaster = client.open(
        JettonMaster.create(roosterMasterAddress)
    );

    const ptonMaster = client.open(
        JettonMaster.create(ptonMasterAddress)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TESTNET POOL DIAGNOSTIC =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(roosterMasterAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(ptonMasterAddress.toString());

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Get Router Jetton Wallets
    // --------------------------------------------------

    let roosterRouterWallet: Address;
    let ptonRouterWallet: Address;

    try {
        roosterRouterWallet =
            await roosterMaster.getWalletAddress(routerAddress);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
        console.log(roosterRouterWallet.toString());
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet ERROR:');
        console.log(String(error));
        return;
    }

    try {
        ptonRouterWallet =
            await ptonMaster.getWalletAddress(routerAddress);

        console.log('');
        console.log('pTON Router Wallet:');
        console.log(ptonRouterWallet.toString());
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('pTON Router Wallet ERROR:');
        console.log(String(error));
        return;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Ask Router for Pool Address
    // --------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROUTER get_pool_address =====');

    try {
        const poolAddress = await router.getPoolAddress({
            token0: roosterRouterWallet,
            token1: ptonRouterWallet,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log('Pool Address:');
        console.log(poolAddress.toString());
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('FAILED');
        console.log(String(error));
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Reverse token order
    // --------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== REVERSE TOKEN ORDER =====');

    try {
        const poolAddressReverse = await router.getPoolAddress({
            token0: ptonRouterWallet,
            token1: roosterRouterWallet,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('SUCCESS');
        console.log('Pool Address:');
        console.log(poolAddressReverse.toString());
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('FAILED');
        console.log(String(error));
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('================================');
}

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';

const SERVER = 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088';
const PUBLIC_KEY = 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=';

const ROUTER = 'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval';

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT ADNL ROUTER GETTER TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET CONNECTION REQUIRED.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Server:', SERVER);
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER);
    console.log('');

    const client = new ADNLClientTCP(SERVER, PUBLIC_KEY);

    client.on('connect', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] connect');
    });

    client.on('ready', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] ready');
    });

    client.on('error', (error: Error) => {
        console.log('[EVENT] error:', error?.message);
    });

    client.on('close', () => {
        console.log('[EVENT] close');
    });

    console.log('===== CONNECT =====');

    await client.connect();

    console.log('===== ADNL READY =====');

    /*
     * We intentionally stop here.
     *
     * This test confirms that the raw ADNL transport
     * is working before we construct a LiteServer query.
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== RESULT =====');
    console.log('RAW ADNL CONNECTION: OK');
    console.log('NEXT STEP: LITESERVER QUERY');

    setTimeout(() => {
        try {
            client.end();
        } catch {}

        process.exit(0);
    }, 1000);
}

main().catch((error: any) => {
    console.log('');
    console.log('===== FAILED =====');
    console.log('Message:', error?.message);
    console.log('Name:', error?.name);
    console.log('Code:', error?.code);
    console.log('Stack:', error?.stack);

    process.exit(1);
});

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

const HASH = Buffer.from(
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86',
    'hex'
);

const servers = [
    {
        name: 'SERVER 1',
        host: 'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787',
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    },
    {
        name: 'SERVER 2',
        host: 'tcp://168.119.82.221:51088',
        key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
    },
];

async function main() {
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT LIBRARY QUERY');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('HASH:', HASH.toString('hex'));
    console.log('');

    for (const s of servers) {
        console.log('-----', s.name, '-----');

        const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: s.host,
            publicKey: Buffer.from(s.key, 'base64'),
        });

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

        try {
            const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

            console.log('Masterchain:', master.last.seqno);

            const result = await client.getLibraries([HASH]);

            console.log('Result count:', result.result.length);

            if (result.result.length > 0) {
                console.log('LIBRARY FOUND');
                console.log('Returned hash:',
                    Buffer.from(result.result[0].hash).toString('hex')
                );
                console.log('Data bytes:', result.result[0].data.length);
            } else {
                console.log('LIBRARY NOT RETURNED');
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('ERROR:', String(e));
        }

        console.log('');
    }
}

main().catch(console.error);

FILE: scripts\directLiteLibraryTest.ts
====================================================================================================
import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {

    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const libraryHashHex =
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

    const libraryHash =
        Buffer.from(libraryHashHex, 'hex');

    const expectedRouterCodeHash =
        'ca4c3052a1396aef50ca358ac355e2a99be4e1683eb87a2c11fbd452bb4acec5';

    const host =
        'tcp://168.119.95.207:27787';

    const publicKey =
        Buffer.from(
            'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
            'base64'
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' DIRECT TON LITE SERVER TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('NO WALLET');
    console.log('NO TONCONNECT');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Server:');
    console.log(host);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(router.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Library Hash:');
    console.log(libraryHashHex);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Creating LiteSingleEngine...');

    const engine =
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host,
            publicKey,
            reconnectTimeout: 5000,
        });

    const client =
        new LiteClient({
            engine,
            batchSize: 1,
        });

    try {

        // ========================================
        // MASTERCHAIN
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('MASTERCHAIN');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        // ========================================
        // ROUTER ACCOUNT
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER ACCOUNT');

        const account =
            await client.getAccountState(
                router,
                master.last
            );

        if (
            account.state === null
        ) {
            console.log(
                'Router state: NULL'
            );
            return;
        }

        console.log(
            'Router state:',
            account.state.storage.state.type
        );

        if (
            account.state.storage.state.type !==
            'active'
        ) {
            console.log(
                'Router is NOT active.'
            );
            return;
        }

        console.log(
            'Router active: YES'
        );

        const activeState =
            account.state.storage.state.state;

        const code:
            Cell | null | undefined =
            activeState.code;

        if (!code) {
            console.log(
                'Router code: MISSING'
            );
            return;
        }

        console.log(
            'Router code: PRESENT'
        );

        // ========================================
        // CODE INFORMATION
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER CODE');

        console.log(
            'Cell type:',
            code.type
        );

        console.log(
            'Bits:',
            code.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Refs:',
            code.refs.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Exotic:',
            code.type !== -1
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // ========================================
        // CODE HASH
        // ========================================

        const actualCodeHash =
            code.hash().toString('hex');

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('CODE HASH');

        console.log(
            'Actual:',
            actualCodeHash
        );

        console.log(
            'Expected:',
            expectedRouterCodeHash
        );

        console.log(
            'MATCH:',
            actualCodeHash ===
                expectedRouterCodeHash
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // ========================================
        // CODE BOC
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('CODE BOC');

        const boc =
            code.toBoc({
                idx: false,
            });

        console.log(
            Buffer.from(boc).toString('base64')
        );

        // ========================================
        // ROOT CELL
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROOT CELL');

        console.log(
            'Cell type:',
            code.type
        );

        console.log(
            'Bits:',
            code.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Refs:',
            code.refs.length
        );

        // ========================================
        // ROOT CELL HASH
        // ========================================

        console.log(
            'Root cell hash:',
            code.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        // ========================================
        // REFERENCES
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROOT REFERENCES');

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < code.refs.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const ref =
                code.refs[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `Reference ${i}`
            );

            console.log(
                'Type:',
                ref.type
            );

            console.log(
                'Bits:',
                ref.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                'Refs:',
                ref.refs.length
            );

            console.log(
                'Hash:',
                ref.hash().toString('hex')
            );
        }

        // ========================================
        // LIBRARY LOOKUP
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('LIBRARY LOOKUP');

        console.log(
            'Request:',
            libraryHashHex
        );

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([
                libraryHash,
            ]);

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Raw result:'
        );

        console.dir(
            result,
            {
                depth: 5,
            }
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Result count:',
            result.result.length
        );

        if (
            result.result.length > 0
        ) {

            console.log(
                'LIBRARY RETURNED: YES'
            );

            for (
                let i = 0;
                i < result.result.length;
                i++
            ) {

                const library =
                    result.result[i];

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `Library ${i}:`
                );

                console.dir(
                    library,
                    {
                        depth: 5,
                    }
                );
            }

        } else {

            console.log(
                'LIBRARY RETURNED: NO'
            );
        }

        // ========================================
        // FAKE LOOKUP
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('FAKE LIBRARY TEST');

        const fakeHash =
            Buffer.alloc(32, 0);

        const fakeResult =
            await client.getLibraries([
                fakeHash,
            ]);

        console.log(
            'Fake result count:',
            fakeResult.result.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Fake library returned:',
            fakeResult.result.length > 0
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // ========================================
        // FINAL
        // ========================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.log(
            'Masterchain: OK'
        );

        console.log(
            'Router active: YES'
        );

        console.log(
            'Router code: YES'
        );

        console.log(
            'Code hash:',
            actualCodeHash ===
                expectedRouterCodeHash
                ? 'MATCH'
                : 'MISMATCH'
        );

        console.log(
            'Library:',
            result.result.length > 0
                ? 'RETURNED'
                : 'NOT RETURNED'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
        );

        console.log(
            '========================================'
        );

    } finally {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Closing Lite Server connection...'
        );

        engine.close();

        console.log(
            'Connection closed.'
        );
    }
}

main().catch(
    (error) => {

        console.error('');
        console.error(
            '========================================'
        );

        console.error(
            'DIRECT LITE SERVER ERROR'
        );

        console.error(
            '========================================'
        );

        console.error(error);

        process.exit(1);
    }
);

import { ADNLClientTCP } from 'adnl';

FILE: scripts\directPoolGetter.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const roosterWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQAmO8BvKx4ta9jkCCDx197VqrUrsaC42Dv6yhDAyKzFpi2S'
    );

    const ptonMaster = Address.parse(
        'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
    );

    console.log('===== DIRECT get_pool_address =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER router wallet:', roosterWallet.toString());
    console.log('pTON master:', ptonMaster.toString());

    const routerProvider = provider.provider(router);

    const ptonWalletResult = await provider.provider(ptonMaster).get(
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(router)
                    .endCell()
            }
        ]
    );

    const ptonRouterWallet = ptonWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'pTON router wallet:',
        ptonRouterWallet.toString()
    );

    try {
        const result = await routerProvider.get(
            'get_pool_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(roosterWallet)
                        .endCell()
                },
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(ptonRouterWallet)
                        .endCell()
                }
            ]
        );

        console.log('POOL:', result.stack.readAddress().toString());
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('GET_POOL_ADDRESS ERROR:', e);
    }
}

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }
}

function sleb(pos, bits = 32, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        if (shift > bits + 7) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    if (shift < 53 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function slebBigInt(pos, bits = 64, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        if (shift > BigInt(bits + 7)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0) {
        value -= 1n << shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function signedHex(n) {
    return n < 0
        ? `-0x${(-n).toString(16)}`
        : `0x${n.toString(16)}`;
}

function needBytes(p, n, end, context) {
    if (p + n > end) {
        throw new Error(
            `Truncated ${context} at ${hex(p)}; need ${n} byte(s)`
        );
    }
}

function readU(p, end, context = 'ULEB') {
    const result = uleb(p, end);
    return { value: result.value, next: result.next, text: String(result.value) };
}

function readS(p, end, context = 'SLEB') {
    const result = sleb(p, 32, end);
    return { value: result.value, next: result.next, text: String(result.value) };
}

function readMemArg(p, end) {
    const align = readU(p, end, 'memarg align');
    const offset = readU(align.next, end, 'memarg offset');

    return {
        next: offset.next,
        text: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
    };
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error('Invalid WASM section size');
    }

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error('Code section not found');
}

const count = uleb(codeStart, codeEnd);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos, codeEnd);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > codeEnd) {
        throw new Error(`Function body ${ordinal} exceeds code section`);
    }

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, { start, end });
    pos = end;
}

const memoryOps = new Set([
    0x28, 0x29, 0x2a, 0x2b,
    0x2c, 0x2d, 0x2e, 0x2f,
    0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x35,
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39,
    0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d, 0x3e,

const fcNames = {
    0: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_s',
    1: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_u',
    2: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_s',
    3: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_u',
    4: 'i64.trunc_sat_f32_s',

function readBytes(start, end) {
    return [...wasm.subarray(start, end)]
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');
}

function decodeFunction(func) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(`Function ${func} not found`);
    }

    let p = body.start;

    const localGroups = uleb(p, body.end);
    p = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p, body.end);
        p = n.next;

        needBytes(p, 1, body.end, 'local type');
        p++;
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < body.end) {
        const start = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = names[op];

        if (!text && op !== 0xfc) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown opcode 0x${op.toString(16)} in function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            needBytes(p, 1, body.end, 'block type');
            const blockType = wasm[p++];

            text += blockType === 0x40
                ? ' void'
                : ` blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
            op === 0x10 ||
            op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 || op === 0x23 || op === 0x24
        ) {
            const imm = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = imm.next;
            text += ` ${imm.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const count = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = count.next;

            const labels = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
                const label = uleb(p, body.end);
                p = label.next;
                labels.push(label.value);
            }

            const fallback = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = fallback.next;

            text += ` labels=[${labels.join(',')}] default=${fallback.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const typeIndex = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = typeIndex.next;

            const tableIndex = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = tableIndex.next;

            text += ` type=${typeIndex.value} table=${tableIndex.value}`;
        } else if (memoryOps.has(op)) {
            const memarg = readMemArg(p, body.end);
            p = memarg.next;
            text += ` ${memarg.text}`;
        } else if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            const mem = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = mem.next;
            text += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x41) {
            const imm = sleb(p, 32, body.end);
            p = imm.next;
            text += ` ${imm.value} (${signedHex(imm.value)})`;
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            const imm = slebBigInt(p, 64, body.end);
            p = imm.next;

            const valueHex = imm.value < 0n
                ? `-0x${(-imm.value).toString(16)}`
                : `0x${imm.value.toString(16)}`;

            text += ` ${imm.value.toString()} (${valueHex})`;
        } else if (op === 0x43) {
            needBytes(p, 4, body.end, 'f32.const');
            text += ` bytes=${readBytes(p, p + 4)}`;
            p += 4;
        } else if (op === 0x44) {
            needBytes(p, 8, body.end, 'f64.const');
            text += ` bytes=${readBytes(p, p + 8)}`;
            p += 8;
        } else if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = sub.next;

            const name = fcNames[sub.value];

            if (!name) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unknown 0xfc subopcode ${sub.value} in function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
                );
            }

            text = `0xfc.${sub.value} ${name}`;

            const imm1 = () => {
                const v = uleb(p, body.end);
                p = v.next;
                return v.value;
            };

            if (sub.value === 8) {
                const dataIndex = imm1();
                const memoryIndex = imm1();
                text += ` data=${dataIndex} memory=${memoryIndex}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 9) {
                text += ` data=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 10) {
                const dstMemory = imm1();
                const srcMemory = imm1();
                text += ` dst_memory=${dstMemory} src_memory=${srcMemory}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 11) {
                text += ` memory=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 12) {
                const elemIndex = imm1();
                const tableIndex = imm1();
                text += ` elem=${elemIndex} table=${tableIndex}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 13) {
                text += ` elem=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 14) {
                const dstTable = imm1();
                const srcTable = imm1();
                text += ` dst_table=${dstTable} src_table=${srcTable}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 15) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 16) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 17) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            }
        }

        if (p > body.end) {
            throw new Error(
                `Instruction overran function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }

        instructions.push({
            start,
            end: p,
            text,
            raw: readBytes(start, p),
        });

        if (op === 0x0b && p === body.end) break;
    }

    return { body, instructions };
}

console.log('\nROOSTER WASM - VERIFY RAW CALL 2034');

for (let func = 83; func <= 9480; func++) {
    try {
        const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(func);

        if (
            targetOffset < body.start ||
            targetOffset >= body.end
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        containingFunctions++;

        console.log('\n============================================================');
        console.log(`CONTAINING FUNCTION: ${func}`);
        console.log(`BODY: ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
        console.log(`BODY SIZE: ${body.end - body.start}`);
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        let nearestIndex = -1;

        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];

            if (
                ins.start <= targetOffset &&
                targetOffset < ins.end
            ) {
                nearestIndex = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (nearestIndex === -1) {
            console.log(
                'TARGET OFFSET IS IN BODY RANGE BUT NOT IN A DECODED INSTRUCTION.'
            );
            continue;
        }

        const from = Math.max(0, nearestIndex - 10);
        const to = Math.min(
            instructions.length - 1,
            nearestIndex + 10
        );

        for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];
            const marker =
                ins.start <= targetOffset && targetOffset < ins.end
                    ? '>>> '
                    : '    ';

            console.log(
                `${marker}${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
                `${ins.raw.padEnd(38)} ${ins.text}`
            );
        }

        const targetInstruction = instructions[nearestIndex];

        console.log('\nVERIFICATION');
        console.log(`INSTRUCTION START: ${hex(targetInstruction.start)}`);
        console.log(`INSTRUCTION END:   ${hex(targetInstruction.end)}`);
        console.log(`DECODED TEXT:      ${targetInstruction.text}`);
        console.log(
            `EXACT START MATCH: ${targetInstruction.start === targetOffset}`
        );
    } catch (error) {
        decodeErrors++;
    }
}

console.log('\n============================================================');
console.log(`CONTAINING FUNCTIONS FOUND: ${containingFunctions}`);
console.log(`DECODE ERRORS: ${decodeErrors}`);

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }
}

function sleb(pos, bits = 32, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        if (shift > bits + 7) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    if (shift < 53 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function slebBigInt(pos, bits = 64, limit = wasm.length) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= limit) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

        if (shift > BigInt(bits + 7)) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0) {
        value -= 1n << shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function signedHex(n) {
    return n < 0
        ? `-0x${(-n).toString(16)}`
        : `0x${n.toString(16)}`;
}

function needBytes(p, n, end, context) {
    if (p + n > end) {
        throw new Error(
            `Truncated ${context} at ${hex(p)}; need ${n} byte(s)`
        );
    }
}

function readU(p, end, context = 'ULEB') {
    const result = uleb(p, end);
    return { value: result.value, next: result.next, text: String(result.value) };
}

function readS(p, end, context = 'SLEB') {
    const result = sleb(p, 32, end);
    return { value: result.value, next: result.next, text: String(result.value) };
}

function readMemArg(p, end) {
    const align = readU(p, end, 'memarg align');
    const offset = readU(align.next, end, 'memarg offset');

    return {
        next: offset.next,
        text: `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
    };
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error('Invalid WASM section size');
    }

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error('Code section not found');
}

const count = uleb(codeStart, codeEnd);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos, codeEnd);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > codeEnd) {
        throw new Error(`Function body ${ordinal} exceeds code section`);
    }

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, { start, end });
    pos = end;
}

const names = {

const memoryOps = new Set([
    0x28, 0x29, 0x2a, 0x2b,
    0x2c, 0x2d, 0x2e, 0x2f,
    0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x35,
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39,
    0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d, 0x3e,
]);

const fcNames = {
    0: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_s',
    1: 'i32.trunc_sat_f32_u',
    2: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_s',
    3: 'i32.trunc_sat_f64_u',
    4: 'i64.trunc_sat_f32_s',
    5: 'i64.trunc_sat_f32_u',
    6: 'i64.trunc_sat_f64_s',
    7: 'i64.trunc_sat_f64_u',
    8: 'memory.init',
    9: 'data.drop',
    10: 'memory.copy',
    11: 'memory.fill',
    12: 'table.init',
    13: 'elem.drop',
    14: 'table.copy',
    15: 'table.grow',
    16: 'table.size',
    17: 'table.fill',
};

function readBytes(start, end) {
    return [...wasm.subarray(start, end)]
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');
}

function decodeFunction(func) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(`Function ${func} not found`);
    }

    let p = body.start;

    const localGroups = uleb(p, body.end);
    p = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p, body.end);
        p = n.next;

        needBytes(p, 1, body.end, 'local type');
        p++;
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < body.end) {
        const start = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = names[op];

        if (!text && op !== 0xfc) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown opcode 0x${op.toString(16)} in function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            needBytes(p, 1, body.end, 'block type');
            const blockType = wasm[p++];

            text += blockType === 0x40
                ? ' void'
                : ` blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
        } else if (
            op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
            op === 0x10 ||
            op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 || op === 0x23 || op === 0x24
        ) {
            const imm = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = imm.next;
            text += ` ${imm.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x0e) {
            const count = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = count.next;

            const labels = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
                const label = uleb(p, body.end);
                p = label.next;
                labels.push(label.value);
            }

            const fallback = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = fallback.next;

            text += ` labels=[${labels.join(',')}] default=${fallback.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x11) {
            const typeIndex = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = typeIndex.next;

            const tableIndex = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = tableIndex.next;

            text += ` type=${typeIndex.value} table=${tableIndex.value}`;
        } else if (memoryOps.has(op)) {
            const memarg = readMemArg(p, body.end);
            p = memarg.next;
            text += ` ${memarg.text}`;
        } else if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            const mem = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = mem.next;
            text += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
        } else if (op === 0x41) {
            const imm = sleb(p, 32, body.end);
            p = imm.next;
            text += ` ${imm.value} (${signedHex(imm.value)})`;
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            const imm = slebBigInt(p, 64, body.end);
            p = imm.next;

            const valueHex = imm.value < 0n
                ? `-0x${(-imm.value).toString(16)}`
                : `0x${imm.value.toString(16)}`;

            text += ` ${imm.value.toString()} (${valueHex})`;
        } else if (op === 0x43) {
            needBytes(p, 4, body.end, 'f32.const');
            text += ` bytes=${readBytes(p, p + 4)}`;
            p += 4;
        } else if (op === 0x44) {
            needBytes(p, 8, body.end, 'f64.const');
            text += ` bytes=${readBytes(p, p + 8)}`;
            p += 8;
        } else if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = uleb(p, body.end);
            p = sub.next;

            const name = fcNames[sub.value];

            if (!name) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unknown 0xfc subopcode ${sub.value} in function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
                );
            }

            text = `0xfc.${sub.value} ${name}`;

            const imm1 = () => {
                const v = uleb(p, body.end);
                p = v.next;
                return v.value;
            };

            if (sub.value === 8) {
                const dataIndex = imm1();
                const memoryIndex = imm1();
                text += ` data=${dataIndex} memory=${memoryIndex}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 9) {
                text += ` data=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 10) {
                const dstMemory = imm1();
                const srcMemory = imm1();
                text += ` dst_memory=${dstMemory} src_memory=${srcMemory}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 11) {
                text += ` memory=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 12) {
                const elemIndex = imm1();
                const tableIndex = imm1();
                text += ` elem=${elemIndex} table=${tableIndex}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 13) {
                text += ` elem=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 14) {
                const dstTable = imm1();
                const srcTable = imm1();
                text += ` dst_table=${dstTable} src_table=${srcTable}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 15) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 16) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            } else if (sub.value === 17) {
                text += ` table=${imm1()}`;
            }
        }

        if (p > body.end) {
            throw new Error(
                `Instruction overran function ${func} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }

        instructions.push({
            start,
            end: p,
            text,
            raw: readBytes(start, p),
        });

        if (op === 0x0b && p === body.end) break;
    }

    return { body, instructions };
}


console.log('\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');

const targetFunction = 2034;
let verifyDecodeErrors = 0;
let callersFound = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Print the complete decoded body of function 2034
// ------------------------------------------------------------

try {
    const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(targetFunction);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION ${targetFunction}`);
    console.log(`BODY ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS ${instructions.length}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    for (const ins of instructions) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw.padEnd(38)} ${ins.text}`
        );
    }
} catch (error) {
    verifyDecodeErrors++;
    console.log(`FAILED TO DECODE FUNC ${targetFunction}`);
    console.log(String(error));
}


const targetOffset = 0x00080505;
let containingFunctions = 0;
let decodeErrors = 0;

console.log('\nROOSTER WASM - VERIFY RAW CALL 2034');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log(`TARGET OFFSET: ${hex(targetOffset)}`);

for (let func = 83; func <= 9480; func++) {
    try {
        const { body, instructions } = decodeFunction(func);

        if (
            targetOffset < body.start ||
            targetOffset >= body.end
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        containingFunctions++;

        console.log('\n============================================================');
        console.log(`CONTAINING FUNCTION: ${func}`);
        console.log(`BODY: ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
        console.log(`BODY SIZE: ${body.end - body.start}`);
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        let nearestIndex = -1;

        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];

            if (
                ins.start <= targetOffset &&
                targetOffset < ins.end
            ) {
                nearestIndex = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (nearestIndex === -1) {
            console.log('OFFSET NOT FOUND IN DECODED INSTRUCTIONS');
            continue;
        }

        const from = Math.max(0, nearestIndex - 10);
        const to = Math.min(
            instructions.length - 1,
            nearestIndex + 10
        );

        for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];
            const marker =
                ins.start <= targetOffset && targetOffset < ins.end
                    ? '>>> '
                    : '    ';

            console.log(
                `${marker}${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
                `${ins.raw.padEnd(38)} ${ins.text}`
            );
        }

        const targetInstruction = instructions[nearestIndex];

        console.log('\nVERIFICATION');
        console.log(`INSTRUCTION START: ${hex(targetInstruction.start)}`);
        console.log(`INSTRUCTION END:   ${hex(targetInstruction.end)}`);
        console.log(`DECODED TEXT:      ${targetInstruction.text}`);
        console.log(
            `EXACT START MATCH: ${targetInstruction.start === targetOffset}`
        );
    } catch (error) {
        decodeErrors++;
    }
}

console.log('\n============================================================');
console.log(`CONTAINING FUNCTIONS FOUND: ${containingFunctions}`);
console.log(`DECODE ERRORS: ${verifyDecodeErrors}`);
console.log('READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTIONS');

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const wasmPath = path.resolve('emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm');
const targets = [1001, 709, 4097];

function extractFunction(lines, target) {
    const marker = new RegExp(
        '^\\s*\\(func\\s+\\(;\\s*' + target + '\\s*;\\)'
    );

    const start = lines.findIndex(line => marker.test(line));

    if (start < 0) {
        return null;
    }

    let end = start + 1;

    while (end < lines.length) {
        if (/^  \((func|table|memory|global|export|start|elem|data|type|import|tag)\b/.test(lines[end])) {
            break;
        }
        end++;
    }

    return lines.slice(start, end).join('\n');
}

async function main() {
    if (!fs.existsSync(wasmPath)) {
        throw new Error(`WASM file not found: ${wasmPath}`);
    }

    const wabtFactory = require('wabt');
    const wabt = await wabtFactory();
    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

    const module = wabt.readWasm(bytes, {
    readDebugNames: true,
    features: {
        threads: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        exceptions: true,
        simd: true,
        multi_value: true,
        reference_types: true,
        tail_call: true,
        extended_const: true,
        memory64: true
    }
});

    module.generateNames();
    module.applyNames();

    const wat = module.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);

    console.log('WASM TARGET DISASSEMBLER');
    console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(`WASM bytes : ${bytes.length}`);
    console.log(`WAT lines  : ${lines.length}`);
    console.log(`Targets    : ${targets.join(', ')}`);

    for (const target of targets) {
        console.log('\n\n================================================');
        console.log(`FUNCTION ${target}`);
        console.log('================================================');

        const result = extractFunction(lines, target);

        if (result === null) {
            console.log('Function marker not found in WAT output.');
            console.log('Nearby function declarations:');

            const candidates = lines.filter(
                line => /^\s*\(func\b/.test(line)
            );

            console.log(candidates.slice(0, 5).join('\n'));
            continue;
        }

        console.log(result);
    }

    module.destroy();
}

main().catch(error => {
    console.error('ERROR:', error.message);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

FILE: scripts\disassemblePushcontRefsV22.ts
