import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function findCell(
    cell: Cell,
    targetHash: string
): Cell | null {

    const hash =
        cell.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase();

    if (hash === targetHash) {
        return cell;
    }

    for (const ref of cell.refs) {
        const found = findCell(ref, targetHash);

        if (found) {
            return found;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function printInstruction(
    instruction: any,
    index: number
) {
    const mnemonic =
        instruction.op?.definition?.mnemonic ??
        instruction.op?.definition?.opcode ??
        'UNKNOWN';

    console.log(
        `${index.toString().padStart(3, ' ')} | ` +
        `offset=${instruction.offset
            .toString()
            .padStart(4, ' ')} | ` +
        `length=${instruction.length
            .toString()
            .padStart(4, ' ')} | ` +
        `${mnemonic}`
    );
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 PUSHCONT REF DISASSEMBLER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {

        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {

            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            Buffer.from(LIBRARY_HASH_HEX, 'hex'),
        ]);

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'getLibraries() returned no Library entry'
            );
        }

        const root =
            Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(result.result[0].data)
            )[0];

        /*
         * Rebuild the complete tree through the nested
         * @ton/core used by @tact-lang/opcode.
         *
         * This avoids duplicate @ton/core runtime/type issues.
         */
        const tactCore: any =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode/node_modules/@ton/core');

        const tactOpcode: any =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode');

        const tactRoot =
            tactCore.Cell.fromBoc(
                root.toBoc()
            )[0];

        const target =
            findCell(
                tactRoot,
                TARGET_HASH_HEX
            );

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found'
            );
        }

        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(
            `Hash: ${target.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(`Bits: ${target.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`Refs: ${target.refs.length}`);

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET REFS');
        console.log('============================================================');

        for (let i = 0; i < target.refs.length; i++) {

            const ref =
                target.refs[i];

            console.log();
            console.log(`REF ${i}`);

            console.log(
                `Hash: ${ref.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Bits: ${ref.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Refs: ${ref.refs.length}`
            );
        }

        /*
         * The PUSHCONT starts at bit 120.
         *
         * Header:
         *   prefix = 8F_
         *   r      = 3
         *   xx     = 68
         *
         * Therefore:
         *   header = 16 bits
         *   code   = 544 bits
         *   refs   = 3
         *
         * Total PUSHCONT operand:
         *   560 bits + 3 refs
         */

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' PUSHCONT');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('Start offset: 120');
        console.log('Header bits: 16');
        console.log('Continuation bits: 544');
        console.log('Continuation refs: 3');
        console.log('Total: 560 bits + 3 refs');

        /*
         * Decode the PUSHCONT itself.
         */

        const pushcont =
            tactOpcode.disassemble({
                source: target,
                offset: {
                    bits: 120,
                    refs: 0,
                },
                limit: {
                    bits: 560,
                    refs: 3,
                },
            });

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `PUSHCONT decode count: ${pushcont.length}`
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < pushcont.length; i++) {
            printInstruction(
                pushcont[i],
                i + 1
            );
        }

        /*
         * Now independently disassemble each referenced Cell.
         */

        for (let i = 0; i < target.refs.length; i++) {

            const ref =
                target.refs[i];

            console.log();
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(` REF ${i} DISASSEMBLY`);
            console.log('============================================================');

            console.log(
                `Hash: ${ref.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Bits: ${ref.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Refs: ${ref.refs.length}`
            );

            console.log();

            try {

                const instructions =
                    tactOpcode.disassemble({
                        source: ref,
                        offset: {
                            bits: 0,
                            refs: 0,
                        },
                        limit: {
                            bits: ref.bits.length,
                            refs: ref.refs.length,
                        },
                    });

                console.log(
                    `Instructions: ${instructions.length}`
                );

                for (
                    let j = 0;
                    j < instructions.length;
                    j++
                ) {
                    printInstruction(
                        instructions[j],
                        j + 1
                    );
                }

            } catch (err: any) {

                console.log(
                    'DISASSEMBLY FAILED'
                );

                console.log(
                    `Error: ${err?.message ?? String(err)}`
                );
            }
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');

    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

FILE: scripts\disassembleTargetLibraryCellV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function findCell(
    cell: Cell,
    targetHash: string,
    path = 'root'
): { cell: Cell; path: string } | null {
    const hash = cell.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase();

    if (hash === targetHash) {
        return { cell, path };
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        const found = findCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            targetHash,
            `${path}.ref${i}`
        );

        if (found) {
            return found;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function printInstructions(instructions: any[]) {
    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DISASSEMBLY RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(`Instructions: ${instructions.length}`);
    console.log();

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const item = instructions[i];

        console.log(
            `[${i}] offset=${item.offset} length=${item.length}`
        );
        console.log(`    op: ${JSON.stringify(item.op)}`);
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 TARGET LIBRARY DISASSEMBLY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`Library: ${LIBRARY_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Target:  ${TARGET_HASH_HEX}`);
    console.log(`Server:  ${LITESERVER_HOST}`);
    console.log();

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host: LITESERVER_HOST,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(
            LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
            'base64'
        ),
    });

    try {
        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {
            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client = new LiteClient({
            engine,
        });

        const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
            LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
            'hex'
        );

        console.log('Calling getLibraries()...');

        const result = await client.getLibraries([
            libraryHash,
        ]);

        console.log(
            `Library entries: ${result.result.length}`
        );

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'getLibraries() returned no entries'
            );
        }

        const root = Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(result.result[0].data)
        )[0];

        console.log(
            `Root hash: ${root.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(`Root bits: ${root.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`Root refs: ${root.refs.length}`);

        console.log();
        console.log('Searching for Target Cell...');

        const found = findCell(
            root,
            TARGET_HASH_HEX
        );

        if (!found) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell was not found'
            );
        }

        const target = found.cell;

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' TARGET FOUND');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(`Path:  ${found.path}`);
        console.log(
            `Hash:  ${target.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );
        console.log(`Bits:  ${target.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`Refs:  ${target.refs.length}`);

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' DIRECT BIT-80 CHECK');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const slice = target.beginParse();

        slice.skip(80);

        const value = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log('Bit offset: 80');
        console.log(
            `32-bit value: 0x${value
                .toString(16)
                .toUpperCase()
                .padStart(8, '0')}`
        );
        console.log(`Decimal: ${value}`);

        if (value === 0x50c6a654) {
            console.log('MATCH: 0x50C6A654');
        } else {
            console.log('MATCH: NO');
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' REBUILD FOR @TACT-LANG/OPCODE');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const tactCore: any =
            require(
                '@tact-lang/opcode/node_modules/@ton/core'
            );

        const boc = target.toBoc();

        const rebuiltCells =
            tactCore.Cell.fromBoc(boc);

        if (!rebuiltCells || rebuiltCells.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'Nested @ton/core failed to rebuild Target Cell'
            );
        }

        const tactTarget = rebuiltCells[0];

        const rebuiltHash =
            tactTarget.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase();

        console.log(`Rebuilt hash: ${rebuiltHash}`);
        console.log(`Rebuilt bits: ${tactTarget.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`Rebuilt refs: ${tactTarget.refs.length}`);

        if (rebuiltHash !== TARGET_HASH_HEX) {
            throw new Error(
                'REBUILT HASH DOES NOT MATCH TARGET'
            );
        }

        console.log('REBUILT HASH VERIFIED');

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' DECOMPILER');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const { disassemble } =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode');

        const instructions =
            disassemble({
                source: tactTarget,
                offset: {
                    bits: 80,
                    refs: 0,
                },
                limit: {
                    bits: tactTarget.bits.length - 80,
                    refs: 0,
                },
            });

        printInstructions(instructions);

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');
    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

FILE: scripts\dump122DispatchRaw.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readFuncBody(target) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const [size, q] = u(p);
    p = q;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
      const [count, q0] = u(p);
      p = q0;

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const [bodySize, q1] = u(p);
        p = q1;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        if (83 + i === target)
          return { start: bodyStart, end: bodyEnd };

        p = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function decode(p, end) {
  const op = b[p];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.get L${x}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.set L${x}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`local.tee L${x}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`i32.const ${x} (0x${x.toString(16)})`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x28) {
    const [align,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [offset,q2] = u(q1);
    return [`i32.load align=${align} offset=${offset}`, q2];
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {
    const [align,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [offset,q2] = u(q1);
    return [`i32.store align=${align} offset=${offset}`, q2];
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const [f,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`CALL ${f}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const [type,q1] = u(p+1);
    const [table,q2] = u(q1);
    return [`CALL_INDIRECT type=${type} table=${table}`, q2];
  }

  if (op === 0x45)
    return ["i32.eqz", p+1];

  if (op === 0x47)
    return ["i32.ne", p+1];

  if (op === 0x4a)
    return ["i32.gt_s", p+1];

  if (op === 0x4b)
    return ["i32.gt_u", p+1];

  if (op === 0x4e)
    return ["i32.lt_s", p+1];

  if (op === 0x4f)
    return ["i32.lt_u", p+1];

  if (op === 0x04)
    return ["IF", p+2];

  if (op === 0x05)
    return ["ELSE", p+1];

  if (op === 0x0b)
    return ["END", p+1];

  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`BR ${x}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const [x,q] = u(p+1);
    return [`BR_IF ${x}`, q];
  }

  if (op === 0x0f)
    return ["RETURN", p+1];

  return [`opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`, p+1];
}

const body = readFuncBody(122);

if (!body)
  throw new Error("FUNC122 not found");

let p = body.start;

const [groups,q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

// Skip locals
for (let i=0; i<groups; i++) {
  const [,q1] = u(p);
  p = q1;
  p++;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 DISPATCH RAW TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

while (p < body.end) {

  const pos = p;
  const [text,q] = decode(p,body.end);

  const interesting =
    text.includes("19534c") ||
    text.includes("CALL") ||
    text.includes("4788") ||
    text.includes("i32.load") ||
    text.includes("i32.store") ||
    text.includes("BR") ||
    text === "IF" ||
    text === "ELSE";

  if (interesting) {
    console.log(
      `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(6,"0")}  ${text}`
    );
  }

  p = q;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgQABcVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAFF0h26AAECPJ8UQwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDdAEAhYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6B+o0s';

function dumpCell(label: string, c: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${label} =====`);
    console.log('BITS:', c.bits.length);
    console.log('REFS:', c.refs.length);
    console.log('BOC HEX:');

    const s = c.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            'OPCODE:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log(

            opcode
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'REMAINING BITS AFTER OPCODE:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING REFS AFTER OPCODE:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log(
            'QUERY ID:',
            queryId.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'REMAINING BITS AFTER QUERY:',
            s.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'REMAINING REFS AFTER QUERY:',
            s.remainingRefs
        );
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < c.refs.length; i++) {
        dumpCell(
            `${label} REF ${i}`,
            c.refs[i]
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dumpDataSegment669.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readInitExpr(pos: number) {
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    const end = wasm[pos++];

    return {
      kind: "i32.const",
      value: x.value >>> 0,
      next: pos,
      end,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23) {
    const x = readUleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    const end = wasm[pos++];

    return {
      kind: "global.get",
      value: x.value,
      next: pos,
      end,
    };
  }

  while (pos < wasm.length) {
    if (wasm[pos++] === 0x0b) break;
  }

  return {
    kind: "unknown",
    value: 0,
    next: pos,
    end: 0,
  };
}

function findDataSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Data section not found");
}

console.log("DUMP DATA SEGMENT #669");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const sec = findDataSection();

let p = sec.start;

const count = readUleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS = ${count.value}`);

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {
  const flags = readUleb(p);
  p = flags.next;

  if (flags.value === 0) {
    const expr = readInitExpr(p);
    p = expr.next;

    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

    if (seg === 669) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(`SEGMENT #${seg}`);
      console.log("============================================================");

      console.log(`FLAGS = ${flags.value}`);
      console.log(`OFFSET = ${hex(expr.value)}`);
      console.log(`FILE PAYLOAD = ${hex(payloadStart)}`);
      console.log(`SIZE = ${size.value}`);

      console.log("");
      console.log("OFFSET      FILE        HEX                     U32");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (let i = 0; i < size.value; i++) {
        const mem = (expr.value + i) >>> 0;
        const file = payloadStart + i;

        let u32 = "";

        if (i + 4 <= size.value) {
          const v =
            wasm[file] |
            (wasm[file + 1] << 8) |
            (wasm[file + 2] << 16) |
            (wasm[file + 3] << 24);

          u32 = hex(v >>> 0);
        }

        console.log(
          `${hex(mem)}  ${hex(file)}  ${wasm[file]
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")} ${u32}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("4-BYTE WORDS");
      console.log("============================================================");

      for (let i = 0; i + 4 <= size.value; i += 4) {
        const mem = (expr.value + i) >>> 0;
        const file = payloadStart + i;

        const v =
          wasm[file] |
          (wasm[file + 1] << 8) |
          (wasm[file + 2] << 16) |
          (wasm[file + 3] << 24);

        console.log(
          `${hex(mem)} = ${hex(v >>> 0)}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("SPECIAL TARGET");
      console.log("============================================================");

      const targetOffset = 0x0019534c - expr.value;

      if (
        targetOffset >= 0 &&
        targetOffset + 4 <= size.value
      ) {
        const file = payloadStart + targetOffset;

        const v =
          wasm[file] |
          (wasm[file + 1] << 8) |
          (wasm[file + 2] << 16) |
          (wasm[file + 3] << 24);

        console.log(
          `MEM[0x19534C] = ${hex(v >>> 0)}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
      console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

      break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  } else if (flags.value === 1) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next + size.value;
  } else if (flags.value === 2) {
    const memory = readUleb(p);
    p = memory.next;

    const expr = readInitExpr(p);
    p = expr.next;

    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next + size.value;
  } else {
    throw new Error(
      `Unsupported data segment flags ${flags.value}`
    );
  }
}

function u32(): number {

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    n += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return n;
    s += 7;
  }

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

const TARGET = 259;

    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += s.value;
}

  throw new Error("Code section not found");

q = count.next;

}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x12680;
const END = 0x12710;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p.v++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readS64LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p.v++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return result;
}

function H(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opName(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x00: return "unreachable";
        case 0x01: return "nop";
        case 0x02: return "block";
        case 0x03: return "loop";
        case 0x04: return "if";
        case 0x05: return "else";
        case 0x0b: return "end";
        case 0x0c: return "br";
        case 0x0d: return "br_if";
        case 0x0e: return "br_table";
        case 0x0f: return "return";
        case 0x10: return "call";
        case 0x11: return "call_indirect";

        case 0x1a: return "drop";

        case 0x20: return "local.get";
        case 0x21: return "local.set";
        case 0x22: return "local.tee";
        case 0x23: return "global.get";
        case 0x24: return "global.set";

        case 0x28: return "i32.load";
        case 0x29: return "i64.load";
        case 0x2a: return "f32.load";
        case 0x2b: return "f64.load";

        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";

        case 0x41: return "i32.const";
        case 0x42: return "i64.const";

        case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
        case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";

        case 0x7c: return "i64.add";
        case 0x7d: return "i64.sub";
        case 0x7e: return "i64.mul";

        case 0x83: return "i64.and";
        case 0x84: return "i64.or";
        case 0x85: return "i64.xor";
        case 0x86: return "i64.shl";
        case 0x87: return "i64.shr_s";
        case 0x88: return "i64.shr_u";

        case 0xad: return "i64.extend32_s";

        case 0x54: return "i64.lt_u";
        case 0x56: return "i64.gt_u";

        default:
            return `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 RAW OPCODE MAP V1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${H(START)} .. ${H(END)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(" RAW BYTES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let a = START; a < END; a += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(a + 16, END);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let x = a; x < e; x++) {
        bytes.push(
            wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `${H(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(" OPCODE BOUNDARIES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const p = { v: START };

while (p.v < END) {
    const pc = p.v;
    const op = wasm[p.v++];

    let operand = "";

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            operand = ` ${idx}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const idx = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            operand = ` ${idx}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const v = readS32LEB(wasm, p);
            operand = ` ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const v = readS64LEB(wasm, p);
            operand = ` ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x29:
        case 0x37: {
            const align = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const offset = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            operand =
                ` align=${align} offset=${offset}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = wasm[p.v++];

            operand =
                ` type=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const depth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            operand = ` ${depth}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const typeIndex = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
            const tableIndex = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

            operand =
                ` type=${typeIndex} table=${tableIndex}`;

            break;
        }
    }

    const bytes =
        wasm
            .subarray(pc, p.v)
            .toString("hex")
            .match(/.{1,2}/g)
            ?.join(" ") ?? "";

    console.log(
        `${H(pc)}  ${bytes.padEnd(32)}  ${opName(op)}${operand}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 446;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32(pos) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(pos) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;
    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        s += 7;
    }
    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << (s + 7));
    return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

function readS64(pos) {
    let v = 0n, s = 0n;
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (s < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                v |= (-1n) << (s + 7n);
            }
            return { value: v, next: pos };
        }
        s += 7n;
    }
}

function readSleb(pos) {
    return readS32(pos);
}

function readBlockType(pos) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    if (b === 0x40) return { value: "void", next: pos };
    if (b === 0x7f) return { value: "i32", next: pos };
    if (b === 0x7e) return { value: "i64", next: pos };
    if (b === 0x7d) return { value: "f32", next: pos };
    if (b === 0x7c) return { value: "f64", next: pos };
    if (b === 0x70) return { value: "funcref", next: pos };

    // type index
    let r = { value: b, next: pos };
    if (b & 0x80) r = readU32(pos - 1);
    return { value: `typeidx:${r.value}`, next: r.next };
}

const names = {
    0x00:"unreachable",
    0x01:"nop",
    0x02:"block",
    0x03:"loop",
    0x04:"if",
    0x05:"else",
    0x0b:"end",
    0x0c:"br",
    0x0d:"br_if",
    0x0e:"br_table",
    0x0f:"return",

    0x10:"call",
    0x11:"call_indirect",

    0x1a:"drop",
    0x1b:"select",

    0x20:"local.get",
    0x21:"local.set",
    0x22:"local.tee",

    0x23:"global.get",
    0x24:"global.set",

    0x28:"i32.load",
    0x29:"i64.load",
    0x2c:"i32.load8_s",
    0x2d:"i32.load8_u",
    0x2e:"i32.load16_s",
    0x2f:"i32.load16_u",

    0x30:"i64.load8_s",
    0x31:"i64.load8_u",
    0x32:"i64.load16_s",
    0x33:"i64.load16_u",
    0x34:"i64.load32_s",
    0x35:"i64.load32_u",

    0x36:"i32.store",
    0x37:"i64.store",
    0x38:"f32.store",
    0x39:"f64.store",

    0x3a:"i32.store8",
    0x3b:"i32.store16",
    0x3c:"i64.store8",
    0x3d:"i64.store16",
    0x3e:"i64.store32",

    0x41:"i32.const",
    0x42:"i64.const",

    0x45:"i32.eqz",
    0x46:"i32.eq",
    0x47:"i32.ne",
    0x48:"i32.lt_s",
    0x49:"i32.lt_u",
    0x4a:"i32.gt_s",
    0x4b:"i32.gt_u",
    0x4c:"i32.le_s",
    0x4d:"i32.le_u",
    0x4e:"i32.ge_s",
    0x4f:"i32.ge_u",

    0x50:"i64.eqz",
    0x51:"i64.eq",
    0x52:"i64.ne",

    0x67:"i32.clz",
    0x68:"i32.ctz",
    0x69:"i32.popcnt",
    0x6a:"i32.add",
    0x6b:"i32.sub",
    0x6c:"i32.mul",
    0x71:"i32.and",
    0x72:"i32.or",
    0x73:"i32.xor",
    0x74:"i32.shl",
    0x75:"i32.shr_s",
    0x76:"i32.shr_u",
    0x77:"i32.rotl",
    0x78:"i32.rotr",

    0x79:"i64.clz",
    0x7a:"i64.ctz",
    0x7b:"i64.popcnt",
    0x7c:"i64.add",
    0x7d:"i64.sub",
    0x7e:"i64.mul",
    0x83:"i64.and",
    0x84:"i64.or",
    0x85:"i64.xor",
    0x86:"i64.shl",
    0x87:"i64.shr_s",
    0x88:"i64.shr_u",
    0x89:"i64.rotl",
    0x8a:"i64.rotr",

    0xa7:"i32.wrap_i64",
    0xac:"i64.extend_i32_s",
    0xad:"i64.extend_i32_u"
};

function decodeInstr(pos) {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    let text = names[op] || `UNKNOWN_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const r = readBlockType(pos);
        return {start, next:r.next, text:`${text} type=${r.value}`};
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const r = readU32(pos);
        return {start,next:r.next,text:`${text} ${r.value}`};
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readU32(pos);
        pos = n.next;
        const labels = [];
        for (let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
            const r=readU32(pos);
            labels.push(r.value);
            pos=r.next;
        }
        const d=readU32(pos);
        pos=d.next;
        labels.push(`default=${d.value}`);
        return {start,next:pos,text:`br_table ${labels.join(",")}`};
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r=readU32(pos);
        return {start,next:r.next,text:`call ${r.value}`};
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a=readU32(pos);
        const b=readU32(a.next);
        return {
            start,
            next:b.next,
            text:`call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
        const r=readU32(pos);
        return {start,next:r.next,text:`${text} ${r.value}`};
    }

    if ((op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)) {
        const a=readU32(pos);
        const b=readU32(a.next);
        return {
            start,
            next:b.next,
            text:`${text} align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r=readS32(pos);
        return {start,next:r.next,text:`i32.const ${r.value}`};
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r=readS64(pos);
        return {start,next:r.next,text:`i64.const ${r.value}`};
    }

    return {start,next:pos,text};
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse sections and locate function 446.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let functionBodies = [];

while (p < buf.length) {
    const sectionStart = p;
    const id = buf[p++];

    const sz = readU32(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sz.value;

    if (id === 3) {
        const c = readU32(p);
        p = c.next;

        const typeIndices = [];
        for (let i=0;i<c.value;i++) {
            const r=readU32(p);
            typeIndices.push(r.value);
            p=r.next;
        }

        globalThis.__functionTypes = typeIndices;
    }

    if (id === 10) {
        const c = readU32(p);
        p = c.next;

        for (let i=0;i<c.value;i++) {
            const bodyStart = p;

            const bodySize = readU32(p);
            p = bodySize.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

            const localDecl = readU32(p);
            p = localDecl.next;

            let localCount = 0;
            for (let j=0;j<localDecl.value;j++) {
                const n = readU32(p);
                p = n.next;
                p++; // valtype
                localCount += n.value;
            }

            functionBodies.push({
                bodyIndex:i,
                start:bodyStart,
                instrStart:p,
                end:bodyEnd,
                locals:localCount
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

const bodyIndex = TARGET_FUNC - 83;

const f = functionBodies[bodyIndex];

if (!f) {
    console.log("FUNC 446 NOT FOUND");
    process.exit(1);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 446 EXACT DISASSEMBLY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION      : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);

console.log(`BODY END      : 0x${f.end.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`);

let q = f.instrStart;
let depth = 0;
let count = 0;

while (q < f.end) {
    const ins = decodeInstr(q);

    const marker =
        ins.text.includes("i32.const") ||
        ins.text.includes("i64.const") ||
        ins.text.includes("load") ||
        ins.text.includes("store") ||
        ins.text.includes("call") ||

        ins.text.includes("sub") ||

        ins.text.includes("xor") ||
        ins.text.includes("shl") ||

    console.log(

    );

    if (ins.text === "end") depth--;
    if (ins.text.startsWith("block") ||
        ins.text.startsWith("loop") ||
        ins.text.startsWith("if")) depth++;

    q = ins.next;
    count++;

    if (q <= ins.start) {
        console.log("DECODER STALLED");

    }

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 446;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 0;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    var bodyCount = count.value;
    break;
  }

  p += s.value;
}

let start = -1;
let end = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const bs = p;
  const be = p + s.value;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    start = bs;
    end = be;
    break;
  }

  p = be;
}

if (start < 0)
  throw new Error("FUNC 446 NOT FOUND");

  `BODY : 0x${start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("RAW BYTES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let q = start; q < end; q += 16) {

  const stop = Math.min(q + 16, end);

  let hex = "";

  for (let x = q; x < stop; x++) {
    hex += wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

  console.log(

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([3785, 3790]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(start: number, end: number) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        const bytes = [];
        const ascii = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            const b = wasm[q];
            bytes.push(b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
            ascii.push(b >= 32 && b <= 126 ? String.fromCharCode(b) : ".");
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ").padEnd(47)}  ${ascii.join("")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function count
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const fc = uleb(codeStart);
const functionCount = fc.value;

let bodyPos = fc.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION RAW DUMP");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length);
console.log("Defined functions :", functionCount);
console.log("Code start :", hex(codeStart));
console.log("Code end   :", hex(codeEnd));
console.log();

// 83 imported functions
const IMPORTS = 83;

for (let body = 0; body < functionCount; body++) {

    const bs = uleb(bodyPos);

    const bodySize = bs.value;
    const bodyStart = bs.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const globalFunction = body + IMPORTS;

    if (TARGETS.has(globalFunction)) {

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(
            `FUNCTION ${globalFunction}  BODY ${body}`
        );
        console.log("============================================================");

        console.log("body size  :", bodySize);
        console.log("body start :", hex(bodyStart));
        console.log("body end   :", hex(bodyEnd));
        console.log();

        console.log("RAW BODY:");
        dump(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

        console.log();

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Search interesting constants only.
        // No opcode decoding.
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("INTERESTING CONSTANT BYTE PATTERNS");
        console.log("============================================================");

        const targets = [
            0x0018744c,
            0x00187724,
            0x0018ab5c,
            0x0018b49c,
            0x00189dec,
            0x00189f9c,
            0x0018a8b0,
            0x0018eddc
        ];

        for (const target of targets) {

            const b0 = target & 0xff;
            const b1 = (target >>> 8) & 0xff;
            const b2 = (target >>> 16) & 0xff;
            const b3 = (target >>> 24) & 0xff;

            for (let q = bodyStart; q + 4 <= bodyEnd; q++) {

                if (
                    wasm[q] === b0 &&
                    wasm[q + 1] === b1 &&

                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(target)} @ ${hex(q)}`

    bodyPos = bodyEnd;

FILE: scripts\dumpInvokeTargetBodies.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

// Confirmed from the element table decoder.
const TARGETS = [
    295,   
    484, 
    871,
    296,
    132,
    241,
    560,
    645,
    1393,
    1746,
    1747,
    2527,
    2532,
    2533,
    2534,
    2530,
    2535,
    2536,
    2537,
    2541,
    2542
];
function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= b.length) {
            throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB");
        }

        const x = b[pos++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: v, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB too large");
    }
}

function getSections() {
    let p = 8;
    let importSection = null;
    let codeSection = null;

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p];
        const size = uleb(p + 1);
        const start = size.next;
        const end = start + size.value;

        if (end > b.length) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid section ${id} boundary`);
        }

        if (id === 2) importSection = { start, end };
        if (id === 10) codeSection = { start, end };

        p = end;
    }

    if (!importSection || !codeSection) {
        throw new Error("Import or Code section not found");
    }

    return { importSection, codeSection };
}

function countImportedFunctions(section) {
    let p = section.start;
    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    let importedFunctions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const moduleLen = uleb(p);
        p = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

        const nameLen = uleb(p);
        p = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            const typeIndex = uleb(p);
            p = typeIndex.next;
            importedFunctions++;
        } else if (kind === 0x01) {
            p++; // element type
            const flags = uleb(p);
            p = flags.next;
            const min = uleb(p);
            p = min.next;
            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x02) {
            const flags = uleb(p);
            p = flags.next;
            const min = uleb(p);
            p = min.next;
            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = uleb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x03) {
            p += 2; // global type and mutability
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    if (p !== section.end) {
        throw new Error("Import section cursor mismatch");
    }

    return importedFunctions;
}

function resolveBodies(section, importedFunctions) {
    let p = section.start;
    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const wanted = new Set(TARGETS);
    const found = new Map();

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
        const size = uleb(p);
        const start = size.next;
        const end = start + size.value;
        const globalIndex = importedFunctions + ordinal;

        if (end > section.end) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid function body at ordinal ${ordinal}`);
        }

        if (wanted.has(globalIndex)) {
            found.set(globalIndex, {
                ordinal,
                start,
                end: end - 1,
                size: size.value
            });
        }

        p = end;
    }

    if (p !== section.end) {
        throw new Error("Code section cursor mismatch");
    }

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        if (!found.has(target)) {
            throw new Error(`Function body not found: ${target}`);
        }
    }

    return found;
}

function main() {
    const { importSection, codeSection } = getSections();
    const importedFunctions = countImportedFunctions(importSection);
    const bodies = resolveBodies(codeSection, importedFunctions);

    console.log("INVOKE TARGET FUNCTION BODIES");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`WASM SIZE          : ${b.length}`);
    console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS : ${importedFunctions}`);
    console.log("");

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        const body = bodies.get(target);

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION : ${target}`);
        console.log(`CODE ORDINAL    : ${body.ordinal}`);
        console.log(`BODY SIZE       : ${body.size}`);
        console.log(
            `BODY RANGE      : 0x${body.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
            `0x${body.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log("RAW BODY:");

        for (let x = body.start; x <= body.end; x += 16) {
            const e = Math.min(x + 15, body.end);
            console.log(
                `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                [...b.slice(x, e + 1)]
                    .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                    .join(" ")
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("DONE");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (err) {
    console.error("INSPECTION FAILED");
    console.error(err);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

function dumpCell(
    cell: Cell,
    name: string,
    level: number = 0
) {
    const indent = '  '.repeat(level);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${indent}--------------------------------------------------`);
    console.log(`${indent}${name}`);
    console.log(`${indent}--------------------------------------------------`);

    console.log(
        `${indent}Bits:`,
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}Refs:`,
        cell.refs.length
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}BOC:`,
        cell.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}Cell string:`
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}${cell.toString()}`
    );

    if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            dumpCell(
                cell.refs[i],
                `${name} -> REF[${i}]`,
                level + 1
            );
        }
    }
}

function inspectRoot(
    name: string,
    body: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${name}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    const root = body.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT REMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(root.remainingBits);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT REMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(root.remainingRefs);

    if (root.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = root.loadUint(32);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOT OPCODE:');
        console.log(opcode.toString());

        console.log(
            'ROOT OPCODE HEX:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('FULL CELL TREE:');

    dumpCell(
        body,
        name,
        0
    );
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi V2.2 LIQUIDITY CELL TREE DUMPER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    // ========================================================
    // REAL POOL
    // ========================================================

    const pool =
        await router.getPool(
            routerProvider,
            {
                token0: ROOSTER,
                token1: PTON,
            }
        );

    const poolAddress = pool.address;

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    // ========================================================
    // POOL DATA
    // ========================================================

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(poolAddress);

    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    const roosterAmount =
        100n * 1_000_000_000n;

    const ptonAmount =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.reserve1 /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromRooster =
        roosterAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        ptonAmount *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const minLpOut =
        expectedLp * 95n / 100n;

    const deadline =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600;

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' PARAMETERS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(poolAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');
    console.log(roosterAmount.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(ptonAmount.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(expectedLp.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Minimum LP:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Deadline:');
    console.log(deadline);

    // ========================================================
    // ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ========================================================

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: roosterAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    // ========================================================
    // pTON TRANSACTION
    // ========================================================

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                proxyTon,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: ptonAmount,
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut,
                deadline,
            }
        );

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER transaction body is missing'
        );
    }

    if (!ptonTx.body) {
        throw new Error(
            'pTON transaction body is missing'
        );
    }

    // ========================================================
    // DESTINATIONS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DESTINATIONS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER destination:');
    console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON destination:');
    console.log(ptonTx.to.toString());

    // ========================================================
    // DUMP
    // ========================================================

    inspectRoot(
        'ROOSTER TRANSACTION BODY',
        roosterTx.body
    );

    inspectRoot(
        'pTON TRANSACTION BODY',
        ptonTx.body
    );

    // ========================================================
    // FINAL
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\dumpPushcontOperandsV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TARGET_HASH_HEX =
    'DB8B6488B1D9B9051532EE339C86B249DF8F923B6E29975E8E474009157F601A';

const LITESERVER_HOST =
    'tcp://49.12.147.32:27842';

const LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY =
    'sU7QavX2F964iI9oToP9gffQpCQIoOLppeqL/pdPvpM=';

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function findCell(
    cell: Cell,
    targetHash: string
): Cell | null {

    const hash =
        cell.hash().toString('hex').toUpperCase();

    if (hash === targetHash) {
        return cell;
    }

    for (const ref of cell.refs) {
        const found = findCell(ref, targetHash);

        if (found) {
            return found;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function operandInfo(operand: any): string {

    if (!operand) {
        return '';
    }

    const type =
        operand.type ?? 'unknown';

    const definition =
        operand.definition ?? {};

    const name =
        definition.name ??
        '';

    let value = '';

    if (operand.value !== undefined) {

        if (typeof operand.value === 'bigint') {
            value = operand.value.toString();
        } else if (
            operand.value &&
            typeof operand.value.toString === 'function'
        ) {
            try {
                value = operand.value.toString();
            } catch {
                value = String(operand.value);
            }
        } else {
            value = String(operand.value);
        }
    }

    let bitcode = '';

    if (operand.bitcode) {
        try {
            bitcode =
                operand.bitcode
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase();
        } catch {
            bitcode = '';
        }
    }

    const parts: string[] = [];

    parts.push(`type=${type}`);

    if (name) {
        parts.push(`name=${name}`);
    }

    if (value) {
        parts.push(`value=${value}`);
    }

    if (bitcode) {
        parts.push(`bitcode=${bitcode}`);
    }

    if (
        operand.offsetBits !== undefined
    ) {
        parts.push(
            `offsetBits=${operand.offsetBits}`
        );
    }

    if (
        operand.limitBits !== undefined
    ) {
        parts.push(
            `limitBits=${operand.limitBits}`
        );
    }

    if (
        operand.limitRefs !== undefined
    ) {
        parts.push(
            `limitRefs=${operand.limitRefs}`
        );
    }

    return parts.join(' | ');
}

function printInstruction(
    instruction: any,
    index: number
) {

    const mnemonic =
        instruction.op?.definition?.mnemonic ??
        'UNKNOWN';

    console.log(
        `${index.toString().padStart(3, ' ')} | ` +
        `offset=${instruction.offset
            .toString()
            .padStart(4, ' ')} | ` +
        `length=${instruction.length
            .toString()
            .padStart(4, ' ')} | ` +
        `${mnemonic}`
    );

    const operands =
        instruction.op?.operands ?? [];

    for (let i = 0; i < operands.length; i++) {

        console.log(
            `      operand[${i}] ` +
            operandInfo(operands[i])
        );
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 PUSHCONT OPERAND DUMPER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    const engine =
        new LiteSingleEngine({
            host: LITESERVER_HOST,
            publicKey: Buffer.from(
                LITESERVER_PUBLIC_KEY,
                'base64'
            ),
        });

    try {

        console.log('Waiting for ADNL READY...');

        const started = Date.now();

        while (!engine.isReady()) {

            if (Date.now() - started > 15000) {
                throw new Error(
                    'LiteServer did not become ready within 15 seconds'
                );
            }

            await sleep(250);
        }

        console.log('ADNL READY');
        console.log();

        const client =
            new LiteClient({
                engine,
            });

        const result =
            await client.getLibraries([
                Buffer.from(
                    LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
                    'hex'
                ),
            ]);

        if (result.result.length === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'getLibraries() returned no Library entry'
            );
        }

        const root =
            Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(
                    result.result[0].data
                )
            )[0];

        const tactCore: any =
            require(
                '@tact-lang/opcode/node_modules/@ton/core'
            );

        const tactOpcode: any =
            require('@tact-lang/opcode');

        const tactRoot =
            tactCore.Cell.fromBoc(
                root.toBoc()
            )[0];

        const target =
            findCell(
                tactRoot,
                TARGET_HASH_HEX
            );

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                'Target Cell not found'
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `Target: ${target.hash()
                .toString('hex')
                .toUpperCase()}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Bits: ${target.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Refs: ${target.refs.length}`
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < target.refs.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const ref =
                target.refs[i];

            console.log();
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(` REF ${i}`);
            console.log('============================================================');

            console.log(
                `Hash: ${ref.hash()
                    .toString('hex')
                    .toUpperCase()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Bits: ${ref.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Refs: ${ref.refs.length}`
            );

            console.log();

            const instructions =
                tactOpcode.disassemble({
                    source: ref,
                    offset: {
                        bits: 0,
                        refs: 0,
                    },
                    limit: {
                        bits: ref.bits.length,
                        refs: ref.refs.length,
                    },
                });

            for (
                let j = 0;
                j < instructions.length;
                j++
            ) {
                printInstruction(
                    instructions[j],
                    j + 1
                );
            }
        }

        console.log();
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log(' COMPLETE');
        console.log('========================================================================');
        console.log('No blockchain transaction was sent.');

    } finally {
        engine.close();
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dumpRefcountOwners.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const WASM_PATH = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const TARGET_BODIES = [
    111, 295, 634, 871, 1010, 1393,
    1643, 2527, 2528, 2529, 3619, 3620
];

const MATCH_OFFSETS = [
    0x00009a50,
    0x0000be57,
    0x0000d6a4,
    0x00016fcb,
    0x0002ba6f,
    0x0004576e,
    0x00074783,
    0x000c591b,
    0x000c5957,
    0x000c5990,
    0x00117648,
    0x001177e6
];

function readULEB(buf, state) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (state.pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[state.pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return value;
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 49) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function hex(n) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function parseCodeSection(buf) {
    const state = { pos: 8 };

    while (state.pos < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[state.pos++];
        const size = readULEB(buf, state);
        const start = state.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > buf.length) {
            throw new Error("Section exceeds file size");
        }

        if (id === 10) {
            const codeState = { pos: start };
            const count = readULEB(buf, codeState);
            const bodies = new Map();

            for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
                const bodySize = readULEB(buf, codeState);
                const bodyStart = codeState.pos;
                const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

                if (bodyEnd > end) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Invalid body for ordinal ${ordinal}`
                    );
                }

                const globalFunction =
                    IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal;

                bodies.set(globalFunction, {
                    globalFunction,
                    ordinal,
                    bodyStart,
                    bodyEnd,
                    bodySize
                });

                codeState.pos = bodyEnd;
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        state.pos = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function dumpBytes(buf, start, end) {
    for (let pos = start; pos < end; pos += 16) {
        const lineEnd = Math.min(pos + 16, end);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let i = pos; i < lineEnd; i++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

function main() {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);
    const bodies = parseCodeSection(buf);

    console.log("REFCOUNT OWNER BODY DUMP");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${buf.length}`);
    console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodies.size}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("A. FULL SMALL OWNER BODIES");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (const fn of TARGET_BODIES) {
        const body = bodies.get(fn);
        if (!body) continue;

        // Full bodies only for these relatively small candidates.
        if (![111, 634, 1010, 2527, 2528, 2529].includes(fn)) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `FUNC ${fn} | BODY ${hex(body.bodyStart)}..${hex(body.bodyEnd)}` +
            ` | SIZE ${body.bodySize}`
        );

        dumpBytes(buf, body.bodyStart, body.bodyEnd);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("B. WINDOWS AROUND RAW CALL MATCHES");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (const offset of MATCH_OFFSETS) {
        let owner = null;

        for (const body of bodies.values()) {
            if (offset >= body.bodyStart && offset < body.bodyEnd) {
                owner = body;
                break;
            }
        }

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `MATCH ${hex(offset)} | OWNER ${
                owner ? owner.globalFunction : "NOT FOUND"
            }`
        );

        if (!owner) continue;

        const start = Math.max(owner.bodyStart, offset - 48);
        const end = Math.min(owner.bodyEnd, offset + 64);

        console.log(
            `WINDOW ${hex(start)}..${hex(end)}` +
            ` | BODY ${hex(owner.bodyStart)}..${hex(owner.bodyEnd)}`
        );

        dumpBytes(buf, start, end);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (err) {
    console.error("FAILED:", err);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}
