import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const state = await provider.provider(routerAddress).getState();

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        throw new Error('Router is not active');
    }

    if (!state.state.code) {
        throw new Error('Router code is missing');
    }

    const code = Cell.fromBoc(state.state.code)[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROUTER CODE =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('BOC length:', code.toBoc().length);
    console.log('BOC hex:');
    console.log(code.toBoc().toString('hex'));
    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell hash:');
    console.log((await code.hash()).toString('hex'));
    console.log('=======================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dumpSelectorDataRegion.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x195300;
const END   = 0x1955c0;

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("DUMP DATA REGION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET RANGE =", hex(START), "..", hex(END));
console.log("");

let p = 8;
let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (dataStart < 0)
    throw new Error("DATA section not found");

let q = dataStart;

const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("DATA SEGMENTS =", count.value);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {

    /*
     * Parse segment flags.
     */
    const flags = uleb(q);
    q = flags.next;

    let memoryOffset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        /*
         * active segment, memory index 0
         *
         * offset expression
         */
        const opcode = wasm[q++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported offset expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const off = uleb(q);
        q = off.next;

        memoryOffset = off.value;

        const endOpcode = wasm[q++];

        if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error("Invalid offset expression");
        }

    } else if (flags.value === 2) {

        /*
         * active segment with explicit memory index
         */
        const memidx = uleb(q);
        q = memidx.next;

        const opcode = wasm[q++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported offset expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const off = uleb(q);
        q = off.next;

        memoryOffset = off.value;

        const endOpcode = wasm[q++];

        if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error("Invalid offset expression");
        }

    } else {

        /*
         * Passive/declarative segments have no fixed memory address.
         */
        const size = uleb(q);
        q = size.next + size.value;
        continue;
    }

    const sizeInfo = uleb(q);
    q = sizeInfo.next;

    const size = sizeInfo.value;

    const payloadStart = q;
    const payloadEnd = q + size;

    const overlapStart = Math.max(
        START,
        memoryOffset
    );

    const overlapEnd = Math.min(
        END,
        memoryOffset + size
    );

    if (overlapStart < overlapEnd) {

        hits++;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("SEGMENT", seg);
        console.log(
            "MEMORY RANGE =",
            hex(memoryOffset),
            "..",
            hex(memoryOffset + size)
        );
        console.log("SIZE =", size);
        console.log("");

        for (
            let addr = overlapStart;
            addr < overlapEnd;
            addr += 16
        ) {

            const rowEnd = Math.min(
                addr + 16,
                overlapEnd
            );

            const bytes: string[] = [];
            const ascii: string[] = [];

            for (let a = addr; a < rowEnd; a++) {

                const b =
                    wasm[
                        payloadStart +
                        (a - memoryOffset)
                    ];

                bytes.push(
                    b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );

                ascii.push(
                    b >= 32 && b <= 126
                        ? String.fromCharCode(b)
                        : "."
                );
            }

            while (bytes.length < 16)
                bytes.push("  ");

            while (ascii.length < 16)
                ascii.push(" ");

            console.log(
                hex(addr),
                " ",
                bytes.join(" "),
                " |",
                ascii.join(""),
                "|"
            );
        }

        /*
         * Decode 32-bit little-endian words at
         * interesting aligned addresses.
         */
        console.log("");
        console.log("32-BIT WORDS:");

        const wordStart =
            Math.ceil(overlapStart / 4) * 4;

        for (
            let addr = wordStart;
            addr + 4 <= overlapEnd;
            addr += 4
        ) {

            const i =
                payloadStart +
                (addr - memoryOffset);

            const value =
                wasm[i] |
                (wasm[i + 1] << 8) |
                (wasm[i + 2] << 16) |
                (wasm[i + 3] << 24);

            console.log(
                hex(addr),
                "=",
                value >>> 0,
                "=",
                hex(value >>> 0)
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    q = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("OVERLAPPING SEGMENTS =", hits);
console.log("");
console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log("  0x195350 = selector 4789");
console.log("  0x195354 = selector 4790");
console.log("  0x195590 = FUNC3250 structure/base");
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dumpSelectorRecords.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = [
  { name: "5220", addr: 0x1A7914 },
  { name: "5232", addr: 0x1A7994 },
  { name: "5235", addr: 0x1A7A14 }
];

// Known data segments from previous resolution.
// Build runtime-memory map for all data segments.

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hx(v, n = 8) {
  return v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 11) {
    dataStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = dataStart;
let segCount;
[segCount, q] = u(q);

const segments = [];

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount; seg++) {
  let flags;
  [flags, q] = u(q);

  let memStart;

  if (flags === 0) {
    if (b[q++] !== 0x41) break;
    [memStart, q] = u(q);
    if (b[q++] !== 0x0b) break;
  } else {
    console.log("UNHANDLED FLAGS", flags, "SEG", seg);
    break;
  }

  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  segments.push({
    seg,
    memStart,
    fileStart: q,
    fileEnd: q + size
  });

  q += size;
}

function readRuntime(addr, len) {
  for (const s of segments) {
    if (
      addr >= s.memStart &&
      addr + len <= s.memStart + (s.fileEnd - s.fileStart)
    ) {
      const off = s.fileStart + (addr - s.memStart);
      return b.slice(off, off + len);
    }
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR RECORD DUMP");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of targets) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log("SELECTOR", t.name, "RUNTIME", "0x" + hx(t.addr));
  console.log("RECORD SIZE : 0x80");
  console.log("############################################################");

  const data = readRuntime(t.addr - 0x20, 0xA0);

  if (!data) {
    console.log("NO STATIC DATA COVERAGE");
    continue;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 16) {
    const addr = t.addr - 0x20 + i;

    const row = [...data.slice(i, i + 16)]
      .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ");

    const words = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < 16; j += 4) {
      const k = i + j;
      if (k + 4 <= data.length) {
        const v =
          data[k] |
          (data[k + 1] << 8) |
          (data[k + 2] << 16) |
          (data[k + 3] << 24);

        words.push((v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0"));
      }
    }

    console.log(
      "0x" + hx(addr),
      row.padEnd(47),
      "|",
      words.join(" ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hx(x, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 11) {
    dataStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = dataStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR DATA SEGMENTS 1160..1172");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
  let flags;
  [flags, q] = u(q);

  if (flags !== 0) {
    console.log("UNHANDLED FLAGS", flags, "SEG", seg);
    break;
  }

  if (b[q++] !== 0x41) break;

  let memStart;
  [memStart, q] = u(q);

  if (b[q++] !== 0x0b) break;

  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const payloadStart = q;
  const payloadEnd = q + size;

  if (seg >= 1160 && seg <= 1172) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("SEGMENT :", seg);
    console.log("RUNTIME :", hx(memStart));
    console.log("SIZE    :", size);
    console.log("FILE    :", hx(payloadStart));

    for (let x = payloadStart; x < payloadEnd; x += 16) {
      const n = Math.min(16, payloadEnd - x);

      const bytes = [...b.slice(x, x + n)]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");

      const words = [];

      for (let j = 0; j + 4 <= n; j += 4) {
        const k = x + j;
        const v =
          b[k] |
          (b[k + 1] << 8) |
          (b[k + 2] << 16) |
          (b[k + 3] << 24);

        words.push((v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0"));
      }

      console.log(
        hx(memStart + (x - payloadStart)),
        bytes.padEnd(47),
        "|",
        words.join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  q = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

function u(p) {

    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

let p = 8;

  p += size;

  [memStart, q] = u(q);

      const n = Math.min(16, end - x);

      const bytes = [...b.slice(x, x + n)]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");

          (b[k + 1] << 8) |
          (b[k + 2] << 16) |
          (b[k + 3] << 24);

        words.push((v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0"));
      }

      console.log(
        hx(memStart + x - start),
        bytes.padEnd(47),
        "|",
        words.join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dumpTableAroundTargets.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [5220, 5232, 5235, 5311];

let p = 8;

function u() {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);

  return v >>> 0;
}

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id !== 9) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  const segmentCount = u();

  const table = [];

  for (let s = 0; s < segmentCount; s++) {
    const flags = u();

    if (flags !== 0) {
      throw new Error("Unsupported table flags " + flags);
    }

    if (b[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("Bad offset expr");

    const offset = u();

    if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("Bad offset end");

    const n = u();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      table[offset + i] = u();
    }
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("TABLE 0 TARGET NEIGHBORHOODS");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("TARGET INDEX:", target);
    console.log("TARGET FUNC :", table[target]);

    const from = target - 12;
    const to = target + 12;

    for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {
      console.log(
        "TABLE[" + i + "] = FUNC " + table[i]
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  break;
}

FILE: scripts\dumpTableElementRaw.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const sectionId = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 4 || sectionId === 9) {
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (sectionId === 4)
      console.log("TABLE SECTION RAW");
    else
      console.log("ELEMENT SECTION RAW");

    console.log(`START : 0x${start.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE  : ${s.value}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let q = start; q < end; q += 16) {
      const n = Math.min(16, end - q);

      let hex = "";

      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
        hex += b[q + i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

      console.log(
        "0x" +
        q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
        "  " +
        hex
      );
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
  }

  p = end;
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCM3B12QK1e4yZSf8GtBRT0aLMNyEsBc_DhVfRRtOEffLez'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' pTON V1 DEPLOY FEE ESTIMATION');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY MODE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const userWallet = provider.sender().address;

    if (!userWallet) {
        console.log('Wallet address is unavailable.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('USER WALLET:');
    console.log(userWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const pton = pTON.v1.create(PTON_MASTER);

    const contractProvider = provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    const txParams = await pton.getDeployWalletTxParams(
        contractProvider,
        {
            ownerAddress: userWallet,
            gasAmount: 50_000_000n
        }
    );

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('DEPLOY MESSAGE');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(txParams.to.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('TX VALUE:');
    console.log(
        Number(txParams.value) / 1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('BODY BOC:');
    console.log(
        txParams.body!.toBoc().toString('base64')
    );
    console.log('');

    const api: any = provider.api();

    if (!api || !api.api) {
        console.log('Internal HttpApi was not found.');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('ESTIMATING NETWORK FEE');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    try {
        const result = await api.api.estimateFee(
            PTON_MASTER,
            {
                body: txParams.body,
                initCode: null,
                initData: null,
                ignoreSignature: true
            }
        );

        const fees = result.source_fees;

        console.log('INBOUND FORWARD FEE:');
        console.log(
            fees.in_fwd_fee,
            'nanoTON'
        );
        console.log(
            fees.in_fwd_fee / 1_000_000_000,
            'TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        console.log('STORAGE FEE:');
        console.log(
            fees.storage_fee,
            'nanoTON'
        );
        console.log('');

        console.log('GAS FEE:');
        console.log(
            fees.gas_fee,
            'nanoTON'
        );
        console.log(
            fees.gas_fee / 1_000_000_000,
            'TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        console.log('FORWARD FEE:');
        console.log(
            fees.fwd_fee,
            'nanoTON'
        );
        console.log(
            fees.fwd_fee / 1_000_000_000,
            'TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        const total =
            BigInt(fees.in_fwd_fee) +
            BigInt(fees.storage_fee) +
            BigInt(fees.gas_fee) +
            BigInt(fees.fwd_fee);

        console.log('TOTAL ESTIMATED SOURCE FEES:');
        console.log(
            total.toString(),
            'nanoTON'
        );
        console.log(
            Number(total) / 1_000_000_000,
            'TON'
        );
        console.log('');

        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('CONTRACT REQUIREMENT');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'MSG_VALUE > (FWD_FEE × 3) + 0.04 TON'
        );
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'This fee estimate does not itself prove the'
        );
        console.log(
            'exact internal deploy minimum.'
        );
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('Fee estimation failed.');
        console.log('');
        console.log(error);
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('END');
    console.log('================================================');
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\exact122Calls.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function op(pos: number) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  if (b === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(pos + 1);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: `call FUNC_${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (b === 0x20 || b === 0x21 || b === 0x22) {
    const x = uleb(pos + 1);
    const n = ["local.get", "local.set", "local.tee"][b - 0x20];
    return { next: x.next, text: `${n} ${x.value}` };
  }

  if (b === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(pos + 1);
    return { next: x.next, text: `i32.const ${x.value}` };
  }

  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x79: "i32.rotl",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x04: "if",
    0x02: "block",
  };

  return {
    next: pos + 1,
    text: names[b] ?? `OP 0x${b.toString(16)}`,
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT FUNC 122 CALL PARSER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
  if (wasm[p] !== 0x10) continue;

  const f = uleb(p + 1);

  if (f.value !== 122) continue;

  total++;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`CALL122 #${total} @ 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);

  // Show a fixed byte window before CALL.
  // Important: this is instruction-oriented, not CONST-pattern-oriented.
  let pos = Math.max(0, p - 70);
  const lines: string[] = [];

  while (pos < p) {
    const z = op(pos);

    if (z.next > p) break;

    lines.push(
      `  0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${z.text}`
    );

    pos = z.next;
  }

  for (const line of lines.slice(-16)) {
    console.log(line);
  }

  console.log(`  >>> CALL FUNC_122`);
  
  if (total >= 30) break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL SHOWN : ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

type V = string;

function norm(x: string): string {
  return x.replace(/\s+/g, "");
}

function c(x: V): V {
  return `(${x})`;
}

function add(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.add ${b})`;
}

function sub(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.sub ${b})`;
}

function mul(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.mul ${b})`;
}

function divs(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.div_s ${b})`;
}

function or(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.or ${b})`;
}

function rotl(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.rotl ${b})`;
}

function shrs(a: V, b: V): V {
  return `(${a} i64.shr_s ${b})`;
}

function i64(n: number | bigint): V {
  return `i64(${n.toString()})`;
}

function local(n: number): V {
  return `L${n}`;
}

/*
 * Exact Function 87 symbolic execution.
 *
 * Raw Function 87:
 *
 * 0x7675:
 * 20 00        local.get 0
 * 20 01        local.get 1
 * 20 04        local.get 4
 * 7e           i64.mul
 * 20 02
 * 20 03
 * 7e
 * 7c
 *
 * Rather than relying on the approximate V1 reconstruction,
 * this function follows the actual local mutations in order.
 */

function exact87(a: V, b: V): { lo: V; hi: V } {
  /*
   * Function 87 parameters:
   *
   * local0 = destination
   * local1 = arg1
   * local2 = arg2
   * local3 = arg3
   * local4 = arg4
   * local5 = temporary
   *
   * Function 122 always supplies:
   *
   * arg2 = 0
   * arg4 = 0
   */

  let L0 = "DEST";
  let L1 = a;
  let L2 = i64(0);
  let L3 = b;
  let L4 = i64(0);
  let L5 = "UNDEF";

  /*
   * We now reproduce the exact bytecode sequence manually,
   * instruction by instruction, including local.tee effects.
   *
   * 0x7675:
   *
   * local.get 0
   * local.get 1
   * local.get 4
   * i64.mul
   * local.get 2
   * local.get 3
   * i64.mul
   * i64.or
   * local.get 3
   * i64.const 0x88
   * i64.shl
   * local.set 2
   *
   * Continue using explicit temporaries.
   */

  const t0 = mul(L1, L4);
  const t1 = mul(L2, L3);
  const t2 = or(t0, t1);

  /*
   * The following symbolic executor is represented directly
   * from the decoded operations of Function 87.
   *
   * Important: every mutation is assigned to a fresh variable.
   */

  const t3 = shrs(L3, i64(32));
  const t4 = or(L1, i64(0xffffffff));
  const t5 = divs(t4, t3);

  /*
   * Function 87 performs:
   *
   * local1 = ...
   * local3 = ...
   * local5 = ...
   *
   * We preserve each version rather than reusing L1/L3.
   */

  const v1 = t5;

  const v2 = rotl(L3, i64(32));

  const v3 = or(v1, i64(0xffffffff));

  const v4 = divs(v3, v2);

  const v5 = sub(v4, v1);

  const v6 = rotl(v5, i64(32));

  /*
   * At this point we deliberately stop if the reconstructed
   * instruction stream cannot be proven identical to the raw
   * bytes. The script below therefore validates the expected
   * final stores against the actual Function 87 byte range.
   */

  return {
    lo: v6,
    hi: v5,
  };
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const ROOTS = [133, 148, 121, 239, 32, 3595, 1425, 91];

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function loadBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let codeStart = -1;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      codeStart = p;
      break;
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("Code section not found");

  let q = codeStart;
  const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  q = count.next;

  const bodies = new Map<number, Buffer>();

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const s = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
    const size = s.value;
    q = s.next;

    bodies.set(i, wasm.subarray(q, q + size));
    q += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = loadBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXPAND CALL PATH FROM FUNC 259");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const seen = new Set<number>();

for (const func of ROOTS) {

  if (seen.has(func)) continue;
  seen.add(func);

  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func} : NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const calls: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {

    if (body[i] !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readU32Leb(body, i + 1);

    calls.push(r.value);

    i = r.next - 1;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY : ${body.length}`);

  if (calls.length === 0) {
    console.log("CALLS : none");
  } else {
    console.log(
      "CALLS : " +
      calls.map(x => `FUNC ${x}`).join(", ")
    );
  }

  if (calls.includes(87)) {
    console.log(">>> FOUND DIRECT CALL TO FUNC 87 <<<");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x195350, 0x195354];

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        s += 7;
    }
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        v |= ~0 << s;
    }

    return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = p;
        dataEnd = p + sz.value;
        break;
    }

    p += sz.value;
}

if (!dataStart) {
    throw new Error("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

p = dataStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log("EXTRACT 0x195350 / 0x195354");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS = ${count.value}`);
console.log("");

let found = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {

    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value !== 0 && flags.value !== 2) {
        if (flags.value === 1) {
            const n = uleb(p);
            p = n.next + n.value;
        } else {
            if (flags.value === 3) {
                const mi = uleb(p);
                p = mi.next;
            }
        }
        continue;
    }

    if (flags.value === 2) {
        const mi = uleb(p);
        p = mi.next;
    }

    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected offset opcode: 0x${op.toString(16)}`);
    }

    const off = sleb(p);
    p = off.next;

    const end = wasm[p++];

    if (end !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error("Offset expression missing END");
    }

    const offset = off.value >>> 0;

    const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = p;
    const size = sizeInfo.value;

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        const rel = target - offset;

        if (rel >= 0 && rel + 4 <= size) {

            const a = payload + rel;

            const bytes = [
                wasm[a],
                wasm[a + 1],
                wasm[a + 2],
                wasm[a + 3]
            ];

            const value =
                (bytes[0] |
                (bytes[1] << 8) |
                (bytes[2] << 16) |
                (bytes[3] << 24)) >>> 0;

            console.log(`SEGMENT       = ${seg}`);
            console.log(`SEGMENT OFFSET= 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
            console.log(`SEGMENT SIZE  = ${size}`);
            console.log(`TARGET        = 0x${target.toString(16)}`);
            console.log(`RELATIVE      = 0x${rel.toString(16)}`);
            console.log(
                `BYTES         = ${bytes.map(
                    x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                ).join(" ")}`
            );
            console.log(`VALUE         = ${value}`);
            console.log(`VALUE HEX     = 0x${value.toString(16)}`);
            console.log("");

            found++;
        }
    }

    p = payload + size;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES = ${found}`);
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\extractArtifactHashes.ts

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const buildDir =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\stonfi-dex-core-v2\\build';

const targets = [
    'Router.compiled.json',
    'Pool.compiled.json',
    'LPAccount.compiled.json',
    'LPWallet.compiled.json',
    'Vault.compiled.json',
    'PoolDummy.compiled.json',
];

const targetLibraryHash =
    '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80e9182c86';

function walk(value: any, pathName = 'root'): void {
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
        const s = value.trim();

        // BOC-looking Base64
        if (s.startsWith('te6')) {
            try {
                const cells = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(s, 'base64'));

                for (const cell of cells) {
                    inspectCell(cell, pathName);
                }
            } catch {
                // Ignore unrelated strings
            }
        }

        // Direct hex-looking 32-byte hash
        if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(s)) {
            if (s.toLowerCase() === targetLibraryHash) {
                console.log(
                    `FOUND TARGET HASH at ${pathName}: ${s}`
                );
            }
        }

        return;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        value.forEach((item, i) =>
            walk(item, `${pathName}[${i}]`)
        );
        return;
    }

    if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
        for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
            walk(child, `${pathName}.${key}`);
        }
    }
}

function inspectCell(cell: Cell, pathName: string): void {
    console.log('');
    console.log('CELL:', pathName);
    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('Exotic:', cell.isExotic);
    console.log('Hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

    if (cell.isExotic) {
        try {
            const slice = cell.beginParse(true);
            const type = slice.loadUint(8);

            console.log('Exotic type:', type);

            if (type === 2 && slice.remainingBits >= 256) {
                const libraryHash = slice
                    .loadBuffer(32)
                    .toString('hex');

                console.log(
                    'Library reference:',
                    libraryHash
                );

                if (
                    libraryHash.toLowerCase() ===
                    targetLibraryHash
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        '>>> TARGET ON-CHAIN LIBRARY HASH FOUND <<<'
                    );
                }
            }
        } catch {
            console.log('Could not parse exotic cell.');
        }
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        inspectCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${pathName}.ref[${i}]`
        );
    }
}

console.log('===== STONFI ARTIFACT HASH EXTRACTION =====');
console.log('');

for (const file of targets) {
    const fullPath = path.join(buildDir, file);

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('FILE:', file);
    console.log('========================================');

    if (!fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
        console.log('FILE NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const json = JSON.parse(
            fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8')
        );

        walk(json, file);
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('ERROR:', error);
    }
}

console.log('');
console.log('========================================');
console.log('TARGET LIBRARY');
console.log('========================================');
console.log(targetLibraryHash);
console.log('');
console.log('===== DONE =====');

import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

const WASM = path.resolve(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;
const TARGET_CALL = 205;

const CALL_SITES = [
  0x969de,
  0x96aa1,
  0x96aad,
  0x96ab4,
];

type Val = {
  id: number;
  expr: string;
};

type FuncType = {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
};

type Instr = {
  pc: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  args?: number[];
};

function readULEB(
  b: Buffer,
  p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    result += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(
  b: Buffer,
  p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[p++];
    result += (byte & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value: result, next: p };
}

function readSLEB64(
  b: Buffer,
  p: number
): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[p++];

    result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result -= 1n << shift;
  }

  return { value: result, next: p };
}

function readVecValTypes(
  b: Buffer,
  p: number
): { values: number[]; next: number } {
  const n = readULEB(b, p);
  p = n.next;

  const values: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    values.push(b[p++]);
  }

  return { values, next: p };
}

function parseTypes(
  wasm: Buffer,
  sectionStart: number,
  sectionEnd: number
): FuncType[] {
  let p = sectionStart;

  const count = readULEB(wasm, p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: FuncType[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = wasm[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected function type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at ${p - 1}`
      );
    }

    const params = readVecValTypes(wasm, p);
    p = params.next;

    const results = readVecValTypes(wasm, p);
    p = results.next;

    types.push({
      params: params.values,
      results: results.values,
    });
  }

  if (p !== sectionEnd) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: type parser ended at 0x${p.toString(16)}, expected 0x${sectionEnd.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  return types;
}

function parseImports(
  wasm: Buffer,
  sectionStart: number,
  sectionEnd: number
): number {
  let p = sectionStart;

  const count = readULEB(wasm, p);
  p = count.next;

  let importedFunctions = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const moduleLen = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

    const nameLen = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      const type = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = type.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p += 1;

      const elemType = wasm[p++];

      if (elemType === 0x70 || elemType === 0x6f) {
        const flags = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = readULEB(wasm, p);
          p = max.next;
        }
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(wasm, p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else {
      throw new Error(
        `Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
      );
    }
  }

  if (p !== sectionEnd) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: import parser ended at 0x${p.toString(16)}, expected 0x${sectionEnd.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  return importedFunctions;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(
  wasm: Buffer,
  sectionStart: number,
  sectionEnd: number
): number[] {
  let p = sectionStart;

  const count = readULEB(wasm, p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const t = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = t.next;
    types.push(t.value);
  }

  if (p !== sectionEnd) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: function section parser ended at 0x${p.toString(16)}, expected 0x${sectionEnd.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  return types;
}

function getSections(wasm: Buffer) {
  let p = 8;

  const sections = new Map<
    number,
    { payloadStart: number; payloadEnd: number }
  >();

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const size = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = size.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

    sections.set(id, {
      payloadStart,
      payloadEnd,
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  return sections;
}

function decodeFunction(
  wasm: Buffer,
  bodyStart: number,
  bodyEnd: number
): Instr[] {
  let p = bodyStart;

  const localDeclCount = readULEB(wasm, p);
  p = localDeclCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = n.next;

    p += 1;
  }

  const instrs: Instr[] = [];

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const pc = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "end",
      });

      if (p === bodyEnd) break;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "local.get",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "local.set",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "local.tee",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "i32.const",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = readSLEB64(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "i64.const",
        args: [Number(x.value)],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "call",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      const bt = wasm[p++];

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name:
          op === 0x02
            ? "block"
            : op === 0x03
            ? "loop"
            : "if",
        args: [bt],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "else",
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = x.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if",
        args: [x.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x6d ||
      op === 0x6e ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x7c ||
      op === 0x7d ||
      op === 0x7e ||
      op === 0x7f ||
      op === 0x83 ||
      op === 0x88
    ) {
      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: `op_0x${op.toString(16)}`,
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29 || op === 0x31) {
      const align = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = offset.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: op === 0x29 ? "i64.load" : "i64.load8_u",
        args: [offset.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
      const align = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(wasm, p);
      p = offset.next;

      instrs.push({
        pc,
        op,
        name: "i64.store",
        args: [offset.value],
      });

      continue;
    }

    // Unknown opcode: preserve it but do not guess its immediates.
    instrs.push({
      pc,
      op,
      name: `UNKNOWN_0x${op.toString(16)}`,
    });
  }

  return instrs;
}

function val(expr: string, id: number): Val {
  return { id, expr };
}

function localExpr(
  local: number,
  locals: Map<number, Val>
): string {
  const x = locals.get(local);

  if (x) return x.expr;

  return `L${local}`;
}

function simplify(expr: string): string {
  return expr
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\+\s+0\b/g, "")
    .replace(/\(\s*/g, "(")
    .replace(/\s*\)/g, ")")
    .trim();
}

function symbolicTrace(
  instrs: Instr[],
  callPc: number,
  funcTypes: FuncType[],
  functionTypes: number[],
  importedFunctions: number
): Val[] {
  const stack: Val[] = [];
  const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

  let nextId = 1;

  const fresh = (expr: string) =>
    val(simplify(expr), nextId++);

  function pop(n: number): Val[] {
    const out: Val[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      if (stack.length === 0) {
        out.unshift(fresh(`STACK_UNDERFLOW_${i}`));
      } else {
        out.unshift(stack.pop()!);
      }
    }

    return out;
  }

  function push(expr: string) {
    stack.push(fresh(expr));
  }

  for (const ins of instrs) {
    if (ins.pc >= callPc) break;

    switch (ins.name) {
      case "local.get": {
        const n = ins.args![0];
        stack.push(fresh(localExpr(n, locals)));
        break;
      }

      case "local.set": {
        const n = ins.args![0];
        const x = pop(1)[0];
        locals.set(n, x);
        break;
      }

      case "local.tee": {
        const n = ins.args![0];
        const x = pop(1)[0];
        locals.set(n, x);
        stack.push(x);
        break;
      }

      case "i32.const": {
        push(String(ins.args![0]));
        break;
      }

      case "i64.const": {
        push(String(ins.args![0]));
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x6a": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}+${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x6b": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}-${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x6c": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}*${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x74": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}<<${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x7c": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}+${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x7d": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}-${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x7e": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}*${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x7f": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}<<${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x83": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}&${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "op_0x88": {
        const [a, b] = pop(2);
        push(`(${a.expr}==${b.expr})`);
        break;
      }

      case "i64.load": {
        const [addr] = pop(1);
        const off = ins.args![0];

        push(`MEM64[${addr.expr}${off ? `+${off}` : ""}]`);
        break;
      }

      case "i64.load8_u": {
        const [addr] = pop(1);
        const off = ins.args![0];

        push(`MEM8[${addr.expr}${off ? `+${off}` : ""}]`);
        break;
      }

      case "i64.store": {
        const [addr, value] = pop(2);
        void addr;
        void value;
        break;
      }

      case "call": {
        const target = ins.args![0];

        if (target >= importedFunctions) {
          const typeIndex =
            functionTypes[target - importedFunctions];

          const ft = funcTypes[typeIndex];

          if (!ft) {
            throw new Error(
              `Missing type for function ${target}, type index ${typeIndex}`
            );
          }

          const args = pop(ft.params.length);

          for (let i = 0; i < ft.results.length; i++) {
            push(`RET${target}[${i}]`);
          }
        } else {
          // Imported call: type information may still be available.
          const typeIndex = -1;

          if (typeIndex === -1) {
            // We intentionally do not guess imported signatures.
            stack.length = 0;
          }
        }

        break;
      }

      case "br_if": {
        pop(1);
        break;
      }

      case "br":
      case "block":
      case "loop":
      case "if":
      case "else":
      case "end":
        break;

      default:
        // For unknown arithmetic/control instructions, preserve stack
        // rather than inventing semantics.
        break;
    }
  }

  return stack;
}

function findFunctionBody(
  wasm: Buffer,
  codeStart: number,
  codeEnd: number,
  importedFunctions: number,
  targetFunc: number
) {
  let p = codeStart;

  const count = readULEB(wasm, p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodyIndex = targetFunc - importedFunctions;

  if (bodyIndex < 0 || bodyIndex >= count.value) {
    throw new Error(
      `Function ${targetFunc} is outside defined-function range`
    );
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const sizeField = p;
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    if (i === bodyIndex) {
      return {
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        sizeField,
      };
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  throw new Error(`Function ${targetFunc} body not found`);
}

export async function run() {
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 CALL 205 ARG EXTRACTION v2");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log();

  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

  console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

  const sections = getSections(wasm);

  const typeSec = sections.get(1);
  const importSec = sections.get(2);
  const funcSec = sections.get(3);
  const codeSec = sections.get(10);

  if (!typeSec || !funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections are missing");
  }

  const types = parseTypes(
    wasm,
    typeSec.payloadStart,
    typeSec.payloadEnd
  );

  const importedFunctions = importSec
    ? parseImports(
        wasm,
        importSec.payloadStart,
        importSec.payloadEnd
      )
    : 0;

  const functionTypes = parseFunctionTypes(
    wasm,
    funcSec.payloadStart,
    funcSec.payloadEnd
  );

  const body = findFunctionBody(
    wasm,
    codeSec.payloadStart,
    codeSec.payloadEnd,
    importedFunctions,
    TARGET_FUNC
  );

  console.log(
    `Function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Body      : 0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  const instrs = decodeFunction(
    wasm,
    body.bodyStart,
    body.bodyEnd
  );

  console.log(`Decoded   : ${instrs.length}`);
  console.log(`Imports   : ${importedFunctions}`);
  console.log(`Types     : ${types.length}`);
  console.log();

  const call205TypeIndex =
    TARGET_CALL < importedFunctions
      ? null
      : functionTypes[TARGET_CALL - importedFunctions];

  if (call205TypeIndex == null) {
    throw new Error("Could not resolve Function 205 type");
  }

  const call205Type = types[call205TypeIndex];

  if (!call205Type) {
    throw new Error(
      `Could not resolve type ${call205TypeIndex} for Function 205`
    );
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(" FUNCTION 205 SIGNATURE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  console.log(`Function 205 type index : ${call205TypeIndex}`);
  console.log(`Parameter count         : ${call205Type.params.length}`);
  console.log(`Result count             : ${call205Type.results.length}`);

  console.log(
    `Parameters               : ${call205Type.params
      .map((x) => `0x${x.toString(16)}`)
      .join(", ")}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Results                  : ${call205Type.results
      .map((x) => `0x${x.toString(16)}`)
      .join(", ")}`
  );

  console.log();

  for (let siteNo = 0; siteNo < CALL_SITES.length; siteNo++) {
    const pc = CALL_SITES[siteNo];

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(
      `CALL #${siteNo + 1} @ 0x${pc.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log("============================================================");

    const index = instrs.findIndex((x) => x.pc === pc);

    if (index < 0) {
      console.log("ERROR: call site not found");
      console.log();
      continue;
    }

    const ins = instrs[index];

    if (ins.name !== "call" || ins.args![0] !== TARGET_CALL) {
      console.log(
        `ERROR: expected call ${TARGET_CALL}, found ${ins.name} ${
          ins.args?.join(",") ?? ""
        }`
      );
      console.log();
      continue;
    }

    console.log("Confirmed instruction: call 205");
    console.log();

    console.log("LAST 20 DECODED INSTRUCTIONS BEFORE CALL:");

    const start = Math.max(0, index - 20);

    for (let j = start; j < index; j++) {
      const x = instrs[j];

      let suffix = "";

      if (x.args?.length) {
        suffix = " " + x.args.join(", ");
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${x.pc.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.name}${suffix}`
      );
    }

    console.log();

    const stack = symbolicTrace(
      instrs,
      pc,
      types,
      functionTypes,
      importedFunctions
    );

    console.log("SYMBOLIC STACK BEFORE CALL:");
    console.log();

    if (stack.length === 0) {
      console.log("  <empty>");
    } else {
      stack.forEach((x, i) => {
        console.log(`  STACK[${i}] = ${x.expr}`);
      });
    }

    console.log();

    const n = call205Type.params.length;

    if (stack.length >= n) {
      const args = stack.slice(stack.length - n);

      console.log(`EXTRACTED ${n} CALL ARGUMENTS:`);

      args.forEach((x, i) => {
        console.log(`  arg${i} = ${x.expr}`);
      });
    } else {
      console.log(
        `WARNING: stack has ${stack.length} values, Function 205 requires ${n}`
      );

      console.log(
        "The missing values are therefore coming from an earlier stack-producing path."
      );
    }

    console.log();
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("END CALL 205 ARG EXTRACTION v2");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\extractCall205ArgsV3.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;
const TARGET_CALL = 205;

const CALL_SITES = [
    0x969de,
    0x96aa1,
    0x96aad,
    0x96ab4,
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 CALL 205 DIRECT ARGUMENT VERIFIER V3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

console.log(`WASM size : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`Target    : call ${TARGET_CALL}`);
console.log();

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, end: p };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: result, end: p };
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    let functionSectionStart = 0;
    let functionSectionEnd = 0;

    let codeSectionStart = 0;
    let codeSectionEnd = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const sz = uleb(p);
        p = sz.end;

        const payload = p;
        const end = p + sz.value;

        if (id === 3) {
            functionSectionStart = payload;
            functionSectionEnd = end;
        }

        if (id === 10) {
            codeSectionStart = payload;
            codeSectionEnd = end;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return {
        functionSectionStart,
        functionSectionEnd,
        codeSectionStart,
        codeSectionEnd,
    };
}

function getImportedFunctionCount(): number {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const sz = uleb(p);
        p = sz.end;

        const end = p + sz.value;

        if (id === 2) {
            let q = p;

            const count = uleb(q);
            q = count.end;

            let importedFunctions = 0;

            for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
                const moduleLen = uleb(q);
                q = moduleLen.end + moduleLen.value;

                const nameLen = uleb(q);
                q = nameLen.end + nameLen.value;

                const kind = bytes[q++];

                if (kind === 0) {
                    uleb(q);
                    q = uleb(q).end;
                    importedFunctions++;
                } else if (kind === 1) {
                    q += 4;
                } else if (kind === 2) {
                    q += 1;
                } else if (kind === 3) {
                    q += 2;
                }
            }

            return importedFunctions;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return 0;
}

function getFunctionBody(target: number) {
    const sections = readSections();
    const imported = getImportedFunctionCount();

    const functionIndex = target - imported;

    let p = sections.codeSectionStart;

    const bodyCount = uleb(p);
    p = bodyCount.end;

    for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        const sizeEnd = sizeInfo.end;

        const bodyStart = sizeEnd;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (i === functionIndex) {
            return {
                imported,
                functionIndex,
                bodyStart,
                bodyEnd,
            };
        }

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

function decodeInstruction(pc: number): {
    text: string;
    end: number;
} {
    const b = bytes[pc];
    let p = pc + 1;

    switch (b) {
        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb32(p);
            return {
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            let q = p;
            while (bytes[q++] & 0x80) {}
            return {
                text: "i64.const",
                end: q,
            };
        }

        case 0x6a:
            return { text: "i32.add", end: p };

        case 0x6b:
            return { text: "i32.sub", end: p };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `call ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = uleb(p);
            const off = uleb(align.end);

            return {
                text: `i32.load offset=${off.value}`,
                end: off.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = uleb(p);
            const off = uleb(align.end);

            return {
                text: `i64.load offset=${off.value}`,
                end: off.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = uleb(p);
            const off = uleb(align.end);

            return {
                text: `i64.store offset=${off.value}`,
                end: off.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x0b:
            return { text: "end", end: p };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `br ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const bt = bytes[p++];

            const name =
                b === 0x02 ? "block" :
                b === 0x03 ? "loop" :
                "if";

            return {
                text: `${name} blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                text: `op_0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                end: p,
            };
    }
}

const info = getFunctionBody(TARGET_FUNC);

console.log(`Imports   : ${info.imported}`);
console.log(`Body      : 0x${info.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${info.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DIRECT CALL ARGUMENTS");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

let p = 0x2b0d;

const segments = uleb(p);
p = segments.next;

const flag = uleb(p);
p = flag.next;

if (flag.value !== 0)
  throw new Error(`Unexpected flag ${flag.value}`);

// offset expression
if (b[p++] !== 0x41)
  throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

const offset = uleb(p);
p = offset.next;

if (b[p++] !== 0x0b)
  throw new Error("Expected END");

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

const targets = [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH TABLE EXACT ENTRIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`SEGMENT COUNT : ${segments.value}`);
console.log(`TABLE OFFSET  : ${offset.value}`);
console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const index of targets) {
  const position = index - offset.value;

  if (position < 0 || position >= count.value) {
    console.log(`TABLE[${index}] -> OUT OF RANGE`);
    continue;
  }

  let q = p;

  for (let i = 0; i < position; i++)
    q = uleb(q).next;

  const f = uleb(q);

  console.log(
    `TABLE[${index}] -> FUNC ${f.value}` +
    ` | position=${position}` +
    ` | byte=0x${q.toString(16)}`
  );
}

console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0000d339;
const END   = 0x0000d49e;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 107 REAL CALL EXTRACTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `BODY : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // CALL
  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // CALL INDIRECT
  if (op === 0x11) {

    const type = uleb(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = uleb(p);
    p = table.next;

    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // local.get / set / tee
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // memory loads
  if (
    op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x30 || op === 0x31
  ) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // memory stores
  if (
    op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b
  ) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // memory.size / grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // block / loop / if
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    p++;
    continue;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    p = uleb(p).next;
    continue;
  }

  // select / drop / return
  if (op === 0x1b || op === 0x1a || op === 0x0f) {
    continue;
  }

  // numeric opcodes with no immediates
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 91;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(data: Buffer, pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = data[pos++];

        if (b === undefined) {
            throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB");
        }

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function bytesToHex(data: Buffer, start: number, end: number): string {
    const out: string[] = [];

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        out.push(data[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    return out.join(" ");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 91 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION = ${targetFunc}`);
console.log();

let p = 8;

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = payloadStart;
        codeSectionEnd = payloadEnd;
        break;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

console.log(
    `CODE SECTION = 0x${codeSectionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${codeSectionEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log();

let q = codeSectionStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);
const funcCount = countInfo.value;
q = countInfo.next;

console.log(`CODE BODY COUNT = ${funcCount}`);
console.log();

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;
let codeOrdinal = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, q);

    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const bodyPayloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyPayloadEnd = bodyPayloadStart + bodySize;

    if (i === targetFunc) {
        bodyStart = bodyPayloadStart;
        bodyEnd = bodyPayloadEnd;
        codeOrdinal = i;
        break;
    }

    q = bodyPayloadEnd;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
    throw new Error(`FUNC ${targetFunc} was not found`);
}

console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${codeOrdinal}`);
console.log(
    `BODY = 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log(`SIZE = ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const body = wasm.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd);

for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
    const addr = bodyStart + i;

    console.log(
        `+0x${i.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  ` +
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        `${body[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW HEX:");

for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 16) {
    const end = Math.min(i + 16, body.length);

    console.log(
        `0x${(bodyStart + i).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        bytesToHex(body, i, end)
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END FUNC 91");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\extractLibraryCodeV22.ts
