import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { LiteClient, LiteSingleEngine } from 'ton-lite-client';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LIBRARY_HASH_HEX =
    'C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TESTNET_CONFIG_URL =
    'https://ton-blockchain.github.io/testnet-global.config.json';

function intToIP(ip: number): string {
    const n = ip >>> 0;

    return [
        (n >>> 24) & 255,
        (n >>> 16) & 255,
        (n >>> 8) & 255,
        n & 255,
    ].join('.');
}

async function getTestnetConfig(): Promise<any> {
    const response = await fetch(TESTNET_CONFIG_URL);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Cannot download Testnet config: HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    return await response.json();
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function waitForEngine(
    engine: LiteSingleEngine,
    timeoutMs: number
): Promise<void> {
    const started = Date.now();

    while (Date.now() - started < timeoutMs) {
        if (engine.isReady()) {
            return;
        }

        if (engine.isClosed()) {
            await sleep(250);
            continue;
        }

        await sleep(250);
    }

    throw new Error(
        `LiteSingleEngine did not become ready within ${timeoutMs} ms`
    );
}

function collectCells(
    root: Cell
): Cell[] {
    const result: Cell[] = [];
    const seen = new Set<string>();

    function visit(cell: Cell) {
        const hash = cell.hash().toString('hex');

        if (seen.has(hash)) {
            return;
        }

        seen.add(hash);
        result.push(cell);

        for (const ref of cell.refs) {
            visit(ref);
        }
    }

    visit(root);

    return result;
}

function getCellBitsHex(cell: Cell): string {
    const bits = cell.bits;

    if (bits.length === 0) {
        return '';
    }

    const byteLength = Math.floor(bits.length / 8);

    if (byteLength === 0) {
        return '';
    }

    try {
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        return slice
            .loadBuffer(byteLength)
            .toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return '(not byte-readable)';
    }
}

function scoreCell(cell: Cell): number {
    let score = 0;

    /*
     * Large cells are more likely to contain TVM code/data.
     */
    if (cell.bits.length >= 256) {
        score += 1;
    }

    if (cell.bits.length >= 512) {
        score += 1;
    }

    if (cell.bits.length >= 900) {
        score += 1;
    }

    /*
     * A Cell with references can represent a continuation
     * of TVM code/data.
     */
    if (cell.refs.length > 0) {
        score += 1;
    }

    /*
     * Byte-aligned cells are easier to inspect.
     */
    if (cell.bits.length % 8 === 0) {
        score += 1;
    }

    return score;
}

async function resolveLibrary(): Promise<Cell> {
    const config = await getTestnetConfig();

    if (
        !config.liteservers ||
        config.liteservers.length === 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'No Testnet LiteServers found'
        );
    }

    const libraryHash = Buffer.from(
        LIBRARY_HASH_HEX,
        'hex'
    );

    let lastError: unknown = null;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < config.liteservers.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const server = config.liteservers[i];

        const ip = intToIP(server.ip);
        const port = server.port;

        const publicKey = Buffer.from(
            server.id.key,
            'base64'
        );

        console.log(
            `Trying LiteServer ${i + 1}/${config.liteservers.length}: ${ip}:${port}`
        );

        let engine: LiteSingleEngine | null = null;

        try {
            engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${ip}:${port}`,
                publicKey,
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            });

            await waitForEngine(
                engine,
                15000
            );

            const client = new LiteClient({
                engine,
            });

            const master =
                await client.getMasterchainInfo({
                    timeout: 10000,
                });

            console.log(
                `Masterchain seqno: ${master.last.seqno}`
            );

            const result =
                await client.getLibraries(
                    [libraryHash],
                    {
                        timeout: 15000,
                    }
                );

            if (result.result.length === 0) {
                engine.close();
                continue;
            }

            const entry = result.result[0];

            const data =
                Buffer.from(entry.data);

            console.log(
                `Library bytes: ${data.length}`
            );

            const cells =
                Cell.fromBoc(data);

            if (cells.length === 0) {
                engine.close();

                throw new Error(
                    'Library BOC returned zero root cells'
                );
            }

            const root = cells[0];

            const actualHash =
                root.hash().toString('hex');

            console.log(
                `Library hash: ${actualHash}`
            );

            if (
                actualHash.toLowerCase() !==
                LIBRARY_HASH_HEX.toLowerCase()
            ) {
                engine.close();

                throw new Error(
                    'Library hash mismatch'
                );
            }

            engine.close();

            return root;
        } catch (e) {
            lastError = e;

            console.log(
                `LiteServer failed: ${String(e)}`
            );

            if (engine) {
                try {
                    engine.close();
                } catch {
                    // ignore
                }
            }
        }
    }

    throw new Error(
        `All LiteServers failed. Last error: ${String(lastError)}`
    );
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIBRARY CODE EXTRACTOR');
    console.log(' ON-CHAIN GET_LIBRARIES');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log('');

    const root =
        await resolveLibrary();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Library resolved successfully.'
    );

    console.log(
        `Root hash: ${root.hash().toString('hex')}`
    );

    const cells =
        collectCells(root);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Unique cells in Library tree: ${cells.length}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' CELL INVENTORY');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    const ranked = cells
        .map((cell, index) => ({
            cell,
            index,
            score: scoreCell(cell),
        }))
        .sort((a, b) => {
            if (b.score !== a.score) {
                return b.score - a.score;
            }

            return (
                b.cell.bits.length -
                a.cell.bits.length
            );
        });

    for (const item of ranked) {
        const cell = item.cell;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `Cell #${item.index}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  hash  : ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  bits  : ${cell.bits.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  refs  : ${cell.refs.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  exotic: ${cell.isExotic}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  score : ${item.score}`
        );

        const hex =
            getCellBitsHex(cell);

        if (hex.length > 0) {
            console.log(
                `  bits hex: ${hex.slice(0, 160)}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' LARGE / CODE-LIKE CELLS');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    for (const item of ranked) {
        const cell = item.cell;

        if (
            cell.bits.length >= 900 ||
            (
                cell.bits.length >= 256 &&
                cell.refs.length > 0
            )
        ) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `Candidate Cell #${item.index}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  hash  : ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  bits  : ${cell.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  refs  : ${cell.refs.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  exotic: ${cell.isExotic}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  bits hex: ${getCellBitsHex(cell)}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' RESULT');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log(
        'ON-CHAIN LIBRARY RESOLVED: TRUE'
    );

    console.log(
        `UNIQUE CELLS: ${cells.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\extractLibraryHashV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAIwAIQgLJWi7SKrUW93+dSJjcRXjnLxiiRI6PaDIzSwtL9QG8eU64SPQ=';

const cell = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(BOC, 'base64'))[0];

console.log('==============================================');
console.log(' LIBRARY REFERENCE HASH EXTRACTOR v2.2');
console.log('==============================================');

console.log('bits =', cell.bits.length);
console.log('refs =', cell.refs.length);
console.log('exotic =', cell.isExotic);

// Exotic Library Reference layout:
// 8 bits  = exotic type
// 256 bits = library hash

const raw = cell.bits;

console.log('raw bits object:', raw.constructor.name);

// Convert individual bits into bytes.
function readBitsAsBuffer(bits: any, offset: number, length: number): Buffer {
    const out = Buffer.alloc(Math.ceil(length / 8));

    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        const bit = bits.at(offset + i);

        if (bit) {
            out[Math.floor(i / 8)] |= 1 << (7 - (i % 8));
        }
    }

    return out;
}

const type = readBitsAsBuffer(raw, 0, 8);
const hash = readBitsAsBuffer(raw, 8, 256);

console.log('');
console.log('EXOTIC TYPE =', type.toString('hex').toUpperCase());
console.log('HASH        =', hash.toString('hex').toUpperCase());
console.log('HASH CHARS  =', hash.toString('hex').length);
console.log('HASH BYTES  =', hash.length);
console.log('');
console.log('remaining bits =', raw.length - 264);
console.log('remaining refs =', cell.refs.length);

if (hash.length !== 32) {
    throw new Error(`INVALID HASH LENGTH: ${hash.length} bytes`);
}

console.log('');
console.log('VALID 256-BIT HASH = true');

FILE: scripts\extractPoolCallbackV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const CALLBACK_OPCODE = 0x06ecd527;

function getOpcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return 'NO_OPCODE';
        }

        return (
            '0x' +
            s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_ERROR';
    }
}

function getBodyHex(body: any): string {
    try {
        return body.toBoc().toString('hex');
    } catch {
        return 'BOC_ERROR';
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 REAL POOL CALLBACK EXTRACTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Target LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Target callback opcode:');
    console.log('0x' + CALLBACK_OPCODE.toString(16));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('FETCHING POOL TRANSACTIONS...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
            archival: true,
        },
    );

    console.log(
        `Fetched ${transactions.length} recent Pool transactions.`
    );

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

        const inMsg: any = tx.inMessage;

        if (!inMsg) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('ERROR: No incoming message.');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('INCOMING MESSAGE');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log('');
        console.log('Source:');
        console.log(
            inMsg.info?.src?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Destination:');
        console.log(
            inMsg.info?.dest?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('Opcode:');
        console.log(getOpcode(inMsg.body));

        console.log('');
        console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');
        const bocHex = getBodyHex(inMsg.body);
        console.log(bocHex);

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC HEX LENGTH:');
        console.log(bocHex.length);

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOC BYTE LENGTH:');
        console.log(bocHex === 'BOC_ERROR'
            ? 'ERROR'
            : (bocHex.length / 2).toString()
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // VERIFY OPCODE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            getOpcode(inMsg.body).toLowerCase() ===
            ('0x' + CALLBACK_OPCODE.toString(16))
        ) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('CALLBACK OPCODE VERIFIED: YES');
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('CALLBACK OPCODE VERIFIED: NO');
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // TRY REAL BOC PARSE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('REAL BOC PARSE TEST');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        try {

            const root = inMsg.body;

            console.log('');
            console.log('Root bits:');
            console.log(root.bits.length);

            console.log('');
            console.log('Root refs:');
            console.log(root.refs.length);

            console.log('');
            console.log('Root BOC Base64:');
            console.log(
                root.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('REAL BOC PARSE: SUCCESS');

        } catch (error) {

            console.log('');
            console.log('REAL BOC PARSE: FAILED');

            console.log('');
            console.log(String(error));
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('EXTRACTION COMPLETE');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        return;
    }

    if (!found) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET LT NOT FOUND IN FETCHED TRANSACTIONS.');

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'The current getTransactions result did not include LT ' +
            TARGET_LT.toString()
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\fetchAndDecodePoolCallbackV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000012';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

async function rpc(method: string, params: any) {
    const response = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            jsonrpc: '2.0',
            id: 1,
            method,
            params,
        }),
    });

    return await response.json();
}

function formatAddress(address: Address | null): string {
    if (!address) return 'null';

    return address.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

function formatAmount(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 REAL POOL CALLBACK FETCH + DECODER');
    console.log(' Target LT:', TARGET_LT);
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nFetching Pool transactions...');

    const result = await rpc('getTransactions', {
        address: POOL,
        limit: 20,
        archival: true,
    });

    if (!result.ok) {
        console.log('\nRPC ERROR:');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
        return;
    }

    const transactions = result.result;

    const tx = transactions.find(
        (t: any) =>
            t.transaction_id?.lt === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        console.log('Returned transactions:', transactions.length);

        for (const t of transactions) {
            console.log(
                'LT:',
                t.transaction_id?.lt,
                'HASH:',
                t.transaction_id?.hash
            );
        }

        return;
    }

    console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
    console.log('LT:', tx.transaction_id.lt);
    console.log('HASH:', tx.transaction_id.hash);

    const inMsg = tx.in_msg;

    console.log('\nINCOMING MESSAGE');
    console.log('Source:', inMsg.source);
    console.log('Destination:', inMsg.destination);
    console.log('Value:', inMsg.value);
    console.log('Message type:', inMsg.message_content?.type);

    const body = inMsg.message_content?.body;

    if (!body) {
        console.log('\nNO BODY FOUND IN in_msg');
        console.log(JSON.stringify(inMsg, null, 2));
        return;
    }

    console.log('\nREAL BODY FROM TONCENTER');
    console.log(body);

    console.log('\nBODY LENGTH');
    console.log('Characters:', body.length);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Detect encoding
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    let bytes: Buffer;

    const first8 = body.substring(0, 8).toLowerCase();

    console.log('\nBODY PREFIX:', first8);

    if (first8 === 'b5ee9c72') {
        console.log('Detected: HEX BOC');
        bytes = Buffer.from(body, 'hex');
    } else {
        console.log('Detected: BASE64 BOC');
        bytes = Buffer.from(body, 'base64');
    }

    console.log('Decoded bytes:', bytes.length);
    console.log(
        'Magic:',
        bytes.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex')
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Decode the REAL BOC
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(bytes);

    console.log('\nBOC ROOT COUNT:', roots.length);

    const cell = roots[0];
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('\nHEADER');
    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    if (opcode !== 0x06ecd527) {
        console.log(
            '\nWARNING: Expected 0x06ecd527 but got',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16)
        );
    }

    console.log('\nREMAINING ROOT CELL');
    console.log('Bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Print raw structure safely
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
        console.log('\nRemaining bits exist.');
    }

    if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        console.log('\nREFERENCES:');

        let i = 0;

        while (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const ref = s.loadRef();

            console.log(`\nREF [${i}]`);
            console.log('Bits:', ref.bits.length);
            console.log('Refs:', ref.refs.length);
            console.log(
                'BOC:',
                ref.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            i++;
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' FETCH + DECODE COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

const METADATA_URL =

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('METADATA URL:');
    console.log(METADATA_URL);
    console.log('');

    try {
        const response = await fetch(METADATA_URL);

        console.log('HTTP STATUS:', response.status);
        console.log('HTTP STATUS TEXT:', response.statusText);
        console.log('');

        if (!response.ok) {
            console.log('METADATA FETCH FAILED');
            console.log('');
            console.log('READ ONLY: YES');
            console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
            return;
        }

        const text = await response.text();

        console.log('RAW METADATA:');
        console.log(text);
        console.log('');

        try {
            const json = JSON.parse(text);

            console.log('==============================================================');
            console.log(' DECODED METADATA');
            console.log('==============================================================');
            console.log('');

            console.log('NAME:', json.name ?? 'N/A');
            console.log('SYMBOL:', json.symbol ?? 'N/A');
            console.log('DECIMALS:', json.decimals ?? 'N/A');
            console.log('IMAGE:', json.image ?? 'N/A');
            console.log('DESCRIPTION:', json.description ?? 'N/A');

            console.log('');

            console.log('ALL METADATA FIELDS:');
            console.log(JSON.stringify(json, null, 2));
        } catch {
            console.log('RESPONSE IS NOT VALID JSON');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log('FETCH ERROR:');
        console.log(error);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log(' TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log('==============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\filterCall96Pattern.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const pattern = [
  0x41, 0x18,
  0x10, 0x60,
  0x22, 0x00
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FILTER CALL 96 — VERIFIED PATTERN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("PATTERN : 41 18 10 60 22 00");
console.log("MEANING : i32.const 24 / call 96 / local.tee 0");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i <= buf.length - pattern.length; i++) {
  let ok = true;

  for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
    if (buf[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
      ok = false;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!ok) continue;

  hits++;

  console.log(
    `HIT #${hits} : 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL VERIFIED PATTERN HITS :", hits);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ============================================================

function format9(value: bigint): string {

    const whole =
        value / 1000000000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1000000000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : `${whole}`;
}


function pass(label: string, value: boolean) {

    console.log(
        `${value ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );
}


function warn(label: string) {

    console.log(
        `WARN  ${label}`
    );
}


function section(title: string) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ` ${title}`
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

    );


    // ========================================================
    // 6. BUILD ACTUAL SDK TX PARAMS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '6. REBUILD ACTUAL SDK TRANSACTION PARAMS'
    );


    const proxyTon =
        pTON.v2_1.create(
            PTON_MASTER
        );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // ROOSTER -> Router
    //
    // routerWalletAddress is intentionally NOT supplied.
    // SDK derives it from otherTokenAddress + Router.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress: USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,
        });


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX destination:');
    console.log(
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX value nanoTON:');
    console.log(
        roosterTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX value TON:');
    console.log(
        format9(
            BigInt(roosterTx.value)
        )
    );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // pTON / TON -> Router
    //
    // sendTokenAddress is intentionally NOT supplied.
    // SDK uses proxyTon + otherTokenAddress.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            proxyTon,
        });


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX destination:');
    console.log(
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX value nanoTON:');
    console.log(
        ptonTx.value.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX value TON:');
    console.log(
        format9(
            BigInt(ptonTx.value)
        )
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. REQUIRED TON
    // ========================================================

    section('7. REQUIRED TON');


    const roosterRequiredTon =
        BigInt(
            roosterTx.value
        );


    const ptonRequiredTon =
        BigInt(
            ptonTx.value
        );


    const totalTransactionValue =
        roosterRequiredTon +
        ptonRequiredTon;


    const recommendedMinimumBalance =
        totalTransactionValue +
        EXTRA_TON_RESERVE;


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX value:');
    console.log(
        format9(roosterRequiredTon)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX value:');
    console.log(
        format9(ptonRequiredTon)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('TOTAL TX values:');
    console.log(
        format9(totalTransactionValue)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Extra safety reserve:');
    console.log(
        format9(EXTRA_TON_RESERVE)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Recommended minimum TON balance:');
    console.log(
        format9(recommendedMinimumBalance)
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 8. TON BALANCE CHECK
    // ========================================================

    section('8. TON BALANCE CHECK');


    const enoughTonForTransactions =
        tonBalance >=
        totalTransactionValue;


    const enoughTonWithSafety =
        tonBalance >=
        recommendedMinimumBalance;


    pass(
        'TON covers both transaction values',
        enoughTonForTransactions
    );


    if (enoughTonWithSafety) {

        pass(
            'TON also covers 0.1 TON safety reserve',
            true
        );

    } else {

        warn(
            'TON does not include the full 0.1 TON safety reserve'
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 9. INDIVIDUAL TX CHECK
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '9. INDIVIDUAL TRANSACTION CHECK'
    );


    pass(
        'TON covers ROOSTER TX',
        tonBalance >= roosterRequiredTon
    );


    pass(
        'TON covers pTON TX',
        tonBalance >= ptonRequiredTon
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 10. ROOSTER INPUT
    // ========================================================

    section('10. ROOSTER INPUT CHECK');


    console.log('');
    console.log('Required ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Available ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        format9(roosterBalance)
    );


    if (
        roosterBalance >=
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    ) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('Remaining after 100 ROOSTER:');

        console.log(
            format9(
                roosterBalance -
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT
            )
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 11. pTON INPUT
    // ========================================================

    section('11. pTON INPUT CHECK');


    console.log('');
    console.log('Required pTON:');
    console.log(
        format9(PTON_AMOUNT)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON amount is:',
        '0.000249908 pTON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Native TON required by SDK for this leg:'
    );

    console.log(
        format9(ptonRequiredTon),
        'TON'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 12. DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    section('12. DEADLINE');


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Current machine time will be used when'
    );

    console.log(
        'the final transaction parameters are built.'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'The two previously decoded BOCs must not'
    );

    console.log(
        'be reused after their deadlines expire.'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 13. LP CALCULATION
    // ========================================================

    section('13. LP CALCULATION');


    const lpFromRooster =
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;


    const lpFromPton =
        PTON_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;


    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster <
        lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;


    console.log('');
    console.log('LP from ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('LP from pTON:');
    console.log(
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected LP:');
    console.log(
        expectedLp.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('minLpOut:');
    console.log(
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );


    pass(
        'minLpOut <= expected LP',
        MIN_LP_OUT <= expectedLp
    );


    const lpProtectionPercent =
        Number(MIN_LP_OUT) /
        Number(expectedLp) *
        100;


    console.log('');
    console.log('minLpOut / expected LP:');
    console.log(
        lpProtectionPercent.toFixed(2) + '%'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 14. FINAL STATUS
    // ========================================================

    section('14. FINAL READ-ONLY STATUS');


    const allCriticalChecks =
        routerVersion.major === 2 &&
        routerVersion.minor === 2 &&
        routerData.isLocked === false &&
        poolCorrect &&
        poolData.isLocked === false &&
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        ) &&
        roosterBalance >= ROOSTER_AMOUNT &&
        tonBalance >= totalTransactionValue &&
        expectedLp > 0n &&
        MIN_LP_OUT <= expectedLp;


    console.log('');


    if (allCriticalChecks) {

        console.log(
            'STATUS: READY FOR FINAL SEND REVIEW'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            'STATUS: NOT READY'
        );
    }


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('CRITICAL CHECKS');


    pass(
        'Router v2.2',
        routerVersion.major === 2 &&
        routerVersion.minor === 2
    );


    pass(
        'Router unlocked',
        routerData.isLocked === false
    );


    pass(
        'Correct Pool',
        poolCorrect
    );


    pass(
        'Pool unlocked',
        poolData.isLocked === false
    );


    pass(
        'Pool Router correct',
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        )
    );


    pass(
        'ROOSTER balance sufficient',
        roosterBalance >= ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    );


    pass(
        'TON balance covers TX values',
        tonBalance >= totalTransactionValue
    );


    pass(
        'Expected LP positive',
        expectedLp > 0n
    );


    pass(
        'minLpOut <= expected LP',
        MIN_LP_OUT <= expectedLp
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 15. SAFETY
    // ========================================================

    section('15. SAFETY CONFIRMATION');


    console.log('');
    console.log('send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED');
    console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
    console.log('Wallet transfer: NO');
    console.log('State change: NO');


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'PREFLIGHT FINISHED'
    );
}

import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";
import * as fs from "fs";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const USER_WALLET =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const EXPECTED_GAS = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS = 240000000n;

const EXPECTED_DEADLINE = 1790586870;

const SDK_BOC_FILE =
  "./sdk_generated_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

const EXACT_BOC_FILE =
  "./exact_sdk_matched_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

const EXPECTED_SDK_HASH =
  "1a1651ed7a231143443f7cb52b6170f7cebe6a6da217d5519bf2ea9b35affe79";

function hashCell(cell: Cell): string {
  return cell.hash().toString("hex");
}

function loadBocFile(path: string): Cell {
  if (!fs.existsSync(path)) {
    throw new Error(`BOC file not found: ${path}`);
  }

  const base64 =
    fs.readFileSync(path, "utf8").trim();

  return Cell.fromBoc(
    Buffer.from(base64, "base64")
  )[0];
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("=================================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 FINAL EXACT SDK PREFLIGHT");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");

  const master =
    Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

  const user =
    Address.parse(USER_WALLET);

  const router =
    Address.parse(ROUTER);

  const ptonMaster =
    Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerPtonWallet =
    Address.parse(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET);

  let allPass = true;

  // =================================================
  // 1. USER ROOSTER WALLET
  // =================================================

  console.log("\n===== 1. ROOSTER WALLET =====");

  const masterProvider =
    provider.provider(master);

  const walletResult =
    await masterProvider.get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(user)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const userJettonWallet =
    walletResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(
    "Derived user ROOSTER wallet:"
  );
  console.log(
    userJettonWallet.toString()
  );

  const expectedUserJettonWallet =
    "EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh";

  const userWalletPass =
    userJettonWallet.toString() ===
    expectedUserJettonWallet;

  console.log(
    "User ROOSTER wallet:",
    userWalletPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= userWalletPass;

  // =================================================
  // 2. pTON MASTER -> ROUTER WALLET
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 2. pTON ROUTER WALLET ====="
  );

  const ptonProvider =
    provider.provider(ptonMaster);

  const ptonResult =
    await ptonProvider.get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(router)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const derivedRouterPtonWallet =
    ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(
    "Derived router pTON wallet:"
  );
  console.log(
    derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString()
  );

  const ptonPass =
    derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString() ===
    routerPtonWallet.toString();

  console.log(
    "pTON router wallet:",
    ptonPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= ptonPass;

  // =================================================
  // 3. SDK TX PARAMETERS
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 3. SDK TX PARAMETERS ====="
  );

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(router);

  const routerProvider =
    provider.provider(router);

  const txParams =
    await routerContract
      .getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        routerProvider,
        {
          userWalletAddress: user,
          sendTokenAddress: master,
          otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
         sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
deadline: EXPECTED_DEADLINE,
gasAmount: EXPECTED_GAS,
          forwardGasAmount:
            EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS,
          transferExcessAddress: user,
        }
      );

  console.log(
    "SDK TX generated: PASS"
  );

  // =================================================
  // 4. TX DESTINATION
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 4. TX DESTINATION ====="
  );

  console.log("Expected:");
  console.log(
    userJettonWallet.toString()
  );

  console.log("Actual:");
  console.log(
    txParams.to.toString()
  );

  const destinationPass =
    txParams.to.toString() ===
    userJettonWallet.toString();

  console.log(
    "Destination:",
    destinationPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= destinationPass;

  // =================================================
  // 5. GAS
  // =================================================

  console.log("\n===== 5. GAS =====");

  const gasPass =
    BigInt(txParams.value.toString()) ===
    EXPECTED_GAS;

  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    EXPECTED_GAS.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Actual:",
    txParams.value.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Gas:",
    gasPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= gasPass;

  // =================================================
  // 6. SDK BODY
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 6. SDK BODY ====="
  );

  if (!txParams.body) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK transaction body is empty."
    );
  }

  const sdkBody =
    Cell.fromBoc(
      txParams.body.toBoc()
    )[0];

  const sdkHash =
    hashCell(sdkBody);

  const sdkBoc =
    sdkBody.toBoc();

  console.log(
    "SDK hash:"
  );
  console.log(sdkHash);

  console.log(
    "SDK bits:",
    sdkBody.bits.length
  );

  console.log(
    "SDK refs:",
    sdkBody.refs.length
  );

  console.log(
    "SDK bytes:",
    sdkBoc.length
  );

  console.log(
    "SDK chars:",
    sdkBoc.toString("base64").length
  );

  const sdkHashPass =
    sdkHash === EXPECTED_SDK_HASH;

  console.log(
    "SDK hash:",
    sdkHashPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= sdkHashPass;

  // =================================================
  // 7. SAVED SDK BOC
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 7. SAVED SDK BOC ====="
  );

  const savedSdkBody =
    loadBocFile(SDK_BOC_FILE);

  const savedSdkHash =
    hashCell(savedSdkBody);

  const savedSdkBytes =
    savedSdkBody.toBoc();

  const savedSdkPass =
    savedSdkHash === sdkHash;

  console.log(
    "Saved SDK hash:"
  );
  console.log(savedSdkHash);

  console.log(
    "SDK generated hash:"
  );
  console.log(sdkHash);

  console.log(
    "Saved SDK BOC:",
    savedSdkPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= savedSdkPass;

  // =================================================
  // 8. EXACT MANUAL BOC
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 8. EXACT MANUAL BOC ====="
  );

  const exactBody =
    loadBocFile(EXACT_BOC_FILE);

  const exactHash =
    hashCell(exactBody);

  const exactBoc =
    exactBody.toBoc();

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC hash:"
  );
  console.log(exactHash);

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC bits:",
    exactBody.bits.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC refs:",
    exactBody.refs.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC bytes:",
    exactBoc.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC chars:",
    exactBoc.toString("base64").length
  );

  const exactHashPass =
    exactHash === sdkHash;

  console.log(
    "Exact BOC == SDK:",
    exactHashPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= exactHashPass;

  // =================================================
  // 9. BYTE-FOR-BYTE COMPARISON
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 9. BYTE-FOR-BYTE ====="
  );

  const sdkBase64 =
    sdkBoc.toString("base64");

  const exactBase64 =
    exactBoc.toString("base64");

  const byteForBytePass =
    sdkBase64 === exactBase64;

  console.log(
    "SDK BOC == exact BOC:",
    byteForBytePass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= byteForBytePass;

  // =================================================
  // 10. FINAL VALUES
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 10. FINAL VALUES ====="
  );

  console.log(
    "ROOSTER amount:",
    ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "minLpOut:",
    MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Gas:",
    EXPECTED_GAS.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Forward gas:",
    EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Deadline:",
    EXPECTED_DEADLINE.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Router pTON wallet:",
    routerPtonWallet.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "User:",
    user.toString()
  );

  // =================================================
  // FINAL
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n================================================="
  );
  console.log(
    " FINAL PREFLIGHT RESULT"
  );
  console.log(
    "================================================="
  );

  if (allPass) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "STATUS: ALL CHECKS PASSED"
    );
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "ROOSTER wallet: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "pTON router wallet: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "TX destination: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "Gas = 0.30 TON: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "SDK body hash: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "Saved SDK BOC: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "Exact manual BOC: PASS"
    );
    console.log(
      "Byte-for-byte equality: PASS"
    );
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "SDK BOC and exact manual BOC are identical."
    );
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT."
    );
  } else {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "STATUS: FAIL"
    );
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT."
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "================================================="
  );
}

import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';


// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON
// FINAL LIVE BOTH-SIDES PRE-SEND VALIDATOR
//
// READ ONLY
// NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT
// NO WALLET SIGNATURE
// NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL
// ============================================================


const EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);


// ============================================================
// FINAL LIQUIDITY INPUTS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n; // 100 ROOSTER
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;            // 0.000249908 pTON
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;


// v2.2 SDK values verified from generated BOCs
const ROOSTER_EXTERNAL_VALUE = 300_000_000n;
const ROOSTER_FORWARD_TON = 235_000_000n;

const PTON_EXTERNAL_VALUE = 310_249_908n;
const PTON_FORWARD_TON = 300_000_000n;


// Local operational reserve.
// NOT sent to the protocol.
const EXTRA_TON_RESERVE = 100_000_000n;


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function pass(label: string, condition: boolean) {

    console.log(
        `${condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );

    return condition;
}


function section(title: string) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ` ${title}`
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}


function format9(value: bigint): string {

    const whole =
        value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1_000_000_000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : `${whole}`;
}


function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {

    return a.equals(b);
}

// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    let allPass = true;

    );

        ' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL LIVE BOTH-SIDES PRE-SEND'
    );

        ' READ ONLY'

    );

    // 1. WALLET
    // ========================================================

    // 2. ROUTER

        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(

            routerData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 3. CURRENT POOL
    // ========================================================

            poolCorrect
        ) && allPass;


   const poolProvider =
    provider.provider(
        pool.address
    );

const poolData =
    await pool.getPoolData(

    ); 


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool locked:');
    console.log(
        poolData.isLocked
    );

    console.log('Pool Router:');
    console.log(
        poolData.routerAddress.toString()
    );

            poolData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;

        pass(

            sameAddress(
                poolData.routerAddress,
                ROUTER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool token0 = ROOSTER',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token0WalletAddress,
                Address.parse(
                    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
                )
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool token1 = pTON',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token1WalletAddress,
                EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 4. LIVE USER ROOSTER WALLET
    // ========================================================

    section('4. LIVE ROOSTER WALLET');

    const roosterMasterProvider =
        provider.provider(
            ROOSTER_MASTER
        );


    const walletResult =
        await roosterMasterProvider.get(
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell:
                        beginCell()
                            .storeAddress(USER)
                            .endCell()
                }
            ]
        );


    const userRoosterWallet =
        walletResult.stack.readAddress();


    console.log('Derived USER ROOSTER wallet:');
    console.log(

    );

    // ========================================================
    // 5. LIVE ROOSTER BALANCE
    // ========================================================

    section('5. LIVE ROOSTER BALANCE');

    const roosterWalletProvider =

        );


    const roosterData =
        await roosterWalletProvider.get(
            'get_wallet_data',
            []
        );


    const roosterBalance =
        roosterData.stack.readBigNumber();


    console.log('Current ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        format9(roosterBalance)
    );

    console.log('Required ROOSTER:');
    console.log(
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER balance sufficient',
            roosterBalance >= ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 6. LIVE TON BALANCE
    // ========================================================

   section('6. LIVE TON BALANCE');

const userState =
    await provider
        .provider(USER)
        .getState();


const tonBalance =
    userState.balance;


console.log('Current TON nano:');
console.log(
    tonBalance.toString()
);

console.log('Current TON:');
console.log(
    format9(tonBalance)
);


    // ========================================================
    // 7. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    section('7. FRESH DEADLINE');

    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );

    const deadline =
        now + 600;


    console.log('Current Unix time:');
    console.log(now);

    console.log('Fresh deadline:');
    console.log(deadline);

    console.log('Lifetime:');
    console.log(
        `${deadline - now} seconds`
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Fresh deadline valid',
            deadline > now
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 8. FRESH ROOSTER SDK TX
    // ========================================================

    section('8. FRESH ROOSTER SDK TRANSACTION');

    const roosterTx =
        await routerContract
            .getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
                routerProvider,
                {
                    userWalletAddress: USER,
                    sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                    otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
                    sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
                    minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,

                }

    console.log(
        roosterTx.to.toString()

            BigInt(
                roosterTx.value.toString()
            )
        )

            'ROOSTER external destination',

            )
        ) && allPass;

            ) === ROOSTER_EXTERNAL_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 9. FRESH pTON SDK TX
    // ========================================================

    section('9. FRESH pTON SDK TRANSACTION');

            .getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(

                    userWalletAddress: USER,

                    sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
                    minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                    receiverAddress: USER,
                    refundAddress: USER,
                    excessesAddress: USER,
                    deadline
                }
            );


    console.log('pTON external destination:');
    console.log(
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log('pTON external value:');

            BigInt(
                ptonTx.value.toString()
            )
        )
    );

        pass(
            'pTON external destination',
            sameAddress(
                ptonTx.to,
                EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;

        pass(

            ) === PTON_EXTERNAL_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 10. TON TOTAL
    // ========================================================

    section('10. TOTAL TON REQUIREMENT');

    console.log(
        format9(EXTRA_TON_RESERVE),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('Recommended minimum:');
    console.log(
        format9(recommendedBalance),
        'TON'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'TON covers both TX values',

        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(

            tonBalance >= recommendedBalance
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 11. ROOSTER BOC VALIDATION
    // ========================================================

    section('11. FRESH ROOSTER BOC');


    if (!roosterTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER SDK body is empty.'
        );
    }


    const roosterBody =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            roosterTx.body.toBoc()
        )[0];


    const roosterSlice =
        roosterBody.beginParse();


    const roosterTransferOpcode =
        roosterSlice.loadUint(32);

    const roosterQueryId =
        roosterSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    const roosterAmount =
        roosterSlice.loadCoins();

    const roosterDestination =
        roosterSlice.loadAddress();

    const roosterResponse =
        roosterSlice.loadAddress();

    const roosterCustom =
        roosterSlice.loadMaybeRef();

    const roosterForwardTon =
        roosterSlice.loadCoins();

    const roosterForwardPresent =
        roosterSlice.loadBit();

    const roosterForwardPayload =
        roosterSlice.loadRef();


    const provideSlice =
        roosterForwardPayload.beginParse();


    const provideOpcode =
        provideSlice.loadUint(32);

    const provideRouterWallet =
        provideSlice.loadAddress();

    const provideRefund =
        provideSlice.loadAddress();

    const provideExcesses =
        provideSlice.loadAddress();

    const provideDeadline =
        provideSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    const provideInner =
        provideSlice.loadRef();


    const innerSlice =
        provideInner.beginParse();


    const minLpOut =
        innerSlice.loadCoins();

    const receiver =
        innerSlice.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive =
        innerSlice.loadUint(1);

    const customForwardGas =
        innerSlice.loadCoins();

    const customPayload =
        innerSlice.loadMaybeRef();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER transfer opcode',
            roosterTransferOpcode === 0x0f8a7ea5
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER amount = 100',
            roosterAmount === ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER destination = Router',
            sameAddress(
                roosterDestination,
                ROUTER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER response = USER',
            sameAddress(
                roosterResponse,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom payload absent',
            roosterCustom === null
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward TON = 0.235',
            roosterForwardTon === ROOSTER_FORWARD_TON
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward payload present',
            roosterForwardPresent === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            provideOpcode === 0x37c096df
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER leg router pTON wallet',
            sameAddress(
                provideRouterWallet,
                EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                provideRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                provideExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER deadline',
            provideDeadline === BigInt(deadline)
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER minLpOut',
            minLpOut === MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                receiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive = true',
            bothPositive === 1
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom forward gas = 0',
            customForwardGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom payload absent',
            customPayload === null
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 12. FRESH pTON BOC VALIDATION
    // ========================================================

    section('12. FRESH pTON BOC');


    if (!ptonTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'pTON SDK body is empty.'
        );
    }


    const ptonBody =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            ptonTx.body.toBoc()
        )[0];


    const ptonSlice =
        ptonBody.beginParse();


    const ptonOpcode =
        ptonSlice.loadUint(32);

    const ptonQueryId =
        ptonSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    const ptonAmount =
        ptonSlice.loadCoins();

    const ptonRefund =
        ptonSlice.loadAddress();

    const ptonForwardPresent =
        ptonSlice.loadBit();

    const ptonForwardPayload =
        ptonSlice.loadRef();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
            ptonOpcode === 0x01f3835d
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON query ID = 0',
            ptonQueryId === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON amount',
            ptonAmount === PTON_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON forward payload present',
            ptonForwardPresent === true
        ) && allPass;


    const ptonProvideSlice =
        ptonForwardPayload.beginParse();


    const ptonProvideOpcode =
        ptonProvideSlice.loadUint(32);

    const ptonOtherTokenWallet =
    ptonProvideSlice.loadAddress();

    const ptonProvideRefund =
        ptonProvideSlice.loadAddress();

    const ptonProvideExcesses =
        ptonProvideSlice.loadAddress();

    const ptonDeadline =
        ptonProvideSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    const ptonInner =
        ptonProvideSlice.loadRef();


    const ptonInnerSlice =
        ptonInner.beginParse();


    const ptonMinLpOut =
        ptonInnerSlice.loadCoins();

    const ptonReceiver =
        ptonInnerSlice.loadAddress();

    const ptonBothPositive =
        ptonInnerSlice.loadUint(1);

    const ptonCustomForwardGas =
        ptonInnerSlice.loadCoins();

    const ptonCustomPayload =
        ptonInnerSlice.loadMaybeRef();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            ptonProvideOpcode === 0x37c096df
        ) && allPass;


    // On the pTON leg, the SDK places the
    // ROOSTER wallet as the other-token wallet.

allPass =
    pass(
        'pTON leg Pool ROOSTER wallet',
        sameAddress(
            ptonOtherTokenWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) && allPass;
   


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonProvideRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonProvideExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON deadline',
            ptonDeadline === BigInt(deadline)
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON minLpOut',
            ptonMinLpOut === MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON bothPositive = true',
            ptonBothPositive === 1
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON custom forward gas = 0',
            ptonCustomForwardGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON custom payload absent',
            ptonCustomPayload === null
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 13. CROSS-CHECKS
    // ========================================================

    section('13. CROSS-CHECKS');


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same USER',
            sameAddress(
                USER,
                receiver
            ) &&
            sameAddress(
                USER,
                ptonReceiver
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same minLpOut',
            minLpOut ===
            ptonMinLpOut
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same deadline',
            provideDeadline ===
            ptonDeadline
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'bothPositive on both legs',
            bothPositive === 1 &&
            ptonBothPositive === 1
        ) && allPass;

    allPass =
    pass(
        'Opposite token wallets consistent',
        sameAddress(
            provideRouterWallet,
            EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
        ) &&
        sameAddress(
            ptonOtherTokenWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) && allPass;
   

    // ========================================================
    // 14. FINAL
    // ========================================================

    section('14. FINAL READ-ONLY RESULT');


    console.log('');

    if (allPass) {

        console.log(
            'STATUS: READY FOR FINAL SEND REVIEW'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            'STATUS: NOT READY'
        );
    }


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Current TON:',
        format9(tonBalance),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Required TX values:',
        format9(totalValue),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Recommended with reserve:',
        format9(recommendedBalance),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Current ROOSTER:',
        format9(roosterBalance)
    );

    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER:',
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' SAFETY CONFIRMATION'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet transfer: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'State change: NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FINAL LIVE BOTH-SIDES PREFLIGHT FINISHED'
    );
}
