import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';

const RPC_ENDPOINT = 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

// ===== TEST LIQUIDITY =====

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n; // 100 ROOSTER
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;             // 0.000249908 TON

// 95% safety threshold
const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

// Expected transaction value from verified SDK v2.2
const EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE = 610_249_908n;

function fmtTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

function fmtRooster(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac
        ? `${whole}.${frac}`
        : `${whole}`;
}

function mulDiv(
    a: bigint,
    b: bigint,
    denominator: bigint
): bigint {
    return (a * b) / denominator;
}

function readAddressFromResultItem(item: any): Address {
    if (!item || item.type !== 'slice') {
        throw new Error('Expected slice result item');
    }

    return item.cell.beginParse().loadAddress();
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL LIVE POOL PREFLIGHT');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: RPC_ENDPOINT,
    });

    console.log('\n===== CURRENT BLOCK =====');

    const lastBlock = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        'Workchain:',
        lastBlock.last.workchain
    );

    console.log(
        'Shard:',
        lastBlock.last.shard
    );

    console.log(
        'Seqno:',
        lastBlock.last.seqno
    );

    console.log('\n===== CONFIGURATION =====');

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('Pool:', POOL.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER master:', ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('pTON master:', PTON_MASTER.toString());
    console.log('User:', USER.toString());

    console.log('\n===== TEST INPUT =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtRooster(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtTon(PTON_AMOUNT),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Configured minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== LIVE POOL DATA =====');

    const poolResult = await client.runMethod(
        lastBlock.last.seqno,
        POOL,
        'get_pool_data'
    );

    if (poolResult.exitCode !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `get_pool_data failed with exit code ${poolResult.exitCode}`
        );
    }

    const result = poolResult.result;

    if (result.length !== 12) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected pool data length: ${result.length}`
        );
    }

    const totalLp = BigInt(
        (result[2] as any).value
    );

    const reserveRooster = BigInt(
        (result[3] as any).value
    );

    const reservePton = BigInt(
        (result[4] as any).value
    );

    const poolRoosterWallet =
        readAddressFromResultItem(result[5]);

    const poolPtonWallet =
        readAddressFromResultItem(result[6]);

    const lpFee = BigInt(
        (result[7] as any).value
    );

    const protocolFee = BigInt(
        (result[8] as any).value
    );

    const locked = BigInt(
        (result[10] as any).value
    );

    console.log(
        'Total LP:',
        totalLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        reserveRooster.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtRooster(reserveRooster),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        reservePton.toString(),
        '=',
        fmtTon(reservePton),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool ROOSTER wallet:',
        poolRoosterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool pTON wallet:',
        poolPtonWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP fee:',
        lpFee.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Protocol fee:',
        protocolFee.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Locked:',
        locked.toString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== WALLET CHECKS =====');

    const roosterWalletPass =
        poolRoosterWallet.equals(
            EXPECTED_ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET
        );

    const ptonWalletPass =
        poolPtonWallet.equals(
            EXPECTED_PTON_POOL_WALLET
        );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER Pool wallet:',
        roosterWalletPass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON Pool wallet:',
        ptonWalletPass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('\n===== LP CALCULATION =====');

    /*
     * For proportional liquidity:
     *
     * LP from ROOSTER =
     * ROOSTER amount * total LP / ROOSTER reserve
     *
     * LP from pTON =
     * pTON amount * total LP / pTON reserve
     *
     * The smaller value is the limiting amount.
     */

    const lpFromRooster = mulDiv(
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
        totalLp,
        reserveRooster
    );

    const lpFromPton = mulDiv(
        PTON_AMOUNT,
        totalLp,
        reservePton
    );

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Configured minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Safety ratio:',
        `${Number(MIN_LP_OUT * 10000n / expectedLp) / 100}%`
    );

    const minLpPass =
        MIN_LP_OUT <= expectedLp;

    console.log(
        'minLpOut <= expected LP:',
        minLpPass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('\n===== LIQUIDITY BALANCE CHECK =====');

    const difference =
        lpFromRooster > lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster - lpFromPton
            : lpFromPton - lpFromRooster;

    console.log(
        'LP-side difference:',
        difference.toString()
    );

    const proportionalPass =
        difference <= 1n;

    console.log(
        'Proportional amounts:',
        proportionalPass ? 'PASS' : 'CHECK'
    );

    console.log('\n===== TRANSACTION VALUE CHECK =====');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX value:',
        '0.300000000 TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX value:',
        '0.310249908 TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected total:',
        fmtTon(EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction value:',
        EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE === 610_249_908n
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('\n===== FINAL RESULT =====');

    const allPass =
        roosterWalletPass &&
        ptonWalletPass &&
        minLpPass &&
        proportionalPass;

    if (allPass) {
        console.log('POOL DATA: PASS');
        console.log('POOL WALLETS: PASS');
        console.log('LP CALCULATION: PASS');
        console.log('MIN LP OUT: PASS');
        console.log('PROPORTIONALITY: PASS');
        console.log('TRANSACTION VALUE: PASS');

        console.log('\nSTATUS: PASS');
    } else {
        console.log('STATUS: CHECK REQUIRED');
    }

    console.log('\n================================================');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log(' READ ONLY PREFLIGHT COMPLETE');
    console.log('================================================');
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\finalLivePreSendCheckV22.ts

import { Address, beginCell, Cell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const USER_WALLET =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET =
  "EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const EXPECTED_GAS = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS = 240000000n;

function hashCell(cell: Cell): string {
  return cell.hash().toString("hex");
}

function nanoToTon(value: bigint): string {
  const whole = value / 1000000000n;

  const frac =
    (value % 1000000000n)
      .toString()
      .padStart(9, "0")
      .replace(/0+$/, "");

  return frac.length
    ? `${whole}.${frac}`
    : `${whole}`;
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("=================================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 LIVE PRE-SEND CHECK");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("=================================================");

  const master =
    Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

  const user =
    Address.parse(USER_WALLET);

  const router =
    Address.parse(ROUTER);

  const ptonMaster =
    Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

  const routerPtonWallet =
    Address.parse(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET);

  let allPass = true;

  // =================================================
  // 1. CURRENT TIME / FRESH DEADLINE
  // =================================================

  console.log("\n===== 1. CURRENT TIME =====");

  const now =
    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

  const deadline =
    now + 900;

  console.log("Current Unix time:");
  console.log(now);

  console.log("Fresh SDK deadline:");
  console.log(deadline);

  console.log("Lifetime:");
  console.log(`${deadline - now} seconds`);

  const deadlinePass =
    deadline > now;

  console.log(
    "Fresh deadline:",
    deadlinePass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= deadlinePass;

  // =================================================
  // 2. ROOSTER WALLET
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 2. ROOSTER JETTON WALLET ====="
  );

  const masterProvider =
    provider.provider(master);

  const walletResult =
    await masterProvider.get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(user)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const userJettonWallet =
    walletResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(
    "Derived user ROOSTER wallet:"
  );

  console.log(
    userJettonWallet.toString()
  );

  const userWalletPass =
    userJettonWallet.toString() ===
    EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET;

  console.log(
    "ROOSTER wallet:",
    userWalletPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= userWalletPass;

  // =================================================
  // 3. pTON ROUTER WALLET
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 3. pTON ROUTER WALLET ====="
  );

  const ptonProvider =
    provider.provider(ptonMaster);

  const ptonResult =
    await ptonProvider.get(
      "get_wallet_address",
      [
        {
          type: "slice",
          cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(router)
            .endCell(),
        },
      ]
    );

  const derivedRouterPtonWallet =
    ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log(
    "Derived router pTON wallet:"
  );

  console.log(
    derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString()
  );

  const ptonPass =
    derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString() ===
    routerPtonWallet.toString();

  console.log(
    "pTON router wallet:",
    ptonPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= ptonPass;

  // =================================================
  // 4. USER ROOSTER BALANCE
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 4. USER ROOSTER BALANCE ====="
  );

  const jettonWalletProvider =
    provider.provider(userJettonWallet);

  const balanceResult =
    await jettonWalletProvider.get(
      "get_wallet_data",
      []
    );

  const balance =
    balanceResult.stack.readBigNumber();

  console.log(
    "Current ROOSTER balance:"
  );

  console.log(
    balance.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Current ROOSTER display:"
  );

  console.log(
    nanoToTon(balance)
  );

  console.log(
    "Required ROOSTER:"
  );

  console.log(
    ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
  );

  const roosterBalancePass =
    balance >= ROOSTER_AMOUNT;

  console.log(
    "ROOSTER balance:",
    roosterBalancePass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= roosterBalancePass;

  // =================================================
  // 5. FRESH SDK TRANSACTION
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 5. FRESH SDK TX PARAMETERS ====="
  );

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(router);

  const routerProvider =
    provider.provider(router);

  const txParams =
    await routerContract
      .getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
        routerProvider,
        {
          userWalletAddress: user,
          sendTokenAddress: master,
          otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
          sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
          minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
          deadline: deadline,
          gasAmount: EXPECTED_GAS,
          forwardGasAmount:
            EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS,
          transferExcessAddress: user,
        }
      );

  console.log(
    "Fresh SDK transaction: PASS"
  );

  // =================================================
  // 6. DESTINATION
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 6. DESTINATION ====="
  );

  console.log("Expected:");
  console.log(
    userJettonWallet.toString()
  );

  console.log("Actual:");
  console.log(
    txParams.to.toString()
  );

  const destinationPass =
    txParams.to.toString() ===
    userJettonWallet.toString();

  console.log(
    "Destination:",
    destinationPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= destinationPass;

  // =================================================
  // 7. GAS
  // =================================================

  console.log("\n===== 7. GAS =====");

  const actualGas =
    BigInt(txParams.value.toString());

  const gasPass =
    actualGas === EXPECTED_GAS;

  console.log(
    "Expected:",
    EXPECTED_GAS.toString(),
    `(${nanoToTon(EXPECTED_GAS)} TON)`
  );

  console.log(
    "Actual:",
    actualGas.toString(),
    `(${nanoToTon(actualGas)} TON)`
  );

  console.log(
    "Gas:",
    gasPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= gasPass;

  // =================================================
  // 8. SDK BODY
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 8. FRESH SDK BODY ====="
  );

  if (!txParams.body) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK transaction body is empty."
    );
  }

  const sdkBody =
    Cell.fromBoc(
      txParams.body.toBoc()
    )[0];

  const sdkBoc =
    sdkBody.toBoc();

  const sdkHash =
    hashCell(sdkBody);

  console.log("SDK hash:");
  console.log(sdkHash);

  console.log(
    "Bits:",
    sdkBody.bits.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Refs:",
    sdkBody.refs.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Bytes:",
    sdkBoc.length
  );

  console.log(
    "Base64 chars:",
    sdkBoc.toString("base64").length
  );

  const bodyStructurePass =
    sdkBody.bits.length === 720 &&
    sdkBody.refs.length === 1 &&
    sdkBoc.length === 266 &&
    sdkBoc.toString("base64").length === 356;

  console.log(
    "SDK body structure:",
    bodyStructurePass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= bodyStructurePass;

  // =================================================
  // 9. DECODE BODY
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 9. FRESH BODY CONTENT ====="
  );

  const rootSlice =
    sdkBody.beginParse();

  const transferOpcode =
    rootSlice.loadUint(32);

  const queryId =
    rootSlice.loadUintBig(64);

  const encodedAmount =
    rootSlice.loadCoins();

  const encodedDestination =
    rootSlice.loadAddress();

  const encodedResponseDestination =
    rootSlice.loadAddress();

  rootSlice.loadMaybeRef();

  const encodedForwardTonAmount =
    rootSlice.loadCoins();

  const forwardIsRef =
    rootSlice.loadBit();

  const forwardPayload =
    rootSlice.loadRef();

  const provideSlice =
    forwardPayload.beginParse();

  const provideOpcode =
    provideSlice.loadUint(32);

  const encodedRouterWallet =
    provideSlice.loadAddress();

  const refundAddress =
    provideSlice.loadAddress();

  const excessesAddress =
    provideSlice.loadAddress();

  const encodedDeadline =
    Number(
      provideSlice.loadUintBig(64)
    );

  const nestedParameters =
    provideSlice.loadRef();

  const nestedSlice =
    nestedParameters.beginParse();

  const encodedMinLpOut =
    nestedSlice.loadCoins();

  const receiverAddress =
    nestedSlice.loadAddress();

  const bothPositive =
    nestedSlice.loadUint(1);

  const customForwardGas =
    nestedSlice.loadCoins();

  nestedSlice.loadMaybeRef();

  console.log(
    "Transfer opcode:",
    "0x" +
      transferOpcode.toString(16)
  );

  console.log(
    "Query ID:",
    queryId.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Encoded amount:",
    encodedAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Encoded destination:",
    encodedDestination.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Encoded response destination:",
    encodedResponseDestination.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Forward TON amount:",
    encodedForwardTonAmount.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Forward payload reference:",
    forwardIsRef
  );

  console.log(
    "Provide LP opcode:",
    "0x" +
      provideOpcode.toString(16)
  );

  console.log(
    "Router wallet:",
    encodedRouterWallet.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Refund address:",
    refundAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Excesses address:",
    excessesAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Encoded deadline:",
    encodedDeadline
  );

  console.log(
    "Encoded minLpOut:",
    encodedMinLpOut.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "Receiver address:",
    receiverAddress.toString()
  );

  console.log(
    "bothPositive:",
    bothPositive
  );

  console.log(
    "Custom payload forward gas:",
    customForwardGas.toString()
  );

  // =================================================
  // 10. CONTENT VALIDATION
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n===== 10. CONTENT VALIDATION ====="
  );

  const opcodePass =
    transferOpcode ===
      0x0f8a7ea5 &&
    provideOpcode ===
      0x37c096df;

  const queryPass =
    queryId === 0n;

  const amountPass =
    encodedAmount ===
    ROOSTER_AMOUNT;

  const destinationContentPass =
    encodedDestination.toString() ===
    router.toString();

  const responseContentPass =
    encodedResponseDestination.toString() ===
    user.toString();

  const forwardAmountPass =
    encodedForwardTonAmount ===
    EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS;

  const routerWalletPass =
    encodedRouterWallet.toString() ===
    routerPtonWallet.toString();

  const refundPass =
    refundAddress.toString() ===
    user.toString();

  const excessesPass =
    excessesAddress.toString() ===
    user.toString();

  const deadlineContentPass =
    encodedDeadline ===
    deadline;

  const minLpPass =
    encodedMinLpOut ===
    MIN_LP_OUT;

  const receiverPass =
    receiverAddress.toString() ===
    user.toString();

  const bothPositivePass =
    bothPositive === 1;

  const customGasPass =
    customForwardGas === 0n;

  console.log(
    "Opcodes:",
    opcodePass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Query ID:",
    queryPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "ROOSTER amount:",
    amountPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Destination:",
    destinationContentPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Response destination:",
    responseContentPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Forward TON amount:",
    forwardAmountPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Router pTON wallet:",
    routerWalletPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Refund address:",
    refundPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Excesses address:",
    excessesPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Deadline:",
    deadlineContentPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "minLpOut:",
    minLpPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Receiver:",
    receiverPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "bothPositive:",
    bothPositivePass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log(
    "Custom payload gas:",
    customGasPass
      ? "PASS"
      : "FAIL"
  );

  allPass &&= opcodePass;
  allPass &&= queryPass;
  allPass &&= amountPass;
  allPass &&= destinationContentPass;
  allPass &&= responseContentPass;
  allPass &&= forwardAmountPass;
  allPass &&= routerWalletPass;
  allPass &&= refundPass;
  allPass &&= excessesPass;
  allPass &&= deadlineContentPass;
  allPass &&= minLpPass;
  allPass &&= receiverPass;
  allPass &&= bothPositivePass;
  allPass &&= customGasPass;

  // =================================================
  // FINAL
  // =================================================

  console.log(
    "\n================================================="
  );

  console.log(
    " FINAL LIVE PRE-SEND RESULT"
  );

  console.log(
    "================================================="
  );

  if (allPass) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "STATUS: ALL LIVE READ-ONLY CHECKS PASSED"
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "Fresh deadline: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "ROOSTER balance: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "ROOSTER wallet: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "pTON router wallet: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "TX destination: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "Gas = 0.30 TON: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "SDK body structure: PASS"
    );

    console.log(
      "SDK body content: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT."
    );
  } else {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "STATUS: FAIL"
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT."
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "================================================="
  );
}

FILE: scripts\finalPreflightV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;

const EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

const ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300_000_000n;
const PTON_TX_VALUE = 310_249_908n;

const ROOSTER_FORWARD_TON = 235_000_000n;
const PTON_FORWARD_TON = 300_000_000n;

function loadCell(path: string): Cell {
    return Cell.fromBoc(
        readFileSync(path)
    )[0];
}

function pass(name: string) {
    console.log(`${name}: PASS`);
}

function fail(name: string) {
    console.log(`${name}: FAIL`);
}

function checkAddress(
    name: string,
    actual: Address,
    expected: Address
) {
    if (actual.equals(expected)) {
        pass(name);
    } else {
        fail(name);

        console.log(
            '  ACTUAL  :',
            actual.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            '  EXPECTED:',
            expected.toString()
        );
    }
}

function checkBigInt(
    name: string,
    actual: bigint,
    expected: bigint
) {
    if (actual === expected) {
        pass(name);
    } else {
        fail(name);

        console.log(
            '  ACTUAL  :',
            actual.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            '  EXPECTED:',
            expected.toString()
        );
    }
}

function decodeRooster() {

    const root = loadCell(
        './rooster_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const destination = s.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    const rootHasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (rootHasCustomPayload) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER forward payload missing'
        );
    }

    const payload = s.loadRef();

    const p = payload.beginParse();

    const provideOpcode = p.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet = p.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress = p.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress = p.loadAddress();

    const deadline = p.loadUintBig(64);

    const nested = p.loadRef();

    const n = nested.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = n.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress = n.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive = n.loadBit();

    const customGas = n.loadCoins();

    const nestedHasCustomPayload = n.loadBit();

    if (nestedHasCustomPayload) {
        n.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        rootBits: root.bits.length,
        rootRefs: root.refs.length,

        opcode,
        queryId,
        amount,
        destination,
        responseDestination,

        forwardTonAmount,

        provideOpcode,
        routerWallet,
        refundAddress,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        customGas,

        hasCustomPayload:
            rootHasCustomPayload ||
            nestedHasCustomPayload,

        rootRemainingBits:
            s.remainingBits,

        rootRemainingRefs:
            s.remainingRefs,

        provideRemainingBits:
            p.remainingBits,

        provideRemainingRefs:
            p.remainingRefs,

        nestedRemainingBits:
            n.remainingBits,

        nestedRemainingRefs:
            n.remainingRefs,
    };
}

function decodePton() {

    const root = loadCell(
        './pton_v22_generated.boc'
    );

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const tonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        throw new Error(
            'pTON forward payload missing'
        );
    }

    const payload = s.loadRef();

    const p = payload.beginParse();

    const provideOpcode = p.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet = p.loadAddress();

    const refundProvide = p.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress = p.loadAddress();

    const deadline = p.loadUintBig(64);

    const nested = p.loadRef();

    const n = nested.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = n.loadCoins();

    const receiverAddress = n.loadAddress();

    const bothPositive = n.loadBit();

    const customGas = n.loadCoins();

    const nestedHasCustomPayload = n.loadBit();

    if (nestedHasCustomPayload) {
        n.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        rootBits: root.bits.length,
        rootRefs: root.refs.length,

        opcode,
        queryId,
        tonAmount,
        refundAddress,

        provideOpcode,
        routerWallet,
        refundProvide,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        customGas,

        hasCustomPayload:
            nestedHasCustomPayload,

        rootRemainingBits:
            s.remainingBits,

        rootRemainingRefs:
            s.remainingRefs,

        provideRemainingBits:
            p.remainingBits,

        provideRemainingRefs:
            p.remainingRefs,

        nestedRemainingBits:
            n.remainingBits,

        nestedRemainingRefs:
            n.remainingRefs,
    };
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 FINAL PREFLIGHT');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log(' NO WALLET SIGNATURE');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    // ================================================================
    // 1. BASIC IDENTIFICATION
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('1. IDENTIFICATION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROUTER:',
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'POOL:',
        POOL.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'USER:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER:',
        ROOSTER_MASTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON:',
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 2. LIVE POOL DATA
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('2. LIVE POOL DATA');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const poolContract =
        DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL);

    const poolProvider =
        provider.provider(POOL);

    const poolData =
        await poolContract.getPoolData(
            poolProvider
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'POOL:',
        POOL.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LOCKED:',
        poolData.isLocked
    );

    console.log(
        'ROUTER:',
        poolData.routerAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL LP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'RESERVE 0:',
        poolData.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'RESERVE 1:',
        poolData.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'TOKEN 0 WALLET:',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'TOKEN 1 WALLET:',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 3. LIVE POOL ADDRESS CHECKS
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('3. LIVE POOL CHECKS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    checkAddress(
        'POOL ROUTER',
        poolData.routerAddress,
        ROUTER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'POOL TOKEN 0 WALLET',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress,
        ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    checkAddress(
        'POOL TOKEN 1 WALLET',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress,
        ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 4. RESERVES
    // ================================================================

    const reserve0 =
        poolData.reserve0;

    const reserve1 =
        poolData.reserve1;

    const totalLp =
        poolData.totalSupplyLP;

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('4. RESERVE VALUES');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER RESERVE:',
        reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER RESERVE DISPLAY:',
        Number(reserve0) / 1_000_000_000,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON RESERVE:',
        reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON RESERVE DISPLAY:',
        Number(reserve1) / 1_000_000_000,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL LP:',
        totalLp.toString()
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 5. LP CALCULATION
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('5. LP CALCULATION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const lpFromRooster =
        (
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT *
            totalLp
        ) /
        reserve0;

    const lpFromPton =
        (
            PTON_AMOUNT *
            totalLp
        ) /
        reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster <
        lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;

    const calculatedMinLpOut =
        (
            expectedLp *
            95n
        ) /
        100n;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER INPUT:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON INPUT:',
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP FROM ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP FROM pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LIMITING SIDE:',
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? 'ROOSTER'
            : 'pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'EXPECTED LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'CALCULATED 95% MIN LP:',
        calculatedMinLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC MIN LP:',
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 6. LOAD BOC FILES
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('6. LOAD GENERATED BOC FILES');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const rooster =
        decodeRooster();

    const pton =
        decodePton();

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC: LOADED'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC: LOADED'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 7. ROOSTER BOC
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('7. ROOSTER BOC VALIDATION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER TRANSFER OPCODE',
        BigInt(rooster.opcode),
        0x0f8a7ea5n
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP OPCODE',
        BigInt(rooster.provideOpcode),
        0x37c096dfn
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER AMOUNT',
        rooster.amount,
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER DESTINATION',
        rooster.destination,
        ROUTER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER RESPONSE',
        rooster.responseDestination,
        USER
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER FORWARD TON',
        rooster.forwardTonAmount,
        ROOSTER_FORWARD_TON
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER ROUTER pTON WALLET',
        rooster.routerWallet,
        ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER RECEIVER',
        rooster.receiverAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER REFUND',
        rooster.refundAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'ROOSTER EXCESSES',
        rooster.excessesAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER MIN LP',
        rooster.minLpOut,
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOTH POSITIVE:',
        rooster.bothPositive
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'ROOSTER CUSTOM GAS',
        rooster.customGas,
        0n
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
        rooster.hasCustomPayload
            ? 'FAIL'
            : 'PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER ROOT REMAINING:',
        rooster.rootRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        rooster.rootRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER PROVIDE REMAINING:',
        rooster.provideRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        rooster.provideRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER NESTED REMAINING:',
        rooster.nestedRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        rooster.nestedRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 8. pTON BOC
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('8. pTON BOC VALIDATION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON TRANSFER OPCODE',
        BigInt(pton.opcode),
        0x01f3835dn
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON PROVIDE_LP OPCODE',
        BigInt(pton.provideOpcode),
        0x37c096dfn
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON AMOUNT',
        pton.tonAmount,
        PTON_AMOUNT
    );

    checkAddress(
        'pTON REFUND',
        pton.refundAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'pTON ROUTER ROOSTER WALLET',
        pton.routerWallet,
        ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
    );

    checkAddress(
        'pTON RECEIVER',
        pton.receiverAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'pTON PROVIDE REFUND',
        pton.refundProvide,
        USER
    );

    checkAddress(
        'pTON EXCESSES',
        pton.excessesAddress,
        USER
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON MIN LP',
        pton.minLpOut,
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOTH POSITIVE:',
        pton.bothPositive
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON CUSTOM GAS',
        pton.customGas,
        0n
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
        pton.hasCustomPayload
            ? 'FAIL'
            : 'PASS'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON ROOT REMAINING:',
        pton.rootRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        pton.rootRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON PROVIDE REMAINING:',
        pton.provideRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        pton.provideRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON NESTED REMAINING:',
        pton.nestedRemainingBits,
        'bits /',
        pton.nestedRemainingRefs,
        'refs'
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 9. MIN LP SAFETY
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('9. MIN LP SAFETY');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    if (
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT <=
        expectedLp
    ) {
        pass(
            'MIN LP <= EXPECTED LP'
        );
    } else {
        fail(
            'MIN LP <= EXPECTED LP'
        );
    }

    if (
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT ===
        calculatedMinLpOut
    ) {
        pass(
            'MIN LP = 95% EXPECTED LP'
        );
    } else {
        fail(
            'MIN LP = 95% EXPECTED LP'
        );

        console.log(
            'CALCULATED:',
            calculatedMinLpOut.toString()
        );

        console.log(
            'BOC:',
            EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
        );
    }

    // ================================================================
    // 10. TRANSACTION VALUE CHECK
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('10. TRANSACTION VALUE CHECK');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX VALUE:',
        ROOSTER_TX_VALUE.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX VALUE:',
        Number(ROOSTER_TX_VALUE) /
        1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX VALUE:',
        PTON_TX_VALUE.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX VALUE:',
        Number(PTON_TX_VALUE) /
        1_000_000_000,
        'TON'
    );

    const calculatedPtonTxValue =
        PTON_AMOUNT +
        PTON_FORWARD_TON +
        10_000_000n;

    checkBigInt(
        'pTON TX VALUE FORMULA',
        calculatedPtonTxValue,
        PTON_TX_VALUE
    );

    // ================================================================
    // 11. DEADLINES
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('11. DEADLINE CHECK');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const now =
        BigInt(
            Math.floor(
                Date.now() / 1000
            )
        );

    console.log(
        'CURRENT UNIX:',
        now.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER DEADLINE:',
        rooster.deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON DEADLINE:',
        pton.deadline.toString()
    );

    if (
        rooster.deadline > now
    ) {
        pass(
            'ROOSTER DEADLINE FUTURE'
        );
    } else {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER DEADLINE FUTURE'
        );
    }

    if (
        pton.deadline > now
    ) {
        pass(
            'pTON DEADLINE FUTURE'
        );
    } else {
        fail(
            'pTON DEADLINE FUTURE'
        );
    }

    // ================================================================
    // 12. FINAL CONDITIONS
    // ================================================================

    const calculationOk =
        calculatedMinLpOut ===
        EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT;

    const poolOk =
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(ROUTER) &&
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        ) &&
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
        );

    const roosterOk =
        rooster.opcode ===
            0x0f8a7ea5 &&

        rooster.provideOpcode ===
            0x37c096df &&

        rooster.amount ===
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT &&

        rooster.destination.equals(
            ROUTER
        ) &&

        rooster.responseDestination.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        rooster.forwardTonAmount ===
            ROOSTER_FORWARD_TON &&

        rooster.routerWallet.equals(
            ROUTER_PTON_WALLET
        ) &&

        rooster.receiverAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        rooster.refundAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        rooster.excessesAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        rooster.minLpOut ===
            EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT &&

        rooster.bothPositive &&

        rooster.customGas ===
            0n &&

        !rooster.hasCustomPayload &&

        rooster.rootRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        rooster.rootRemainingRefs ===
            0 &&

        rooster.provideRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        rooster.provideRemainingRefs ===
            0 &&

        rooster.nestedRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        rooster.nestedRemainingRefs ===
            0;

    const ptonOk =
        pton.opcode ===
            0x01f3835d &&

        pton.provideOpcode ===
            0x37c096df &&

        pton.tonAmount ===
            PTON_AMOUNT &&

        pton.refundAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        pton.routerWallet.equals(
            ROUTER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        ) &&

        pton.receiverAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        pton.refundProvide.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        pton.excessesAddress.equals(
            USER
        ) &&

        pton.minLpOut ===
            EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT &&

        pton.bothPositive &&

        pton.customGas ===
            0n &&

        !pton.hasCustomPayload &&

        pton.rootRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        pton.rootRemainingRefs ===
            0 &&

        pton.provideRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        pton.provideRemainingRefs ===
            0 &&

        pton.nestedRemainingBits ===
            0 &&

        pton.nestedRemainingRefs ===
            0;

    const deadlinesOk =
        rooster.deadline > now &&
        pton.deadline > now;

    // ================================================================
    // 13. FINAL RESULT
    // ================================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL PREFLIGHT RESULT');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    console.log(
        'LIVE POOL:',
        poolOk
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'LP CALCULATION:',
        calculationOk
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC:',
        roosterOk
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC:',
        ptonOk
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'DEADLINES:',
        deadlinesOk
            ? 'PASS'
            : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (
        poolOk &&
        calculationOk &&
        roosterOk &&
        ptonOk &&
        deadlinesOk
    ) {

        console.log(
            '**************************************************************'
        );

        console.log(
            ' FINAL PREFLIGHT: PASS'
        );

        console.log(
            '**************************************************************'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'READ ONLY: YES'
        );

        console.log(
            'TRANSACTION SENT: NO'
        );

        console.log(
            'WALLET SIGNATURE: NO'
        );

        console.log(
            'POOL MODIFIED: NO'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            '**************************************************************'
        );

        console.log(
            ' FINAL PREFLIGHT: FAIL'
        );

        console.log(
            '**************************************************************'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'DO NOT SIGN OR SEND ANY TRANSACTION.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '======================================================================'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\finalReadOnlyPreflightV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';


// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON
// FINAL READ-ONLY PREFLIGHT
//
// IMPORTANT:
// NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT
// NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL
// NO send()
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// ADDRESSES
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);


// ============================================================
// TEST LIQUIDITY AMOUNTS
// ============================================================

// 100 ROOSTER with 9 decimals
const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;

// 0.000249908 pTON with 9 decimals
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;

// Previously calculated safety minimum
const EXPECTED_LP_OUT = 158009753n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function format9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1000000000n;
    const fraction = (value % 1000000000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : `${whole}`;
}


function percentNumerator(
    numerator: bigint,
    denominator: bigint
): string {

    if (denominator === 0n) {
        return 'N/A';
    }

    const scaled =
        numerator * 10000n / denominator;

    const whole =
        scaled / 100n;

    const fraction =
        (scaled % 100n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(2, '0');

    return `${whole}.${fraction}%`;
}


function printCheck(
    label: string,
    result: boolean
) {
    console.log(
        `${result ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL READ-ONLY PREFLIGHT');
    console.log(' ROOSTER / pTON');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log(' NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL');
    console.log('============================================================');


    // ========================================================
    // 1. BASIC ADDRESSES
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 1. ADDRESSES =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());


    // ========================================================
    // 2. ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 2. ROUTER CHECK =====');

    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );

    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();

    console.log('');
    console.log('routerId:');
    console.log(routerData.routerId.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('dexType:');
    console.log(routerData.dexType);

    console.log('');
    console.log('isLocked:');
    console.log(routerData.isLocked);

    const routerVersion =
        await router.getRouterVersion();

    console.log('');
    console.log('routerVersion:');
    console.log(routerVersion);

    printCheck(
        'Router is v2.2',
        routerVersion.major === 2 &&
        routerVersion.minor === 2
    );

    printCheck(
        'Router is unlocked',
        routerData.isLocked === false
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 3. RESOLVE POOL FROM ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 3. POOL RESOLUTION =====');

    const pool =
        await router.getPool({
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER
        });

    const resolvedPool =
        pool.address;

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router-derived Pool:');
    console.log(resolvedPool.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected Pool:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_POOL.toString());

    const poolAddressMatches =
        resolvedPool.equals(EXPECTED_POOL);

    printCheck(
        'Router-derived Pool matches expected Pool',
        poolAddressMatches
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 4. READ CURRENT POOL DATA
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 4. CURRENT POOL DATA =====');

    const poolContract =
        provider.open(pool);

    const poolData =
        await poolContract.getPoolData();

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool locked:');
    console.log(poolData.isLocked);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool router:');
    console.log(poolData.routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP total supply:');
    console.log(
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER reserve:');
    console.log(
        poolData.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve display:',
        format9(poolData.reserve0)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON reserve:');
    console.log(
        poolData.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve display:',
        format9(poolData.reserve1)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Token0 wallet:');
    console.log(
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Token1 wallet:');
    console.log(
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP fee:');
    console.log(
        poolData.lpFee.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Protocol fee:');
    console.log(
        poolData.protocolFee.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 5. POOL INTEGRITY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 5. POOL INTEGRITY =====');

    printCheck(
        'Pool unlocked',
        poolData.isLocked === false
    );

    printCheck(
        'Pool router matches actual Router',
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        )
    );

    printCheck(
        'Token0 is ROOSTER wallet',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString() !== ''
    );

    printCheck(
        'Token1 is pTON wallet',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString() !== ''
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 6. INPUT AMOUNTS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 6. LIQUIDITY INPUTS =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER input:');
    console.log(
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER display:',
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON input:');
    console.log(
        PTON_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON display:',
        format9(PTON_AMOUNT)
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. CURRENT RESERVE RATIOS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 7. RESERVE RATIO CHECK =====');

    if (
        poolData.reserve0 === 0n ||
        poolData.reserve1 === 0n
    ) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'ERROR: Pool reserve is zero'
        );

        return;
    }


    const roosterRatioNumerator =
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT;

    const roosterRatioDenominator =
        poolData.reserve0;

    const ptonRatioNumerator =
        PTON_AMOUNT;

    const ptonRatioDenominator =
        poolData.reserve1;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER addition / reserve:'
    );

    console.log(
        percentNumerator(
            roosterRatioNumerator,
            roosterRatioDenominator
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON addition / reserve:'
    );

    console.log(
        percentNumerator(
            ptonRatioNumerator,
            ptonRatioDenominator
        )
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 8. CALCULATE EXPECTED LP
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 8. EXPECTED LP CALCULATION =====');

    /*
       For balanced two-sided liquidity:

       LP from token0 =
           amount0 * totalLP / reserve0

       LP from token1 =
           amount1 * totalLP / reserve1

       The limiting side determines the LP minted.
    */


    const lpFromRooster =
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;


    const lpFromPton =
        PTON_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;


    console.log('');
    console.log('LP from ROOSTER side:');
    console.log(
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('LP from pTON side:');
    console.log(
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );


    const calculatedExpectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;


    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculated expected LP:');
    console.log(
        calculatedExpectedLp.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Previously expected LP:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_LP_OUT.toString()
    );


    const expectedDifference =
        calculatedExpectedLp >= EXPECTED_LP_OUT
            ? calculatedExpectedLp - EXPECTED_LP_OUT
            : EXPECTED_LP_OUT - calculatedExpectedLp;


    console.log('');
    console.log('Difference:');
    console.log(
        expectedDifference.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 9. MIN LP OUT
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 9. MIN LP OUT =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log('minLpOut:');
    console.log(
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );


    const minLpBelowExpected =
        MIN_LP_OUT <= calculatedExpectedLp;


    printCheck(
        'minLpOut <= calculated expected LP',
        minLpBelowExpected
    );


    if (calculatedExpectedLp > 0n) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'minLpOut / expected LP:'
        );

        console.log(
            percentNumerator(
                MIN_LP_OUT,
                calculatedExpectedLp
            )
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 10. INPUT BALANCE REQUIREMENT
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 10. REQUIRED INPUTS =====');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER:',
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log(
        'Required pTON:',
        format9(PTON_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'NOTE: This section does not send anything.'
    );

    console.log(
        'User wallet balance verification is performed'
    );

    console.log(
        'through the actual Jetton wallet contracts.'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 11. DERIVED ROUTER JETTON WALLETS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== 11. ROUTER JETTON WALLETS =====');

    const ptonRouterWallet =
        await provider
            .open(
                // pTON v2.1 interface is intentionally
                // created through the public SDK API.
                pTON.v2_1.create(
                    PTON_MASTER
                )
            )
            .getWalletAddress(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON wallet of Router:');
    console.log(
        ptonRouterWallet.toString()
    );


    const roosterRouterWallet =
        await provider
            .open(
                // Jetton minter interface used by the
                // STON.fi SDK through the router flow.
                DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                    ROUTER_ADDRESS
                )
            )
            .getPoolAddress({
                token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                token1: PTON_MASTER
            });


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Pool resolved again from Router:'
    );

    console.log(
        roosterRouterWallet.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 12. BUILD-SAFETY SUMMARY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL PREFLIGHT SUMMARY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    printCheck(
        'Router v2.2',
        routerVersion.major === 2 &&
        routerVersion.minor === 2
    );

    printCheck(
        'Router unlocked',
        routerData.isLocked === false
    );

    printCheck(
        'Pool address correct',
        poolAddressMatches
    );

    printCheck(
        'Pool unlocked',
        poolData.isLocked === false
    );

    printCheck(
        'Pool Router correct',
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        )
    );

    printCheck(
        'ROOSTER amount = 100',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT === 100000000000n
    );

    printCheck(
        'pTON amount = 0.000249908',
        PTON_AMOUNT === 249908n
    );

    printCheck(
        'Expected LP is positive',
        calculatedExpectedLp > 0n
    );

    printCheck(
        'minLpOut is below expected LP',
        MIN_LP_OUT <= calculatedExpectedLp
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 13. FINAL DECISION DATA
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' IMPORTANT VALUES');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Current ROOSTER reserve:');
    console.log(
        format9(poolData.reserve0)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Current pTON reserve:');
    console.log(
        format9(poolData.reserve1)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Current LP supply:');
    console.log(
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculated expected LP:');
    console.log(
        calculatedExpectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Configured minLpOut:');
    console.log(
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER input:');
    console.log(
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON input:');
    console.log(
        format9(PTON_AMOUNT)
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 14. SAFETY STATUS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' TRANSACTION SAFETY STATUS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transaction sent: NO');
    console.log('Tonkeeper approval: NO');
    console.log('Wallet transfer: NO');
    console.log('State changed: NO');

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' PREFLIGHT COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
}

FILE: scripts\finalSdkTxPreflightV22.ts

import { Address, Cell, beginCell } from "@ton/core";
import { DEX } from "@ston-fi/sdk";
import * as fs from "fs";

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
  "EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx";

const USER_WALLET =
  "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix";

const ROUTER =
  "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3";

const PTON_MASTER =
  "EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S";

const ROUTER_PTON_WALLET =
  "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3";

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const EXPECTED_GAS = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS = 240000000n;

const BOC_FILE = "./final_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

function hashCell(cell: Cell): string {
  return cell.hash().toString("hex");
}

export async function run(provider: any) {
  console.log("==============================================");
  console.log(" STON.fi v2.2 SDK TX PARAMETER PREFLIGHT");
  console.log(" READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT");
  console.log("==============================================");

  const master = Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);
  const user = Address.parse(USER_WALLET);
  const router = Address.parse(ROUTER);
  const ptonMaster = Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);
  const routerPtonWallet = Address.parse(ROUTER_PTON_WALLET);

  console.log("\n===== 1. DERIVE USER ROOSTER JETTON WALLET =====");

  const masterProvider = provider.provider(master);

  const result = await masterProvider.get("get_wallet_address", [
    {
      type: "slice",
      cell: beginCell()
        .storeAddress(user)
        .endCell(),
    },
  ]);

  const userJettonWallet = result.stack.readAddress();

  console.log("ROOSTER master:");
  console.log(master.toString());

  console.log("User wallet:");
  console.log(user.toString());

  console.log("Derived user ROOSTER Jetton Wallet:");
  console.log(userJettonWallet.toString());

  console.log("\n===== 2. VERIFY pTON MASTER -> ROUTER WALLET =====");

  const ptonMasterProvider = provider.provider(ptonMaster);

  const ptonResult = await ptonMasterProvider.get(
    "get_wallet_address",
    [
      {
        type: "slice",
        cell: beginCell()
          .storeAddress(router)
          .endCell(),
      },
    ]
  );

  const derivedRouterPtonWallet =
    ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

  console.log("pTON master:");
  console.log(ptonMaster.toString());

  console.log("Expected router pTON wallet:");
  console.log(routerPtonWallet.toString());

  console.log("Derived router pTON wallet:");
  console.log(derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString());

  const ptonWalletPass =
    derivedRouterPtonWallet.toString() ===
    routerPtonWallet.toString();

  console.log(
    "pTON router wallet:",
    ptonWalletPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  if (!ptonWalletPass) {
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");
    return;
  }

  console.log("\n===== 3. SDK ROUTER =====");

  const routerContract =
    DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(router);

  console.log("Router:");
  console.log(routerContract.address.toString());

  console.log("\n===== 4. SDK getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams =====");

  const routerProvider = provider.provider(router);

  const txParams =
    await routerContract.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
      routerProvider,
      {
        userWalletAddress: user,
        sendTokenAddress: master,
        otherTokenAddress: ptonMaster,
        sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
        minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
        gasAmount: EXPECTED_GAS,
        forwardGasAmount: EXPECTED_FORWARD_GAS,
        transferExcessAddress: user,
      }
    );

  console.log("SDK transaction parameters generated.");

  console.log("\n----- SDK TX TO -----");
  console.log(txParams.to.toString());

  console.log("\n----- SDK TX VALUE -----");
  console.log(txParams.value.toString());

  console.log("\n===== 5. VERIFY TX DESTINATION =====");

  const destinationPass =
    txParams.to.toString() ===
    userJettonWallet.toString();

  console.log("Expected TX to:");
  console.log(userJettonWallet.toString());

  console.log("Actual SDK TX to:");
  console.log(txParams.to.toString());

  console.log(
    "TX destination:",
    destinationPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log("\n===== 6. VERIFY GAS VALUE =====");

  const gasPass =
    BigInt(txParams.value.toString()) ===
    EXPECTED_GAS;

  console.log("Expected:", EXPECTED_GAS.toString());
  console.log("Actual:", txParams.value.toString());

  console.log(
    "Gas value:",
    gasPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  if (!txParams.body) {
    throw new Error(
      "SDK returned empty transaction body."
    );
  }

  const sdkBodyCell =
    Cell.fromBoc(txParams.body.toBoc())[0];

const sdkBodyBase64 =
  sdkBodyCell.toBoc().toString("base64");

const SDK_BOC_FILE =
  "./sdk_generated_v22_rooster_liquidity.boc";

fs.writeFileSync(
  SDK_BOC_FILE,
  sdkBodyBase64,
  "utf8"
);

console.log("\nSDK BOC saved:");
console.log(SDK_BOC_FILE);

console.log("SDK BOC base64:");
console.log(sdkBodyBase64);

  console.log("\n===== 7. SDK BODY =====");

  console.log("SDK body SHA256:");
  console.log(hashCell(sdkBodyCell));

  console.log("SDK body BOC chars:");
  console.log(
    sdkBodyCell.toBoc().toString("base64").length
  );

  console.log("SDK body BOC bytes:");
  console.log(
    sdkBodyCell.toBoc().length
  );

  console.log("\n===== 8. COMPARE PREPARED BOC =====");

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_FILE)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Prepared BOC not found: ${BOC_FILE}`
    );
  }

  const preparedBase64 =
    fs.readFileSync(BOC_FILE, "utf8").trim();

  const preparedBodyCell =
    Cell.fromBoc(
      Buffer.from(preparedBase64, "base64")
    )[0];

  const preparedHash =
    hashCell(preparedBodyCell);

  const sdkHash =
    hashCell(sdkBodyCell);

  console.log("Prepared BOC SHA256:");
  console.log(preparedHash);

  console.log("SDK body SHA256:");
  console.log(sdkHash);

  const bodyPass =
    preparedHash === sdkHash;

  console.log(
    "SDK body == prepared BOC:",
    bodyPass ? "PASS" : "FAIL"
  );

  console.log("\n===== 9. FINAL RESULT =====");

  const allPass =
    ptonWalletPass &&
    destinationPass &&
    gasPass &&
    bodyPass;

  if (!allPass) {
    console.log("STATUS: FAIL");
    console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");
    return;
  }

  console.log("STATUS: PASS");
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "pTON master -> router pTON wallet: PASS"
  );
  console.log(
    "TX destination = user ROOSTER Jetton Wallet: PASS"
  );
  console.log(
    "Gas value = 0.30 TON: PASS"
  );
  console.log(
    "SDK body = prepared BOC: PASS"
  );
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "ALL READ-ONLY SDK PREFLIGHT CHECKS PASSED."
  );
  console.log("NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.");
}

FILE: scripts\finalSendPayloadReviewV22.ts
