import { Address } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { createHash } from 'crypto';

// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2 FINAL SEND PAYLOAD REVIEW
//
// READ ONLY
// NO send()
// NO sender.send()
// NO Tonkeeper approval
// NO broadcast
// NO state change
//
// PURPOSE:
// Build the exact final ROOSTER + pTON payloads from SDK,
// serialize their BOCs, calculate hashes, and independently
// verify all final transaction parameters.
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// 1. USER
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);


// ============================================================
// 2. TOKEN MASTERS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);


// ============================================================
// 3. CURRENT STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER
// ============================================================

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);


// ============================================================
// 4. KNOWN POOL
// ============================================================

const POOL_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);


// ============================================================
// 5. POOL JETTON WALLETS
// ============================================================

const POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const POOL_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);


// ============================================================
// 6. USER ROOSTER WALLET
// ============================================================

const USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);


// ============================================================
// 7. LIQUIDITY
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;

const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;


// ============================================================
// 8. VALUES
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300000000n;

const PTON_FORWARD_TON = 300000000n;

const PTON_TRANSFER_GAS = 10000000n;

const PTON_TX_VALUE =
    PTON_AMOUNT +
    PTON_FORWARD_TON +
    PTON_TRANSFER_GAS;

const TOTAL_TX_VALUE =
    ROOSTER_TX_VALUE +
    PTON_TX_VALUE;


// ============================================================
// 9. OPCODES
// ============================================================

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 260734629;

const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 32736093;

const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;


// ============================================================
// 10. SAFETY
// ============================================================

const SAFETY_RESERVE = 100000000n;


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {
    return a.equals(b);
}


function pass(
    label: string,
    condition: boolean
): boolean {

    console.log(
        `${condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );

    return condition;
}


function nanoToTon(
    value: bigint
): number {

    return Number(value) / 1e9;
}


function sha256Hex(
    data: Buffer
): string {

    return createHash('sha256')
        .update(data)
        .digest('hex');
}


function shortHash(
    hash: string
): string {

    return (
        hash.slice(0, 16) +
        '...' +
        hash.slice(-16)
    );
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    let allPass = true;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL SEND PAYLOAD REVIEW'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO BROADCAST'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 1. WALLET
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '1. WALLET'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const sender =
        provider.sender();


    if (!sender.address) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: wallet address unavailable'
        );

    }


    console.log('Connected wallet:');

    console.log(
        sender.address.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Wallet = USER',
            sameAddress(
                sender.address,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 2. ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '2. ROUTER'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();


    console.log('Router:');

    console.log(
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Router ID:');

    console.log(
        routerData.routerId
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Locked:');

    console.log(
        routerData.isLocked
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Router unlocked',
            routerData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 3. POOL
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '3. POOL'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const pool =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(
                POOL_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData();


    console.log('Pool:');

    console.log(
        POOL_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool router:');

    console.log(
        poolData.routerAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool token0:');

    console.log(
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool token1:');

    console.log(
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool router correct',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.routerAddress,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool ROOSTER wallet correct',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token0WalletAddress,
                POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool pTON wallet correct',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token1WalletAddress,
                POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool unlocked',
            poolData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 4. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '4. FRESH DEADLINE'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );


    const deadline =
        now + 600;


    console.log('Current Unix:');

    console.log(
        now
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Deadline:');

    console.log(
        deadline
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Lifetime:');

    console.log(
        deadline - now,
        'seconds'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Fresh 600-second deadline',
            deadline - now === 600
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 5. BUILD ROOSTER LEG
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '5. BUILD FRESH ROOSTER LEG'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline,

        });


    console.log('TO:');

    console.log(
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('VALUE:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(roosterTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER destination = USER ROOSTER wallet',
            sameAddress(
                roosterTx.to,
                USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER value = 0.3 TON',
            BigInt(roosterTx.value) ===
                ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    if (!roosterTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER body unavailable'
        );

    }


    // ========================================================
    // 6. ROOSTER BOC
    // ========================================================

    const roosterBoc =
        roosterTx.body.toBoc();


    const roosterHash =
        sha256Hex(
            roosterBoc
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC bytes:');

    console.log(
        roosterBoc.length
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC SHA-256:');

    console.log(
        roosterHash
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC hash short:');

    console.log(
        shortHash(
            roosterHash
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER BOC exists',
            roosterBoc.length > 0
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 7. DECODE ROOSTER BOC
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '7. ROOSTER BOC DECODE'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const r =
        roosterTx.body.beginParse();


    const rOpcode =
        r.loadUint(32);


    const rQuery =
        r.loadUint(64);


    const rAmount =
        r.loadCoins();


    const rDestination =
        r.loadAddress();


    const rResponse =
        r.loadAddress();


    const rCustom =
        r.loadBit();


    const rForwardTon =
        r.loadCoins();


    const rForward =
        r.loadBit();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER opcode',
            rOpcode ===
                JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER amount',
            rAmount ===
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER body destination = Router',
            sameAddress(
                rDestination,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER response = USER',
            sameAddress(
                rResponse,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom payload absent',
            rCustom === false
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward TON = 0.235',
            rForwardTon ===
                235000000n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward payload present',
            rForward === true
        ) && allPass;


    const rProvideRef =
        r.loadRef();


    const rProvide =
        rProvideRef.beginParse();


    const rProvideOpcode =
        rProvide.loadUint(32);


    const rOtherWallet =
        rProvide.loadAddress();


    const rRefund =
        rProvide.loadAddress();


    const rExcesses =
        rProvide.loadAddress();


    const rDeadline =
        rProvide.loadUint(64);


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            rProvideOpcode ===
                PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER other wallet = pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                rOtherWallet,
                POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                rRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                rExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER deadline',
            rDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    const rLpRef =
        rProvide.loadRef();


    const rLp =
        rLpRef.beginParse();


    const rMinLp =
        rLp.loadCoins();


    const rReceiver =
        rLp.loadAddress();


    const rBothPositive =
        rLp.loadBit();


    const rCustomGas =
        rLp.loadCoins();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER minLpOut',
            rMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                rReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive = true',
            rBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom gas = 0',
            rCustomGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 8. BUILD pTON LEG
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '8. BUILD FRESH pTON LEG'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            proxyTon:
                DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(
                    PTON_MASTER
                ),

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline,

        });


    console.log('TO:');

    console.log(
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('VALUE:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(ptonTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON destination = pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                ptonTx.to,
                POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON value correct',
            BigInt(ptonTx.value) ===
                PTON_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    if (!ptonTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON body unavailable'
        );

    }


    // ========================================================
    // 9. pTON BOC
    // ========================================================

    const ptonBoc =
        ptonTx.body.toBoc();


    const ptonHash =
        sha256Hex(
            ptonBoc
        );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON BOC bytes:');

    console.log(
        ptonBoc.length
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON BOC SHA-256:');

    console.log(
        ptonHash
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON BOC hash short:');

    console.log(
        shortHash(
            ptonHash
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON BOC exists',
            ptonBoc.length > 0
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 10. DECODE pTON BOC
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '10. pTON BOC DECODE'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const p =
        ptonTx.body.beginParse();


    const pOpcode =
        p.loadUint(32);


    const pQuery =
        p.loadUint(64);


    const pAmount =
        p.loadCoins();


    const pRefund =
        p.loadAddress();


    const pForward =
        p.loadBit();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
            pOpcode ===
                TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON query ID = 0',
            pQuery === 0
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON amount',
            pAmount ===
                PTON_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                pRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON forward payload present',
            pForward === true
        ) && allPass;


    const pProvideRef =
        p.loadRef();


    const pProvide =
        pProvideRef.beginParse();


    const pProvideOpcode =
        pProvide.loadUint(32);


    const pOtherWallet =
        pProvide.loadAddress();


    const pRefundNested =
        pProvide.loadAddress();


    const pExcesses =
        pProvide.loadAddress();


    const pDeadline =
        pProvide.loadUint(64);


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            pProvideOpcode ===
                PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON other wallet = ROOSTER Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                pOtherWallet,
                POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                pRefundNested,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                pExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON deadline',
            pDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    const pLpRef =
        pProvide.loadRef();


    const pLp =
        pLpRef.beginParse();


    const pMinLp =
        pLp.loadCoins();


    const pReceiver =
        pLp.loadAddress();


    const pBothPositive =
        pLp.loadBit();


    const pCustomGas =
        pLp.loadCoins();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON minLpOut',
            pMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                pReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON bothPositive = true',
            pBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON custom gas = 0',
            pCustomGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 11. FINAL CROSS CHECK
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '11. FINAL CROSS-CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same USER receiver',
            sameAddress(
                rReceiver,
                pReceiver
            ) &&
            sameAddress(
                rReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same minLpOut',
            rMinLp ===
                pMinLp &&
            rMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same deadline',
            rDeadline ===
                pDeadline &&
            rDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER → pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                rOtherWallet,
                POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON → ROOSTER Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                pOtherWallet,
                POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER TX value correct',
            BigInt(roosterTx.value) ===
                ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TX value correct',
            BigInt(ptonTx.value) ===
                PTON_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Total TX value correct',
            BigInt(roosterTx.value) +
                BigInt(ptonTx.value) ===
                TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 12. BALANCE SAFETY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '12. BALANCE SAFETY'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const userState =
        await provider
            .provider(USER)
            .getState();


    const tonBalance =
        userState.balance;


    const remaining =
        tonBalance -
        TOTAL_TX_VALUE;


    console.log('Current TON:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            tonBalance
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Total TX values:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Remaining:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            remaining
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Safety reserve:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            SAFETY_RESERVE
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Safety reserve remains >= 0.1 TON',
            remaining >=
                SAFETY_RESERVE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 13. FINAL PAYLOAD SUMMARY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '13. FINAL PAYLOAD SUMMARY'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER LEG'
    );

    console.log(
        'TO:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(roosterTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC BYTES:',
        roosterBoc.length
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC SHA256:',
        roosterHash
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON LEG'
    );

    console.log(
        'TO:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(ptonTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC BYTES:',
        ptonBoc.length
    );

    console.log(
        'BOC SHA256:',
        ptonHash
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 14. FINAL RESULT
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '14. FINAL RESULT'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    if (allPass) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'STATUS: FINAL PAYLOADS VERIFIED'
        );

    } else {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'STATUS: PAYLOAD REVIEW FAILED'
        );

    }


    // ========================================================
    // 15. ABSOLUTE SAFETY CONFIRMATION
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        '15. SAFETY CONFIRMATION'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'sender.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet transfer: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'State change: NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'FINAL SEND PAYLOAD REVIEW FINISHED'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\finalSendPreflightV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { JettonMaster } from '@ton/ton';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import crypto from 'crypto';


// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2
// ROOSTER / pTON
//
// FINAL SEND PREFLIGHT
//
// READ ONLY
// NO SEND
// NO BROADCAST
// NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// ADDRESSES
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);


// ============================================================
// INPUTS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
// 100 ROOSTER

const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
// 0.000249908 pTON

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;


// ============================================================
// EXPECTED OPCODES
// ============================================================

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;


// ============================================================
// SAFETY
// ============================================================

const EXTRA_TON_RESERVE = 100000000n;
// 0.1 TON


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function format9(value: bigint): string {

    const whole =
        value / 1000000000n;

    const fraction =
        (value % 1000000000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')
            .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction.length > 0
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : `${whole}`;
}


function pass(
    label: string,
    condition: boolean
): boolean {

    console.log(
        `${condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );

    return condition;
}


function section(
    title: string
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ` ${title}`
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}


function addressEqual(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {

    return a.equals(b);
}


function bodyHash(
    body: Cell
): string {

    return crypto
        .createHash('sha256')
        .update(
            body.toBoc({
                idx: false
            })
        )
        .digest('hex');
}


function unixNow(): bigint {

    return BigInt(
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        )
    );
}


function iso(
    timestamp: bigint
): string {

    return new Date(
        Number(timestamp) * 1000
    ).toISOString();
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    section(
        'STON.fi v2.2 FINAL SEND PREFLIGHT'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO BROADCAST'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TONKEEPER APPROVAL'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO STATE CHANGE'
    );


    let allPass = true;


    // ========================================================
    // 1. USER
    // ========================================================

    section('1. USER');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'User:',
        USER.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 2. ROUTER
    // ========================================================

    section('2. ROUTER VERIFICATION');

    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();


    const routerVersion =
        await router.getRouterVersion();


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Router:',
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Router ID:',
        routerData.routerId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'DEX type:',
        routerData.dexType
    );

    console.log(
        'Locked:',
        routerData.isLocked
    );

    console.log(
        'Version:',
        routerVersion
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Router v2.2',
            routerVersion.major === 2 &&
            routerVersion.minor === 2
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Router unlocked',
            routerData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 3. POOL
    // ========================================================

    section('3. POOL VERIFICATION');

    const pool =
        await router.getPool({
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER
        });


    const poolContract =
        provider.open(pool);


    const poolData =
        await poolContract.getPoolData();


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Resolved Pool:',
        pool.address.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected Pool:',
        EXPECTED_POOL.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool address correct',
            addressEqual(
                pool.address,
                EXPECTED_POOL
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool unlocked',
            poolData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool Router correct',
            addressEqual(
                poolData.routerAddress,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER reserve:',
        format9(poolData.reserve0)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON reserve:',
        format9(poolData.reserve1)
    );

    console.log(
        'LP total supply:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 4. CURRENT BALANCES
    // ========================================================

    section('4. CURRENT WALLET BALANCES');


    const roosterMaster =
        provider.open(
            JettonMaster.create(
                ROOSTER_MASTER
            )
        );


    const roosterWalletAddress =
        await roosterMaster.getWalletAddress(
            USER
        );


    const roosterWalletProvider =
        provider.provider(
            roosterWalletAddress
        );


    const roosterWalletResult =
        await roosterWalletProvider.get(
            'get_wallet_data',
            []
        );


    const roosterBalance =
        roosterWalletResult.stack.readBigNumber();


    const userState =
        await provider
            .provider(USER)
            .getState();


    const tonBalance =
        userState.balance;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER wallet:',
        roosterWalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER balance:',
        format9(roosterBalance)
    );

    console.log(
        'TON balance:',
        format9(tonBalance)
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER balance >= 100',
            roosterBalance >= ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 5. LP CALCULATION
    // ========================================================

    section('5. LP CALCULATION');


    const lpFromRooster =
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve0;


    const lpFromPton =
        PTON_AMOUNT *
        poolData.totalSupplyLP /
        poolData.reserve1;


    const expectedLp =
        lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
            ? lpFromRooster
            : lpFromPton;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'LP from ROOSTER:',
        lpFromRooster.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'LP from pTON:',
        lpFromPton.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Expected LP > 0',
            expectedLp > 0n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'minLpOut <= expected LP',
            MIN_LP_OUT <= expectedLp
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 6. BUILD FRESH SDK TRANSACTIONS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '6. BUILD FRESH SDK TRANSACTION PARAMS'
    );


    const proxyTon =
        pTON.v2_1.create(
            PTON_MASTER
        );


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // ROOSTER LEG
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,
        });


    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // pTON / TON LEG
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            proxyTon,
        });


    const roosterBody =
    roosterTx.body;

const ptonBody =
    ptonTx.body;


if (!roosterBody) {
    throw new Error(
        'ROOSTER transaction body is missing'
    );
}


if (!ptonBody) {
    throw new Error(
        'pTON transaction body is missing'
    );
}


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX to:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX value:',
        format9(
            BigInt(roosterTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON TX to:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX value:',
        format9(
            BigInt(ptonTx.value)
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. FRESH BOC INFORMATION
    // ========================================================

    section('7. FRESH BOC INFORMATION');


    const roosterBoc =
        roosterBody
            .toBoc({
                idx: false
            })
            .toString('base64');


    const ptonBoc =
        ptonBody
            .toBoc({
                idx: false
            })
            .toString('base64');


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC:'
    );

    console.log(
        roosterBoc
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BODY SHA256:'
    );

    console.log(
        bodyHash(roosterBody)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON BOC:'
    );

    console.log(
        ptonBoc
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON BODY SHA256:'
    );

    console.log(
        bodyHash(ptonBody)
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 8. DECODE FRESH ROOSTER BOC
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '8. DECODE FRESH ROOSTER BOC'
    );


    const decodedRoosterRoot =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                roosterBoc,
                'base64'
            )
        )[0];


    const roosterSlice =
        decodedRoosterRoot.beginParse();


    const roosterOpcode =
        roosterSlice.loadUint(32);


    const roosterQueryId =
        roosterSlice.loadUintBig(64);


    const decodedRoosterAmount =
        roosterSlice.loadCoins();


    const roosterDestination =
        roosterSlice.loadAddress();


    const roosterResponseDestination =
        roosterSlice.loadAddress();


    const roosterCustomPayload =
        roosterSlice.loadBit();


    if (
        roosterCustomPayload
    ) {

        roosterSlice.loadRef();
    }


    const roosterForwardAmount =
        roosterSlice.loadCoins();


    const roosterForwardPayloadIsRef =
        roosterSlice.loadBit();


    const roosterForwardPayload =
        roosterForwardPayloadIsRef
            ? roosterSlice.loadRef().beginParse()
            : roosterSlice;


    const roosterProvideOpcode =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadUint(32);


    const roosterRouterWallet =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const roosterRefund =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const roosterExcesses =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const roosterDeadline =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadUintBig(64);


    const roosterAdditionalData =
        roosterForwardPayload.loadRef()
            .beginParse();


    const roosterMinLpOut =
        roosterAdditionalData.loadCoins();


    const roosterReceiver =
        roosterAdditionalData.loadAddress();


    const roosterBothPositive =
        roosterAdditionalData.loadBit();


    const roosterCustomGas =
        roosterAdditionalData.loadCoins();


    const roosterCustomPayloadBit =
        roosterAdditionalData.loadBit();


    if (
        roosterCustomPayloadBit
    ) {

        roosterAdditionalData.loadRef();
    }


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + roosterOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'Amount:',
        format9(decodedRoosterAmount),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        roosterDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Response:',
        roosterResponseDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward TON:',
        format9(roosterForwardAmount),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Nested opcode:',
        '0x' + roosterProvideOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'Router wallet:',
        roosterRouterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Refund:',
        roosterRefund.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses:',
        roosterExcesses.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        roosterDeadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline UTC:',
        iso(roosterDeadline)
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        roosterMinLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Receiver:',
        roosterReceiver.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        roosterBothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom gas:',
        roosterCustomGas.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 9. VERIFY ROOSTER BOC
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '9. VERIFY ROOSTER BOC'
    );


    const now =
        unixNow();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER opcode correct',
            roosterOpcode ===
            JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER amount correct',
            decodedRoosterAmount ===
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER destination = Router',
            addressEqual(
                roosterDestination,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER response = USER',
            addressEqual(
                roosterResponseDestination,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER nested opcode = PROVIDE_LP',
            roosterProvideOpcode ===
            PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER router wallet = Pool pTON wallet',
            addressEqual(
                roosterRouterWallet,
                poolData.token1WalletAddress
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER refund = USER',
            addressEqual(
                roosterRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER excesses = USER',
            addressEqual(
                roosterExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER minLpOut correct',
            roosterMinLpOut ===
            MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER receiver = USER',
            addressEqual(
                roosterReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive = 1',
            roosterBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER deadline is in the future',
            roosterDeadline > now
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 10. DECODE FRESH pTON BOC
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '10. DECODE FRESH pTON BOC'
    );


    const decodedPtonRoot =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                ptonBoc,
                'base64'
            )
        )[0];


    const ptonSlice =
        decodedPtonRoot.beginParse();


    const ptonOpcode =
        ptonSlice.loadUint(32);


    const ptonQueryId =
        ptonSlice.loadUintBig(64);


    const decodedPtonAmount =
        ptonSlice.loadCoins();


    const ptonDestination =
        ptonSlice.loadAddress();


    const ptonForwardPayloadIsRef =
        ptonSlice.loadBit();


    const ptonForwardPayload =
        ptonForwardPayloadIsRef
            ? ptonSlice.loadRef().beginParse()
            : ptonSlice;


    const ptonProvideOpcode =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadUint(32);


    const ptonRouterWallet =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const ptonRefund =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const ptonExcesses =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadAddress();


    const ptonDeadline =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadUintBig(64);


    const ptonAdditionalData =
        ptonForwardPayload.loadRef()
            .beginParse();


    const ptonMinLpOut =
        ptonAdditionalData.loadCoins();


    const ptonReceiver =
        ptonAdditionalData.loadAddress();


    const ptonBothPositive =
        ptonAdditionalData.loadBit();


    const ptonCustomGas =
        ptonAdditionalData.loadCoins();


    const ptonCustomPayloadBit =
        ptonAdditionalData.loadBit();


    if (
        ptonCustomPayloadBit
    ) {

        ptonAdditionalData.loadRef();
    }


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' + ptonOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON amount:',
        format9(decodedPtonAmount),
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        ptonDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Nested opcode:',
        '0x' + ptonProvideOpcode.toString(16)
    );

    console.log(
        'Router wallet:',
        ptonRouterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Refund:',
        ptonRefund.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses:',
        ptonExcesses.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        ptonDeadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline UTC:',
        iso(ptonDeadline)
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        ptonMinLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Receiver:',
        ptonReceiver.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        ptonBothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom gas:',
        ptonCustomGas.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 11. VERIFY pTON BOC
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '11. VERIFY pTON BOC'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON opcode = TON_TRANSFER',
            ptonOpcode ===
            TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON amount correct',
            decodedPtonAmount ===
            PTON_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON destination = USER',
            addressEqual(
                ptonDestination,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON nested opcode = PROVIDE_LP',
            ptonProvideOpcode ===
            PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON router wallet = Pool ROOSTER wallet',
            addressEqual(
                ptonRouterWallet,
                poolData.token0WalletAddress
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            addressEqual(
                ptonRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON excesses = USER',
            addressEqual(
                ptonExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON minLpOut correct',
            ptonMinLpOut ===
            MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON receiver = USER',
            addressEqual(
                ptonReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON bothPositive = 1',
            ptonBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON deadline is in the future',
            ptonDeadline > now
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 12. DEADLINE CONSISTENCY
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '12. DEADLINE CONSISTENCY'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Current timestamp:',
        now.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Current UTC:',
        iso(now)
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER deadline:',
        iso(roosterDeadline)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON deadline:',
        iso(ptonDeadline)
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER deadline >= current time',
            roosterDeadline > now
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON deadline >= current time',
            ptonDeadline > now
        ) && allPass;


    const deadlineDifference =
        roosterDeadline >
        ptonDeadline
            ? roosterDeadline - ptonDeadline
            : ptonDeadline - roosterDeadline;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Deadline difference:',
        deadlineDifference.toString(),
        'seconds'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Deadline difference <= 5 seconds',
            deadlineDifference <= 5n
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 13. TRANSACTION VALUE / GAS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '13. TRANSACTION VALUE / GAS'
    );


    const roosterValue =
        BigInt(
            roosterTx.value
        );


    const ptonValue =
        BigInt(
            ptonTx.value
        );


    const totalValue =
        roosterValue +
        ptonValue;


    const requiredWithReserve =
        totalValue +
        EXTRA_TON_RESERVE;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX value:',
        format9(roosterValue),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX value:',
        format9(ptonValue),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'TOTAL TX values:',
        format9(totalValue),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Safety reserve:',
        format9(EXTRA_TON_RESERVE),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Recommended minimum:',
        format9(requiredWithReserve),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Current wallet:',
        format9(tonBalance),
        'TON'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'TON covers both TX values',
            tonBalance >= totalValue
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'TON covers TX values + 0.1 TON reserve',
            tonBalance >= requiredWithReserve
        ) && allPass;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TON remaining after TX values:',
        format9(
            tonBalance - totalValue
        ),
        'TON'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 14. TRANSACTION DESTINATION CHECK
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '14. TRANSACTION DESTINATION CHECK'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER TX goes to USER ROOSTER wallet',
            addressEqual(
                roosterTx.to,
                roosterWalletAddress
            )
        ) && allPass;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX destination:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log(
        'Expected ROOSTER wallet:',
        roosterWalletAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'pTON TX destination:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log(
        'Expected pTON pool wallet:',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TX destination = pool pTON wallet',
            addressEqual(
                ptonTx.to,
                poolData.token1WalletAddress
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 15. FINAL INPUT SUMMARY
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '15. FINAL INPUT SUMMARY'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER input:',
        format9(ROOSTER_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON input:',
        format9(PTON_AMOUNT)
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected LP:',
        expectedLp.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX value:',
        format9(roosterValue),
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX value:',
        format9(ptonValue),
        'TON'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 16. FINAL STATUS
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '16. FINAL STATUS'
    );


    console.log('');


    if (allPass) {

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log(
            ' STATUS: READY TO SEND'
        );

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

    } else {

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log(
            ' STATUS: NOT READY'
        );

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );
    }


    // ========================================================
    // 17. ABSOLUTE SAFETY
    // ========================================================

    section(
        '17. SAFETY CONFIRMATION'
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'wallet.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet state change: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool state change: NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FINAL SEND PREFLIGHT FINISHED'
    );
}
