import { Address } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

// ============================================================
// STON.fi v2.2 FINAL TRANSACTION REVIEW
//
// READ ONLY
// NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT
// NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED
// NO BROADCAST
//
// PURPOSE:
// Build BOTH fresh SDK transactions and independently validate
// destinations, values, BOC bodies and cross-consistency.
// ============================================================


// ============================================================
// 1. USER
// ============================================================

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);


// ============================================================
// 2. TOKEN MASTERS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);


// ============================================================
// 3. STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER / POOL
// ============================================================

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const POOL_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);


// ============================================================
// 4. POOL JETTON WALLETS
// ============================================================

// ROOSTER Pool wallet
const EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

// pTON Pool wallet
const EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);


// ============================================================
// 5. USER ROOSTER WALLET
// ============================================================

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);


// ============================================================
// 6. LIQUIDITY PARAMETERS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n; // 100 ROOSTER

const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;


// ============================================================
// 7. GAS / VALUE PARAMETERS
// ============================================================

const ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300000000n;

const PTON_FORWARD_TON = 300000000n;

const PTON_TRANSFER_GAS = 10000000n;

const PTON_TX_VALUE =
    PTON_AMOUNT +
    PTON_FORWARD_TON +
    PTON_TRANSFER_GAS;

const TOTAL_TX_VALUE =
    ROOSTER_TX_VALUE +
    PTON_TX_VALUE;


// ============================================================
// 8. OPCODES
// ============================================================

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 260734629;

const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 32736093;

const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;


// ============================================================
// 9. SAFETY RESERVE
// ============================================================

const SAFETY_RESERVE = 100000000n;


// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {
    return a.equals(b);
}


function pass(
    label: string,
    condition: boolean
): boolean {

    console.log(
        `${condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${label}`
    );

    return condition;
}


function nanoToTon(
    value: bigint
): number {

    return Number(value) / 1e9;
}


// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    let allPass = true;


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL TRANSACTION REVIEW'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED'
    );
    console.log(
        ' NO BROADCAST'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 1. CONNECTED WALLET
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 1. CONNECTED WALLET'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const sender = provider.sender();


    if (!sender.address) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: connected wallet address unavailable'
        );

    }


    const connectedWallet =
        sender.address;


    console.log('Connected wallet:');

    console.log(
        connectedWallet.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Connected wallet = USER',
            sameAddress(
                connectedWallet,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 2. CONSTANTS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 2. CONSTANTS'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('ROUTER:');

    console.log(
        ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');

    console.log(
        POOL_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER MASTER:');

    console.log(
        ROOSTER_MASTER.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON MASTER:');

    console.log(
        PTON_MASTER.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER amount:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON amount:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            PTON_AMOUNT
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('MIN LP OUT:');

    console.log(
        MIN_LP_OUT.toString()
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 3. ROUTER LIVE CHECK
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 3. ROUTER LIVE CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();


    console.log('Router ID:');

    console.log(
        routerData.routerId
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Router locked:');

    console.log(
        routerData.isLocked
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Router v2.2 unlocked',
            routerData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 4. POOL LIVE CHECK
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 4. POOL LIVE CHECK'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const pool =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(
                POOL_ADDRESS
            )
        );


    const poolData =
        await pool.getPoolData();


    console.log('Pool:');

    console.log(
        POOL_ADDRESS.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool router:');

    console.log(
        poolData.routerAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool token0:');

    console.log(
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool token1:');

    console.log(
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool locked:');

    console.log(
        poolData.isLocked
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool unlocked',
            poolData.isLocked === false
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool Router = v2.2 Router',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.routerAddress,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool token0 = ROOSTER wallet',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token0WalletAddress,
                EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool token1 = pTON wallet',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token1WalletAddress,
                EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 5. USER ROOSTER WALLET
    //
    // SDK v2.2 Router does not expose getJettonWallet().
    // The already independently verified user wallet is used.
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 5. USER ROOSTER WALLET'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const userRoosterWallet =
        EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET;


    console.log('USER ROOSTER wallet:');

    console.log(
        userRoosterWallet.toString()
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'USER ROOSTER wallet correct',
            sameAddress(
                userRoosterWallet,
                EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 6. LIVE BALANCES
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 6. LIVE BALANCES'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const userState =
        await provider
            .provider(USER)
            .getState();


    const tonBalance =
        userState.balance;


    console.log('TON balance:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            tonBalance
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'TON covers both TX values',
            tonBalance >=
                TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'TON covers TX values + safety reserve',
            tonBalance >=
                TOTAL_TX_VALUE +
                SAFETY_RESERVE
        ) && allPass;


    const roosterState =
        await provider
            .provider(userRoosterWallet)
            .getState();


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER wallet state:');

    console.log(
        roosterState.state
    );


    // ========================================================
    // 7. FRESH DEADLINE
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 7. FRESH DEADLINE'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const currentUnix =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );


    const deadline =
        currentUnix + 600;


    console.log('Current Unix:');

    console.log(
        currentUnix
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Deadline:');

    console.log(
        deadline
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Lifetime:');

    console.log(
        deadline - currentUnix,
        'seconds'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Deadline = current + 600 seconds',
            deadline - currentUnix === 600
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 8. FRESH ROOSTER TRANSACTION
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 8. FRESH ROOSTER TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            sendTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT,

            otherTokenAddress:
                PTON_MASTER,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline,

        });


    console.log('ROOSTER TX destination:');

    console.log(
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER TX value:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                roosterTx.value
            )
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER TX destination = USER ROOSTER wallet',
            sameAddress(
                roosterTx.to,
                userRoosterWallet
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER TX value = 0.3 TON',
            BigInt(
                roosterTx.value
            ) ===
                ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 9. ROOSTER BODY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 9. ROOSTER BODY REVIEW'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    if (!roosterTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: ROOSTER SDK body is empty'
        );

    }


    const roosterOuter =
        roosterTx.body.beginParse();


    const roosterOpcode =
        roosterOuter.loadUint(32);


    const roosterQueryId =
        roosterOuter.loadUint(64);


    const roosterAmount =
        roosterOuter.loadCoins();


    const roosterDestination =
        roosterOuter.loadAddress();


    const roosterResponse =
        roosterOuter.loadAddress();


    const roosterCustomBit =
        roosterOuter.loadBit();


    const roosterForwardTon =
        roosterOuter.loadCoins();


    const roosterHasForward =
        roosterOuter.loadBit();


    console.log('ROOSTER opcode:');

    console.log(
        '0x' +
        roosterOpcode.toString(16)
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER transfer opcode',
            roosterOpcode ===
                JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER amount = 100',
            roosterAmount ===
                ROOSTER_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER destination = Router',
            sameAddress(
                roosterDestination,
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER response = USER',
            sameAddress(
                roosterResponse,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom payload absent',
            roosterCustomBit === false
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward TON = 0.235',
            roosterForwardTon ===
                235000000n
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER forward payload present',
            roosterHasForward === true
        ) && allPass;


    const roosterProvideRef =
        roosterOuter.loadRef();


    const roosterProvide =
        roosterProvideRef.beginParse();


    const roosterProvideOpcode =
        roosterProvide.loadUint(32);


    const roosterOtherWallet =
        roosterProvide.loadAddress();


    const roosterRefund =
        roosterProvide.loadAddress();


    const roosterExcesses =
        roosterProvide.loadAddress();


    const roosterDeadline =
        roosterProvide.loadUint(64);


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            roosterProvideOpcode ===
                PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER leg other wallet = pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                roosterOtherWallet,
                EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                roosterRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                roosterExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER deadline',
            roosterDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    const roosterLpRef =
        roosterProvide.loadRef();


    const roosterLp =
        roosterLpRef.beginParse();


    const roosterMinLp =
        roosterLp.loadCoins();


    const roosterReceiver =
        roosterLp.loadAddress();


    const roosterBothPositive =
        roosterLp.loadBit();


    const roosterCustomGas =
        roosterLp.loadCoins();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER minLpOut',
            roosterMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                roosterReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive = true',
            roosterBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER custom gas = 0',
            roosterCustomGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 10. FRESH pTON TRANSACTION
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 10. FRESH pTON TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({

            userWalletAddress:
                USER,

            proxyTon:
                DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(
                    PTON_MASTER
                ),

            otherTokenAddress:
                ROOSTER_MASTER,

            sendAmount:
                PTON_AMOUNT,

            minLpOut:
                MIN_LP_OUT,

            receiverAddress:
                USER,

            refundAddress:
                USER,

            excessesAddress:
                USER,

            deadline,

        });


    console.log('pTON TX destination:');

    console.log(
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX value:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            BigInt(
                ptonTx.value
            )
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TX destination = pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                ptonTx.to,
                EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TX value correct',
            BigInt(
                ptonTx.value
            ) ===
                PTON_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 11. pTON BODY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 11. pTON BODY REVIEW'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    if (!ptonTx.body) {

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: pTON SDK body is empty'
        );

    }


    const ptonOuter =
        ptonTx.body.beginParse();


    const ptonOpcode =
        ptonOuter.loadUint(32);


    const ptonQueryId =
        ptonOuter.loadUint(64);


    const ptonAmount =
        ptonOuter.loadCoins();


    const ptonRefund =
        ptonOuter.loadAddress();


    const ptonHasForward =
        ptonOuter.loadBit();


    console.log('pTON opcode:');

    console.log(
        '0x' +
        ptonOpcode.toString(16)
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
            ptonOpcode ===
                TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON query ID = 0',
            ptonQueryId === 0
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON amount',
            ptonAmount ===
                PTON_AMOUNT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonRefund,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON forward payload present',
            ptonHasForward === true
        ) && allPass;


    const ptonProvideRef =
        ptonOuter.loadRef();


    const ptonProvide =
        ptonProvideRef.beginParse();


    const ptonProvideOpcode =
        ptonProvide.loadUint(32);


    const ptonOtherWallet =
        ptonProvide.loadAddress();


    const ptonRefundNested =
        ptonProvide.loadAddress();


    const ptonExcesses =
        ptonProvide.loadAddress();


    const ptonDeadline =
        ptonProvide.loadUint(64);


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            ptonProvideOpcode ===
                PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON leg other wallet = ROOSTER Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                ptonOtherWallet,
                EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON refund = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonRefundNested,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON excesses = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonExcesses,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON deadline',
            ptonDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    const ptonLpRef =
        ptonProvide.loadRef();


    const ptonLp =
        ptonLpRef.beginParse();


    const ptonMinLp =
        ptonLp.loadCoins();


    const ptonReceiver =
        ptonLp.loadAddress();


    const ptonBothPositive =
        ptonLp.loadBit();


    const ptonCustomGas =
        ptonLp.loadCoins();


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON minLpOut',
            ptonMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON receiver = USER',
            sameAddress(
                ptonReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON bothPositive = true',
            ptonBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON custom gas = 0',
            ptonCustomGas === 0n
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 12. CROSS CHECKS
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 12. CROSS-CHECKS'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same USER on both legs',
            sameAddress(
                roosterReceiver,
                ptonReceiver
            ) &&
            sameAddress(
                roosterReceiver,
                USER
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same minLpOut',
            roosterMinLp ===
                ptonMinLp &&
            roosterMinLp ===
                MIN_LP_OUT
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Same deadline',
            roosterDeadline ===
                ptonDeadline &&
            roosterDeadline ===
                deadline
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'bothPositive on both legs',
            roosterBothPositive === true &&
            ptonBothPositive === true
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'ROOSTER leg points to pTON Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                roosterOtherWallet,
                EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON leg points to ROOSTER Pool wallet',
            sameAddress(
                ptonOtherWallet,
                EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Pool token wallets consistent',
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token0WalletAddress,
                EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
            ) &&
            sameAddress(
                poolData.token1WalletAddress,
                EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
            )
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 13. TX VALUE ARITHMETIC
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 13. TX VALUE ARITHMETIC'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('ROOSTER TX:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON amount:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            PTON_AMOUNT
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON forward TON:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            PTON_FORWARD_TON
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON transfer gas:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            PTON_TRANSFER_GAS
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON TX:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            PTON_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('TOTAL:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'pTON TX arithmetic',
            PTON_TX_VALUE ===
                PTON_AMOUNT +
                PTON_FORWARD_TON +
                PTON_TRANSFER_GAS
        ) && allPass;


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Both TX values consistent',
            BigInt(
                roosterTx.value
            ) +
            BigInt(
                ptonTx.value
            ) ===
                TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 14. FINAL BALANCE SAFETY
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 14. FINAL BALANCE SAFETY'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    const remainingAfterBoth =
        tonBalance -
        TOTAL_TX_VALUE;


    console.log('Current TON:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            tonBalance
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Required TX values:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Remaining after both TX values:'
    );

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            remainingAfterBoth
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Safety reserve:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            SAFETY_RESERVE
        )
    );


    allPass =
        pass(
            'Remaining TON >= 0.1 TON reserve',
            remainingAfterBoth >=
                SAFETY_RESERVE
        ) && allPass;


    // ========================================================
    // 15. FINAL RESULT
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' 15. FINAL RESULT'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    if (allPass) {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'STATUS: READY FOR SEND REVIEW'
        );

    } else {

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'STATUS: NOT READY'
        );

    }


    console.log('');
    console.log('Current TON:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            tonBalance
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Required TX values:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            TOTAL_TX_VALUE
        )
    );


    console.log('');
    console.log('Safety reserve:');

    console.log(
        nanoToTon(
            SAFETY_RESERVE
        )
    );


    // ========================================================
    // SAFETY CONFIRMATION
    // ========================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' SAFETY CONFIRMATION'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'sender.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet transfer: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'State change: NO'
    );


    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'FINAL TRANSACTION REVIEW FINISHED'
    );
}
